### [1] [Lesions Involving Medial Anterior Forebrain Pathway Circuitry Destabilize Phrase Timing in Adult Canary Song](https://www.biorxiv.org/content/10.64898/2026.07.11.737998v3)
*Hulsey-Vincent, M. R.,Vengrovski, G.,Sova, E.,Gardner, T. J.*

Main category: bioRxiv.Neuroscience

TL;DR: 该研究通过双侧兴奋性毒性损毁成年金丝雀AFP的内侧与外侧亚区，并开发高通量歌曲标注流程，发现内侧AFP参与成鸟歌曲乐句时长的在线控制，挑战了AFP在歌曲学习完成后即非必需的旧观点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基底神经节-丘脑-皮质环路对学习复杂运动序列至关重要，但其如何控制灵活运动行为仍不清楚。鸣禽前脑前通路（AFP）是同源结构，既往认为只参与歌曲运动学习而不参与歌曲表演，但近期证据显示AFP可影响某些物种的歌曲句法，这一争议需要重新检验。

Method: 对成年金丝雀实施靶向AFP内侧与外侧亚区的双侧兴奋性毒性损毁；开发高通量歌曲标注流程，在自监督TweetyBERT模型中加入有监督分类器，消除UMAP聚类的内存瓶颈，实现每只鸟数千首歌曲的短语级分析。

Result: 涉及内侧AFP的损毁产生类似口吃的行为（过渡前音节重复延长且多变），乐句时长变异性显著增加；该效应在内侧+外侧联合损毁的鸟中最强，而仅损毁外侧AFP的鸟中未观察到。变异性增加在损毁后整个记录期持续存在，并伴随音节声学结构的微小变化。

Conclusion: 内侧AFP参与成年金丝雀歌曲乐句时长的持续控制，说明AFP在歌曲学会后并非可有可无；内侧AFP可作为理解基底神经节与皮质动力学如何维持复杂习得运动序列的可操作模型。

Abstract: Basal ganglia-thalamocortical circuits are essential for learning complex motor sequences, yet how they control flexible motor behavior remains poorly understood. The homologous songbird Anterior Forebrain Pathway (AFP) drives song motor learning and was previously thought not to play a role in song performance, as early lesions showed no effect. This perspective has recently been revised by evidence that the AFP influences song syntax in some species. We revisit this question in adult canaries by performing bilateral excitotoxic lesions targeting the lateral and medial subdivisions of the AFP. To measure behavioral changes, we developed a high-throughput song-annotation pipeline that incorporates a supervised classifier into the self-supervised TweetyBERT model. This removed the memory bottleneck of UMAP clustering, enabling phrase-level analysis across thousands of songs per bird. We find that lesions involving the medial AFP produce a stuttering-like behavior, defined here as prolonged, variable syllable repetition before transitions, resulting in a significant increase in phrase-duration variability. This effect was strongest in birds with medial and lateral AFP lesions, and was not observed in birds with lateral-only AFP lesions. The increased variability persisted throughout the post-lesion recording period and was accompanied by small changes in the acoustic structure of syllables. Our results implicate the medial AFP in the ongoing control of phrase duration in adult canary song, challenging the view that the AFP is dispensable once song is learned. These findings position the medial AFP as a tractable model for understanding how basal ganglia and cortical dynamics maintain complex learned motor sequences.

</details>


### [2] [Human-Specific Regulation of CHD2 Controls the Tempo of Cortical Development through Lysosomal Homeostasis](https://www.biorxiv.org/content/10.1101/2025.01.21.634145v2)
*Leonardi, O.,Lewerissa, E.,Aprile, D.,Barakat, T. S.,Culotta, L.,Deng, R.,Haazen, L.,Linda, K.,Majoie, M.,Mordelt, A.,Ortale, A.,Oudakker, A.,Prazzoli, F.,Puvogel, S.,Scheefhals, N.,Schelhaas, H. J.,Schoenmaker, C.,van Hugte, E.,van Bokhoven, H.,Verhoeven, J.,Villa, E.,Wypych, P.,Vitriolo, A.,Boeckx, C.,Testa, G.,Kasri, N. N.*

Main category: bioRxiv.Neuroscience

TL;DR: 本文发现一个人类特异性增强子变异通过降低染色质重塑因子CHD2表达，影响溶酶体功能与自噬流，进而调控神经元成熟速度，并揭示该轴是皮层发育进化与神经发育疾病的因果可干预机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基因调控的进化改变如何连接脑发育与神经发育疾病易感性，其机制仍不清楚；作者试图寻找可解释人类特异性脑发育特征及其相关疾病风险的分子通路。

Method: 鉴定人类特异性增强子变异并验证其对CHD2表达的调控；结合基因组编辑、跨灵长类iPSC模型、皮层类器官、单细胞转录组、患者来源细胞及神经网络分析，考察CHD2剂量对溶酶体功能与神经元成熟的影响，并通过遗传或药理手段恢复溶酶体功能进行救援实验。

Result: CHD2剂量双向调控溶酶体功能与自噬体流，从而决定神经元成熟节奏；祖先化及非人灵长类模型中较高的CHD2表达增强溶酶体降解能力，加速树突与突触成熟；CHD2单倍不足导致相反缺陷并扰乱更广泛的神经发育转录程序；遗传或药理恢复溶酶体功能可挽救CHD2单倍不足神经元的成熟缺陷。

Conclusion: CHD2与溶酶体稳态构成调控皮层发育速度的关键分子轴，溶酶体功能障碍是CHD2相关神经发育缺陷的因果且可治疗机制，该轴贯穿进化与疾病过程。

Abstract: The mechanisms linking evolutionary changes in gene regulation to brain development and neurodevelopmental disease susceptibility remain poorly understood. Here, we identify a human-specific variant in an enhancer region that reduces expression of the chromatin remodeler CHD2. We investigate the variant's functional consequences using genome editing, cross-primate induced pluripotent stem cell models, cortical organoids, single-cell transcriptomics, patient-derived cells, and neuronal network analyses. We demonstrate that CHD2 dosage bidirectionally regulates lysosomal function and autophagosome flux to set the tempo of neuronal maturation. Higher CHD2 expression, as found in ancestralized and non-human primate models, enhances lysosomal degradative capacity and accelerates dendritic and synaptic maturation. Conversely, CHD2 haploinsufficiency yields reciprocal defects and disrupts broader neurodevelopmental transcriptional programs. Restoring lysosomal function genetically or pharmacologically rescues neuronal maturation in CHD2-haploinsufficient neurons, establishing lysosomal dysfunction as a causal and therapeutically tractable mechanism. These findings reveal that CHD2 and lysosomal homeostasis constitute a critical molecular axis regulating the pace of cortical development across evolution and disease.

</details>


### [3] [Ion modulation restores local synaptic function in APOE4 neuronal networks despite persistent impairments in global network organization](https://www.biorxiv.org/content/10.64898/2026.09.08.750089v1)
*Groenlie, M. B.,Mikalsen Kollstroem, A.,Sandvig, A.,Sandvig, I.*

Main category: bioRxiv.Neuroscience

TL;DR: 本研究以等基因APOE3为对照，发现APOE4人源神经元网络出现进行性结构功能退化（神经突增长、分支减少、放电率与活跃电极数下降）、代偿性超同步化及突触AMPAR水平降低；离子稳态调控可恢复局部放电动态并稳定AMPAR水平，但无法改善超同步化与形态异常，说明APOE4网络保留局部适应可塑性，却无法恢复高阶网络组织。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: APOE4是散发性阿尔茨海默病最强的遗传风险因素，而离子功能障碍被认为是APOE4相关病理的核心机制，关系膜兴奋性、突触传递与可塑性。作者前期已证明离子功能障碍导致早期AD网络易损，且通过离子调控激活突触可塑性机制可恢复神经退行性疾病的网络平衡，因此希望验证离子调控引发的突触动员能否改善人源APOE4神经元网络的功能障碍。

Method: 采用人源神经元网络，比较APOE4与等基因APOE3对照；评估网络的结构与功能指标（神经突长度、神经突分支、放电率、活跃记录电极数、同步化水平、突触AMPAR水平），并通过调控离子稳态以动员突触可塑性机制，观察其对局部放电动态与突触AMPAR水平的影响。

Result: APOE4网络表现出进行性结构与功能退化：神经突长度增加、分支减少，放电率与活跃记录电极数下降；伴随代偿性超同步化，使网络易受病理扩散影响，且突触AMPAR水平较等基因APOE3对照降低。离子稳态调控可恢复局部放电动态并稳定突触AMPAR水平，但未能缓解APOE4网络升高的同步化与异常形态。

Conclusion: APOE4神经元网络保留了通过突触可塑性实现适应性重塑、恢复局部神经元功能的能力，但该恢复不能扩展到高阶网络组织，提示局部功能修复与网络级同步化/形态异常之间存在解离，后者可能是APOE4相关病理更难干预的环节。

Abstract: The apolipoprotein E epsilon 4 (APOE4) allele is the strongest genetic risk factor for developing sporadic Alzheimer's disease (AD), which accounts for the vast majority of AD cases. Ion dysfunction has emerged as a central mechanism involved in APOE4-related pathology, reflecting the importance of ion homeostasis for cell membrane excitability, synaptic transmission, and plasticity. We have previously demonstrated that ion dysfunction contributes to early AD-related network vulnerability, and that engaging synaptic plasticity mechanisms via ion modulation can restore network balance in neurodegenerative disease. Here, we investigated whether synaptic mobilization by ion modulation could ameliorate APOE4-associated dysfunction in human neuronal networks compared with isogenic APOE3 controls. APOE4 networks exhibited progressive structural and functional deterioration, manifesting as increased neurite length, reduced neurite branching, and reduced firing rate and number of active recording electrodes. This was accompanied by compensatory hypersynchronization, rendering networks vulnerable to pathological spread, and reduced synaptic AMPAR levels compared with isogenic APOE3 control networks. Modulating ion homeostasis restored the local firing dynamics and stabilized synaptic AMPAR levels. However, the elevated synchronization and aberrant morphology of APOE4 networks were not alleviated by engaging synaptic plasticity mechanisms, demonstrating that APOE4 networks retain a capacity for adaptive plasticity enabling restoration of local neuronal function, but that this recovery does not extend to higher-order network organization.

</details>


### [4] [Weak electric fields of deep brain stimulation can entrain spiking in multi-compartment cortical neuron models](https://www.biorxiv.org/content/10.64898/2026.09.08.750085v1)
*Bosman, M.,Doorn, N.,Meijer, H. G. E.,Heida, T.,Schwab, B. C.*

Main category: bioRxiv.Neuroscience

TL;DR: 该研究通过多隔室计算模型证明，DBS产生的弱皮层电场可直接调制部分皮层神经元的放电时序，其效应随电场幅值和频率增加，并依赖神经元类型与电场方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DBS的疗效机制尚不明确。既往认为其作用经由受刺激的皮层下结构及网络间接影响皮层，但新近研究显示DBS期间皮层电场强度可与经颅交流电刺激调制神经活动的强度相当甚至更强，故需探究这些弱电场能否直接调制皮层放电时序。

Method: 构建覆盖全部皮层层的五类神经元多隔室计算模型，施加模拟DBS脉冲的电场；电场幅值覆盖DBS期间皮层电场的典型范围，并变化频率与方向；以刺激前后时间直方图评估锁相，用相位锁定值（PLV）定量。

Result: 弱DBS电场通过提高或降低刺激脉冲后立即放电的概率来调制部分神经元的放电时序，表现为对刺激的锁相；PLV随电场幅值和频率升高而增大；调制的幅度与方向因神经元类型而异，并依赖电场方向。

Conclusion: DBS的皮层电场可能直接作用于部分皮层神经元，与既有的经皮层下靶点及其网络介导的间接皮层效应并存，为理解DBS的皮层作用及其疗效与副作用的潜在机制提供了新视角。

Abstract: Background: Deep brain stimulation (DBS) is widely used to treat neurological disorders, but how it exerts its therapeutic effects remains an open question. Although DBS primarily acts through stimulated subcortical structures and their networks, recent studies have demonstrated that cortical electric field (E-field) strengths generated during DBS are comparable to, and can exceed, those shown to modulate neuronal activity with transcranial alternating current stimulation. We therefore investigated whether and how these weak DBS fields can directly modulate cortical spike timing. Methods: We used multi-compartment computational models of five neuron types across all cortical layers and exposed them to E-fields modeled as DBS pulses. E-field amplitudes spanned the typical range of cortical E-field strengths during DBS, while frequency and orientation were varied. Entrainment was assessed using peri-stimulus time histograms and quantified by the phase locking value (PLV). Results: Weak DBS fields modulated spike timing of some neurons by either increasing or decreasing the likelihood of firing immediately following the stimulation pulse. This modulation reflected entrainment to the stimulation, with the PLV increasing with E-field amplitude and frequency. The magnitude and direction of spike-timing modulation varied across neuron types and depended on the orientation of the field. Conclusion: These findings suggest that cortical E-fields of DBS may directly influence activity of some cortical neurons, alongside the established indirect cortical effects mediated by subcortical targets and their networks. This provides a new perspective on how DBS may influence cortical activity and offers insights into its potential mechanisms of therapeutic and/or side effects.

</details>


### [5] [An electronically steerable epidural ultrasound interface for deep brain neuromodulation in freely moving rats](https://www.biorxiv.org/content/10.64898/2026.09.10.750612v1)
*Ratz, L.,Rivandi, H.,Wardhana, G. K.,Sarkar, E.,Aqamolaei, M.,Tong, Y.,Desmarais, S.,Sohail, L.,Donaldson, P. D.,Aloia, V.,Arlotti, M.,Coenen, V. A.,Muratore, D. G.,Spyropoulos, G. D.,Costa, T. L.,Dobrossy, M.*

Main category: bioRxiv.Neuroscience

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可植入硬膜外聚焦超声（eFUS）器件，采用二维压电换能器阵列与定制ASIC集成，实现连续、慢性、多靶点可电子调控的深部脑神经调控，并在大鼠中验证其靶向腹侧被盖区可增加伏隔核多巴胺释放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有低强度聚焦超声（LI-FUS）仅能经颅施加，只适合间歇性、阶段性刺激；而多项神经与精神疾病临床应用需要慢性、连续刺激才能获得长期稳定的治疗效果。因此亟需一种可植入、可长期连续工作且能多靶点调控的超声神经调控方案。

Method: 设计并制作微型化eFUS器件：将二维压电换能器阵列直接集成在定制ASIC上，通过软件定义焦点坐标实现电子可操控的聚焦超声，用于大鼠脑原理验证。开展体外声学表征验证波束转向与聚焦精度，并进行在体实验，在清醒自由活动大鼠中靶向刺激腹侧被盖区，结合光纤光度记录检测伏隔核多巴胺释放，最后对靶区进行死后组织学分析。

Result: 体外声学表征证实波束转向与聚焦准确，并显示该技术具备能量效率；在体实验表明可可靠刺激深部皮层下靶点并产生可测生理效应——刺激腹侧被盖区引起伏隔核多巴胺释放增加，经光纤光度记录确认。组织学分析显示靶区无炎症标志物，但器件的硬膜外放置伴随轻度组织损伤。

Conclusion: 该研究从体外证实eFUS技术的能量效率与可操控性，并在在体层面提供了对深部皮层下脑结构具有神经调控能力的生理学证据，为慢性、连续、多靶点神经调控提供了新型可植入方案。

Abstract: Low-intensity focused ultrasound (LI-FUS) clinical trials exploit either the neural activity modulating, or the blood-brain barrier opening, capacity of this stimulation modality. However, LI-FUS currently is applied only transcranially which means that it is conducive only for episodic and intermittent stimulation, although, clinical data shows that in numerous neurological and psychiatric applications, chronic and continuous stimulation is required for long-term, stable therapeutic effect. The paper presents experimental data on a novel and innovative approach describing an implantable, epidural focus ultrasound (eFUS) device, designed for continuous, chronic and multi-site steerable neuromodulation. The miniaturized eFUS device consists of a two-dimensional piezoelectric transducer array directly integrated onto a custom ASIC, specifically engineered for proof-of-principle neuromodulation studies in the rat brain. The system generates electronically steerable focused ultrasound with software-defined focal coordinates, sufficient to stimulate neuronal activity in deep brain structures. In vitro acoustic characterization confirmed accurate beam steering and focusing, while in vivo validation demonstrated reliable stimulation of a deep subcortical target with measurable physiological effects. eFUS-mediated targeting of the ventral tegmental area in awake and freely moving rats produced increase in dopamine release in the nucleus accumbens as confirmed using fiber photometry recordings. Post-mortem histological analysis of the target regions showed the absence of inflammatory markers, although the epidural placement of the eFUS device was associated with mild tissue damage. Overall, the study provides in vitro data demonstrating the energy efficiency, and steerability of the technology, and in vivo physiological evidence of neuromodulatory ability of a deep, subcortical brain structure.

</details>


### [6] [A precise atlas of the human subcortex](https://www.biorxiv.org/content/10.64898/2026.02.13.705755v2)
*Friedrich, H.,Sahin, A. I.,ALHO, E. J. L.,Rajamani, N.,Milanese, V.,Oxenford, S.,Brammerloh, M.,Mustin, M.,Zvarova, P.,Krüger, L.,Demann, R.,Goede, L.,Meyer, G.,Kirilina, E.,Matthies, C.,Volkmann, J.,Pijar, J.,Horisawa, S.,Howard, C.,Garimella, A.,Li, N.,Fox, M. D.,Friedrich, M.,Bochtler, A.,Edlow, B. L.,Neudorfer, C.,Horn, A.*

Main category: bioRxiv.Neuroscience

TL;DR: 该研究构建了一个各向同性分辨率100 μm的高分辨率人脑图谱，包含95个手工分割的灰质与白质结构及82条白质纤维束，可精确形变配准到个体脑解剖，为皮质下立体定向手术、影像研究与神经调控提供基础资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床干预与皮质下神经影像需要超越现有可形变脑图谱分辨率与解剖细节的解剖学定义，尤其是基底节、丘脑、底丘脑、中脑和小脑等区域的精细结构界定。

Method: 整合离体MRI、组织学、纤维剥离解剖与神经解剖学教材等多来源资料，手工分割95个灰质与白质结构及82条白质纤维束，在100 μm各向同性分辨率下定义图谱，并实现向个体受试者脑解剖的精确形变配准。

Result: 得到一个可在100 μm分辨率下精确定义皮质下灰白质结构、且能精确形变至个体脑解剖的高分辨率脑图谱，覆盖基底节、丘脑、底丘脑、中脑及小脑及其周边结构。

Conclusion: 该图谱为立体定向手术、皮质下脑影像研究以及下一代神经调控策略的开发提供了基础性资源。

Abstract: Clinical interventions and neuroimaging in the subcortex require anatomical definitions that exceed the resolution and anatomical detail of currently available deformable brain atlases. Here, we introduce a high-resolution human brain atlas comprising 95 manually segmented grey and white matter structures as well as 82 white matter tracts compiled from a multitude of resources including ex-vivo MRI, histology, fibre dissections, and neuroanatomy textbooks. The atlas is defined at an isotropic resolution of 100 m and can be precisely deformed to individual subject brain anatomy. By providing precise definitions of both grey and white matter structures within and around the basal ganglia, thalamus, subthalamus, midbrain and cerebellum, the atlas provides a foundational resource for stereotactic surgery and subcortical brain imaging research, as well as for development of next-generation neuromodulation strategies.

</details>


### [7] [Soluble pathogenic tau transmission to astrocytes drives acute oxidative damage, cellular senescence, and neurovascular uncoupling in a model of Alzheimers tauopathy](https://www.biorxiv.org/content/10.64898/2026.09.08.750215v2)
*makhlouf, H.,Dorigatti, A.,Hernandez, S.,de Rosa, N.,Miller, L.,Hussong, S.,Holstein, D.,Lechleiter, J.,logan, S.,Kayed, R.,Galvan, V.*

Main category: bioRxiv.Neuroscience

TL;DR: 可溶性病理性tau聚集体经肝素敏感途径进入星形胶质细胞，引发线粒体功能障碍与衰老，进而以非细胞自主方式损害神经元结构和神经血管耦合；线粒体ROS清除可部分缓解该表型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前研究已发现可溶性病理性tau寡聚体可进入脑微血管内皮细胞，诱导细胞衰老与微血管功能障碍。作者希望进一步明确tau在星形胶质细胞中的传递及其对神经血管单元和神经元结构的影响，以揭示tau病变中神经血管耦合受损的新机制。

Abstract: We previously found that soluble pathogenic tau aggregates (tau oligomers) enter brain microvascular endothelial cells and induce cellular senescence and microvascular dysfunction in a tauopathy mouse model. This study shows that soluble pathogenic tau is also transmitted to astrocytes, where it induces mitochondrial dysfunction, mediates senescence, and impairs neurovascular coupling responses. Single-cell RNA sequencing of hTau mouse cortex revealed astrocytes as one of the most transcriptionally altered cell type, showing coordinated downregulation of electron transport chain genes and upregulation of stress-induced and inflammatory markers, also elevated in hTau mouse brain and astrocyte-enriched fractions. Mitochondrial ROS scavenging with Mito TEMPO reduced tau-induced SASP cytokine activation in astrocytes in vitro and in vivo. Astrocyte-targeted SOD2 overexpression attenuated the hTau-related deficit in evoked cerebral blood flow responses; however, this partial improvement in response magnitude and duration did not achieve statistical significance compared to hTau mice expressing GFP. The model accounted for these observations through selective top-down gain amplification of apical dendritic inputs, accompanied by reduced reward-prediction errors and increased confidence in outcome predictions. Upon rule-reversal, the lateral orbitofrontal cortex, through disinhibitory VIP interneurons, encoded a context-prediction error signaling a loss of confidence. The hierarchy of reward- and context-prediction errors across cortical areas is mirrored in top-down signals modulating apical activity of simulated pyramidal neurons in the primary sensory cortex. The model explains how contextual changes are detected and how reward- and context-prediction error signals, originating in different cortical regions, interact to reshape the sensory representation.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 两种搜索任务招募相同的皮质区域、振荡频率和加工层级；动态时间规整显示两条神经轨迹互为时间拉伸版本，同一时间缩放也出现在同一条件内快慢试次之间，表明其反映反应速度而非任务类型差异。α频段振荡通过两种协同机制支持该时间缩放：同步后部皮质网络的活动，并在两种条件下以相同偏好相位门控局部高频活动（70–150 Hz）。

Abstract: How the brain produces different behaviors from a fixed anatomical substrate is a foundational question in neuroscience. The dominant account holds that behavioral flexibility requires distinct neural circuits: dedicated systems selectively recruited for different processing modes. An alternative proposes that flexibility arises not from switching between circuits, but from dynamically controlling the speed at which a shared circuit operates. Here, we test this account directly using intracranial EEG recordings from nineteen human patients performing two visual search tasks of markedly different difficulty: an easy color-singleton search and a difficult orientation search, a fast vs. slow dissociation attributed for four decades to distinct anatomical and oscillatory neural systems. We show that both search tasks recruit the same cortical regions, oscillatory frequencies, and processing hierarchy. Dynamic time warping reveals that the two neural trajectories are time-stretched versions of one another; the same temporal scaling appears within each condition between faster and slower trials, indexing response speed rather than the distinction between tasks. Alpha-band oscillations (~8-10 Hz) support this temporal scaling through two coordinated mechanisms: synchronizing activity across a posterior cortical network and phase-gating local high-frequency activity (70-150 Hz) within those regions at the same preferred phase in both conditions. These findings establish that, for the two tasks studied here, fast and slow visual search are not implemented by selecting between distinct dedicated circuits, but by adjusting the speed of a single alpha-coordinated architecture. The same principle may apply to other search tasks, and to other domains in which dual-process architectures have been invoked, a testable prediction for future studies.

Conclusion: 所提出的皮层-皮层下注意回路可能是哺乳动物认知能力的生物学基础。

Abstract: The neocortex is central to mammalian cognition, yet a computational framework that is both biologically constrained and capable of performing complex cognitive tasks remains missing. Here we show that cortico-thalamic circuits are well suited to implement multi-head self- and cross-attention, the mechanism underlying the cognitive abilities of transformer networks. We propose that layer 2/3 pyramidal cells maintain a recurrent key-value memory, while layer 5 pyramidal cells decode the memory retrieved by an incoming query. The computation of keys, values and queries maps onto core and matrix thalamo-cortical projections, distributed across the micro- and macro-columns of a cortical area. One cortical area forms an attention head, and cortex a multi-head self-attention network. The same thalamo-cortical microcircuit also calculates sensory prediction errors guiding gradient-based synaptic plasticity. A reward-prediction error gates via basal ganglia the cortical output and the re-activation of hippocampal memories. The trained network aligns with human intracranial recordings during speech perception. Overall, the suggested cortico-subcortical attention circuit may represent a substrate for the cognitive capacity of mammals.

### [12] [The Reuse-and-Append Memory Principle: Application to Latent Cause Inference](https://www.biorxiv.org/content/10.64898/2026.09.09.750332v1)
*Ben Houidi, Z.,Gershman, S. J.*

Main category: bioRxiv.Neuroscience

TL;DR: 提出Reuse-and-Append Memory (RAM)机制框架，用稀疏编码、Hebbian学习与模式匹配实现记忆的"重用-追加"推断，解释恐惧条件化中的习得、消退、更新与自发恢复等现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜在原因理论(Latent cause theory)虽给出大脑决定更新旧记忆还是形成新记忆的计算解释，但未说明其神经实现机制。本文旨在为这一推断过程提供具体的神经机制原理。

Method: 基于三条机制原则构建计算模型：(1)稀疏神经编码；(2)Hebbian学习；(3)模式匹配动力学。核心机制为：编码既往经验的神经元被相似刺激自动重激活，而未承诺(uncommitted)神经元被募集以编码真正新颖的方面（含时间流逝）。

Result: 模型复现了恐惧条件化中的习得、消退、更新(renewal)与自发恢复。RAM将通常被视为单一预测误差的更新/形成驱动信号分解为两个独立信号：由覆盖度分配新神经元产生的即时新颖性信号，以及结果不符(mismatch)信号（在预期结果未出现时激活安全回路）。RAM还复现了恢复对提醒刺激时序的依赖(Monfils-Schiller效应)，但预测该效应本质上脆弱，与混合的实验证据一致。

Conclusion: RAM表明Monfils-Schiller效应（常被归于再巩固）并不破坏原始恐惧记忆：桥接事件通过离线共提取将安全信息带入新情境。更广泛地，本文所解释的现象均无需修改既有记忆；最稳定的构型是该原则的极端形式——始终追加、从不覆写。

Abstract: The latent cause theory of memory modification provides a computational account of how the brain decides whether to update existing memories or form new ones, but leaves unspecified the neural mechanisms implementing this inference. We propose Reuse-and-Append Memory (RAM), a set of mechanistic principles that achieve the same goal through sparse neural coding, Hebbian learning, and pattern-matching dynamics. The core idea is that neurons encoding prior experiences are automatically reactivated by similar stimuli, while uncommitted neurons are recruited to encode genuinely novel aspects of each experience, including the passage of time. We present a computational model instantiating these principles and show that it reproduces acquisition, extinction, renewal, and spontaneous recovery in fear conditioning. By committing to a neural mechanism, we found that RAM separates what is typically modeled as a single prediction error driving memory updating or formation into two independent signals: an immediate novelty signal that emerges from coverage-based allocation of new neurons, and an outcome mismatch signal that later activates safety circuits when an expected outcome fails to arrive. RAM also reproduces the dependence of recovery on the timing of stimulus reminders (the Monfils-Schiller effect), but predicts it to be inherently fragile, consistent with the mixed empirical record. RAM suggests that the Monfils-Schiller effect, often attributed to reconsolidation, leaves the original fear memory intact: a bridging event carries safety information to new contexts through offline co-retrieval. More broadly, none of the phenomena we explain require modifying existing memories, and the most stable configuration is the extreme form of the principle: always append, never overwrite.

</details>

Abstract: We investigate the structural information retained in the distribution of explosion ion trajectories from proteins subjected to strong ionization. Using molecular-dynamics simulations and machine-learning analysis benchmarked against an analytical approach, we show that low-resolution structural information can be retrieved from the explosion distributions alone. An ensemble of convolutional neural networks trained on simulated spherical ion maps recovers the radius of gyration and the three semi-axes of an ellipsoidal molecular envelope with prediction errors of 1.2~\textÅ and 1.5~\textÅ, respectively, while an analytical ellipsoid charge model returns similar estimates and performs best for globular structures. We further investigate how higher-level structural information and symmetries can be extrapolated from ion measurements. This study supports the viability for structural determination of single proteins without large-scale X-ray facilities.

</details>

*Ran Tao,Nathaniel C. Esteves,Wanho Lee,Lauren Altman,Liuni Chen,David Gao,Ling Li,Yongsam Kim,Jun Zhu,Sookkyung Lim,Arnold J. T. M. Mathijssen*

Main category: physics.bio-ph

TL;DR: 本文结合微流控、定向进化、遗传学、全息成像与流体动力学模拟，揭示细菌逆流游动（趋流性）的生物物理决定因素：多鞭毛（周生鞭毛）通过稳定近壁停留和剪切驱动的"风向标效应"重定向细胞逆流，比游动速度更关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细菌逆流游动驱动其扩散与表面定植，病原菌在肠道、肺和泌尿道感染时会遇到流体流动，因此理解细菌如何利用鞭毛对抗流动对疾病与治疗具有重要意义。但形态与鞭毛排布如何调控抗流运动仍属未知。

Method: 结合微流控、定向进化、遗传学、三维全息成像与流体动力学模拟，开展逆流游动竞争实验、运动性选择实验、过表达主调控因子flhD/C，并进行单细胞追踪与完全解析鞭毛排布和流固耦合的模拟。

Result: 周生鞭毛的大肠杆菌和肠炎沙门氏菌在逆流竞争中迅速胜过单鞭毛的铜绿假单胞菌和霍乱弧菌，上游富集密度高出可达五个数量级，尽管弧菌游动速度快三倍。运动性选择实验显示趋流性随鞭毛数目和长度增加而增强。三维全息与单细胞追踪表明多鞭毛稳定表面停留并促进"风向标效应"使细胞上游重定向，模拟进一步支持该机制。

Conclusion: 多鞭毛是细菌上游导航的关键促进因素，其机制由近壁停留和剪切驱动的重定向主导，而非游动速度。

Abstract: Upstream swimming drives bacterial spreading and surface colonization. Many pathogens encounter fluid flows as they infect the intestines, lungs, and urinary tract, so how bacteria use their flagella to counter these flows matters for disease and treatment. Yet how morphology and flagellar arrangement govern motility against flow remains unknown. Here, we investigate the biophysical determinants of rheotaxis by combining microfluidics, directed evolution, genetics, holography, and hydrodynamics simulations. Using upstream swimming competitions, we find that peritrichous E. coli and S. enterica rapidly outcompete monotrichous P. aeruginosa and V. cholerae, accumulating upstream at densities up to five orders of magnitude higher, even though Vibrio swims three times as fast. Motility selection experiments show that rheotaxis increases with flagellar number and length, confirmed by overexpressing the master regulator flhD/C. Three-dimensional holography and single-cell tracking reveal that multiflagellarity stabilizes surface residence and promotes the weathervane effect that reorients cells upstream, a mechanism further supported by simulations that fully resolve flagellar arrangement and fluid-structure interactions. These results establish multiflagellarity as a key facilitator of upstream navigation, governed by near-wall residence and shear-driven reorientation rather than by swimming speed.

</details>

### [15] [nBMS, a Neuromorphic Battery Management System with a Silicon-Validated Spiking State-of-Charge Core for eVTOL Aircraft](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13506)
*İsmail Can Dikmen*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 该研究为eVTOL设计事件驱动脉冲神经网络SoC估计器（nBMS神经形态BMS核心），在22电芯eVTOL数据集上评估，RMSE 2.45%略高于LSTM的1.74%，但计算量、抗噪性和能耗显著占优，并已在Artix-7 FPGA上实现比特精确部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: eVTOL的SoC估计需在严格的能量与适航认证预算下机载运行，且其工况与汽车领域文献差异显著，传统方法难以兼顾精度、鲁棒性与能效。

Method: 提出事件驱动脉冲网络作为nBMS神经形态电池管理架构的状态估计核心：采用delta与群体编码器、二阶sigma-delta脉冲层和速率累加读出，共34,433个int16参数，零稠密MAC操作；在公开22电芯eVTOL数据集上逐时间步估计SoC，并做汽车场景交叉验证，与调优LSTM及自适应LIF基线对比，最后在Artix-7 FPGA上部署。

Result: RMSE为2.45%（LSTM为1.74%，差距归因于巡航时序混合限制）；在传感器噪声下退化速度比自适应LIF慢1.6倍；每步约需3,500次加法，LSTM需67,700次乘累加；FPGA占87% BRAM，50 MHz满足时序，在491步真实飞行片段上比特精确复现定点参考；每次推理1.30 μJ，0.5 Hz任务节奏下平均0.65 μW。

Conclusion: 该脉冲估计器以轻微精度损失换取计算、鲁棒性与能效的显著优势，并完成低成本车规FPGA上的比特精确硬件验证，证明神经形态方案在eVTOL机载SoC估计中的可行性。

Abstract: State-of-charge (SoC) estimation for electric vertical take-off and landing (eVTOL) aircraft must run on the vehicle under hard energy and certification budgets, on a duty cycle unlike anything in the automotive literature. In this study an event-driven spiking network, the state-estimation core of the nBMS neuromorphic battery management architecture, is designed for per-timestep SoC estimation and evaluated on a public 22-cell eVTOL dataset with an automotive cross-check. A delta and population encoder, a second-order sigma-delta spiking layer, and a rate-accumulator readout hold 34{,}433 int16 parameters with zero dense multiply-accumulate (MAC) operations. The estimator reaches 2.45\% root-mean-square error (RMSE) against 1.74\% for a tuned long short-term memory (LSTM) baseline, a gap characterized as a temporal-mixing limit in cruise; in exchange it degrades 1.6 times slower than an adaptive leaky integrate-and-fire control under sensor noise and needs roughly 3{,}500 additions per step where the LSTM needs 67{,}700 multiply-accumulates. The full core is deployed on a low-cost automotive-qualified Artix-7 field-programmable gate array at 87\% block-RAM utilization, meets timing at 50~MHz, and reproduces the frozen fixed-point reference bit-exactly over a real 491-step flight segment on silicon. Fully annotated post-implementation analysis gives 1.30~$μ$J per inference, an average of 0.65~$μ$W at the 0.5~Hz mission cadence.

</details>


### [16] [Early-Stopping Thresholds for ES-HyperNEAT: A Data-Driven Approach from Fitness Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13533)
*Romain Claret,Arthur Gygax,Michael O'Neill,Paul Cotofrei,Pascal Felber*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 该论文将ES-HyperNEAT的早停问题建模为基于早期适应度轨迹的二分类任务，通过阈值规则在验证试验上取得F1=0.872，节省41.6%计算成本，且比Hyperband更高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数ES-HyperNEAT超参数配置会导致网络停滞在随机猜测水平，浪费大量计算资源，因此需要一种有效的早停机制来减少无效计算。

Method: 将早停视为对早期适应度轨迹的二元分类：对每次试验计算"每代最优适应度"的累积中位数，并与阈值比较；阈值由初始90次试验数据集上最大化F1得分确定，得到规则（G*=3，T*=0.140）。

Result: 在180次独立验证试验上F1=0.872，保留超过90%的成功试验，同时削减41.6%计算成本；与Hyperband相比效率高64%且平均适应度更高，但Hyperband偶尔能发现更高的峰值解；在已收敛的搜索种群上规则过于激进（召回率31.1%）。

Conclusion: 该阈值规则具有ES-HyperNEAT特异性，但"从适应度动态分类中推导停止准则"的方法论可推广到其他易停滞超参数空间的进化算法；未来需引入自适应阈值以应对收敛种群。

Abstract: Most hyperparameter configurations for Evolvable-Substrate HyperNEAT (ES-HyperNEAT) produce networks that stagnate at random-guessing performance, wasting computational resources. We frame early stopping as binary classification on early fitness trajectories: for each trial, we compute the cumulative median of best-per-generation fitness and test it against a threshold derived by maximizing the F1 score on an initial 90-trial dataset. The resulting rule (generation G* = 3, threshold T* = 0.140) achieves F1 = 0.872 on 180 independent validation trials, retaining over 90% of successful trials while cutting computational cost by 41.6%. Compared to Hyperband, our domain-specific rule is 64% more efficient with higher mean fitness, though Hyperband occasionally discovers higher peak solutions. On a converged search population the rule becomes too aggressive (recall 31.1%), motivating adaptive thresholds. The specific thresholds are ES-HyperNEAT-specific, but the methodology, deriving stopping criteria from fitness dynamics classification, is applicable to other evolutionary algorithms with stagnation-prone hyperparameter spaces.

</details>


### [17] [Surrogate-Assisted Genetic Programming with Phenotypic Characterisation in Dynamic Multi-Mode Project Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14418)
*Yuan Tian,Yi Mei,Mengjie Zhang*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 该研究探讨在动态多模式资源受限项目调度中，如何为代理辅助遗传编程设计表型特征编码方案，发现二值编码配合欧氏距离提供最有效且稳健的代理引导，其机制在于去除表型冗余子代并保持行为多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态多模式资源受限项目调度需在紧前约束、资源有限、多种执行模式及不确定活动工期下决策。遗传编程可自动演化启发式规则，但基于仿真的适应度评估计算代价高昂，因此在固定全仿真评估预算下如何提升规则质量成为关键问题；核心疑问是GP个体应如何编码为表型特征以支持有效的适应度估计。

Method: 设计三种信息丰富度不同的表型特征编码方案：保留规则原始输出的优先级值编码、刻画候选排序的秩编码、表示最终调度决策的二值编码；并将这些编码与不同距离度量结合，以衡量GP个体间的行为相似性，在固定全仿真适应度评估预算下开展代理辅助GP实验，并进行消融实验分析重复去除与代理预选择的作用。

Result: 实验表明，二值编码配合欧氏距离提供最有效且最稳健的代理引导。进一步分析显示，代理估计精度本身并不能完全解释性能差异；表型特征表示还决定了表型冗余子代被去除的效率以及预选择后行为多样性的保留程度。消融实验证明重复去除与代理预选择具有互补收益，二者结合带来最大改进。

Conclusion: 有效的代理辅助遗传编程不仅取决于识别有前景的子代，还取决于在演化搜索中控制冗余并保持有用多样性；表型特征编码方案的选择是决定代理引导成效的关键因素。

Abstract: Dynamic multi-mode resource-constrained project scheduling requires decisions to be made under precedence constraints, limited resources, multiple execution modes, and uncertain activity durations. Genetic programming (GP) can automatically evolve heuristic rules for such problems, but its simulation-based fitness evaluation is computationally expensive. This study investigates phenotypic characterisation (PC) in surrogate-assisted GP to evolve higher-quality scheduling heuristics under a fixed budget of full simulation-based fitness evaluations. A key question is how GP individuals should be encoded into phenotypic characterisations to support effective fitness estimation. To answer this question, three PC encoding schemes with different levels of information richness are designed: priority-value encoding, which preserves raw rule outputs; rank encoding, which captures candidate ordering; and binary encoding, which represents final scheduling decisions. These encodings are combined with different distance metrics to measure behavioural similarity between GP individuals. The experimental results show that binary encoding with Euclidean distance provides the most effective and robust surrogate guidance. Further analyses show that surrogate estimation accuracy alone does not fully explain the performance differences. The PC representation also determines how effectively phenotypically redundant offspring are removed and how much behavioural diversity is retained after preselection. Ablation experiments further demonstrate that duplicate removal and surrogate preselection provide complementary benefits, with their combination producing the largest improvement. These findings highlight that effective surrogate-assisted GP depends not only on identifying promising offspring, but also on controlling redundancy and preserving useful diversity during evolutionary search.

</details>


### [18] [A Memristive Synapse for Online STDP Learning and Inference in SNNs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15339)
*Elia Mateu-Barriendos,Álvaro Gómez-Pau,Daniel Arumí,Rosa Rodríguez-Montañés,Salvador Manich*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 提出一种全模拟忆阻突触电路，可在脉冲神经网络运行中直接由前后脉冲实现在线STDP学习，无需外部数字控制或显式波形合成；130 nm CMOS后布局仿真验证了电导的时序依赖更新及2x2 SNN的无监督神经元特化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现SNN在线STDP学习通常依赖外部数字控制或显式STDP波形合成，硬件开销大、集成度低，因此需要一种能在网络正常运行中自发完成学习的全模拟突触方案。

Method: 在突触内集成局部STDP电路，利用忆阻器作为可调电导元件，由前、后突触脉冲直接产生渐进式时序依赖的电导更新；基于130 nm CMOS工艺完成电路设计与后布局仿真，并以2x2 SNN验证无监督学习行为。

Result: 后布局仿真显示该忆阻突触在SNN运行过程中呈现脉冲时序依赖的电导自适应；2x2 SNN仿真进一步表现出通过无监督学习实现的在线神经元特化。

Conclusion: 该全模拟忆阻突触电路可在无外部数字控制和显式STDP波形合成的条件下实现在线STDP学习，为高集成度SNN硬件提供可行路径。

Abstract: This work presents a fully analog memristive synaptic circuit for online spike-timing-dependent plasticity (STDP) learning in spiking neural networks (SNNs). The proposed synapse integrates a local STDP circuit generating gradual timing-dependent conductance updates directly from pre- and post-synaptic spikes. Learning occurs during normal network operation without requiring external digital control or explicit STDP waveform synthesis.
  Post-layout simulations of the memristive synapse implemented in a 130 nm CMOS technology show spike-timing-dependent conductance adaptation during SNN operation. A 2x2 SNN simulation further illustrates online neuron specialization through unsupervised learning.

</details>


### [19] [Big Brains and Changing Environments: Cause or Consequence?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15569)
*Sian Heesom-Green,Jonathan Shock,Geoff Nitschke*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文通过人工季节性觅食任务中的神经演化实验，比较了"仅在变化环境中演化"与"先在稳定环境演化再转入变化环境"的智能体，发现较大的神经网络主要源于前期的稳定环境演化，且这类智能体在不可预测变化下表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大脑袋代谢代价高昂，而认知缓冲假说（CBH）认为大脑变大是为了应对多变环境。但环境变化与大脑大小的相关性并不等于因果——大脑也可能先在稳定环境中演化出来，之后才帮助生物拓殖到多变环境。作者希望检验这一"拓殖假说"是否成立。

Method: 采用神经演化（neuro-evolution）方法，构建人工季节性觅食任务，设置两种演化路径的智能体进行对比：(1) 自始至终只在变化环境中演化；(2) 先在静态（稳定）环境演化，再转入变化环境。比较两者的神经网络规模与在不可预测变化下的性能表现。

Result: 在动态环境中出现的大型神经网络，主要来自前期在稳定环境中的演化积累（演化历史效应）；这类"先稳定后变化"的智能体在不可预测的环境变化下取得了更优的表现。

Conclusion: 结果挑战了CBH的严格预测，为基于拓殖的解释提供了计算（基于智能体）层面的支持，并强调了演化历史在大脑尺寸演化中的关键作用。大脑变大可能并非为应对变化而生，而是先形成、后助力拓殖。

Abstract: Large brains are metabolically costly, and associations with changing environments do not imply they evolved there, as the Cognitive Buffer Hypothesis (CBH) would suggest. They may instead evolve in stable conditions and later facilitate colonization of changing environments. Using neuro-evolution in an artificial seasonal foraging task, we compared agents evolving exclusively in changing environments to agents first evolved in static environments before transitioning. Results show that larger neural networks in dynamic environments arise mainly from prior static evolution, achieving superior performance under unpredictable changes. Our results challenge strict CBH predictions, provide agent-based (computational) support for a colonization-based account and highlight the role of evolutionary history in brain size evolution.

</details>


### [20] [Complete Suffix Prediction for Recommendation via Latent Retrieval over Process Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15692)
*Sarra Madad,Myriam Maumy,Fr{é}d{é}ric Bertrand,Yoann Valero*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种基于图的度量学习框架，将完整后缀预测重新表述为过程图上的潜在检索任务，利用边条件图神经网络编码前缀和后缀，并在两个真实过程数据集上取得最佳整体表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 完整后缀预测在序贯决策场景中具有挑战性，因为同一前缀可能与多种合理后缀兼容。传统序贯后缀预测方法难以同时建模事件级活动与转移级持续时间，且面临结构性约束和KPI相关约束，因此需要一种能够捕捉过程结构信息的替代方案。

Method: 将前缀和后缀表示为有向属性图，使用边条件图神经网络（edge-conditioned GNN）联合建模事件级活动与转移级持续时间；通过预测器将前缀表示投影到潜在后缀空间，并采用重建与对比联合目标训练，辅以过程感知的硬负样本增强；引入谱归一化对编码器和预测器施加Lipschitz约束，以稳定学习到的检索几何并提升检索鲁棒性。

Result: 在两个真实过程数据集上，该框架在几乎所有评估指标上取得最佳整体结果：以归一化Damerau-Levenshtein距离衡量的语义后缀准确率得到提升；Recall@1、Recall@5和MRR@5显示良好的检索质量；平均绝对误差（MAE）表明时间合理性得到保持。

Conclusion: 基于图的潜在检索是序贯后缀预测的有效替代方案，适用于在结构约束和KPI相关约束下面向推荐的过程监控。

Abstract: Complete suffix prediction is challenging in sequential decision settings, where the same prefix can remain compatible with several plausible suffixes. We propose a graphbased metric-learning framework that reformulates complete suffix prediction as latent retrieval over process graphs. Prefixes and suffixes are represented as directed attributed graphs and encoded by edge-conditioned graph neural networks, allowing event-level activities and transition-level durations to be modelled jointly. Prefix representations are projected into the latent suffix space through a predictor trained with a joint reconstruction and contrastive objective strengthened using process-aware hard negatives. To stabilise the learned retrieval geometry, spectral normalisation, and retrieval robustness, spectral normalisation is applied to enforce a Lipschitz constraint on both encoders and predictor. Experiments on two real-life process datasets demonstrate that the proposed framework achieves the best overall results across nearly all evaluated criteria. It improves semantic suffix accuracy measured by normalized Damerau-Levenshtein distance, yields strong retrieval quality through Recall@1, Recall@5, and MRR@5, and maintains temporal plausibility according to Mean Absolute Error. These results show that graph-based latent retrieval is an effective alternative to sequential suffix prediction for recommendation-oriented process monitoring under structural and KPI-related constraints.

</details>


### [21] [Event-Native Symbolic-Temporal Spike Encoding Framework for Heterogeneous Cyber Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15772)
*Dalton Diez,Peyton Andras,Max Shroyer,James Ghawaly*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 提出事件原生符号-时序脉冲编码框架，将异构网络事件直接映射为稀疏脉冲输入，服务循环SNN。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络监控数据异步到达，攻击行为常体现于事件序列的时序模式，与SNN的事件驱动特性契合。但网络流并非纯数值信号，其信息还来自类别标识、非规则时序和局部行为上下文；传统rate/population编码不适配此类异构语义，而传统IDS将原始事件转成flow、固定聚合窗或稠密张量，虽利于常规分类器，却引入缓冲延迟、掩盖原生时序结构、削弱神经形态计算优势。

Method: 提出event-native symbolic-temporal spike encoding：为语义身份、局部频率上下文、事件间计时分别分配编码角色，直接映射异构网络事件为稀疏脉冲兼容输入，保留类别语义与时序动态。在packet级Network IDS验证并扩展至message级CAN IDS，评估该编码是否能为直接在原生事件流上运行的recurrent SNN暴露可用结构。实验在面向边缘、μCaspian对齐的硬件约束下进行。

Result: 在边缘导向硬件约束下，紧凑recurrent SNN取得较强异常检测性能：综合运营指标J_hybrid在Network IDS上为0.987，在CAN IDS上为0.980。

Conclusion: 事件原生符号-时序脉冲编码可无需flow/窗口/稠密张量转换，直接从异构原生事件流为循环SNN提供可用结构，并在两类IDS任务上实现高检测性能，同时保留事件驱动神经形态计算的低功耗潜力。

Abstract: Spiking neural networks (SNNs) have shown promise for sparse, event-driven computation through stateful processing that is naturally compatible with low-power edge hardware. These properties align with cyber monitoring, where data arrives asynchronously, and malicious behavior often emerges through temporal patterns across event sequences. However, cyber streams are not composed solely of continuous numeric signals: their informative structure is also carried by categorical identifiers, irregular timing, and local behavioral context. Traditional rate- and population-based spike encodings are not naturally suited to these heterogeneous semantics, while conventional intrusion detection system (IDS) pipelines typically resolve the mismatch by converting raw events into flows, fixed aggregation windows, or dense tensors. Although useful for conventional classifiers, these transformations introduce buffering latency, obscure native temporal structure, and weaken the computational advantages of event-driven neuromorphic processing.
  We introduce an event-native symbolic-temporal spike encoding framework that maps heterogeneous cyber events directly into sparse, spike-compatible inputs. By assigning encoding roles to semantic identity, local frequency context, and inter-event timing, the framework preserves categorical semantics and temporal dynamics. We validate the approach on packet-level Network IDS and extend it to message-level CAN IDS, using both domains to evaluate whether the encoding exposes usable structure for recurrent SNNs operating directly on native event streams. Under edge-oriented, $μ$Caspian-aligned hardware constraints, compact recurrent SNNs achieve strong anomaly detection performance, with an operational hybrid metric ($J_{hybrid}$) of 0.987 on Network IDS and 0.980 on CAN IDS.

</details>

### [22] [Planning as Dynamics Relaxation: Hippocampal Recurrent Network Realizes Optimal Goal-Directed Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13219)
*Yuhang He,Junfeng Zuo,Tianhao Chu,Si Wu*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 该研究提出海马网络通过适当的循环连接和弛豫动力学，可自然实现复杂环境中的最优目标导向导航，其活动场在数学上等价于LMDP的期望场，局部对数梯度指示导航方向，且对环境局部变化仅需低秩连接更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间认知图的神经关联已有充分记录，但神经回路如何在包含障碍物的复杂环境中执行空间导航（如到达目标同时避障）仍不清楚，本文旨在揭示其计算原理。

Method: 构建具有合适循环连接的海马网络，神经元间循环权重表示所编码空间位置间的转移概率，墙壁和阻塞走廊等障碍体现为连接权重消失；该连接模式可通过行为时间尺度突触可塑性（BTSP）在动物探索环境时学习。给定目标信号后，网络动力学弛豫到代表目标位置的活动场，理论证明该场等价于线性可解马尔可夫决策过程（LMDP）的期望场，其局部对数梯度指示导航方向。

Result: 理论分析与仿真表明，该循环网络动力学介导的导航在复杂障碍布局环境中高效且鲁棒；当环境发生局部变化时，仅需对网络连接模式进行低秩更新。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了一种基于循环网络弛豫动力学的导航机制，可能为大脑在空间导航之外的抽象理性图中进行规划的通用回路原理提供启示。

Abstract: Neural correlates of spatial cognitive map are well documented, yet exactly how neural circuits perform spatial navigation in complex environments - e.g., reaching a goal while avoiding obstacles - remains largely unclear. Here, we show that a hippocampal network with appropriate recurrent connections can naturally achieve optimal goal-directed navigation via its relaxation dynamics. Specifically, we consider that the recurrent weights between the neurons represent the transition probabilities between spatial locations encoded by neurons; obstacles such as walls and blocked corridors are therefore reflected by the vanishing of connection weights. This connection pattern can be learned in the hippocampus via behavioral-timescale synaptic plasticity (BTSP) while the animal is exploring the environment. When a goal signal is presented, the network dynamics will relax into an activity field representing the goal location. We prove that this field is mathematically equivalent to the desirability field of a Linearly-solvable Markov Decision Process (LMDP), and the local log-gradient of the field indicates the navigation direction. Both theoretical analyses and simulations demonstrate that this recurrent network dynamics-mediated navigation is efficient and robust in environments with complex obstacle layouts. Moreover, only low-rank updates of the network's connection pattern are needed when the environment has local changes. We hope this study offers insight into a general circuit principle for planning in abstract rational maps in the brain beyond spatial navigation.

</details>


### [23] [Hierarchical emergence of network bursting in a four-cell central pattern generator model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13858)
*Krishna Pusuluri,Huiwen Wu,Andrey L. Shilnikov*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 该研究通过对海蛞蝓 \textit{Dendronotus iris} 游泳中枢模式发生器（CPG）的4细胞神经回路进行自下而上的建模重构，揭示了节律性爆发如何从无内源爆发能力的神经元网络中涌现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心问题是：当神经回路中没有任何单个神经元能够内源性地产生节律性爆发时，整个回路为何仍能节律性爆发？作者以海蛞蝓游泳CPG为原型，试图厘清节律生成在不同层级（单细胞、半中心振荡器HCO、完整网络）上的起源。

Method: 1. 对游泳中间神经元（SiN）模型进行内源动态区间映射；2. 构建由紧张性放电或静息细胞组成的半中心振荡器（HCO），分析慢互抑制产生的反相爆发，并用慢—快相空间分解解释其释放机制；3. 对4细胞CPG进行参数化，其中细胞1、2静息，细胞3、4紧张性放电；4. 组装完整网络，考察交叉兴奋、交叉抑制与整流电耦合的协同作用；5. 采用基于事件的符号编码与GPU并行参数扫描，结合Lempel-Ziv复杂度刻画非周期符号输出。

Result: 慢互抑制可在由紧张性放电或静息细胞构成的HCO中产生反相爆发；而孤立的细胞3–4 HCO仅表现为紧张性放电或被抑制。节律性爆发仅在完整4细胞网络中、当交叉兴奋、交叉抑制与整流电耦合共同作用时才出现。符号编码与GPU并行参数扫描表明，该高阶集体状态占据扩展的参数域而非单一调谐点，并量化了网络相互作用如何重塑参数域及每簇峰数。符号扫描识别出健康活动区间（周期序列）及其转迁边界；Lempel-Ziv复杂度用于描述非周期符号输出，而非作为混沌或动力学不稳定的证据。

Conclusion: 研究建立了一条节律生成的层级结构——从内源细胞动力学，到条件性HCO爆发，再到仅在完整4细胞CPG中涌现的爆发——并为扰动其化学与电耦合提供了可实验检验的预测。

Abstract: How can a neural circuit rhythmically burst when none of its constituent neurons can endogenously do so? We address this question through a bottom-up reconstruction of a 4-cell neural circuit modeled after the swim central pattern generator (CPG) of the sea slug \textit{Dendronotus iris}. We first map the intrinsic regimes of a swim interneuron (SiN) model neuron and show that slow mutual inhibition can generate anti-phase bursting in a half-center oscillator (HCO) assembled from tonic-spiking or quiescent cells. A slow--fast phase-space deconstruction explains this pairwise rhythm as a release mechanism. We then move on to CPG parametrization, in which cells 1 and 2 are quiescent, while cells 3 and 4 are tonic spikers but their isolated HCO can only exhibit tonic spiking or suppression. Bursting therefore does not arise at either the cellular or HCO level. It appears only after the two modules are assembled into the complete 4-cell network, where cross excitation, cross inhibition, and rectified electrical coupling act together to support a robust network rhythm-generation. Event-based symbolic encoding and GPU-parallel parameter sweeps show that this higher-order collective state occupies extended parameter domains rather than a single tuned point, and they quantify how the network interactions reshape those domains and the spike counts per burst. Symbolic sweeps identify the healthy range of activity regimes (periodic sequences) and transition boundaries; Lempel--Ziv complexity is used as a descriptor of aperiodic symbolic output rather than as proof of chaos or dynamical instability. Our results establish a hierarchy of rhythm generation---from intrinsic cell dynamics, through conditional HCO bursting, to bursting that emerges only in the fully assembled 4-cell CPG---and provide experimentally accessible predictions for perturbing its chemical and electrical couplings.

</details>


### [24] [URCHIN: A Horizontal Spiking Language Model for Data-Constrained Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13899)
*Po-Han Chiang*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: URCHIN 将脉冲神经元与戴尔定律横向连接组结合，构建极简的语言模型，在 BabyLM 三条赛道（Strict-100M、Strict-Small、多语种）上提供具备生物合理性、高效且可直接部署的参考模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BabyLM 挑战关注模型能否从符合发育规律、儿童规模的数据中学习语言，而非依赖互联网规模语料；但既有语言模型忽略了获得人类语言的神经回路所具备的生物约束——即由兴奋性与抑制性群体构成、通过递归横向连接组连接的脉冲神经元。论文希望弥补这一生物学脱节，提出受生物约束的极简语言模型。

Method: 论文提出 URCHIN（Unified Recurrent Connectome with Horizontal Integrate-and-fire Neurons），将 PHCSSM（Parallelized Hierarchical Connectome Spiking State-space Model）应用于语言建模。模型使用漏泄积分-发放（LIF）神经元，并通过遵循戴尔定律的横向连接组耦合；每个 token 通过多轮传递循环（multi-transmission loop）迭代，将活动重循环至不动点。实例化刻意极简：单层水平结构、128 个神经元、无注意力机制、4.23M 参数。两套实现共享同一组权重并产生完全相同的基准分数：一是适合 GPU 训练的高效并行状态空间模型（SSM）扫描；二是事件驱动的递归脉冲神经网络（RSNN），推理成本恒定，适合 CPU 或神经形态边缘部署，无需转换步骤。

Result: 在 BabyLM 全部三条赛道（Strict-100M、Strict-Small、多语种）上均得到结果；URCHIN 的两种实现（并行 SSM 扫描与事件驱动 RSNN）共享权重且基准分数完全一致，实现“一次训练、两种部署、无需转换”。

Conclusion: URCHIN 表明，在生物约束（脉冲神经元、兴奋-抑制群体、戴尔定律横向连接组）下、以极小规模（单层 128 神经元、无注意力、4.23M 参数）即可完成语言建模，并为 BabyLM 提供生物学合理、高效且可直接部署的参考基准，兼具训练期 GPU 效率与推理期恒定成本的优势。

Abstract: The BabyLM challenge measures how much language a model can learn from developmentally-plausible, child-scale data rather than internet-scale corpora, yet prior language models forgo the biological constraints of the neural circuitry that acquires human language: spiking neurons separated into excitatory and inhibitory populations wired by a recurrent lateral connectome. This paper presents URCHIN (Unified Recurrent Connectome with Horizontal Integrate-and-fire Neurons), which applies the Parallelized Hierarchical Connectome Spiking State-space Model (PHCSSM) to language modeling: leaky integrate-and-fire neurons coupled by a Dale's-law lateral connectome resolve each token through a multi-transmission loop that recirculates activity to a fixed point. The instantiation is deliberately minimal: a single horizontal layer of 128 neurons, no attention, and 4.23M parameters. Two implementations share one set of weights and produce identical benchmark scores, so URCHIN is trained once and deployed either way with no conversion step: a parallel state-space model (SSM) scan that is GPU-efficient for training, or an event-driven recurrent spiking neural network (RSNN) with constant-cost inference for CPU or neuromorphic edge deployment. Across all three BabyLM tracks (Strict-100M, Strict-Small, and Multilingual), URCHIN offers a biologically plausible, efficient, and directly deployable reference point.

</details>


### [25] [Nonlinear dynamics of random neural networks with second-order synaptic motifs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14251)
*Jun Yang,Hannah Choi*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文突破了随机神经网络中"连接独立"的经典假设，系统研究了四类二阶突触基序（链式、互惠、汇聚、发散）如何塑造非线性放电率网络的动力学，借助路径积分形式导出的动态平均场理论揭示了基序对Jacobian谱、动力学区间及混沌几何的共同调控机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典随机神经网络理论通常假设连接相互独立，忽略了生物神经回路中普遍存在的局部基序结构。已有研究仅表明链式相关可产生离群特征值，但二阶基序如何共同重塑Jacobian本征值的体分布及其动力学后果尚不清楚，因此需要一套统一的理论框架来刻画基序对网络动力学的系统性影响。

Method: 采用路径积分形式推导动态平均场理论，分析四类二阶突触基序（链式、互惠、汇聚、发散）对非线性放电率网络Jacobian谱的影响；进一步通过Lyapunov谱与参与比维度分析，刻画基序结构对混沌活动几何性质的作用。

Result: 链式基序通过响应核表现为集合平均活动的延迟反馈，产生铁磁态与极限环等丰富动力学区间；足够大的负链式相关导致玻璃态多稳态区间，此前该区间主要与部分对称网络相关联。发散相关主要重标定时间噪声，而汇聚相关通过将非零平均活动转化为淬火异质性来抑制时间混沌。Lyapunov谱与参与比维度分析表明，即便有效谱边固定，基序结构仍会改变混沌活动几何，降低熵产生与吸引子维度。

Conclusion: 二阶基序是支配局部皮层回路动力学区间的根本结构机制，超越了独立连接假设，为理解生物神经网络的动力学组织提供了统一的理论基础。

Abstract: Classical theories of random neural networks typically assume independent connectivity, overlooking the local motif structures prevalent in biological circuits. Here, we investigate how four second-order synaptic motifs (chain, reciprocal, convergent, and divergent) shape the dynamics of nonlinear firing-rate networks. While previous studies have established that chain correlations generate outlier eigenvalues, we demonstrate that these motifs also jointly reshape the Jacobian eigenvalue bulk. Using the path-integral formalism, we derive a dynamic mean-field theory which reveals that the chain motif acts as a retarded feedback of the ensemble-mean activity through the response kernel, producing a rich repertoire of dynamical regimes, including ferromagnetic states and limit cycles. At sufficiently large magnitude, negative chain correlations produce a glassy, multistable regime that was previously mainly associated with partially symmetric networks. Our theory also distinguishes convergent from divergent motifs: divergent correlations primarily rescale temporal noise, while convergent correlations suppress temporal chaos by converting nonzero mean activity into quenched heterogeneity. Finally, analyses of the Lyapunov spectrum and participation-ratio dimension show that motif structure changes the geometry of chaotic activity, reducing entropy production and attractor dimensionality even when the effective spectral edge is held fixed. Together, these findings establish second-order motifs as a fundamental structural mechanism governing the dynamical regimes of local cortical circuits.

</details>


### [26] [When Teachers Smile or Frown: A Profile-Based Analysis of Achievement Emotions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15747)
*Rudra Mukhopadhyay,Satyaki Mazumder,Koel Das*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 研究揭示成就情绪存在三种潜在剖面（热情型、动机缺失型、脆弱型），其转换受教师面部表情影响，并受准备度与认知克制两个调节维度制约。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有研究多关注单一情绪，忽视共现情绪剖面的构成及其动态变化，尤其缺乏对教学情境中教师非言语线索如何驱动情绪剖面转换的考察。

Method: 以78名七、八年级学生为样本，在观看视频讲座时接受不同教师面部表情条件（如高兴、愤怒）的干预；采用自评量表收集成就情绪数据，运用潜在剖面分析识别情绪剖面，分析剖面转换差异，并通过探索性因子分析与贝叶斯结构模型检验调节维度。

Result: 识别出热情型、动机缺失型与脆弱型三种成就情绪剖面；剖面转换随教师表情条件而异，高兴表情促进更适应性的转换，愤怒表情则促进向动机缺失方向的转换；准备度与认知克制是被识别出的与剖面转换相关的调节维度。

Conclusion: 成就情绪应以剖面视角而非单一情绪视角加以理解，教师面部表情可塑造情绪剖面的动态转换，且教学可针对准备度和认知克制等调节维度进行干预以促进适应性情绪转换。

Abstract: Achievement emotions shape how students engage with and learn from academic tasks, yet most studies examine individual emotions rather than co-occurring affective profiles and their dynamics. We examined latent achievement-emotion profiles and their transitions following exposure to different instructor facial expressions during a video lecture. Self-reported data from 78 Grade VII and VIII students revealed three profiles: enthusiastic, demotivated, and vulnerable. Profile transitions differed across instructor conditions, with happy expressions favouring more adaptive transitions and angry expressions favouring transitions toward demotivation. Exploratory factor analysis and Bayesian structural modelling further identified preparedness and cognitive restraint as regulatory dimensions associated with profile switching.

</details>

### [27] [BudgetBench: A Budget-Tiered Protocol and Pilot Harness for Memory Strategy Evaluation in Local Large Language Model Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13149)
*Aditya Karnam Gururaj Rao,Arjun Jaggi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 BudgetBench——一种把每次调用的输入 token 预算视为自变量的主动预算评测协议与参考工具链，用于比较本地大模型智能体的记忆策略；核心是可复用的测量面而非最终排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本地大模型智能体的主动上下文属稀缺资源：记忆容量、prefill 延迟、缓存增长与服务目标共同约束单次调用的输入 token 量。现有评测多以单预算设定进行，隐藏了预算合规失败、质量非单调曲线与真实工作点。

Method: 固定模型、任务、采样器与解码，将每调用输入 token 预算作为独立变量，扫描 2K/4K/8K/16K/32K；记录质量、预算利用率、延迟，并将预算违规率作为一等结果。提供可替换的 MemoryStrategy 契约、显式预算执行、确定性/版本化评分器、prompt 审计元数据与可复现工件。

Result: 在本地 qwen2.5:1.5b（SWE-bench Verified 与 LongBench v2 各 89 项）、托管 50 项 Qwen3 30B-A3B LongBench 复现（精确分词）以及 500 项 LongMemEval oracle 研究（官方 GPT-4o 评测器）中，工具链暴露了预算合规失败、非单调质量曲线与单预算评测所掩盖的工作点。预算化与全上下文方向未定：本地切片近乎零效应，托管复现的点估计偏好全上下文。早期 pilot 的分词器近似低估了部分服务模型 prompt，其违规行仅为分词器近似诊断，不承载结论；所有计时为操作性诊断。

Conclusion: 可复用贡献在于协议、工具链与失败报告纪律，用以规模化固定预算记忆策略评测；结果以 pilot 形式透明呈现，不作最终排名。

Abstract: For local large language model agents, active context is a scarce resource: memory capacity, prefill latency, cache growth, and service objectives all constrain how many input tokens each call can afford. We present BudgetBench, an active-budget protocol and reference harness that treats the per-call input-token budget as the independent variable when comparing memory strategies. Holding the model, task, sampler, and decoding fixed, it sweeps budgets over 2K, 4K, 8K, 16K, and 32K tokens and records quality, budget utilization, latency, and, as a first-class outcome, budget-violation rates. The core contribution is this reusable measurement surface: a swappable MemoryStrategy contract, explicit budget enforcement, deterministic or versioned graders, prompt-audit metadata, and reproducibility artifacts, released at https://github.com/aviskaar/budgetbench.
  We substantiate the protocol with pilot studies rather than final rankings. Across a local qwen2.5:1.5b pilot (89 items each on SWE-bench Verified and LongBench v2), a hosted 50-item Qwen3 30B-A3B LongBench replication with exact tokenization, and a 500-item LongMemEval oracle study scored by the official GPT-4o evaluator, the harness exposes budget-compliance failures, non-monotonic quality curves, and operating points that single-budget evaluation hides. The budgeted-versus-full-context direction remains unresolved: the local slice is near-null and the hosted replication favors full context in point estimate. We report results transparently, including that the early pilot's tokenizer approximation undercounts some served-model prompts, so its violation rows are tokenizer-approximation diagnostics, not claim-bearing results; all timings are operational diagnostics. The reusable contribution is the protocol, harness, and failure-reporting discipline needed to scale fixed-budget memory-strategy evaluation.

</details>


### [28] [A derivative-fidelity failure mode in physics-informed neural networks: strengthened benchmark evidence from function-value training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13171)
*Koji Koyamada*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将"导数保真度"确立为物理信息神经网络（PINNs）的一种失效模式，并通过一维基准实验表明：仅在函数值上训练的网络可很好地拟合函数值，但其自动微分得到的二阶导数误差显著偏大，尤其在高曲率边界区域附近。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PINNs依赖自动微分施加微分方程残差，但函数值上的良好吻合并不必然意味着导数准确；这一"值准确而导数不准确"的潜在风险尚未被系统界定与检验，因此作者将其形式化为一种失效模式并设计诊断实验。

Method: 在一维基准上训练多层感知机，仅使用函数值作为监督信号（sin(x) 与 exp(x)），随后单独评估由自动微分得到的二阶导数。为增强假设检验，进一步考察训练点密度、激活函数类型、端点密集评估，以及L2误差与最大误差两类诊断指标。

Result: 实验显示，函数逼近在视觉上准确的同时，二阶导数误差可以显著更大，且该现象在高曲率边界区域尤为突出；结果在不同训练点密度、激活函数与误差度量下保持一致。

Conclusion: 仅凭函数值吻合不足以判定PINNs的物理残差可靠；本文提供了一个区分"值精度"与"物理残差可靠性"的诊断协议，提示应以导数保真度作为独立的评估维度。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) use automatic differentiation to impose differential-equation residuals, but good agreement in function values does not necessarily imply accurate derivatives. This paper formulates derivative fidelity as a failure mode of PINNs and tests it with one-dimensional benchmarks. Multilayer perceptrons are trained only on function values for sin(x) and exp(x), while second derivatives obtained by automatic differentiation are evaluated separately. The hypothesis is strengthened by additional tests over training-point density, activation functions, endpoint-dense evaluation, and both L2 and maximum-error diagnostics. The results show that visually accurate function approximation can coexist with substantially larger second-derivative errors, especially near high-curvature boundary regions. The experiment provides a diagnostic protocol for distinguishing value accuracy from physics-residual reliability.

</details>


### [29] [Pretraining for Sample-Efficient Neural Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13507)
*Ben Tang,Zachary Spalding,Gregory B. Cogan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MAPA，一种引入解剖区域嵌入与相对位置编码的掩码自编码器，使自监督预训练能够跨异质iEEG记录迁移，在Neuroprobe基准的三种场景下均达到新SOTA，并大幅降低新被试所需标注数据量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高性能神经解码器通常需要为每个新被试采集大量标注数据，成本高昂。自监督预训练可利用跨被试累积的无标注记录来学习通用神经表征，从而降低标注成本。然而在颅内脑电（iEEG）中，由于被试间电极接触位置与神经解剖结构存在差异，自监督学习面临困难，难以学到可迁移的表征。

Method: 提出MAPA：一个结构上保持常规的掩码自编码器（masked autoencoder），额外引入两种空间编码——解剖区域嵌入（anatomical region embedding）和相对位置编码（relative positional encoding）。二者共同作用，使模型能够学习到可迁移至未见被试及多种任务的神经表征。

Result: 在Neuroprobe基准的三种评测场景（within-session、cross-session、cross-subject）下，MAPA在无需微调的情况下均取得新的state of the art。在cross-subject场景中，基于MAPA特征的线性探针仅需约164个标注试次（trials）即可达到无预训练时需要3,500个试次的准确率。

Conclusion: 自监督预训练能够跨异质的iEEG记录进行规模化扩展，并减少对新被试进行高精度解码所需的标注数据量。

Abstract: Brain-computer interfaces (BCIs) decode neural activity to restore lost function. Typically, training a high-performance neural decoder requires a large labeled dataset to be collected from every new subject. One way to reduce the labeled data cost is self-supervised pretraining, which learns general neural representations from unlabeled recordings that accumulate across subjects. However, for intracranial electroencephalography (iEEG) recordings, self-supervised learning has been challenging due to differences in contact placement and neuroanatomy between subjects. We propose MAPA, an otherwise vanilla masked autoencoder with two spatial encodings, an anatomical region embedding and a relative positional encoding, which together enable it to learn neural representations that transfer to unseen subjects and across various tasks. MAPA sets a new state of the art across all three regimes of the Neuroprobe benchmark without fine-tuning: within-session, cross-session, and cross-subject. In the cross-subject regime, a linear probe on MAPA's features needs only ${\sim}164$ labeled trials to reach the accuracy that takes 3,500 without pretraining. Our results show that self-supervised pretraining can scale across heterogeneous iEEG recordings and reduce the labeled data needed for accurate decoding in new subjects.

</details>


### [30] [Land Art as a Big-Data Climate Sensor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13182)
*Alev Cinbarci,Sean Kalaycioglu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究对1984–2025年1,744幅Landsat与Sentinel-2影像芯片提取14维复杂度特征，结合42年气候水文数据，发现影像复杂度（尤其是粗尺度排列熵、亮度与ResNet50第三主成分）与湖泊水位及累积CO₂高度相关，可提前约三年指示水文状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Robert Smithson的《螺旋防波堤》随大盐湖水位涨落而变化。既有小样本研究声称影像熵与全球气温正相关，但缺乏系统验证；本文旨在用大规模遥感与气候数据检验并细化"艺术即温度计"这一隐喻。

Method: 对1,744幅配准的Landsat 4-9与Sentinel-2影像芯片（1984–2025，覆盖各年各月）构建14维复杂度特征：Shannon熵、多尺度排列熵、分形维数、空隙度、灰度共生矩阵纹理、强度统计及ImageNet预训练ResNet50特征；与42年逐月气候水文面板（GISTEMP、USGS NWIS、Open-Meteo、全球碳预算）比对；采用Bootstrap、主成分分析、滞后相关、STL分解与偏相关分析。

Result: Shannon熵仅为弱代理，不支持此前正相关主张；粗尺度排列熵与平均亮度与湖面高程强相关（Spearman 0.85–0.88，置信区间不含零）；ResNet50嵌入第三主成分作为无监督"AI气候轴"与累积CO₂相关0.86、与湖面高程相关-0.83；影像复杂度领先湖面约三年（滞后+3时Pearson r=0.58，95%CI 0.40–0.73）；STL趋势呈非单调，1984–2015上升、其后骤降；控制月份与传感器的偏相关验证稳健性。

Conclusion: 研究将"艺术即温度计"的隐喻修正为"艺术即水文状态领先指标"，并公开发布数据集、特征管线与分析代码作为公共基准。

Abstract: Robert Smithson's 1970 land artwork Spiral Jetty, located in the north arm of Utah's Great Salt Lake, has alternated between submergence and exposure during severe lake decline. We analyze 1,744 co-registered Landsat 4-9 and Sentinel-2 image chips spanning every year and calendar month from 1984 to 2025. A 14-feature complexity signature combines Shannon entropy, multiscale permutation entropy, fractal dimension, lacunarity, gray-level co-occurrence texture, intensity statistics, and ImageNet-pretrained ResNet50 features. These measurements are compared with a 42-year monthly climate and hydrology panel from NASA GISTEMP, USGS NWIS, Open-Meteo, and the Global Carbon Budget. Bootstrap analysis shows that Shannon entropy is a weak proxy and does not support an earlier small-sample claim of positive correlation with global temperature. By contrast, coarse-scale permutation entropy and mean intensity track lake elevation strongly, with Spearman correlations of 0.85 to 0.88 and 95 percent confidence intervals excluding zero. The third principal component of the ResNet50 embeddings emerges without supervision as an AI climate axis, correlating 0.86 with cumulative CO2 and -0.83 with lake elevation. Image complexity leads lake stage by about three years, with Pearson r = 0.58 at lag +3 and a 95 percent confidence interval of 0.40 to 0.73. STL decomposition reveals a non-monotonic trend that rises from 1984 to 2015 and declines sharply thereafter as the lake approaches record-low elevations. Partial correlations controlling for month and sensor confirm robustness to seasonal and sensor effects. These results refine the art-as-thermometer metaphor into an art-as-leading-indicator-of-hydrological-state interpretation. The dataset, feature pipeline, and analysis code are released as a public benchmark.

</details>


### [31] [LLMs or Naive Bayes? Old Gems or New Ways](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13185)
*Mohammad Firas Sada,Dmitry Mishin,John Graham,Seungmin Kim,Mahidhar Tatineni,Frank Würthwein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文比较了Complement Naive Bayes与多种LLM在文本分类任务上的性能、吞吐和能耗，发现当有标注数据时NB仍是资源受限HPC场景下的最优选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回应"LLM时代是否应淘汰Naive Bayes等经典方法"这一研究计算领域的反复出现的疑问，明确在何种数据与资源条件下经典方法仍具优势。

Method: 将Complement Naive Bayes与零样本/少样本LLM进行基准测试，覆盖4个模型家族、参数量跨度37倍（27B到1T参数MoE）；在多个文本分类任务上对比准确率、吞吐量（样本/秒）与能耗；并进行GPU吞吐分析。

Result: LLM仅在零数据场景占优（Amazon Polarity 98.0% vs 88.2%），但该优势易受数据污染；低污染情感任务上NB反超零样本LLM（81.7% vs 73.0%）。有标注数据时（AG News），NB达89.1%，与零样本27B LLM（89.0%）统计上无差异，且优于397B前沿模型（84.8%），在商用CPU上达数千样本/秒。微调DistilBERT为90.6%但吞吐远低。小LLM批量推理比NB CPU推理慢40-486倍，每样本能耗约低两个数量级。

Conclusion: 决策线依赖任务：主题分类中NB约在N~10^4标注量时达到LLM水平，零数据情感任务则始终偏向LLM。对拥有标注数据、资源受限的HPC从业者，NB仍是最优选择。作者还提供Kubernetes Helm operator，用可配置阈值与可验证Prometheus指标自动化模型选择。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) prompt a recurring question in research computing: should classical methods like Naive Bayes (NB) be retired? We benchmark Complement Naive Bayes against zero-shot and few-shot LLMs spanning four model families and a 37x range in scale (27B to a 1T-parameter mixture-of-experts) across text classification tasks. LLMs dominate only in zero-data regimes (98.0% vs 88.2% on Amazon Polarity sentiment), and even that win is contamination-prone: on a low-contamination sentiment task NB beats the zero-shot LLM (81.7% vs 73.0%). However, once labeled data is available (e.g., AG News), NB reaches 89.1% accuracy, statistically indistinguishable from the zero-shot 27B LLM (89.0%) and better than the 397B frontier model (84.8%), at thousands of samples/sec on a commodity CPU. Fine-tuned DistilBERT reaches 90.6% but at far lower throughput than NB at batch size 1 (Table 2). Our measured GPU throughput analysis shows small-LLM batched inference is 40-486x slower than NB CPU inference (the multiplier depends strongly on the host CPU), exposing a structural gap bounded by memory bandwidth, with roughly two orders of magnitude lower energy per sample. For resource-constrained HPC practitioners performing text classification with labeled data, NB remains the optimal choice. We show the decision line is task-dependent (NB reaches LLM parity around $N \sim 10^4$ labels for topic classification, while zero-data sentiment favors the LLM at all N tested) and provide a Kubernetes Helm operator that automates model selection using configurable thresholds and verifiable Prometheus metrics.

</details>


### [32] [Early Prediction of Satellite Collision Probability Using a Hybrid TCN-Transformer Model for a CDM-Based Conjunction Analysis Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13191)
*Rabia Tüylek Tok,Burak Yağlıoğlu,Enes Dağ,Emre Onur Kahya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究提出一种基于学习的卫星交会风险早期预测框架，通过UT传播与PCA进行特征筛选，构建融合TCN-Transformer混合模型，预测同一交会事件下一次CDM更新中的碰撞概率(PoC)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LEO中卫星与轨道碎片快速增长导致近距离接近事件频发，卫星运营商负担加重；对使用电推进的卫星而言，低推力机动能力进一步限制避碰规划时间。现有CDM虽提供相对状态、协方差、最近接近时刻与PoC等，但轨道不确定性的非线性传播及PoC对协方差演化的敏感性使序列CDM解释困难，亟需更早、更一致的风险评估手段。

Method: 首先采用基于Unscented Transform的传播与反向传播框架评估碰撞风险指标对CDM参数的敏感性；随后对数值化CDM参数应用主成分分析(PCA)，识别与PoC变化最相关的特征。基于敏感性分析与PCA结果，筛选原始CDM参数并构造表征相对运动、交会几何及协方差不确定性的派生指标。以由此得到的序列化增强交会数据集，训练混合TCN-Transformer模型，学习交会风险的时间演化规律。

Result: 敏感性分析与PCA结果为所选原始CDM参数及派生指标的构造提供了依据；基于序列化增强数据集的TCN-Transformer混合模型能够学习交会风险的时间演化，并在TÜBİTAK UZAY实际接收与分析的CDM数据上得到应用验证。

Conclusion: 所提学习型框架对LEO卫星交会事件展现出更早且更一致的操作性风险预测潜力，可辅助卫星运营商提前开展避碰规划，尤其适用于受低推力机动时间约束的电推进卫星。

Abstract: The rapid expansion of operational satellites and orbital debris has increased the frequency of close approach events in low Earth orbit (LEO), creating a higher operational burden for satellite operators. This problem is especially critical for satellites using electric propulsion, where low-thrust maneuver capability imposes additional time constraints on collision avoidance planning. In current practice, Conjunction Data Messages (CDMs) provide relative state, covariance, miss distance, time of closest approach, and probability of collision (PoC) information for conjunction assessment. However, the nonlinear propagation of orbital uncertainties and the sensitivity of PoC to covariance evolution make the interpretation of sequential CDMs challenging. This study proposes a learning-based framework for early prediction of satellite conjunction risk by estimating the PoC expected in the subsequent CDM update of the same close approach event. In the proposed methodology, an Unscented Transform-based propagation and backpropagation framework is first used to evaluate the sensitivity of the collision risk metric to CDM parameters. In addition, Principal Component Analysis is applied to the numerical CDM parameters to identify the features most relevant to PoC variation. The results obtained from the sensitivity analysis and PCA are then used to justify the selected raw CDM parameters and to construct derived metrics representing relative motion, encounter geometry, and covariance-related uncertainty. Using the resulting sequential enriched conjunction dataset, a hybrid Temporal Convolutional Network (TCN)-Transformer model is trained to learn the temporal evolution of conjunction risk. The framework is applied to CDMs received and analyzed within TÜBİTAK UZAY, demonstrating its potential for earlier and more consistent operational risk evaluation for LEO satellite conjunctions.

</details>


### [33] [Evaluating LLM-Generated Rules for Heart Disease Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13192)
*Feisal Alaswad,Batoul Aljaddouh,Maher Alrahhal,Wafaa Al Nassan,Talal Bonn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文基于UCI心脏病数据集，对比传统机器学习模型与GPT-4o、Claude Sonnet 4.6生成的规则系统在心脏病预测中的性能。结果表明传统机器学习模型（随机森林最佳，准确率90.2%）普遍优于LLM生成规则系统，但后者提供了可解释的IF-THEN诊断逻辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在医疗人工智能场景中，传统机器学习模型与LLM生成规则系统在预测性能与可解释性之间的权衡关系，为临床决策支持系统的选择提供实证依据。

Method: 使用UCI心脏病数据集，评估Logistic Regression、KNN、SVM、Naive Bayes、Decision Tree、Random Forest等分类器，并与GPT-4o和Claude Sonnet 4.6生成的规则系统进行对比；采用准确率、精确率、召回率和F1-score作为评价指标。

Result: 传统机器学习模型整体优于LLM生成规则系统。Random Forest表现最优：准确率90.2%、精确率0.829、召回率1.0、F1-score 0.906；Naive Bayes次之为88.5%准确率、F1-score 0.881。LLM规则模型中，Claude Sonnet 4.6达80.3%准确率（F1-score 0.833），GPT-4o为70.5%准确率（F1-score 0.690）。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了医疗AI系统中预测性能与可解释性之间的权衡：传统机器学习模型预测能力更强，而LLM生成的规则系统通过可解释的IF-THEN诊断逻辑提升了临床决策的透明度。

Abstract: This study compares traditional machine learning models and Large Language Model (LLM)-generated rule-based systems for heart disease prediction using the UCI Heart Disease dataset. Several classifiers, including Logistic Regression, K-Nearest Neighbors (KNN), Support Vector Machine (SVM), Naive Bayes, Decision Tree, and Random Forest, were evaluated alongside rule-based systems generated using GPT-4o and Claude Sonnet 4.6. Model performance was assessed using accuracy, precision, recall, and F1-score metrics. Experimental results show that traditional machine learning models consistently outperform LLM-generated rule-based systems in predictive performance. Random Forest achieved the best overall performance with 90.2% accuracy, a precision of 0.829, perfect recall of 1.0, and an F1-score of 0.906. Naive Bayes followed closely with 88.5% accuracy and an F1-score of 0.881. In contrast, the LLM-generated rule models achieved lower performance, with Claude Sonnet 4.6 reaching 80.3% accuracy (F1-score: 0.833) and GPT-4o obtaining 70.5% accuracy (F1-score: 0.690). Despite the performance gap, the LLM-generated rules provide interpretable IF-THEN diagnostic logic that enhances explainability and transparency in clinical decision-making. These findings highlight the trade-off between predictive performance and interpretability in medical artificial intelligence systems. The complete implementation of all experiments, including machine learning models and LLM-derived rule classifiers, is publicly available in the GitHub repository at https://github.com/FeisalAlaswad/LLM-Rule-ML-Heart-Disease-Prediction .

</details>


### [34] [Diagnosing Faults in Reinforcement Learning Simulators and World Models with Canonical Polynomial Invariants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13194)
*Tesfay Zemuy Gebrekidan,Hadush Hailu Gebrerufael*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文以代数不变量（精确多项式不变量，规范化为Q上的约化Gröbner基）检验“尊重物理结构的模型预测更好”这一前提，发现精确性对预测收益甚微，但精确规范不变量在故障诊断（定位与归因）上价值显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大量文献将物理结构嵌入学习动力学，其前提是尊重底层物理的模型预测更准。作者希望用可从轨迹恢复的精确多项式不变量来严格检验这一前提，并探索精确不变量是否在预测之外另有价值。

Method: 从轨迹恢复精确多项式不变量并规范化为Q上的约化Gröbner基；在Acrobot上使用一致性正则项与形状势能做预测实验；提出筛选（识别被违反的物理约束）、归因（恢复故障不变量并定位责任物理参数）两种诊断程序，并引入normal-form deflation与quotient-space recovery；进行15个注入故障实验、参考生成器扰动实验（10^-4）及理想等式判定实验（10^-12）；并在11个RL环境的350个发布对上应用诊断。

Result: 预测方面，一致性正则项降低代数残差但rollout保真度基本不变；100%质量误差系统恢复的形状势能与正确势能同等加速学习。诊断方面，15个注入故障中筛选全部定位且无误报（观测空间基线无法定位），归因在7个参数故障上全部恢复责任参数；配对差异检验可检测全部故障，说明优势在于定位而非检测；10^-4扰动保持14–15/15定位，理想等式判定可区分10^-12扰动。350个发布对未发现模拟器动力学变化证据。

Conclusion: 精确物理不变量的价值主要在于诊断而非预测：预测中精确性收益很小，但规范不变量可可靠定位与归因物理约束与参数故障，且筛选不依赖精确性、而代数比较需要精确性；该诊断还揭示了基准实现自身的性质。

Abstract: A large literature builds physical structure into learned dynamics on the premise that models respecting the underlying physics predict better. We test that premise using exact polynomial invariants recovered from trajectories and canonicalised as reduced Gröbner bases over $\mathbb{Q}$. On Acrobot, exactness provides little benefit for prediction: a consistency regulariser reduces algebraic residual while leaving rollout fidelity essentially unchanged, and a shaping potential recovered from a system with a 100% mass error accelerates learning as effectively as the correct potential. Exact canonical invariants instead prove valuable for diagnosis. We develop two procedures: screening, which identifies the violated physical constraint, and attribution, which recovers the faulty invariant and identifies the responsible physical parameter. To enable this, we introduce normal-form deflation and quotient-space recovery. Across fifteen injected faults, screening localises every broken constraint with no false alarms, whereas observation-space baselines do not localise any; attribution recovers the responsible parameter on all seven parameter faults. Paired difference tests detect all faults, showing that the advantage is localisation rather than detection. Perturbing reference generators by $10^{-4}$ preserves 14--15/15 localisations, showing that screening does not require exactness, whereas ideal-equality decisions distinguish perturbations of only $10^{-12}$, showing that exactness is required for algebraic comparison. Applied to 350 release pairs across eleven RL environments, the diagnostic finds no evidence of changed simulator dynamics, instead revealing properties of the benchmark implementations themselves.

</details>


### [35] [Sensory Precision Inference for Multimodal Arbitration under Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15065)
*Tin Mišić,Takato Horii*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种多模态感知推理模型，通过自由能最小化联合更新潜在信念与模态特异性感知精度，并引入可学习的感知精度先验，在含噪、缺失与跨模态冲突下实现鲁棒且可解释的多感官整合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主智能体处理多感官数据时，真实环境中感知流常受噪声、缺失数据和模态间不一致的干扰，需要在竞争性感官假设间自适应仲裁。虽然主动推理提供了不确定性引导的推理框架，但动态推断的感知精度在感官冲突下对生成式多模态仲裁的作用仍相对缺乏探索。

Method: 构建多模态感知推理模型，通过迭代自由能最小化联合更新潜在信念与模态特异性感知精度；感知精度动态不仅反映感官不确定性，还主动塑造多模态冲突下潜在信念的演化；引入感知精度的可学习先验以诱导结构化、类别依赖的精度模式并影响跨模态推理动态。使用合成多模态MNIST数据集（视觉、听觉、触觉表征数字类别），在受控感官噪声与模态间不一致条件下评估。

Result: 动态精度推断在感知证据受损时提升重建鲁棒性，支持从减少的感知证据中进行连贯的潜在推理，并在冲突模态间实现稳定仲裁；可学习的精度先验生成可解释的精度结构，塑造推理动态与跨模态潜在结构。

Conclusion: 研究支持感知精度推断是不确定性下自适应多模态信念形成的机制性控制过程，凸显精度动态作为鲁棒且可解释多感官整合的计算机制。

Abstract: Autonomous agents operating on multisensory data cannot assume that all sensory modalities remain consistently informative. In real environments, sensory streams are frequently corrupted by noise, missing data, or inter-modal incongruence, requiring adaptive arbitration between competing sensory hypotheses. While active inference provides a principled framework for uncertainty-guided inference, the role of dynamically inferred sensory precision in generative multimodal arbitration under sensory conflict remains comparatively underexplored. We propose a multimodal perceptual inference model in which latent beliefs and modality-specific sensory precisions are jointly updated through iterative free-energy minimization. In our proposed model, sensory precision dynamics not only reflect sensory uncertainty but actively shape the evolution of latent beliefs during multimodal conflict. In addition, we introduce a learned prior over sensory precisions that induces structured, class-dependent precision patterns and influences cross-modal inference dynamics. We evaluate the model using a synthetic multimodal MNIST dataset combining visual, auditory, and tactile representations of digit classes under controlled sensory noise and inter-modal incongruence. Results show that dynamic precision inference improves reconstruction robustness under corrupted sensory evidence, supports coherent latent inference from reduced sensory evidence, and enables stable arbitration between conflicting modalities. Furthermore, learned precision priors generate interpretable precision structures that shape inference dynamics and cross-modal latent structure. These findings support sensory precision inference as a mechanistic control process for adaptive multimodal belief formation under uncertainty, highlighting precision dynamics as a computational mechanism for robust and interpretable multisensory integration.

</details>


### [36] [Machine Unlearning for Speech Question Answering in Large Audio-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13195)
*Zhe Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究大音频语言模型（LALMs）在语音问答场景下的机器遗忘，评估梯度上升、任务算术与对齐式微调三类策略，可在降低隐私泄漏率达80%的同时基本保持核心语音能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LALMs在语音理解与问答上表现强劲，但继承自大规模训练数据的隐私风险，存在对敏感信息的无意记忆。相比文本LLM或ASR，语音问答中声学感知与事实知识紧耦合，使遗忘任务更具挑战性，因此需要专门研究该场景下的机器遗忘。

Method: 提出并评估多种遗忘策略：梯度上升（gradient ascent）、任务算术（task arithmetic），以及强制安全拒答响应的对齐式微调方法，目标是在移除隐私知识的同时保留核心能力。

Result: 在多个语音问答数据集上的大量实验表明，这些遗忘方法可将隐私泄漏率最多降低80%，同时在非隐私语音问答与通用语音理解基准上保持接近中性的性能。

Conclusion: 机器遗忘可有效缓解LALMs语音问答中的隐私泄漏，并在隐私保护与核心能力保持之间取得较好平衡。

Abstract: Large Audio-Language Models (LALMs) have recently shown strong capabilities in speech understanding and question answering (QA), but they also inherit privacy risks from large-scale training data, including the unintended memorization of sensitive information. In this work, we study machine unlearning for speech QA in LALMs, a setting that is more challenging than prior work on text-based Large Language Models (LLMs) or Automatic Speech Recognition (ASR) due to the tight coupling between acoustic perception and factual knowledge. We present and evaluate multiple unlearning strategies, including gradient ascent, task arithmetic, and alignment-based fine-tuning methods that enforce safe refusal responses, to remove private knowledge while still preserving performance on core capabilities. Through extensive experiments on speech QA datasets, we show that these unlearning methods can reduce the privacy leakage rate by up to 80% while maintaining near-neutral performance on non-private speech QA and general speech understanding benchmarks.

</details>


### [37] [Ensemble-Conditioned Molecular Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15077)
*Ross Irwin,Alessandro Tibo,Jon Paul Janet,Simon Olsson*

Main category: cs.LG
