TL;DR: 该论文将分子设计重新定义为对构象集合(分布)的模式与性质的联合优化，并提出集合条件引导(ensemble-conditioned guidance)框架，使3D生成模型同时在模式轴和性质轴上被条件化，配合自适应对称学习与可变尺寸生成，在多模式条件化与集合性质优化基准及两个药物发现任务上取得改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统分子设计通常被视作寻找能采用单一生物活性构象的分子，但真实分子以构象分布的形式存在，且决定候选分子是否可行的许多性质取决于整个分布而非某一单构象。因此需要将分子设计的目标从单一构象扩展到构象集合的模式与性质。

Method: 提出集合条件引导框架，将3D分子生成模型同时以模式轴(形状、药效团轮廓或蛋白口袋)与性质轴(在整体分布上计算的聚合标量)为条件。模式条件在推理时通过组合各条件下产生的向量场自适应合成，条件可被趋向或规避、跨模态混合、任意数量组合，单一训练模型即可表达多种设计任务。引入自适应对称学习以组合来自不同参考系的条件，并扩展框架支持可变尺寸生成。

Result: 在多模式条件化与集合性质优化的新基准上进行了评估，并将框架应用于双靶点结合剂设计与活性态选择性激动剂设计两个实际药物发现任务；两种情况下，在额外状态上施加条件相较单状态条件均改善了期望结果。

Conclusion: 分子设计可被有效地重构为对构象集合模式与性质的联合优化，集合条件引导框架通过自适应向量场组合、跨参考系条件组合与可变尺寸生成，实现用单一模型表达广泛设计任务，并在基准与实际药物发现任务中验证了多状态/多模式条件化的增益。

Abstract: Molecular design is typically approached as a problem of finding molecules which can adopt a single bioactive conformation. In reality, molecules occupy a distribution over conformations, and many of the properties which determine whether a candidate is viable depend on that distribution rather than on any single conformer. We reframe molecular design as an optimisation of both the modes and properties of molecules' conformational ensembles, where modes can be represented as shapes, pharmacophore profiles or protein pockets, and properties are aggregate scalars computed over the whole distribution. To realise this we introduce ensemble-conditioned guidance, a framework which conditions 3D molecular generative models on both axes simultaneously. Mode conditions are composed adaptively at inference by combining the vector fields produced under each condition. Conditions may be targeted or avoided, mixed across modalities and combined in arbitrary numbers, allowing a wide range of design tasks to be expressed with a single trained model. We introduce adaptive symmetry learning to allow conditions from different reference frames to be composed, and extend our generative framework to enable flexible-size generation. We evaluate on new benchmarks for multi-mode conditioning and ensemble property optimisation, and apply the framework to two practical drug discovery tasks, dual-target binder design and active-state-selective agonist design, where in both cases conditioning on the additional state improves the desired outcome over single-state conditioning.

</details>


### [38] [Algorithmic Information Dynamics of Learning: A Certified, Differentiable Complexity Controller for Grokking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13197)
*Luan Ozelim,Hector Zenil*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 用可微、可认证的算法复杂度估计器 K^CDM_sF 将算法信息动力学（AID）的微积分引入学习动力学，以 grokking 为对象：瞬时损失"踢"使复杂度估计器成为控制器，加速 grokking；复杂度门控比训练损失门控节省27%干预，且仅映射复杂度能标示转变完成；估计器的独特贡献在于"时机"（何时触发、何时释放）而非归因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AID 通过扰动系统并测量算法复杂度变化来研究系统，但其常用估计器（块分解法 BDM）是分段常数，使微积分只能退化为有限差分，无法在连续意义上做梯度/微分控制。作者希望把这一微积分工具真正带入学习动力学，特别是 grokking 场景——其中存在已知的复杂度序参量，但此前未被"激活"为可操作的控制量。

Method: 采用经认证且可微的估计器 K^CDM_sF 替代 BDM，使复杂度成为可微信号；对训练过程施加瞬态损失"踢"（kick），把估计器当作控制器使用，在 Levin 的描述长度—时间意义下加速 grokking；用数据依赖的 Occam 边界刻画有限尺寸趋势 f_c ~ ln p / p，并以优惠券收集者（coupon-collector）模型解释；做消融对比复杂度门控与训练损失门控；检验认证先验、逐参数 ∇K 归因的可替代性（均匀先验传感器给出位级相同的门控决策，随机支撑在稀疏阈值以上与 ∇K 选择相当）；直接场扰动探测 Occam 场的成核式响应。

Result: 复杂度门控以少 27% 的干预量在拯救失败种子（failing seeds）上匹配训练损失门控；在测试的信号中，只有映射复杂度（map complexity）能标记转变的完成；认证先验与逐参数 ∇K 归因均为可替代的（均匀先验传感器给出位级一致的门控决策；随机支撑在稀疏阈值以上可与 ∇K 选择的支撑匹配）；直接场扰动显示对 Occam 场的类成核响应——在所探测振幅范围内未解析出线性区，因此不支持涨落—耗散替代；有限场响应在接近转变时增长数个数量级。这些测量解释了经验调参的阶梯式脉冲：bang–bang 脉冲、由 yield 触发与释放的停滞。踢的效果可迁移到稀疏奇偶（sparse parity）和 transformer；持续权重空间损失则失败。

Conclusion: 算法复杂度估计器（K^CDM_sF）的核心贡献是"时机"——决定何时触发与何时释放——而非归因；复杂度门控在干预效率与转变完成标记上具优势，类成核的 Occam 场响应与数据依赖的 Occam 边界共同解释了 bang–bang 阶梯脉冲的有效性，且该机制可迁移到稀疏奇偶任务与 transformer，但持续的权重空间损失无效。

Abstract: Algorithmic Information Dynamics (AID) studies systems by perturbing them and measuring changes in algorithmic complexity, but its usual estimator, the Block Decomposition Method, is piecewise constant, restricting the calculus to finite differences. We use $K^{\mathrm{CDM}}_{\mathrm{s}F}$, a certified, differentiable estimator, to bring the calculus into learning dynamics: grokking, where a complexity order parameter is known but has not been made to act. A\empts a transient loss kick, the estimator becomes a controller that accelerates grokking in Levin's description-length--versus-time sense, within a data-dependent Occam boundary whose finite-size trend, $f_c\sim\ln p/p$, is consistent with a coupon-collector interpretation. Ablations show that a complexity gate matches a train-loss gate in rescuing failing seeds with $27\%$ less intervention; among the tested signals, only map complexity marks the transition's completion; the certified prior and the per-parameter $\nabla K$ attribution are both fungible (a uniform-prior sensor makes bit-identical gate decisions, and random supports match $\nabla K$-selected ones above a sparsity threshold); and direct field perturbation shows a nucleation-like response to the Occam field (no linear regime is resolved over the probed amplitudes, so these measurements do not justify a fluctuation--dissipation surrogate), with a finite-field response growing by orders of magnitude toward the transition. These measurements account for the empirically tuned staircase: bang--bang pulses, stall-fired and released on yield, whose iteration plausibly builds the response it exploits. The kick transfers to sparse parity and to a transformer; a sustained weight-space loss fails. The algorithmic estimator's distinct contribution is timing (when to fire and when to release), not attribution.

</details>


### [39] [SPICE: Simple Polysemantic Feature Interpretation via Clustering-based Explanation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13198)
*Sehyun Lee,Dahee Kwon,Damin Lee,Jaesik Choi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出SPICE框架，用于通用化地分析深度视觉模型中的多义性（polysemanticity），无需架构特定规则且自动确定概念簇数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络可解释性中的多义性问题（单个神经元被多个不相关概念激活）阻碍了功能理解。现有方法局限于特定架构，依赖人工启发式（如固定概念簇数量K），限制了通用性和可扩展性，尤其不适用于现代Transformer模型。

Method: 提出SPICE（基于聚类的简单多义特征解释）框架：避免架构依赖的传播规则，自动确定每个神经元的概念簇数量，无需预设K，支持大规模模型的可扩展分析。

Result: 实现了首次跨CNN和Transformer的系统性多义性比较，并全面研究了多义性的产生机制、随深度和架构的变化规律，以及其通过不同计算路径的形成方式。

Conclusion: SPICE为深度视觉架构中的多义性分析提供了通用、可扩展的框架，克服了现有方法的架构特定性和人工启发式依赖。

Abstract: One of the pivotal recent challenges in neural network interpretability is polysemanticity, where a single neuron is activated by multiple, often unrelated concepts, hindering clear functional understanding. Although prior work has explored this phenomenon, existing approaches remain architecture-specific and depend on manual heuristics such as a fixed number of concept clusters ($K$), limiting their generality and scalability--especially for modern Transformer-based models. To address these limitations, we introduce SPICE (\textbf{S}imple \textbf{P}olysemantic Feature \textbf{I}nterpretation via \textbf{C}lustering-based \textbf{E}xplanation), a generalizable framework for analyzing polysemanticity in deep vision architectures. SPICE avoids architecture-dependent propagation rules, enabling the first systematic comparison of polysemanticity across both CNNs and Transformers, and automatically determines the number of concept clusters per neuron, eliminating reliance on a preset $K$ and supporting scalable analysis for large models. Using SPICE, we conduct a comprehensive investigation into how polysemanticity emerges, varies across depth and architecture, and forms through distinct computational pathways.

</details>


### [40] [Discovering and Preserving Category Correlation Knowledge via Adaptive Reciprocal Knowledge Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13199)
*Dawen Jiang,Zhishu Shen,Zeyu Liu,Tiehua Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出自适应互惠知识蒸馏（AR-KD），通过对教师模型的类别相关矩阵与学生关系表示进行互惠适配，简化教师输出分布，缓解类间暗知识崩塌，从而提升轻量学生模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识蒸馏多采用静态、单向的“教师到学生”范式，忽视了学生学习的动态性，且无法针对难样本提供有针对性的指导；同时教师与学生模型间巨大的规模差距常阻碍有效的知识迁移，过自信的教师会导致类间暗知识崩塌。

Method: 提出 AR-KD，在教师模型上执行互惠适配：将教师的类别相关矩阵与学生的关系表示相匹配，从而重塑教师的预测结构，使其更契合学生的容量。这种关系对齐缓解了由过度自信教师导致的类间暗知识崩塌，使学生能从更丰富、更兼容的监督信号中学习。

Result: 在 CIFAR-100 与 ImageNet-1k 分类数据集上评估 AR-KD，其性能优于当前最先进的知识蒸馏基线。在同构与异构设置下，学生模型精度最高提升 7.13%，平均比原始 KD 高 1.42% 至 4.15%，且与其他先进方法结合时可进一步提升。代码已开源。

Conclusion: AR-KD 通过互惠适配教师输出分布，有效改善了知识迁移效果，在多种蒸馏场景中取得一致且显著的性能提升，并具备与其他先进蒸馏方法良好的兼容性。

Abstract: Knowledge distillation aims to improve the performance of lightweight student models by transferring knowledge from larger and more powerful teacher models. However, a substantial size gap between teacher and student models often impedes effective knowledge transfer. Most existing approaches adopt a static, one-way teacher-to-student distillation paradigm, which overlooks the dynamic nature of student learning and fails to provide targeted guidance on hard samples. In this paper, we propose adaptive reciprocal knowledge distillation (AR-KD), a novel method that improves knowledge transfer by simplifying the teacher's output distribution. Specifically, AR-KD performs reciprocal adaptation on the teacher by matching its class correlation matrix to the student's relational representation, which reshapes the teacher's prediction structure to better suit the student's capacity. This relational alignment mitigates the collapse of inter-class dark knowledge caused by overconfident teachers, enabling the student to learn from richer and more compatible supervisory signals. We evaluate AR-KD on CIFAR-100 and ImageNet-1k classification datasets, where it outperforms state-of-the-art knowledge distillation baselines. Specifically, AR-KD improves student performance across homogeneous and heterogeneous setups: up to 7.13% accuracy gain for students, 1.42% to 4.15% higher than vanilla KD on average, and further improvements when integrated with other advanced methods. Our code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/ARKD/.

</details>


### [41] [Criticality in Dissimilar Decomposition and Undersampling of Random Datasets with Anomalies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13201)
*Ghurumuruhan Ganesan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文将LLM生成的数据视为与主数据点"链接"的异常点，利用冗余图与迭代技术，推导强相异分解最小规模的界，并揭示由主数据点主导转向异常点主导的相变现象，同时给出随机下采样数据强相似性的规模临界性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未来LLM的训练数据将包含大量由当前LLM生成的AI文本/图像数据。因此需要理解这些AI生成数据如何影响批次分解，进而影响新LLM的性能。

Method: 将AI生成数据建模为"链接"到主数据点的异常点；采用冗余图（redundancy graphs）与迭代技术，对整体随机数据集的分解与下采样性质进行分析，推导强相异（SD）分解最小规模的界。

Result: 得到了强相异分解最小规模的界；证明了相变现象：当异常点数量较少时，最小规模本质上由主数据点决定，超过某一阈值后则由异常点"接管"；并建立了随机下采样数据强相似性的规模临界性结果。以类别型数据集（整体空间规模远大于数据集规模）为例进行了说明。

Conclusion: AI生成数据的比例会显著改变数据集的分解结构与下采样性质，并存在明确的阈值相变；相关理论为理解含AI生成数据的训练集提供了刻画与临界尺度依据。

Abstract: Training datasets for upcoming LLMs would include a significant amount of AI text/image data generated from current LLMs. In such a scenario, it is important to understand how this affects batch decompositions and thereby, the performance of the resultant new LLM. In this paper, we consider AI generated data as anomalies ``linked" to main data points and study decomposition and undersampling properties of the overall random dataset. We use redundancy graphs and iteration techniques to obtain bounds for the minimum size of a strongly dissimilar (SD) decomposition and demonstrate a phase transition phenomena, wherein the minimum size is essentially determined by the \emph{main} data points when the number of anomalies is small and is ``taken" over by the anomalies above a certain threshold. We also establish a size criticality result for the strong similarity of a randomly undersampled dataset and illustrate our results with examples involving categorical datasets, whose overall space size is much larger than the size of the dataset.

</details>


### [42] [Do Tabular Foundation Models Still Need Feature Engineering?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13202)
*Yifan WU,Pinjun Dong,Jiran Tao,Binyan Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究探讨表格基础模型(TFM)能力增强后，人工特征工程是否仍有价值。通过在TabArena基准上对两大TFM家族的多个版本进行受控实验，发现特征工程收益集中于早期模型代，对最强模型可忽略；但从相关数据集中添加上下文信息仍能提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 特征工程长期是表格机器学习的基石，而基于大规模表格数据预训练、通过上下文学习应用的表格基础模型(TFM)兴起，引出一个自然疑问：随着模型能力增强，人工特征构建是否仍然重要。作者希望系统检验这一假设。

Method: 对两大主流TFM家族的多个版本开展受控研究，在TabArena基准数据集上测试多种现有特征工程技术；并辅以补充实验，考察从相关数据集中引入上下文信息的影响。

Result: 特征工程增益呈现一致规律：集中于较早的模型代，而对最强模型增益可忽略。补充实验表明，加入来自相关数据集的上下文信息仍能提升性能。

Conclusion: 更强的TFM对显式特征工程的依赖降低，即重新表征现有输入的效果减弱；但提供额外任务相关上下文仍然有益，说明强TFM性能增益的来源发生了转移。

Abstract: Feature engineering has long been a cornerstone of tabular machine learning. Tabular foundation models (TFMs) are pretrained on a wide range of tabular datasets and applied via in-context learning. Their rise raises a natural question: does manual feature construction still matter as these models become more capable? To answer this, we perform a controlled study across several versions of two major TFM families, testing a wide range of existing feature engineering techniques on benchmark datasets from TabArena. We find a consistent pattern: feature engineering gains are concentrated in earlier model generations and become negligible for the strongest models. These results suggest that stronger TFMs depend less on explicitly engineered input representations. In a complementary experiment, however, adding in-context information from related datasets still improves performance. Our findings indicate a shift in the source of performance gains for stronger TFMs: re-representing existing inputs becomes less effective, while providing additional task-relevant context remains beneficial.

</details>


### [43] [Scalable partial information decomposition for symptom networks via supervised embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13203)
*Cillian Hourican,Eric Dignum,Rick Quax,Debraj Roy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于嵌入的偏信息分解(ePID)方法,将全体非焦点症状压缩为低基数离散嵌入,从而在可计算的两源PID框架下,为每对有向症状组合给出源独有、剩余独有、冗余与协同四类信息分量,实现可扩展、模型无关的症状网络分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 症状网络分析通常以标量边权表示症状间的成对关系,无法刻画两个症状对第三个症状的信息是否重叠,或信息是否仅以组合形式出现。PID可弥补这一缺陷,但当源变量超过少数几个时计算上不可行,因此需要一种可扩展的替代方案。

Method: 提出ePID流程:将非焦点症状压缩为低基数离散嵌入,再计算可处理的两源PID,输出源独有、剩余独有、冗余与协同分量。在标定至PHQ-9的合成贝叶斯网络以及83个真实数据集上,用5种PID度量对13种候选嵌入进行基准测试;其中采用有监督的凝聚条件信息瓶颈(ACIB)嵌入。

Result: ACIB嵌入在13种嵌入中恢复参考分解最准确,且对每种PID度量均给出非负原子(压缩4个及以上症状时协同恢复r=0.92)。PHQ-9网络(UK Biobank,N=154,291;新乡学生样本,N=24,292)中,周边症状背景通过冗余与剩余独有通道承载了大部分成对依赖,协同贡献6%–9%,两个队列均无有向边以协同为主;而在28项人际反应指针(IRI)中,45%的源对以协同为主。

Conclusion: ePID通过分离重叠信息与交互依赖信息,为区分观察相关中的冗余与协同贡献提供了可扩展、模型无关的方法,可作为标准症状网络方法学的补充。

Abstract: Pairwise relationships among mental-health symptoms are routinely summarised asscalar edge weights, which cannot express whether two symptoms carry overlapping information about a third or information that appears only in combination. Partial information decomposition (PID) addresses this gap but is computationally intractable beyond a few sources.
  We introduce embedding-based PID (ePID), a scalable pipeline that compresses all nonfocal symptoms into a low-cardinality discrete embedding and computes a tractable two-source PID, yielding source-unique, remainder-unique, redundant, and synergistic components for each ordered source-target pair. We benchmarked 13 candidate embeddings on synthetic Bayesian networks calibrated to PHQ-9 and on 83 real-world datasets across five PID measures.
  A supervised Agglomerative Conditional Information Bottleneck (ACIB) embedding recovered the reference decomposition most accurately of the 13 embeddings tested, and did so for every PID measure yielding non-negative atoms once four or more symptoms were compressed (synergy recovery r = 0.92). The two instruments then diverged sharply. In PHQ-9 networks (UK Biobank,N = 154,291; Xinxiang student sample, N = 24,292) the surrounding symptom context carried most pairwise dependence through redundant and remainder-unique channels; synergy contributed 6 to 9%, and no directed edge was synergy-dominated in either cohort. In the 28-item Interpersonal Reactivity Index, 45% of source pairs were. The identical pipeline, applied without parameter changes, therefore returned opposite profiles for the two instruments, each consistent with how that instrument was constructed.
  By separating overlapping from interaction-dependent information, ePID provides a scalable, model-agnostic complement to standard symptom-network methodology to distinguish redundant and synergistic contributions to observed correlations.

</details>


### [44] [GradRepair-ODE: Certified Gradient Repair for Neural ODE Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13204)
*Ziqian Bi,Xin Liang Chia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 GradRepair-ODE，一个在优化器步处对神经常微分方程梯度进行校验、修复与拒绝的可靠性框架，通过多候选梯度、方向有限差分检查与求解器诊断，识别并缓解数值失效模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经常微分方程在训练循环内使用数值求解器，求解器既决定前向轨迹又影响回传梯度，二者耦合导致在松步长、刚性动力学、混沌敏感性或事件不连续等情形下，可微 ODE 流水线可能返回方向可疑的有限梯度，给科学机器学习与连续时间生成建模（如扩散概率流 ODE、流匹配模型）带来可靠性隐患。

Method: GradRepair-ODE 计算多个梯度候选，利用方向有限差分检查与求解器诊断进行比对，诊断可能的数值失效模式，并通过路径切换或更严格的重计算来修复所选梯度，同时拒绝无法证明下降方向的更新步。

Result: 在六个合成 ODE 系统上，该方法对低风险系统保持原状；将 Robertson 与 Lorenz 的梯度修复至与严格参考的余弦相似度 1.000；将不安全接受步从 37 降至 0；对事件不连续案例选择拒绝而非施加未经认证的更新。

Conclusion: 论文主张对训练契约做一处简单改变：ODE 梯度在到达优化器时应附带数值证据，以保证其可靠性。

Abstract: Neural ordinary differential equations use numerical solvers inside the training loop. The solver determines the forward trajectory and also affects the gradient passed to the optimizer. That coupling creates a reliability problem for scientific machine learning and continuous-time generative modeling, including diffusion probability-flow ordinary differential equations and flow-matching models. Under loose step sizes, stiff dynamics, chaotic sensitivity, or event discontinuities, a differentiable ODE pipeline can return a finite gradient whose direction is numerically suspect. We introduce GradRepair-ODE, a reliability framework for checking, repairing, and rejecting ODE gradients at the optimizer step. The method computes several gradient candidates, compares them with directional finite-difference checks and solver diagnostics, diagnoses likely numerical failure modes, repairs selected gradients through path switching or stricter recomputation, and rejects steps whose descent direction cannot be certified. In six synthetic ODE systems, GradRepair-ODE leaves low-risk systems unchanged, repairs Robertson and Lorenz gradients to cosine similarity 1.000 against a strict reference, reduces unsafe accepted steps from 37 to 0, and rejects an event-discontinuous case instead of applying an uncertified update. The paper argues for a simple change in the training contract: an ODE gradient should reach the optimizer with numerical evidence attached.

</details>


### [45] [Task-Aware Federated Fine-Tuning for MoE-based Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13395)
*Tingqi Wang,Hongyu Ke,Haoxin Wang,Rafal Angryk,Zhipeng Cai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FedTAR，一种面向MoE大语言模型的任务感知联邦微调方法，通过路由输出建立本地更新与任务偏好的关联，实现任务对齐的聚合，解决异构客户端下的专家特化退化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE架构因稀疏专家激活而适合资源受限的分布式环境，但在客户端数据异构（任务偏好不同）时，直接聚合本地更新会削弱专家特化，并在共享专家上引入冲突的更新方向，导致破坏性干扰。

Method: FedTAR通过路由输出建立本地更新与任务偏好的关联：对路由特征和本地更新分别应用SVD，提取低维任务坐标与更新方向；基于任务坐标进行相似任务偏好客户端的簇内聚合与跨任务组的簇间聚合；再通过学习的任务到更新映射重建聚合更新，使最终更新保持与任务特定优化方向一致。

Result: 在四种基准任务、不同non-IID设置下评估，FedTAR持续优于强联邦微调基线，达到state-of-the-art性能。

Conclusion: FedTAR通过任务感知的聚合机制保留专家特化并缓解异构客户端间的破坏性干扰，为MoE大语言模型的联邦微调提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) has become a widely adopted architecture for Large Language Models (LLMs), as it improves model capacity while limiting computational overhead through sparse expert activation. This property makes MoE-based LLMs particularly attractive for resource-constrained distributed environments. However, federated fine-tuning of MoE-based LLMs remains challenging under heterogeneous client data. Since clients often correspond to different task preferences, directly aggregating their local updates may weaken expert specialization and introduce conflicting update directions on shared experts. To address these challenges, we propose FedTAR, a task-aware federated fine-tuning method for MoE-based LLMs. FedTAR establishes the association between local updates and task preference via routing outputs. Specifically, we apply Singular Value Decomposition (SVD) to both routing features and local updates to extract low-dimensional task coordinates and update directions. Based on the task coordinates, FedTAR performs intra-cluster aggregation among clients with similar task preferences and inter-cluster aggregation across different task groups. The aggregated update is then reconstructed through the learned task-to-update mapping, ensuring that the final update remains aligned with task-specific optimization directions. In this way, FedTAR preserves expert specialization and mitigates destructive interference among heterogeneous clients. We evaluate FedTAR on four benchmark tasks under different non-IID settings. Experimental results demonstrate that FedTAR consistently outperforms strong federated fine-tuning baselines and achieves state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [46] [Specification Oracles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13415)
*Atticus Cull,Justin McCarthy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探究语言模型能否作为紧凑的"活规范"（living specification oracle），比较外部文本笔记与权重微调两种事实存储方式，发现权重适配更能利用结构化信息但存储代价更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 规范设计存在基本权衡：省略细节会导致关键问题无法回答，逐条记录全部细节则使规范冗长庞大。作者希望探索能否用语言模型作为紧凑的规范预言机，通过学习目标事实并直接回答问题，从而缓解这一权衡。

Method: 构建四类共596个事实的"世界"（结构化与非结构化），在两种Qwen2.5模型规模上比较两种事实存储方式：外部文本笔记（note-sheet）与模型权重修改（weight-only adapter），并跨不同存储容量评估其问答准确率。

Result: 权重型预言机从结构中获益显著更多：以7B模型为例，在跨存储容量积分的准确率上，结构化世界比非结构化世界高18.5个百分点，而笔记型预言机仅高1.1个百分点。该优势伴随显著存储成本：最小的适配器约需175 KiB，而笔记预算上限仅为16 KiB。

Conclusion: 适配权重能更成功地利用潜在结构，而外部笔记所需的对象专属存储明显更少。两种方案在紧凑性与结构利用能力之间存在权衡。

Abstract: Specifications face a basic tradeoff: leave details out, and important questions go unanswered; record every detail separately, and the specification becomes large and prolix. We investigate whether a language model can serve as a compact, living specification oracle by learning facts about a target and answering questions about it directly. We compare two ways of storing the learned facts: external text notes and changes to the model's weights. Across four families of 596-fact worlds and two Qwen2.5 model sizes, weight-only oracles benefited substantially more from structure: with the 7B model, their accuracy integrated across storage capacities was 18.5 percentage points higher on structured than unstructured worlds, compared with 1.1 points for note-sheet oracles. This advantage came at a substantial storage cost, with the smallest adapter requiring approximately 175 KiB compared with a maximum note budget of 16 KiB. Adapted weights therefore exploited latent structure more successfully, while external notes required substantially less object-specific storage.

</details>


### [47] [ReCAST: Reward Credit Assignment across Timesteps for Online Diffusion Reinforcement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13425)
*Yihang Chen,Yuanhao Ban,Kuei-Chun Kao,Cho-Jui Hsieh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 ReCAST，首个用于扩散模型多奖励微调中"逐奖励、逐时间步"信用分配的方法，通过 Rényi 判别增益量化每个奖励在各去噪步的信息量，在用户偏好与时间分配之间解耦，显著提升训练奖励并泛化到留出评测器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用多奖励训练扩散模型时需区分两件事：用户偏好决定各奖励在总目标中的权重；奖励信息量决定其反馈在去噪何时有用。有些奖励在全局结构出现时即可评估样本，有些则需样本接近干净时才有效。现有方法未能同时解决这两个问题。

Method: 提出 ReCAST（Reward Credit ASsignment across Timesteps）。构造奖励×时间步的权重矩阵 W：行和匹配用户给定的奖励预算 λ，列和相等（每个去噪步总权重相同）。在此边际约束下，按各奖励每一步的 Rényi 判别增益分配权重，该增益可望远镜式累加为奖励诱导正策略与当前策略间的总判别度，作为时间信用分配依据。

Result: 在 SD3.5-Medium 上以两种四奖励设置、每种五个奖励预算 λ 进行评测。ReCAST 在一组设置中提升训练奖励、另一组中持平；两组设置下均提升所有留出评测器表现，并获得独立 LLM-as-a-Judge 的偏好。

Conclusion: ReCAST 带来的改进可泛化到训练奖励之外，验证其核心原则：在去噪步中，对每个奖励在其反馈最具信息量的时间步赋予更大权重。

Abstract: Training diffusion models with multiple rewards requires distinguishing user preference from reward informativeness. User preference determines how much each reward should contribute to the overall objective; reward informativeness determines when its feedback is useful during denoising. Some rewards can meaningfully evaluate a sample as soon as global structure emerges, but others become informative only when the sample is nearly clean. To address both questions jointly, we propose ReCAST (Reward Credit ASsignment across T}imesteps), the first method, to our knowledge, for per-reward, timestep-dependent credit assignment in diffusion reward fine-tuning. ReCAST separates user preferences from temporal allocation through a reward-by-timestep weight matrix $W$, whose row sums match the user-specified reward budgets $λ$, while its column sums are equal, assigning the same total weight to each denoising step. Under these marginal constraints, ReCAST allocates weight according to each reward's informativeness, quantified by its Rényi discriminability gain at each step. These gains telescope to the total discriminability between the reward-induced positive policy and the current policy, providing a basis for temporal credit assignment. We evaluate ReCAST by training SD3.5-Medium under two distinct four-reward settings, each across five reward budgets $λ$. ReCAST improves the training rewards in one setting and matches them in the other, improves every held-out judge in both, and is preferred by an independent LLM-as-a-Judge. Together, these results show that ReCAST yields improvements that generalize beyond the training rewards and support its core principle: assigning each reward greater weight at the denoising timesteps where its feedback is most informative.

</details>


### [48] [Certifiably Interpretable Training of ReLU-MLPs for Boolean Tasks with Guaranteed Truth-Table Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13439)
*Hrad Ghoukasian,Anastasis Kratsios*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 MACCHIATO 训练算法，联合构造显式结构化的 ReLU-MLP 与基于有符号文字、含 AND/OR/XOR 门的布尔电路，为 AI 系统可解释性提供证书与统计保证，并在随机 junta 任务上表现优于匹配的 Adam-trained MLP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着算力、模型与训练算法的进步，AI 系统愈发强大但其可解释性却在退化，亟需能保障可解释性的方法。

Method: MACCHIATO 迭代地将布尔函数的残差投影到低维 {AND,OR,XOR}-电路类上，并将所得电路精确编译为 ReLU-MLP；结合 ReLU-MLP 电路编译、ESPRESSO 逻辑最小化与基于影响力的变量选择。

Result: 在影响恢复条件下，若每一阶段残差至多依赖 log2(B) 位，样本分割变体用 T 个观测训练得到六层 ReLU-MLP（含输入层），宽度 O(mB)，真值表误差 O(sqrt(m(B+log(m/δ))/T))。在合成随机 junta 任务上，数据稀疏或投影对齐时优于深度与隐藏宽度匹配的 Adam-trained MLP；在 PyEDA 实现中迭代过程可在平坦环境维度 ESPRESSO 超过三小时预算的场景下完成。

Conclusion: MACCHIATO 通过联合构造显式 ReLU-MLP 与布尔电路证书，在提供统计可解释保证的同时兼顾效率与部分场景下的性能优势。

Abstract: As compute scales, models evolve, and training algorithms advance, our ability to explain the increasingly powerful AI systems they enable is eroding. To help safeguard interpretability, we introduce a specialized training algorithm (MACCHIATO) that jointly constructs (i) an explicitly structured $\operatorname{ReLU}$-MLP from partial truth-table observations and (ii) an explicit Boolean circuit over signed literals with $\{\operatorname{AND},\operatorname{OR},\operatorname{XOR}\}$ gates certifying what its subnetworks compute and how they compose. Intuitively, we iteratively project the residuals of a Boolean function onto low-dimensional $\{\operatorname{AND},\operatorname{OR},\operatorname{XOR}\}$-circuit classes and exactly compile the resulting circuit into a $\operatorname{ReLU}$-MLP; we combine $\operatorname{ReLU}$-MLP circuit compilation, ESPRESSO logic minimization, and influence-based variable selection.
  Roughly speaking, our interpretability certificate is complemented by a statistical guarantee: under the theorem's influence-recovery conditions, if each of the $m$ stage-wise residuals depends on at most $\log_2(B)$ bits, a sample-splitting variant of our algorithm trained on $T$ observations returns a six-layer $\operatorname{ReLU}$-MLP (counting the input layer) of width $\mathcal{O}(mB)$ with truth-table error $\mathcal{O}\bigl(\sqrt{m(B+\log(m/δ))/T}\bigr)$.
  On synthetic random-junta tasks, our networks outperform depth- and hidden-width-matched Adam-trained MLPs in several data-sparse or projection-aligned regimes, while the trained ReLU-MLPs are stronger in others. Moreover, in our explicit PyEDA truth-table implementation, the iterative procedure completes in regimes where flat ambient-dimensional ESPRESSO exceeds the three-hour computational budget.

</details>


### [49] [Efficient Online Inverse Optimization with $O(d)$ Regret](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13440)
*Yang Cai,Anupam Gupta,Vineet Gupta,Guru Guruganesh,Yanchen Jiang,Christopher Liaw,Aranyak Mehta,Renato Paes Leme,Grigoris Velegkas,Di Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了在线逆线性优化的确定性算法，达到 $O(d)$ 的遗憾界（对时间范围一致），每轮时间复杂度为 $O(d^2)$，是首个兼具高效性与恰当性（proper）的此类算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前 Dewasurendra 虽得到了同阶的 $O(d)$ 遗憾界，但所用规则为“非恰当（improper）”算法——即在每个尺度上枚举覆盖集，每轮开销高达 $T^{\Theta(d)}$，无法实用。因此需要一个既高效又恰当（proper）的算法来落实这一遗憾界，并回答 Gollapudi 等与 Oki、Sakaue 的问题。

Method: 算法建立在 Sakaue 等的变度量（variable-metric）框架之上，引入自归一化秩一更新（self-normalized rank-one update）；关键改动是以迹幂 $\tr(H^{-1/2})$ 替换原有的 $\log\det$ 势函数，该量本身有界，从而消除了 $\ln T$ 项，实现与时间范围无关的遗憾界。

Result: 得到每轮 $O(d^2)$ 时间、遗憾 $O(d)$（对时间范围一致）的确定性算法；给出了首个高效的恰当算法。相关界同样适用于不进行优化的专家（expert），并给出了抗污染（corruption-robust）与秩自适应（rank-adaptive）变体，以及在凸最小化上的应用。

Conclusion: 通过变度量框架下的秩一更新与有界迹幂势函数，作者首次实现了在线逆线性优化中高效且恰当的最优阶遗憾界，并拓展至鲁棒、自适应及凸优化应用场景。

Abstract: We give a deterministic algorithm for online inverse linear optimization with regret $O(d)$, uniform in the horizon and $O(d^{2})$ time per round. A bound of this order was obtained recently by Dewasurendra, settling a question of Gollapudi et al.\ and of Oki and Sakaue, but by an improper rule that enumerates covers at every scale and costs $T^{Θ(d)}$ a round; ours is the first efficient such bound and the first proper one. We build on the variable-metric framework of Sakaue et al., adding a self-normalized rank-one update, and we replace the $\log\det$ potential by the trace power $\tr(H^{-1/2})$, which is bounded outright and removes the $\ln T$. The bound also holds against an expert that does not optimize, and we give corruption-robust and rank-adaptive variants, and an application to convex minimization.

</details>


### [50] [Learning to Solve Hard Problems in RL for LLMs by Never Giving Up](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13443)
*Michael Noukhovitch,Hamish Ivison,Nathan Lambert,Aaron Courville*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LLM的RL训练存在"马太效应"：易题提升大、难题提升小。作者提出NGU自适应采样方法，持续为每题生成样本直至正确，借助异步RL把算力从易题转移到难题，在数学与代码任务上提升单位算力性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RL训练对数据集的提升并不均衡，LLM已有的强项（易题）提升显著，而难题提升有限，呈现"富者愈富"的累积优势现象。作者认为仅用"难题需更多算力"不足以解释，现代RL方法本身在易题上浪费了过多算力，应当动态重分配算力。

Method: 提出Never Give Up（NGU）自适应采样法：对某问题持续生成样本直到获得一个正确解，再用于训练。结合异步RL，天然减少易题所需采样数、把更多算力投入难题。论文还研究影响NGU的设计选择（如off-policy鲁棒性），并总结出一套最佳实践。

Result: 在数学基准Deepscaler上，NGU提高了单位算力性能，难题上尤为明显。在近期代码任务Manufactoria上，按测试给奖励的标准GRPO无法完全解决涵盖易/难测试的问题；NGU迭代改进，逐步攻克更难的测试，最终学会完整解决代码问题。

Conclusion: RL for LLMs存在"马太效应"，根源之一是采样算力分配不当。NGU通过"直到做对为止"的自适应采样与异步RL重分配算力，在数学和代码任务上验证了单位算力效率与难题解决能力的提升。

Abstract: We demonstrate that training LLMs with RL does not improve performance equally across a dataset. RL shows large improvements on easy problems that an LLM is already good at solving, but small improvements on hard problems. We call this the Matthew Effect in RL for LLMs, after the phenomenon of cumulative advantage from economics and network science summarized as "the rich get richer". The naive explanation is that hard problems require more compute to find a solution. We argue that modern RL methods are exacerbating the issue by wasting too much compute on easy problems and instead should dynamically reallocate how they use compute. We introduce Never Give Up (NGU), a simple adaptive sampling method that keeps generating samples for a problem until one is correct. By leveraging asynchronous RL, this naturally uses fewer samples to filter out easy problems and allocates more compute to solving harder problems. We investigate the design choices that affect NGU, such as off-policy robustness, and develop a set of best practices. On the math benchmark Deepscaler, NGU improves performance per compute, especially on harder problems. On a recent coding task, Manufactoria, standard GRPO with a per-test reward fails to fully solve problems that have a range of easy and difficult tests. NGU iteratively improves, solving harder and harder tests, until it learns to fully solve coding problems.

</details>


### [51] [A Machine Learning API for Earth Observation Data Cubes Based on openEO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13453)
*Brian Pondi,Jonas Hurst,Rolf Simoes,Jonas Starke,Marius Appel,Edzer Pebesma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对地球观测数据立方体与机器学习输入范式不匹配的问题，提出openEO过程级ML规范，分模型初始化、模型动作、模型管理三阶段，并在R/Python三个原型后端验证跨后端互操作性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地球观测数据日益以时空数据立方体组织，而机器学习方法依赖表格特征矩阵或结构化张量输入，二者不匹配导致大量平台特定转换，难以复现与跨云迁移。openEO虽提供跨异构后端的统一数据访问与处理接口，却缺乏标准化的机器学习集成方案。

Method: 提出openEO的过程级ML规范，分为三阶段：模型初始化、模型动作（训练、调参、推理、验证）、模型管理。支持随机森林、SVM等经典算法，以及面向时间序列和空间图块的深度学习架构（TempCNN、Temporal Attention Encoder、基础模型推理）。实现R与Python三个原型后端，并通过作物类型制图用例，将相同过程图提交至独立的R和Python后端，比较预测结果与评价指标；另有两个用例分别验证时间序列深度学习与基础模型推理。

Result: 作物类型制图用例实现跨后端互操作，R与Python后端的预测及评价指标可比；深度学习与基础模型推理用例在专用后端成功执行。但实验显示，仅靠过程级规范无法实现完全跨后端可移植性：仍缺乏序列化格式与执行语义的深度协调，后端库版本与规范边界之外的预处理约定也影响可复现性。

Conclusion: 该规范提升了EO数据立方体上ML工作流在云平台间的可复现性、可移植性与可访问性。通过显式后端一致性配置文件同时解决序列化格式与执行语义、以及库版本和预处理约定问题，是最重要的近期方向。

Abstract: Earth Observation (EO) data are increasingly organized as spatio-temporal data cubes, while machine learning (ML) methods operate on tabular feature matrices or structured tensor inputs. This mismatch forces platform-specific transformations that are difficult to reproduce or transfer across cloud infrastructures. The openEO specification provides a unified interface for EO data access and processing across heterogeneous backends, but lacks a standardized approach for ML integration.
  We propose a process-level ML specification for openEO structured into three stages: model initialization, model actions (training, tuning, inference, validation), and model management. It supports classical algorithms such as Random Forest and SVM, as well as deep learning architectures for time-series and spatial patch-based modeling, including TempCNN, Temporal Attention Encoders, and foundation model inference. Three prototype implementations in R and Python demonstrate feasibility across diverse technology stacks. A crop type mapping use case demonstrates cross-backend interoperability by submitting an identical process graph to independent R and Python backends and comparing predictions and evaluation metrics.
  Two further use cases demonstrate deep learning on time series and foundation model inference, each executed on a dedicated backend. The prototypes reveal, however, that full cross-backend portability requires deeper harmonization of serialization formats and execution semantics than the process level alone can enforce; backend library versions and preprocessing conventions outside the specification's boundary also affect reproducibility. Addressing both through explicit backend conformance profiles represents the most important near-term direction. The specification advances the reproducibility, portability, and accessibility of ML workflows on EO data cubes across cloud platforms.

</details>


### [52] [On the Potential of Multi-Task Learning in Predictive Process Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13477)
*Lukas Kirchdorfer,Keyvan Amiri Elyasi,Heiner Stuckenschmidt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次对预测性流程监控（PPM）中的多任务学习（MTL）进行了系统性实证研究，评估了不同任务组合、神经架构与优化方法。结果显示，MTL能显著提升下一活动预测性能，并天然缓解类别不平衡问题，任务平衡在低容量模型下尤为关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PPM旨在预测正在进行的组织流程走向，使信息系统从执行支持转向主动分析与监控。尽管深度学习提升了PPM的预测精度，但多数方法采用单任务学习（STL），即为每个任务单独训练模型，既增加维护成本又忽视任务间的潜在协同。MTL作为联合学习多个预测目标的替代方案虽具前景，但其在PPM中的有效性尚未被充分探索：MTL是否及在何种设置下优于STL、哪些预测任务最受益于联合学习、哪些任务组合最具协同效应、以及任务是否需要及如何平衡，均不明确。

Method: 本文开展首个面向PPM的MTL综合实证研究，系统评估多种任务组合、多种神经网络架构以及多种优化方法，比较MTL与STL的表现，并考察任务平衡策略的影响。

Result: 实验结果表明，MTL是PPM中一种强有力的范式：在下一活动预测任务上取得显著提升，并能内在缓解类别不平衡问题；同时，任务平衡在低容量模型下尤为关键。

Conclusion: MTL在PPM中具有良好的适用性与优势，可作为优于STL的建模范式；未来研究应关注任务组合选择与任务平衡策略，特别是在模型容量受限的场景下。

Abstract: Predictive Process Monitoring (PPM) forecasts how ongoing organizational processes unfold, enabling information systems to move beyond execution support toward proactive analysis and monitoring. Although deep learning has improved prediction accuracy in PPM, most approaches follow a single-task learning (STL) setup, training a separate model per task. This increases maintenance effort and overlooks potential synergies. Multi-task learning (MTL), which jointly learns multiple prediction targets in one model, offers a promising alternative, yet its effectiveness in PPM remains underexplored. It remains unclear whether and under which settings MTL improves upon STL, which prediction tasks benefit most from joint learning, which task combinations are particularly synergistic, and if and how tasks should be balanced. To fill this gap, we present the first comprehensive empirical study of MTL for PPM, evaluating a variety of task combinations, neural architectures, and optimization methods. Overall, our results position MTL as a strong paradigm for PPM: we see substantial improvements in next-activity prediction and inherent mitigation of class imbalance using MTL, while task balancing is especially critical under low-capacity models.

</details>


### [53] [One Spectrum, Two Resources: Data-Memory Scaling in Autoregressive Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13500)
*Chiwun Yang,Xiaoyu Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在正熵自回归检索源中，数据与记忆两种资源由同一"预测-能量谱"支配，论文证明了极小极大律 R*_value(n,B) ≍ Φ_μ(n^{-1}) + Φ_μ(τ_B)，并给出注意力头实现与实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心问题是：多少"已学习记忆"才值得用更多数据去获益？即数据与记忆两种资源如何权衡、如何共同决定预测性能，需要统一的理论刻画。

Method: 在正熵自回归检索源框架下，将每个坐标的贡献定义为查询概率乘以其未知 logit 半径的平方，得到预测-能量谱 μ。基于该谱证明极小极大律，其中数据决定分辨率 1/n，记忆通过最优比特分配决定的水平 τ_B 决定截断。进一步给出掩码 query-key 注意力头的实现机制（路由、格式、算术误差）及指数自适应分配、有限精度实现、双侧算术假设下的算力-精度律。

Result: 证明了极小极大律 R*_value(n,B) ≍ Φ_μ(n^{-1}) + Φ_μ(τ_B)；完整曲线可恢复正谱；发现能量-维度配对是本质的——块能量与块维度边缘相同的两个因果源具有不同的数据与记忆指数；实验恢复了数据-记忆坍缩与耦合指数，解释了路由与分配机制，并在六个预训练模型尺度上考察了仅权重量化。

Conclusion: 数据与记忆由单一预测-能量谱统一支配，二者不可分离；能量-维度配对不可或缺；所提注意力头能以显式误差实现该律，理论与实验一致。

Abstract: How much learned memory is needed to benefit from more data? We show that the two resources are governed by one predictive-energy spectrum in a positive-entropy autoregressive retrieval source. Each coordinate contributes its query probability times the squared radius of its unknown logit. Writing $μ$ for the resulting energy spectrum, we prove the minimax law $\mathfrak R^*_{\rm value}(n,B)\asymp_R Φ_μ(n^{-1})+Φ_μ(τ_B), Φ_μ(t)=\int\min\{x,t\}\,μ(\mathrm dx),$ for $n$ prediction blocks and a learned state with at most $2^B$ values. Data set the resolution $1/n$; memory sets the level $τ_B$ reached by optimal bit allocation. The complete curve also recovers the positive spectrum. Energy-dimension pairing is essential: two causal sources with identical block-energy and block-dimension marginals have different data and memory exponents. A masked query-key attention head learns the route and values, realizing the law with explicit routing, format, and arithmetic errors. Further results give exponent-adaptive allocation, finite-precision realization, and compute-precision laws under two-sided arithmetic assumptions. Experiments recover the data-memory collapse and coupling exponents, explain the routing and allocation mechanisms, and examine weight-only quantization across six pretrained-model scales.

</details>


### [54] [A Three-Axis Stress Test of LLM vs Classical ML for Network Intrusion Detection under Distribution Shift and Adversarial Evasion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13511)
*Muhammad Ebad Atif,Muhammad Haider Ali*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究指出NIDS领域仅用同数据集评估LLM的范式不完整，通过在两个独立NetFlow v2网络上从同数据集、跨数据集迁移和对抗逃逸三个维度比较XGBoost与RoBERTa-LoRA，发现不存在通用赢家。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在网络入侵检测（NIDS）中越来越多地与经典机器学习进行对比，但几乎总是采用同数据集评估；作者认为这种评估协议不完整，无法反映模型在真实分布偏移和对抗场景下的鲁棒性，因此需要多维度评估框架。

Method: 在两个独立采集的NetFlow v2网络上评估XGBoost与RoBERTa-LoRA，沿三个轴进行测试：同数据集性能、跨数据集迁移、对抗逃逸；并加入分阶段的特征泄漏消融实验以分析跨数据集迁移机制。

Result: 同数据集下两模型统计持平；跨数据集分布偏移下XGBoost以F1高15点、平衡准确率高25点显著胜出，RoBERTa-LoRA在目标网络FPR达0.78；对抗逃逸下RoBERTa-LoRA以约17点F1显著胜出，两模型FPR均低于0.01。特征泄漏消融呈非单调改善，表明泄漏信号分布于特征表示中而非局限于少数列；两个网络间的跨数据集迁移具有强方向性。

Conclusion: 推荐哪个模型完全取决于所测试的轴而非同数据集准确率；NIDS模型应沿多个独立鲁棒性轴评估，且每轴需使用多个指标。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly benchmarked against classical machine learning for network intrusion detection (NIDS), almost always using same-dataset evaluation, and that protocol turns out to be incomplete. Evaluating XGBoost and RoBERTa-LoRA on two independently collected NetFlow v2 networks across three axes (same-dataset performance, cross-dataset transfer, and adversarial evasion) reveals no universal winner. The two models are statistically tied same-dataset. XGBoost wins decisively under cross-dataset distribution shift, by 15 points of F1 and 25 points of balanced accuracy; on the target network RoBERTa-LoRA's false positive rate reaches 0.78, leaving it barely above chance despite a superficially moderate F1. RoBERTa-LoRA wins decisively under adversarial evasion, by roughly 17 points of F1 at a representative mid-range perturbation strength, while both models hold false positive rates below 0.01 throughout. The model an evaluator would recommend therefore depends entirely on which axis is tested, not on same-dataset accuracy alone. A staged feature-leakage ablation improves cross-dataset transfer non-monotonically, indicating the leakage signal is distributed across the feature representation rather than confined to a few columns, and cross-dataset transfer between our two networks is strongly directional. These results argue for evaluating NIDS models along multiple independent robustness axes, and with more than one metric per axis.

</details>


### [55] [Adaptive Phase-Switching for Communication-Efficient Federated LoRA Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13512)
*Jerry Adams Franklin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文测量双向 B-only 联邦 LoRA 协议的逐轮上传/下载字节数，将五种方法（三种来自已有工作）置于统一的通信-质量前沿上；提出自适应相位切换聚合器 ReverseAdaptive，通过监测全局训练损失的相对改进并对比无量纲阈值来定位前沿拐点。在 TinyLlama-1.1B-Chat + Alpaca 上，其往返通信节省达 40.5%，留出指令跟随损失代价仅 0.0063，且阈值可跨模型规模迁移、无需重调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦 LoRA 微调虽降低了单客户端可训练参数量，但客户端-服务器通信仍是主要开销。已有联邦 LoRA 协议的通信核算存在两个缺陷：一是忽略了协议切换聚合模式时的不对称过渡轮次（asymmetric transition round），二是所报告的节省未考虑分组查询注意力（GQA）的形状差异，导致评估失真。

Method: 1) 对双向 B-only 联邦 LoRA 协议逐轮计量上传与下载字节数；2) 将五种方法（含三种已有方法）置于同一通信-质量前沿（frontier）上比较；3) 提出自适应相位切换聚合器 ReverseAdaptive，以全局训练损失相对改进量对比一个无量纲阈值来动态定位前沿拐点（knee），而非预先固定相位边界；4) 与冻结首个 LoRA 因子的 FFA-LoRA 对比，并统计每方法种子标准差以判断显著性；5) 检验阈值跨模型规模的可迁移性及过渡质量代价跨数据集的稳定性。

Result: 在 TinyLlama-1.1B-Chat + Alpaca 上，ReverseAdaptive 相对 FLoRA 实现实测往返通信节省 40.5%，留出指令跟随损失代价为 0.0063；在留出损失上优于 FFA-LoRA 0.0182，超过该指标上最大单方法种子标准差的二十余倍；同一阈值跨模型规模无需重调即可迁移；过渡带来的质量代价在所测两个数据集上保持稳定。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在冻结前先学习该 LoRA 因子可产生更优适配器；采用基于损失相对改进的无量纲阈值进行自适应相位切换，能以较小质量代价显著降低联邦 LoRA 的通信开销，且该阈值具备跨模型规模的迁移性与跨数据集的质量稳定性。

Abstract: Federated fine-tuning of large language models with low-rank adaptation reduces per-client trainable parameters, but client-to-server communication remains the dominant cost. Existing accounting for federated LoRA protocols omits the asymmetric transition round when a protocol changes aggregation mode, and reports savings that ignore grouped-query attention shapes. This paper measures per-round upload and download bytes for a bidirectional B-only federated LoRA protocol and places five methods, three from prior work, on a single communication-quality frontier. The frontier has a knee, which an adaptive phase-switching aggregator, ReverseAdaptive, locates by monitoring the relative improvement in global training loss against a dimensionless threshold rather than by fixing a phase boundary in advance. On TinyLlama-1.1B-Chat with Alpaca, ReverseAdaptive attains 40.5 percent measured round-trip savings over FLoRA at a held-out instruction-following loss cost of 0.0063. It outperforms FFA-LoRA, which freezes the first of the two LoRA factors at initialization, by 0.0182 in held-out loss, more than twenty times the largest per-method seed standard deviation on that metric, so learning that factor before freezing it produces better adapters. The same threshold transfers across model scales without retuning, and the quality cost of the transition is stable across the two datasets tested.

</details>


### [56] [Operational Range Bounding in Spectroscopy: A Safety Cage Framework for Machine Learning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13514)
*Nikki Grens,Luís F. Simões,Kai Hou Yip,Theresa Lueftinger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种模块化"安全笼"架构，作为并行监控层评估黑盒机器学习预测的有效性，在Ariel数据挑战赛数据上验证：单一指标无法覆盖全部失效模式，融合指标后仅损失约20%数据覆盖即可使误差降低45%–65%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在空间任务等安全关键场景中，黑盒机器学习模型缺乏地面真值验证，其对未建模的仪器异常、恒星活动和域偏移高度敏感，带来未量化的风险。尤其是在从系外行星光变曲线提取透射光谱这类任务中，亟需一种不修改原有估计器即可监控预测可靠性的机制。

Method: 提出模块化的"安全笼"（safety cage）架构，作为并行监控层运行，不改变底层估计器。通过监测多种运行时指标（不确定性量化、域外检测、影响函数）约束模型运行域至已验证区域。在2019与2021两届Ariel数据挑战赛数据集上，开展域内与跨域的受控评估，并借助形式化的覆盖度-风险（coverage-risk）框架系统分析指标组合。

Result: 模型失效是多方面的，没有任何单一指标能捕捉所有失效模式，因此需要指标融合。采用安全驱动的拒识策略后，数据覆盖仅减少约20%，误差即在不同域和评估指标下降低45%至65%。通过覆盖度-风险框架，识别出能最大化风险排序准确性、优化覆盖度与科学性能权衡的指标组合配置。

Conclusion: 安全笼为检测不可靠预测提供了透明机制，是数据驱动模型在天体物理等地面真值稀缺的科学应用中安全部署的关键一步。

Abstract: Ensuring the reliability of black-box machine learning models in safety-critical space missions remains a significant challenge, particularly when ground-truth is unavailable for validation. Although machine learning models offer a powerful means to augment standard pipelines by extracting transmission spectra from complex exoplanetary light curves, their susceptibility to unmodelled instrument anomalies, stellar activity, and domain shifts introduces unquantified risks. This study evaluates a modular safety cage architecture that operates as a parallel monitoring layer to assess the validity of a prediction without modifying the underlying estimator. By monitoring different runtime indicators, including uncertainty quantification, out-of-domain detection, and influence functions, the framework constrains the model's operational domain to a verified region. A controlled evaluation is conducted under both in-domain and cross-domain conditions, using datasets from the 2019 and 2021 editions of the Ariel Data Challenges. The results reveal that model failure is multifaceted and that no single indicator captures all failure modes, demonstrating the need for indicator fusion. The application of safety-driven rejection strategies shows that a modest 20% reduction in data coverage results in error reductions between 45% and 65% across different domains and evaluation metrics. Using a formalised coverage-risk framework, a systematic analysis of indicator combinations is performed to identify configurations that maximise risk-ranking accuracy and optimise the trade-off between data coverage and scientific performance. Safety cages provide a transparent mechanism for detecting unreliable predictions and represent a critical step towards the safe deployment of data-driven models in scientific applications, such as astrophysics, where ground truth is seldom available.

</details>


### [57] [GeoTTER: Leveraging Local Geometry of Optimal Transport for Zero-Shot Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13518)
*Wei-Yang Alex Lee,Rudrasis Chakraborty,Vishnu Lokhande*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GeoTTER 提出将局部图-拉普拉斯平滑与聚类引导的全局代价融合进最优传输，解决零样本分类中标签抖动与角度漂移问题，中位提升 +6.82%（对比零样本）与 +2.13%（对比 OTTER）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统零样本分类依赖预训练模型嵌入导出的固定代价矩阵，导致标定失准与适应性不足，缺乏对数据流形结构与全局几何约束的刻画。

Method: 面向两个失效模式分别设计技术：(1) 高频标签锯齿（样本级流形抖动，相邻嵌入被分到不同类）——通过图-拉普拉斯平滑将局部几何结构引入最优传输公式，基于谱图理论强制邻域一致性；(2) 相干角度漂移（低频方向偏差，大群体样本相对真实标签原型共享同一角度偏移）——融合聚类引导代价分量与全局调整后的传输代价，形成兼顾全局分布约束与潜在数据结构的多目标优化。

Result: 在多样化基准上表现稳健：相对零样本分类中位提升 +6.82%，相对 OTTER 中位提升 +2.13%。

Conclusion: 将局部几何正则（平滑）与全局聚类结构约束统一进最优传输，可同时缓解高频抖动与低频角度漂移，提升零样本分类的标定质量与适应性。

Abstract: We present GeoTTER, a novel framework that redefines optimal transport in the realm of zero-shot classification. Conventional methods often suffer from miscalibration and a lack of adaptability, as they rely on fixed cost matrices derived solely from pre-trained model embeddings. In contrast, GeoTTER addresses these limitations by incorporating two key techniques. First, to alleviate high-frequency label jaggedness (sample-level manifold jitter that assigns neighboring embeddings to different classes), GeoTTER integrates local geometric structure into the optimal transport formulation via graph-Laplacian smoothing, a technique grounded in spectral graph theory that enforces neighborhood consistency. Second, to correct coherent angular drift (a low-frequency orientation bias in which large groups of samples share the same angular offset from their true label prototypes), we fuse clustering-guided cost components with a globally adjusted transport cost, achieving a multi-objective optimization that respects both global distribution constraints and latent data structure. With a median improvement of +6.82% compared to zero-shot and +2.13% compared to OTTER, GeoTTER shows robust improvements across a diverse set of benchmarks.

</details>


### [58] [Rolling Day-Wise Mortality Prediction in Critically Ill Patients With AKI on CRRT Utilizing Machine Pressure Waveforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13524)
*Shehan Irteza Pranto,Joanna Yang,Joshua Lambert,Stuart L. Goldstein,Lili Chan,Girish N. Nadkarni,Tiago K. Colicchio,Javier A. Neyra,Jin Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究首次将CRRT机器分钟级回路压力波形转化为患者级死亡风险信号：通过对齐、去噪与掩码清洗数据，构建基于Transformer的堆叠集成模型（晚期融合机器序列与EHR），在CRRTnet队列（976例、4585治疗日）上实现一天死亡率AUROC 0.766，优于单用机器（0.625）或单用EHR（0.717），SHAP显示回路不稳定特征将机器特征在前15特征中的占比从20.0%提升至46.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 危重AKI行CRRT患者死亡率高，但现有风险评估仅依赖EHR临床指标，忽略了机器记录的分钟级回路压力波形。该连续信号虽能反映体外循环与患者的交互，却因共享设备记录污染、非生理分钟、传感器伪迹而被丢弃；临床医生只能在抽血化验时才重新评估风险，无法实时察觉恶化。研究动机在于把这一被废弃的床旁流变为连续风险信号。

Method: 数据清洗：将机器记录对齐到已记录的therapy区间以防跨患者泄漏，剔除预冲（priming）与停机分钟，在合成尖峰注入基准上调参去噪，并对未观测区间做掩码处理而非插补。任务与模型：定义滚动式按天任务，构建基于Transformer的堆叠集成——将窗口降采样序列Transformer与使用回路不稳定特征和临床EHR变量的经典模型进行晚期融合。评估：在CRRTnet多中心队列（976例、4585治疗日）上做防泄漏基准，并用SHAP做特征归因。

Result: 单用机器流预测价值最低（AUROC 0.625），单用EHR为0.717，EHR与机器流融合后一天死亡率AUROC达0.766。SHAP归因显示回路不稳定描述符使机器特征在前15个组合模型特征中的数量由3个增至7个（20.0%→46.7%），其中滤器压力、跨膜压（TMP）和动静脉压差（ARD）贡献突出。

Conclusion: 这是首个纳入CRRT机器数据的患者级死亡预测研究，证明被丢弃的床旁连续流可经规范清洗与融合建模转为有效的连续风险信号，为CRRT危重患者的动态风险监测提供了新路径。

Abstract: Critically ill patients with acute kidney injury (AKI) on continuous renal replacement therapy (CRRT) face high mortality, yet current risk assessment relies primarily on clinical parameters from electronic health records (EHR) and ignores minute-level circuit pressure waveforms generated by CRRT machines that track the extracorporeal circuit's interaction with the patient. Clinicians therefore cannot see deterioration as it develops. Risk is reassessed only when labs are drawn, while this continuous record is discarded because it is contaminated by shared-device records, non-physiological minutes, and sensor artifacts. To make the stream usable, we aligned machine records to charted therapy intervals to prevent cross-patient leakage, removed priming and downtime minutes, tuned denoising on a synthetic spike-injection benchmark, and masked unobserved intervals rather than imputing them. On this cleaned stream, we define a rolling day-wise task and a transformer-based stacked ensemble that late-fuses a window-reduced sequence transformer with classical models using circuit-instability features and clinical EHR variables. In a leak-safe benchmark on the multi-center CRRTnet cohort (976 patients, 4,585 treatment days), the machine-only model had the lowest standalone prognostic value (AUROC 0.625), followed by the EHR-only model (0.717). Integrating EHR and machine streams reached a one-day mortality AUROC of 0.766. SHAP attribution showed that circuit-instability descriptors raised the machine share of the top 15 combined-model features from 3 to 7 (20.0% to 46.7%), highlighting filter pressure, transmembrane pressure (TMP), and access-to-return difference (ARD). To our knowledge, this is the first patient-level mortality prediction incorporating CRRT machine data, turning a discarded bedside stream into a continuous risk signal.

</details>


### [59] [Generative Interpretability via Scalable Neuro-Symbolic Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13529)
*Xiaocong Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文主张从"事后可解释性"转向"生成式可解释性"，即模型推理过程本身暴露可供人类理解并能进行因果干预的语义检查点，并提出神经符号模型作为具体实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型从聊天机器人进入智能体系统，模型输出会转化为对现实产生不可逆后果的动作。现有主流范式——事后可解释性——只能在行为发生后进行解释，无法在推理计算做出输出承诺之前进行审计或干预，因此在安全可信部署上存在结构性不足。

Method: 作者通过概念论证与范式对比，将生成式可解释性与其他可解释性研究范式进行比较，阐明其优势，并提出以神经符号模型作为生成式可解释性的具体实现方案。

Result: 论文论证了生成式可解释性作为一种架构属性的合理性，并指出神经符号模型可作为其可落地的实例化路径。

Conclusion: 为满足智能体系统的安全与可信需求，可解释性研究应从面向事后解释的范式转向内生于推理过程的生成式可解释性，神经符号模型是可行方向。

Abstract: As the use of Large Language Models moves from chatbots into agentic systems, where outputs become actions with irreversible consequences on reality, the existing paradigm on AI Interpretability research, post-hoc interpretability, is structurally inadequate for safe and trustworthy model deployment: it explains behavior after the fact but cannot audit or intervene in an inference computation before it commits to an output. We therefore argue for a shift toward \emph{generative interpretability}, an architectural property under which a model's inference pass natively exposes semantically meaningful checkpoints that are human-understandable and amenable to causal intervention. We show the merits of generative interpretability as comparison to other interpretability research paradigms, and propose Neuro-Symbolic Models as a concrete instantiation.

</details>


### [60] [Attention Is All You Need (to Avoid Spurious Oscillations)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13531)
*Jinyoung Jeong,Joseph B. Choi,Xinlun Cheng,H. S. Udaykumar,Sanghun Choi,Stephen S. Baek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该工作提出一种守恒、固定网格的有限体积格式，用受CFL条件约束的注意力通量按当前时间步所需输运量选择上游信息，从而在单个更新步内以较大时间步长稳健地跨多个网格输运激波。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心问题：注意力能否在一次更新中把激波跨多个网格移动而不破坏它。研究动机在于将注意力机制作为可学习的信息模板（information stencil），以突破传统显式格式在大时间步下面临的CFL稳定性与激波抹平之间的权衡。

Method: 采用守恒固定网格有限体积格式，通量为受CFL条件约束的注意力通量，依据当前时间步所需输运量选择上游信息。以一维无粘Burgers输运为核心机理测试，并与五阶WENO配合三阶SSP-RK3（WENO-5+SSP-RK3）以及受控的前向Euler对比，以分离通量选择与时间积分的作用。进一步通过推理时干预与重训练消融检验输运尺度信息与状态相关选择性的贡献；并在二维标量Burgers输运和一维浅水系统上验证该“守恒—尺度—选择”原则的可迁移性。

Result: 同一学习通量在常规小步长下保持可靠；时间步放大四倍时，仍能保持尖锐激波，且每次更新仅用一个阶段。学习到的注意力随局部输运可达范围向上游移动，并在激波附近更具选择性。推理时干预与重训练消融表明，输运尺度信息与状态相关选择直接贡献于性能。二维标量Burgers与一维浅水系统验证了该原则在标量设定之外的转移性。

Conclusion: 结果支持将注意力视为面向守恒大时间步激波输运的可学习信息模板，同时指出有限候选可达范围与问题相关的稳健性是当前局限。

Abstract: Can attention move a shock across several cells in one update without breaking it? We develop a conservative, fixed grid finite-volume scheme in which a CFL-conditioned attention flux selects upstream information according to the transport required by the current time step. One-dimensional inviscid Burgers transport is used as the central mechanism test: the same learned flux remains reliable in the conventional small-step regime and, with a time step four times larger, preserves sharp shocks while using one stage per update. A standard fifth-order WENO scheme with third-order strong-stability-preserving Runge-Kutta time integration (WENO-5+SSP-RK3) is included alongside controlled Forward Euler comparisons to separate flux selection from time integration. The learned attention shifts upstream with the local transport reach and becomes more selective near shocks; inference-time interventions and retrained ablations show that transport-scale information and state-dependent selection contribute directly to performance. Directional two-dimensional scalar Burgers transport and the one-dimensional shallow-water system then test whether the conservation-scale-selection principle transfers beyond the original scalar setting. The results support attention as a learnable information stencil for conservative large-step shock transport, while identifying finite candidate reach and problem-dependent robustness as the present limits.

</details>


### [61] [Toward Optimal Switching Regret for Multi-Armed Bandits with Oblivious Adversary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13547)
*Mengxiao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了对抗性多臂老虎机中的切换遗憾问题，证明单一算法可在无知情对手下对任意未知切换次数 $S$ 达到 $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(\sqrt{(S+1)KT})$ 的期望遗憾，解决了 Auer 等人 [2019b] 的开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当切换次数 $S$ 已知时，Auer 等人 [2002] 已给出最优期望遗憾 $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(\sqrt{(S+1)KT})$；但当 $S$ 未知时，Marinov 和 Zimmert [2021] 证明在自适应对手下该保证不可能实现。因此，在无知情对手下能否用单一算法对每个 $S$ 都达到这一最优遗憾，是 Auer 等人 [2019b] 留下的开放问题。

Method: 算法结合了以较小学习率初始化的 fixed-share 学习器，以及若干 dyadic-interval 子程序；这些子程序使用随机学习率和隐式探索来搜索局部改进。关键设计是非均匀先验偏向跟随主学习器，从而保持维护大量子程序的代价很小。当子程序相对主学习器积累足够改进时，主学习器的学习率翻倍，从而适应未知的比较序列切换次数 $S$。

Result: 所提单一算法在无知情对手下，对每一个未知的 $S$ 均能达到 $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(\sqrt{(S+1)KT})$ 的期望遗憾。

Conclusion: 该工作解决了 $S$ 未知情形下对抗性多臂老虎机切换遗憾的开放问题，表明无需预先知道 $S$ 即可实现最优遗憾保证，代价是需假设对手为无知情型。

Abstract: We study switching regret in adversarial multi-armed bandits, where the learner competes with an arm sequence that changes at most $S$ times. When $S$ is known, an optimal expected regret of $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(\sqrt{(S+1)KT})$ is obtainable [Auer et al., 2002]. However, when $S$ is unknown, Marinov and Zimmert [2021] show that this guarantee is impossible under an adaptive adversary. In this paper, we show that a single algorithm achieves $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(\sqrt{(S+1)KT})$ expected regret for every $S$ against an oblivious adversary, resolving an open problem of Auer et al. [2019b]. Our algorithm combines a fixed-share learner initialized with a small learning rate and dyadic-interval subroutines that search for local improvements using randomized learning rates and implicit exploration. Importantly, a non-uniform prior favors following the main learner, keeping the cost of maintaining many subroutines small. When the subroutines accumulate sufficient improvement over the main learner, its learning rate doubles, allowing adaptation to the unknown number of comparator switches $S$.

</details>


### [62] [When Greedy Sampling Explores: KL-Regularized Contextual Bandits without Eluder-Dimension Dependence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13564)
*Zichen Wang,Haoyang Hong,Huazheng Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究奖励与偏好反馈下的KL正则化上下文赌博问题，证明贪心采样可实现不显式依赖eluder维度的对数遗憾，并揭示贪心采样与UCB式探索在正则化强度上的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有上下文赌博算法（如UCB式方法）的遗憾界通常显式依赖eluder维度，维数高时代价大；作者希望探究在KL正则化下，能否用更简单的贪心采样获得与eluder维度无关的更优保证，并扩展到偏好反馈情境。

Method: 针对奖励反馈，提出直接从估计奖励诱导的Gibbs策略中采样的简单贪心算法；将结果扩展到偏好反馈，覆盖一般偏好模型与Bradley-Terry模型，并进一步优化现有依赖维度的保证。

Result: 奖励反馈下建立不显式依赖eluder维度的对数遗憾界；偏好反馈下（一般偏好与Bradley-Terry模型）均获得相应保证，同时加强了已有的依赖维度结果；刻画了贪心采样与UCB式探索的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 当KL正则化足够强时贪心采样保证更强；正则化减弱时，额外探索（UCB式方法）变得更可取。这为KL正则化上下文赌博中算法选择提供了理论依据。

Abstract: We study KL-regularized contextual bandits under both reward and preference feedback. We show that greedy sampling can achieve logarithmic regret without explicit dependence on the eluder dimension. For reward feedback, we establish an eluder-dimension-independent regret bound for a simple greedy algorithm that directly samples from the Gibbs policy induced by the estimated reward. We further extend this result to preference feedback under both the general preference and Bradley--Terry models, while also sharpening existing dimension-dependent guarantees. Our analysis reveals a trade-off between greedy sampling and upper confidence bound-style exploration: greedy sampling enjoys stronger guarantees when KL regularization is sufficiently strong, whereas additional exploration becomes preferable as the regularization weakens.

</details>


### [63] [AttnFuse: A Composable DSL for Compiling Attentions to Fused GPU Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13612)
*Varun Kumar Dasoju,Tian Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出面向注意力机制的DSL工具AttnFuse，将RoPE等乘法前变换作为一等操作，编译生成单个融合GPU核函数，在保持灵活性的同时逼近手工优化的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer的注意力操作占据主要计算与访存开销，研究者不断提出新的注意力变体以提升质量、效率或上下文长度，但每个变体都需要专家手写GPU代码才能达到可用速度。PyTorch的flex_attention允许用Python描述自定义注意力模式并编译为融合核函数，但其设计仅支持中心矩阵乘法之后的修改，无法涵盖所有主流LLM使用的RoPE等前置位置编码变换。

Method: 作者设计了AttnFuse，一种注意力专用的轻量级DSL，通过十个高层构建块组合描述注意力变体，并将RoPE等乘法前变换提升为一等操作。其编译器为整个计算生成单个融合GPU核函数。此外，作者提出"旋转微积分"（Rotation Calculus），从GPU的计算带宽比出发推导是否应融合RoPE的临界条件。

Result: 在RTX 3090上，AttnFuse在RoPE+因果模式上相较flex_attention取得2.10倍加速；在H100上运行完整Llama-3-8B训练步时，性能与PyTorch手工调优后端相差5%以内。所推导的融合/独立应用RoPE的临界点与实测结果一致。

Conclusion: AttnFuse表明，一个小巧的、注意力专用的编译器能够弥合灵活的研究代码与生产级核函数之间的性能差距，使研究者无需手写GPU代码即可获得高效实现。

Abstract: Modern AI systems are built on the Transformer architecture, whose core operation, attention, accounts for the majority of computation and memory cost. Researchers continually propose new attention variants to improve quality, efficiency, or context length, but each variant currently requires expert-written GPU code to run at usable speeds. PyTorch's recent flex\_attention lets researchers describe custom attention patterns in Python and compile them to fused kernels, but its design is limited to modifications applied after the central matrix multiplication, excluding Rotary Position Embedding (RoPE), the positional encoding used by every major LLM.
  We introduce AttnFuse, a small DSL for attention that makes pre-multiplication transformations like RoPE first-class operations. Researchers compose ten high-level building blocks to describe a variant, and AttnFuse's compiler emits a single fused GPU kernel for the entire computation. On an RTX 3090, AttnFuse achieves a 2.10$\times$ speedup over flex\_attention on the RoPE+causal pattern. On an H100, it runs a full Llama-3-8B training step within 5\% of PyTorch's hand-tuned backend. Our investigation reveals the Rotation Calculus: whether to fuse RoPE or apply it separately depends on the GPU's compute-to-bandwidth ratio, with a derived crossover that matches measurement. AttnFuse demonstrates that a small, attention-specific compiler can close the gap between flexible research code and production kernels.

</details>


### [64] [An Efficient and Modular Framework for Targeted Harm Mitigation in LLMS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13624)
*Roberto Campbell,Momin Abbass,Muneeza Azmat,Michal Ulewicz,Raya Horesh,Kristjan Greenewald,Rogério Abreu de Paula,Nathalie Baracaldo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个模块化纠正框架：用 Activated LoRA(aLoRA) 适配器作为专家模块，配合上下文感知路由机制，在生成过程中对未对齐输出做低延迟、定向纠正，在安全基准上提升对齐性同时保持任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 虽具备强零样本能力，但仍易与人类偏好失配，产生偏见、有害等输出。现有对齐方法成本高，且与模型紧耦合，缺乏灵活性与可扩展性。

Method: 模块化纠正框架：为预训练 LLM 挂载 Activated LoRA(aLoRA) 适配器作为专家，每个专家针对特定危害（如偏见、毒性）进行检测与抑制；引入学习得到的路由器，基于模型中间输出动态选择专家。专家可在序列中途激活且不使 KV cache 失效，实现生成过程中的低延迟定向纠正。

Result: 在标准安全基准上提升了模型对齐表现，同时保持原有任务性能。

Conclusion: 该框架为更安全、更可控的 LLM 部署提供了一条轻量高效的路径，兼顾对齐与任务能力。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are powerful zero-shot learners but remain prone to misalignment with human preferences, often producing biased, toxic, or otherwise harmful outputs. Existing alignment methods, while effective, are costly and tightly coupled to the model, limiting flexibility and scalability. We propose a modular correction framework that augments pretrained LLMs with Activated LoRA (aLoRA) adapters and a context-aware routing mechanism to eliminate harms from misaligned model responses. Our approach enables expert adapters to activate mid-sequence without invalidating the KV cache, allowing low-latency, targeted correction during generation. Each expert is trained to detect and mitigate specific harms, such as bias or toxicity. A learned router dynamically selects appropriate experts based on the models intermediate outputs. We demonstrate that our system improves alignment on standard safety benchmarks while preserving task performance, offering a lightweight and efficient path toward safer and more controllable LLM deployments.

</details>


### [65] [FlowTSFM: Turning Encoder Depth into Quantile Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13640)
*Bahaeddine Abdessalem,Shifeng Xie,Zehao Xiao,Youssef Attia El Hili,Ambroise Odonnat,Jianfeng Zhang,Lujia Pan,Keli Zhang,Malik Tiomoko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FlowTSFM 将编码器深度视为循环传输过程：共享参数的单个 Transformer 块被迭代应用，并用分位数流目标监督中间状态从先验分布沿规定轨迹向最终预测演进，以 38.8M 参数取得有竞争力的预测性能与更结构化的预测轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于编码器的时间序列基础模型通常堆叠独立参数化的 Transformer 层，仅最终预测被监督，中间表示缺乏显式预测作用，导致参数冗余且深层表示语义不明。

Method: 提出 FlowTSFM 编码器架构：将深度解释为循环传输过程，迭代应用带共享参数的单一 Transformer 块；采用分位数流目标监督中间状态，使其沿从先验分布到最终预测的规定轨迹演进；目标函数结合 pinball 预测损失与路径级位置匹配。

Result: 仅 38.8M 参数下，FlowTSFM 在 GIFT-Eval 与 TIME 上表现有竞争力，MASE 与更强基线相差 1.8–4.6%，参数约为 12 层 Chronos-2（119.5M）的 1/3；并提出尺度无关诊断指标 CosMean，在匹配中间状态探测协议下得分为 0.919，而 Chronos-2 为 0.350。

Conclusion: 循环参数共享结合路径监督，可在更优的精度—效率权衡下产生显著更具结构性的预测轨迹，表明中间状态具备显式预测角色可提升时间序列基础模型的可解释性与效率。

Abstract: Encoder-based time series foundation models (TSFMs) typically rely on deep stacks of independently parameterized Transformer layers, where only the final forecast is supervised and intermediate representations have no explicit predictive role. We introduce FlowTSFM, an encoder architecture that interprets depth as a recurrent transport process: a single Transformer block is iteratively applied with shared parameters, while a quantile-flow objective supervises intermediate states along a prescribed trajectory from a prior distribution toward the final forecast. The objective combines pinball forecasting loss with path-level position matching. With only 38.8M parameters, FlowTSFM achieves competitive performance on GIFT-Eval and TIME, remaining within 1.8-4.6% MASE of stronger baselines while using approximately $3\times$ fewer parameters than a 12-layer Chronos-2 model (119.5M). Beyond accuracy, we introduce CosMean, a scale-free diagnostic measuring whether recurrent updates consistently align toward the final prediction. Under a matched intermediate-state probing protocol, FlowTSFM achieves a CosMean score of 0.919 compared with 0.350 for Chronos-2, suggesting that recurrent parameter sharing combined with path supervision is associated with substantially more structured predictive trajectories at a favorable accuracy-efficiency trade-off.

</details>


### [66] [When Compliance Data Masquerades as Evaluation: Measurement Validity for Deployed AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13642)
*Hung-Yu Lin,Xingran Huang,Qiming Guo,Jinwen Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出将运营监测或合规数据当作比较性评估证据是AI系统评估中的常见失效，并以自动驾驶脱离/事故报告为案例，提出"评估契约"来显式化比较性评估所需的对齐假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署后AI系统的评估常出现一种反复出现的失效：为运营监测或监管合规收集的数据被当作设计用于比较性评估的数据来解读。自动驾驶是典型场景——美国脱离与事故报告制度产生有价值的运营证据，但报告范围、暴露量、部署域、事件捕获和比较对象构建的差异，限制了仅凭这些测量所能支持的安全结论。作者希望把该问题从"交通领域数据局限"上升为AI评估中的测量效度问题。

Method: 将问题概念化为测量效度问题，论证比较性主张需要对六个要素进行对齐：预期能力、测量结果、暴露机会、部署域、数据生成过程和评估比较对象。以自动驾驶安全评估为案例研究，提出一个"评估契约"，在将运营数据解读为比较性性能证据之前，先显式声明上述假设。

Result: 通过自动驾驶案例展示了报告范围、暴露量、部署域、事件捕获、比较对象构建等差异如何限制从运营数据推导比较性安全主张；评估契约提供了在解读前显式化假设的框架。

Conclusion: 更广泛的含义是：对监测部署后AI系统有用的数据，并不自动构成评估这些系统的有效基准；比较性评估需要测量效度层面的假设对齐。

Abstract: We argue that a recurring failure in the evaluation of deployed AI systems occurs when data collected for operational monitoring or regulatory compliance are interpreted as if they were designed for comparative evaluation. Automated driving provides a concrete example of this problem. U.S. disengagement and crash-reporting regimes produce valuable operational evidence, but differences in reporting scope, exposure, deployment domain, event capture, and comparator construction limit the safety claims that can be supported from these measurements alone. We frame this issue as a measurement-validity problem in AI evaluation rather than as a transportation-specific data limitation. We argue that comparative claims about deployed AI systems require alignment between the intended capability, measured outcome, exposure opportunity, deployment domain, data-generation process, and evaluation comparator. Using automated-driving safety evaluation as a case study, we propose an evaluation contract that makes these assumptions explicit before operational data are interpreted as evidence of comparative performance. The broader implication is that data useful for monitoring deployed AI systems are not automatically valid benchmarks for evaluating them.

</details>


### [67] [Curvature-Independent Regret Bounds for Distributed Online Optimization on Hadamard Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13646)
*Zhanyuan Cai,Emre Sahinoglu,Shahin Shahrampour*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究Hadamard流形上的去中心化在线黎曼优化，针对horospherical凸（h-convex）函数放松了曲率依赖，提出D-ROGD算法并证明曲率无关的遗憾界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有测地凸性（g-convexity）下的分析通常需要曲率信息（如截面曲率下界），曲率还可能进入步长或切空间黎曼共识的收缩因子，限制了适用范围。作者希望在更窄的h-convex函数类上摆脱曲率依赖。

Method: 提出Distributed Riemannian Online Gradient Descent（D-ROGD），将局部黎曼h-次梯度更新与隐式Fréchet均值共识相结合，在Hadamard流形上实现去中心化在线优化。

Result: 对h-convex与强h-convex局部目标，分别建立O(√T)与O(log T)静态遗憾，与欧氏情形关于T的速率一致，网络依赖仅由谱隙决定；实验在双曲嵌入上验证了预测速率且无因曲率导致的性能退化。

Conclusion: 这是首批Hadamard流形上去中心化在线优化的曲率无关遗憾保证，表明对h-convex类可放宽曲率依赖，理论与实验均支持该结果。

Abstract: This work addresses decentralized online Riemannian optimization on Hadamard manifolds. Prior work under geodesic convexity (g-convexity) may require curvature information in the optimization analysis, typically through a finite lower bound on the sectional curvature. Curvature may also enter the step size or contraction factor of tangent-space Riemannian consensus schemes. In this work, we relax the curvature dependence for a narrower class of horospherical convex (h-convex) functions. We study Distributed Riemannian Online Gradient Descent (D-ROGD), which combines local Riemannian h-subgradient updates with an implicit Fréchet-mean consensus. For h-convex and strongly h-convex local objectives, we establish $O(\sqrt{T})$ and $O(\log T)$ static regret, respectively, matching the corresponding Euclidean rates with respect to $T$, with network dependence governed solely by the spectral gap. To our knowledge, these are the first curvature-independent regret guarantees for decentralized online optimization on Hadamard manifolds. Experiments on hyperbolic embeddings corroborate the predicted rates, with no observable degradation due to curvature.

</details>


### [68] [Online Bayesian Node Classification on Inductive Graphs under Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13655)
*Jinwen Xu,Gonzalo Mateos Buckstein,Qin Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出在线高斯变分贝叶斯最后一层（GVBLL）方法，在动态图上同时实现归纳泛化与校准不确定性的节点分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态演化的图要求节点分类器既能对新到节点进行归纳泛化（应对分布漂移），又能为安全敏感应用提供校准的不确定性。标准GNN训练一次即固定，无法满足这两项需求。

Method: 在确定性GNN编码器之上放置随机最后一层参数以构建贝叶斯最后一层（BLL）。由于分类所需softmax类别似然破坏高斯共轭性，训练后验与测试时流式更新均无闭式解。为此提出变分贝叶斯最后一层（VBLL）目标，通过最大化带蒙特卡洛期望对数似然的ELBO联合训练编码器与近似后验；测试时冻结编码器，对最后一层后验执行在线拉普拉斯更新，该更新等价于带指数遗忘和KL锚定训练后验的幂先验贝叶斯模型。

Result: 在五个分布漂移下的节点分类基准上，在线GVBLL是唯一在所有数据集上同时取得最佳准确率与负对数似然的方法。相比最强非GVBLL基线，Cora准确率最高提升17个百分点，ogbn-arxiv提升14个百分点，在校准方面与MC Dropout、深度集成、温度缩放及高斯过程分类器保持竞争力。

Conclusion: GVBLL通过联合变分训练与在线拉普拉斯更新，有效统一了动态图节点分类的归纳泛化与不确定性校准，并持续优于现有基线。

Abstract: On evolving graphs, node classifiers must satisfy two key requirements: inductive generalization to newly arriving nodes under distribution shift and calibrated uncertainty for safety-sensitive applications. Standard graph neural networks (GNNs) are typically trained once and address neither requirement. We adapt the Bayesian last-layer (BLL) model by placing random last-layer parameters on top of a deterministic GNN encoder for uncertainty quantification. The categorical softmax likelihood required for classification breaks Gaussian conjugacy, so neither the training posterior nor the test-time streaming update has a closed-form solution. To address both challenges, we introduce a variational Bayesian last-layer (VBLL) objective that jointly trains the encoder and an approximate last-layer posterior by maximizing an evidence lower bound with a Monte Carlo expected log-likelihood. At test time, we freeze the encoder and apply an online Laplace update to the last-layer posterior. This update corresponds to a power-prior Bayesian model with exponential forgetting and a Kullback-Leibler anchor to the training posterior. Across five node-classification benchmarks under distribution shift, online GVBLL is the only method to achieve the best accuracy and negative log-likelihood on every dataset. It improves accuracy by up to 17 percentage points on Cora and 14 percentage points on ogbn-arxiv over the strongest non-GVBLL baseline, while remaining competitive in calibration with MC Dropout, Deep Ensembles, Temperature Scaling, and Gaussian-process classifiers.

</details>


### [69] [Leakage-Safe and Scheduler-Aware Machine Learning for Grid Job Runtime Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13701)
*Ashfaq Ali Shafin,Khandaker Mamun Ahmed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在 GWA-T-4 AuverGrid 负载上，将 CPU burst time 预测重构为无泄漏的作业级预执行运行时预测：仅用提交时刻属性、按时间与冷启动设置评估。经时间验证调参的 CatBoost 表现最优（R²=0.239，MAE=27,019，RMSE=46,587，LogMAE=2.646）；单服务器仿真显示预测驱动的 SJF 较 FCFS 平均等待时间降低 50.92%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升网格与分布式计算中调度感知的资源管理需要准确的作业运行时预测，但既有模型多在随机交叉验证下评估，存在信息泄漏与评估过于乐观的问题，无法反映真实部署约束。因此需在现实约束下重新审视该预测任务。

Method: 以作业级运行时为目标，仅使用提交时刻属性并排除执行后变量，构造无泄漏的预执行预测任务；在时间与冷启动设置下评估。对比标准回归器、按时间顺序的历史基线、类别编码策略及原生处理类别的 CatBoost；引入时间超参数调优、运行时可预测性分析、特征消融、按作业长度的误差分析与最小化调度仿真。

Result: 时间验证调参后，CatBoost 取得最强部署导向结果：R²=0.239，MAE=27,019，RMSE=46,587，LogMAE=2.646。在全部 69,523 个留出时间测试作业上的单服务器仿真中，预测驱动的 SJF 相比 FCFS 使平均等待时间降低 50.92%。随机划分评估会高估性能；类别原生提升有助于时间泛化；长作业低估仍是调度相关难点。

Conclusion: 该研究强调评估协议对预测结论的实质影响：随机划分评估不可靠，应采用时间与冷启动验证；原生处理类别特征的提升方法更利于时间泛化；长作业低估问题需在调度场景中进一步解决。

Abstract: Accurate job runtime prediction can improve scheduling-aware resource management in grid and distributed computing environments, but prediction models must be evaluated under realistic deployment constraints. This paper revisits CPU burst time prediction on the GWA-T-4 AuverGrid workload trace and reformulates it as leakage-safe pre-execution job runtime prediction. We define the target as job-level runtime, use only submission-time attributes, exclude post-execution variables, and evaluate models under temporal and cold-start settings rather than relying only on random cross-validation. We compare standard regressors, chronological historical baselines, categorical encoding strategies, and CatBoost with native categorical handling. We further add temporal hyperparameter tuning, runtime predictability analysis, feature ablation, error analysis by job length, and a minimal scheduling simulation. After temporal-validation tuning, CatBoost achieves the strongest deployment-oriented result with R^2=0.239, MAE=27,019, RMSE=46,587, and LogMAE=2.646 on the held-out temporal test set. A single-server simulation over all 69,523 held-out temporal test jobs shows that prediction-informed SJF reduces average waiting time by 50.92% relative to FCFS. The results show that random-split evaluation overestimates performance, categorical-native boosting improves temporal generalization, and long-job underestimation remains a scheduler-relevant challenge.

</details>


### [70] [Certifying Model Upgrades with Slice-Wise Non-Regression and Incumbent Fallback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13714)
*Shengwei Zhang,Tao Wu,Fei Qian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种可复现的模型发布/检查点选择协议：将候选搜索与独立的配对评估分离，在非劣性认证失败时严格保留原模型（incumbent），并给出有限样本保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新模型可能提升整体聚合指标，却损害下游用户关心的特定切片（slice）。关键问题在于区分"未检测到损害"与"认证非劣性"：在评估噪声下，前者会以高概率发布有害更新。

Method: 面向相对保留 incumbent 的非回归容忍度进行检查点选择。发布流程将候选搜索与独立的配对评估分离；采用交集-并集（intersection-union）与 Learn-then-Test 原则，针对单个冻结候选、有限候选库以及预先指定的检验顺序给出有限样本保证。

Result: 联合发布决策无需对切片数量施加 Bonferroni 惩罚，但认证功效仍随切片数增加而下降。有界分数模拟中，32 切片设定下"未检测到损害"门控在 99.7% 的试验中发布了有害候选，而精确非劣性门控在 5% 目标下仅为 2.6%。构造的双区块族在匹配候选数下比标量路径获得更大的已认证效用。公开 digits 实验（含后续提升平均聚合准确率的延伸实验）由于认证功效不足，每次运行均返回 incumbent。

Conclusion: 结果确立了可审计的发布协议及其局限，但并未证实在基础模型或多语言翻译升级场景中的收益。

Abstract: An updated model can improve an aggregate metric while degrading a slice that matters to a downstream user. We study checkpoint selection subject to non-regression tolerances relative to a retained incumbent. The central distinction is between failing to detect harm and certifying non-inferiority: the former can release harmful updates with high probability when evaluation is noisy. We give a reproducible release procedure that separates candidate search from independent, paired evaluation and returns the exact incumbent when certification fails. Applying established intersection-union and Learn-then-Test principles, we state finite-sample guarantees for one frozen candidate, a finite candidate library, and a prespecified testing order. A joint release decision does not require a slice-count Bonferroni penalty, although certification power can still decrease with the number of slices. In bounded-score simulations, a no-detected-harm gate releases a harmful candidate in 99.7% of trials in one 32-slice setting, compared with 2.6% for an exact non-inferiority gate at a 5% target. A constructed two-block family yields larger certified utility than a scalar path under matched candidate counts. Public digits experiments, including a subsequent continuation that improves average aggregate accuracy, return the incumbent in every run because certification is underpowered. These results establish an auditable protocol and its limitations; they do not establish benefits on foundation-model or multilingual translation upgrades.

</details>


### [71] [A Variational Optimal Transport Operator on Incompressible Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13729)
*Jinjin He,Shenyifan Lu,Sinan Wang,Zhiqi Li,Duowen Chen,Bo Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 VIOT，一种用于摊销式不可压缩密度输运的生成式神经算子，通过前馈推理直接预测无散速度场与完整输运轨迹，在 2D/3D 基准上实现秒级求解，相比逐对从零优化的基线获得约 $10^4\times$ 的在线加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有不可压缩最优输运依赖伴随流体求解器与可微仿真基线，需对每个新的源-目标密度对单独优化，耗时可达小时级，无法复用、也难以实时交互。作者希望将不可压缩输运转化为可跨配对与跨网格分辨率复用的神经算子。

Method: VIOT 由三部分组成：(1) 流函数或矢量势表示，通过构造本身保证无散性；(2) 正则化不可压缩输运目标，平衡端点精度与流场平滑性；(3) Fourier Neural Operator 主干，在新密度对与网格分辨率之间摊销求解。推理阶段为前馈预测，直接输出无散速度场并生成完整输运轨迹。

Result: 在 2D 与 3D 密度输运基准上验证：2D 与 3D rollout 均可在每对秒级完成；2D 对比中逐实例基线需从零优化每个新配对，耗时约一小时量级，故实现约 $10^4\times$ 的在线加速。此外，生成能力可外推至训练分布之外，可在实时交互系统中为用户手绘的源-目标对生成不可压缩输运。

Conclusion: VIOT 将不可压缩输运变为可复用的生成式神经算子，兼顾无散性保证、轨迹生成与实时交互，并显著降低在线求解成本；其分布外泛化与跨分辨率摊销特性支持多种输运过程的统一处理。

Abstract: We present the Variational Incompressible Optimal Transport (VIOT) operator, a generative neural operator for amortized incompressible density transport. Given a new source-target density pair, VIOT predicts a divergence-free velocity field and generates the full transport trajectory by feed-forward inference, replacing the hour-scale per-pair optimization used by adjoint fluid solvers and differentiable simulation baselines. The system consists of three components: a stream-function or vector-potential representation that enforces incompressibility by construction, a regularized incompressible transport objective that balances endpoint accuracy and flow smoothness, and a Fourier Neural Operator backbone that amortizes the solve across new pairs and grid resolutions. Together, these components make incompressible transport a reusable neural operator that facilitates various transport processes. Further, the generative capability extends beyond the training distribution, with VIOT producing incompressible transports for user-drawn source-target pairs in a real-time interactive system. We demonstrate VIOT on 2D and 3D density-transport benchmarks.
