Both 2D and 3D rollouts complete in seconds per pair, while per-instance baselines in our 2D comparisons optimize each new pair from scratch and require on the order of an hour, a roughly $10^4\times$ online speedup.

</details>


### [72] [JumpStart Your Policy Learning with Lessons from 160,000 Training Runs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13730)
*Nabil Omi,Eric Bae,Chung Yik Edward Yeung,Siddhartha Sen,Ali Farhadi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对离线强化学习与模仿学习开展大规模实证研究，训练超16万个策略、覆盖114个数据集，揭示超参数调优与基准构成会显著影响算法排名，并提出数据集条件化推荐器及JumpStart资源套件以提升研究可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线策略学习领域已有研究指出结果对报告方式、超参数调优和数据集属性敏感，但缺乏足够规模、系统性的联合考察，导致算法比较结论可能不可靠。因此需要在大规模、多条件下统一研究这些变异来源。

Method: 在114个数据集上训练超过160,000个策略，系统开展离线强化学习与模仿学习的实证研究；分析超参数调优、基准构成对算法排名的影响，研究超参数敏感性与跨环境迁移，并据此设计简单策略获取强默认配置。

Result: 在如此规模下没有算法占据绝对优势：最强方法的总体性能接近，但领先者随环境显著变化；适当的超参数调优常会重排算法排名，基准构成也可能导致相互冲突的结论；同时识别出可迁移的强默认配置策略，并开发出数据集条件化的算法推荐器。

Conclusion: 研究强调离线策略学习需要谨慎报告、良好调优的基线及多样化条件评估，并发布JumpStart资源套件（含全部训练策略、模型分数与超参数、强基线、训练与评估代码及可扩展网站）以提升研究可复现性与可靠性，支撑后续研究。

Abstract: Reliable progress in offline policy learning depends on careful reporting, well-tuned baselines, and evaluation across diverse conditions. Prior work has shown that results can be sensitive to reporting choices, hyperparameter tuning, and dataset properties, but these sources of variability have not been systematically investigated together at the scale needed to understand how they shape conclusions. To address this gap, we present a large-scale empirical study of offline reinforcement and imitation learning, training over 160,000 policies across 114 datasets. At this scale, no algorithm dominates: aggregate performance among the strongest methods is often close, but the leaders differ substantially across environments. We find that proper hyperparameter tuning frequently reshuffles perceived algorithm rankings and that benchmark composition can produce conflicting conclusions. We also study hyperparameter sensitivity and transfer across environments, identifying a simple strategy for deriving strong default configurations. We use our findings to develop a dataset-conditioned recommender that provides task-specific algorithm recommendations for practitioners. Finally, we release JumpStart: a resource suite containing every trained policy, per-model scores and hyperparameters, strong baselines across all environments, training and evaluation code, and an extensible website for retrieving, analyzing, and contributing results. Together, these resources aim to make offline policy-learning research more reliable and enable future work beyond the scope of this study.

</details>


### [73] [HarnessBandit: Joint Learnability-Transferability Scheduling for Multi-Harness Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13739)
*Hongliang Wei,Xiaobing Tu,Yinggui Wang,Zhengxi Liu,Rongkun Xue,Jinkui Ren,Xiantao Zhang,Debin Zhao,Xiaopeng Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 HarnessBandit，一个在线调度器，在每个优化器步只选择一个 harness 进行 GRPO 训练，并以"可学习性 + 可迁移性"双信号驱动选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型智能体常通过不同 harness（系统提示、工具模式、控制循环、轨迹格式各异）部署，同一模型在不同接口下表现不均，因此对 harness 变化的鲁棒性很重要。用多 harness 混合训练虽可提升鲁棒性，但引入调度问题：每步应选择既能提供有用学习信号、又能使其更新惠及其他 harness 的那个 harness。

Method: HarnessBandit 每步选择一个 harness。在 GRPO 更新后观测两个信号：可学习性（批上平均绝对优势）与可迁移性（当前 harness 的低维梯度草图与其余 harness 指数移动平均之间的余弦相似度）。两信号经池化滑动窗口 min-max 归一化后融合，再以访问相关 bonus 与显式探索下限进行采样。

Result: 在 ClawGym 上跨六个 harness 训练 Qwen3.5-2B，并在 PinchBench（留出任务、同分布 OpenClaw）与 ClawEval（留出任务与 harness）上评测，HarnessBandit 均优于混合批的多 harness 训练。

Conclusion: 训练诊断表明可学习性与可迁移性提供两个不同且随时间演化的信号，HarnessBandit 是一种有效的多 harness 鲁棒性训练调度方法。

Abstract: Language-model agents are increasingly deployed through diverse harnesses that differ in system prompts, tool schemas, control loops, and trajectory formats. The same model can perform unevenly across these interfaces, making robustness to harness variation an important objective. A natural approach is to train a shared policy through multiple harnesses, but doing so introduces a scheduling problem: each training step should favor a harness that currently provides a useful learning signal while also producing an update that benefits the other harnesses. We develop HarnessBandit, an online scheduler that selects one harness per optimizer step. After a group-relative policy optimization (GRPO) update, it observes learnability -- the mean absolute advantage on the batch -- and transferability -- the cosine between a low-dimensional gradient sketch of the current harness and exponential moving averages of the remaining harnesses. The two signals are fused after pooled sliding-window min-max normalization and sampled with a visit-dependent bonus and an explicit exploration floor. We train Qwen3.5-2B across six harnesses on ClawGym and evaluate on PinchBench (held-out tasks, in-distribution OpenClaw) and ClawEval (held-out tasks and harness). HarnessBandit improves over mixed-batch multi-harness training on both benchmarks, while training diagnostics indicate that learnability and transferability provide distinct, evolving signals.

</details>


### [74] [Training Specialist Models without Reasoning Trajectories for Domain Expert Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13770)
*Yilei Tu,Zihao Li,Shaoxiong Ji,Jörg Tiedemann,Fei Yuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将学生蒸馏作为"探针"，揭示专家模型在缺乏显式推理监督时，其隐式选择的潜在推理轨迹分布，并发现在27组专家-学生配对中，二者的专业化-泛化轮廓高度相关，可通过控制专家分布漂移来系统调控这一权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专家蒸馏依赖教师生成的推理轨迹来传递领域知识，但当专家模型仅用问答对训练、缺乏显式推理监督时，其所生成的轨迹由什么决定尚不明确。作者希望厘清这一潜在轨迹空间的选择机制及其对下游学生的影响。

Method: 作者不将学生蒸馏视为下游目标，而是作为一个"不可知探针"——因为学生不继承专家的参数化或优化约束，仅继承采样得到的轨迹本身。通过这一探针，在化学、物理和多语言等多种场景下，对27组专家-学生配对进行实证分析，并显式控制专家的分布漂移，观察其对教师及蒸馏学生的影响。

Result: 实验发现专家的专业化-泛化轮廓与其蒸馏学生的对应轮廓呈极强相关。显式控制专家的分布漂移能够系统性地同时移动教师及其蒸馏学生，在领域精度与通用能力保持之间形成可控的权衡。该现象在化学、物理、多语言场景以及不同模型家族间均稳定复现。

Conclusion: 研究建立了关于专家训练的新视角：当缺乏黄金推理（gold reasoning）时，微调选择直接决定了传递给下游模型的潜在监督信号。学生蒸馏可作为一种有效的诊断工具来观察专家隐式选择的轨迹分布。

Abstract: Specialist distillation effectively transfers domain expertise to student models via teacher-generated reasoning trajectories. However, when these specialists are trained solely on question--answer pairs without explicit reasoning supervision, what governs the trajectories they generate? In this work, we show that specialist optimization implicitly selects from this latent trajectory space. To isolate and observe this latent distribution, we leverage student distillation not as a downstream goal, but as an agnostic probe---since students inherit no parameterization or optimization constraints from the specialist, inheriting only the sampled trajectories themselves. Through this probe, our empirical analysis unveils a tight governing relationship: across 27 specialist--student pairings, their specialization--generalization profiles correlate exceptionally strongly. Crucially, explicitly controlling the specialist's distributional drift systematically shifts both the teacher and its distilled student along a controllable trade-off between domain precision and general-capability retention. Across chemistry, physics, and multilingual settings, distilled students systematically reflect these specialist-induced profiles, even across divergent model families. Our findings establish a new view of specialist training: when gold reasoning is absent, tuning choices directly control the latent supervision passed to downstream models.

</details>


### [75] [Does Reasoning Improve Psychological Depth in Large Language Models? It Depends on Who's Judging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13773)
*Ruichen Zheng,Yihe Wang,Fabrice Y Harel-Canada,Sara Khosravi,Zeynep Senahan Yildiz,Amit Sahai,Nanyun Peng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究以短篇小说的"心理深度"为测试场景，发现LLM评判者在原始标量数据集上与人评分相关（ρ=0.646），但在分布偏移的配对比较中失效：人类读者偏好无一致推理优势且评分者间一致性接近随机（α=0.070），而LLM评判者在89.0%的维度比较和60对中的59对上系统性偏向推理模型，且其分数与句长、词汇多样性等表层特征相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-as-a-Judge被广泛用于开放式生成内容的评分，但评判者在其开发集上与人评分的高相关性并不保证在输出高度接近、人类偏好主观的分布偏移场景下仍具测量效度。作者希望揭示这一失效模式，并检验"点估计式评判"是否会掩盖主观人类评价中的异质性。

Method: 以短篇小说的心理深度为测量构念。选取七名人类读者和一个在原始Psychological Depth Scale（PDS）标量数据集上筛选出的LLM评判者集成（ρ=0.646），对60对盲测、提示词匹配的故事进行配对评估，涉及GPT-5 vs. GPT-4o与DeepSeek-R1 vs. DeepSeek-V3两组对比。分析人类偏好的方向与强度、评分者间一致性（Krippendorff's α）、读者内一致性及权重模式，并考察LLM评判者得分与句长、词汇多样性等表层特征的关联。

Result: 人类偏好未显示普遍的推理优势：GPT-5相对GPT-4o仅被小幅偏好（60.0–62.9%），而DeepSeek-R1反而落后于V3（42.9%）；评分者间一致性接近随机（Krippendorff's α=0.070），但读者内一致性与反复出现的权重模式表明存在结构化异质性而非随机作答。相比之下，LLM评判者在89.0%的维度级比较中偏向推理模型的输出，并在聚合PDS上于60对中的59对上偏向推理模型，且在全部五种评判者配置下表现一致；其分数与句长、词汇多样性等表层特征相关。

Conclusion: 开发集性能不足以作为分布偏移下部署效度的证据；点估计式LLM评判者会掩盖主观人类评价中的异质性，因此需要更关注分布偏移下的验证与对评分者异质性的建模。

Abstract: LLM-as-a-Judge evaluators are increasingly used to score open-ended generation, yet a judge's correlation with human ratings on its development set may not guarantee valid measurement when outputs are closely matched and human preferences are subjective. We study this failure mode through psychological depth in short stories. Seven human readers and an LLM-judge ensemble selected on the original scalar Psychological Depth Scale dataset ($ρ= 0.646$) evaluated 60 blinded, prompt-matched story pairs from GPT-5 vs.\ GPT-4o and DeepSeek-R1 vs.\ DeepSeek-V3. Human preferences showed no universal reasoning advantage: GPT-5 was modestly preferred over GPT-4o (60.0--62.9\%), whereas DeepSeek-R1 trailed V3 (42.9\%), and inter-reader agreement was near chance (Krippendorff's $α= 0.070$), with within-reader consistency and recurring weighting patterns suggesting structured heterogeneity rather than random responding. The judge, by contrast, favored reasoning outputs in 89.0\% of dimension-level comparisons and 59 of 60 pairs on aggregate PDS, uniformly across all five evaluator configurations, and its scores were associated with surface features such as sentence length and lexical diversity. These results suggest that development-set performance is insufficient evidence for deployment validity on a shifted distribution, and that point-estimate judges can obscure the heterogeneity in subjective human evaluation.

</details>


### [76] [PPDL: A Real-world Industrial User Retention Ratio Forecasting Framework Integrating Physical Priors with Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13789)
*Zibo Zhao,Zhengxiong Guan,Chaoli Zhang,Linyuan Geng,Xuanbing Zhu,Zhonglong Zheng,Fan Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PPDL框架，将物理先验与深度学习结合用于多通道付费获客的留存率预测，在工业级短视频平台数据上显著优于线上现有方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多通道付费获客中，早期准确预测通道级用户留存率对预算分配优化至关重要。留存曲线具有明显时序规律：先高流失后长期趋稳，伴随季节性周期波动且序列自相关强，适合时序预测建模。但在大规模短视频平台上存在三大挑战：通道间异质性强、整体呈现衰减后饱和趋势、回看窗口短。

Method: 提出PPDL框架，融合物理先验与深度学习，包含三部分：(1) 趋势-残差分解组件，趋势项用Weibull分布建模，参数由MLP学习；(2) 残差项在深度学习主干上设计辅助嵌入模块，保持通道身份感知；(3) 设计多尺度趋势惩罚损失函数，增强模型对趋势的敏感性。

Result: 在工业级数据集上开展全面实验，覆盖三个应用、每个平均30+通道。结果显示PPDL在不同主干网络上均取得提升，并显著优于现有线上方案。

Conclusion: PPDL通过趋势-残差分解、Weibull趋势建模、通道身份辅助嵌入与多尺度趋势惩罚损失，有效应对通道异质性、衰减饱和趋势和短回看窗口等挑战，验证了物理先验与深度学习结合在多通道留存率预测中的有效性，具备工业落地价值。

Abstract: In multi-channel paid user acquisition, early and accurate prediction of user retention at the channel level is crucial for optimizing budget allocation. User retention curves display a pronounced temporal pattern: an initial period of high churn transitions into long-term stability. This pattern is further characterized by regular fluctuations attributable to seasonality and exhibits high serial autocorrelation. These intrinsic properties make such curves highly suitable for analysis within a time-series forecasting framework. However, forecasting user retention ratio for large-scale short-video platform faces three major challenges: significant heterogeneity across channels, pronounced global trend of decay followed by saturation, and short look-back windows. To address these challenges, we propose PPDL, a novel forecasting framework that integrates physical priors with deep learning. We first introduce a trend-residual decomposition component. The trend is modeled using the Weibull distribution, whose parameters are learned via a Multilayer Perceptron (MLP). Secondly, for the residual component, we design an auxiliary embedding module on top of a deep learning backbone to maintain the channel identity awareness. Finally, to enhance the model's sensitivity to trends, we design a Multiscale Trend-penalized loss function. The proposed approach PPDL is validated through comprehensive experiments on industrial-scale datasets, covering three applications with an average of 30+ channels each. Experimental results show that PPDL achieves improvements across different backbones and significantly outperforms existing online solutions.

</details>


### [77] [Benchmarking Optimizers to Solve Inverse Problems with Differentiable Physics Simulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13819)
*Xiang Chen,Huanhuan Xia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对可微物理仿真求解逆问题中的优化性能问题，构建了覆盖多物理领域的12个可微仿真器及三类逆问题，系统基准测试了不同优化器的表现并给出选型与设计启示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可微物理仿真结合了严格数值物理仿真的物理正确性与自动微分、梯度优化带来的高效性，有望推动科学发现与工程设计。但该范式当前在优化环节存在性能问题，且缺乏对优化器性能的系统性评估。

Method: 构建12个可微物理仿真器，涵盖离散力学、连续力学、原子模拟、渲染和半经验物理模型；基于这些仿真器设计对应逆问题，分为参数辨识、逆设计和最优控制三类；开展大量实验，对比一阶方法、近似二阶方法及全局优化器在这些逆问题上的性能。

Result: 获得不同优化器在各类可微物理逆问题上的性能对比结果，并提炼出关于如何为可微编程选择与设计优化器的洞见。

Conclusion: 该基准可启发更有效优化器的发展，并进一步推动可微编程在科学与工程各领域的应用。

Abstract: Solving inverse problems with differentiable physics simulators holds the potential to revolutionize scientific discovery and engineering design, as it enjoys both the strict physical correctness from rigorous numerical physics simulators, and the high efficiency and effectiveness from automatic differentiation and gradient-based optimization. However, currently, this paradigm faces performance issues in optimization. In this work, we target benchmarking the performance of different optimizers to solve various inverse problems. We construct 12 differentiable physics simulators spanning physics domains including discrete mechanics, continuous mechanics, atomistic simulations, rendering, and semi-empirical physics models. Based on these simulators, we design corresponding inverse problems that can be categorized into parameter identification, inverse design, and optimal control. Finally, we conduct extensive experiments to compare the performance of different optimizers, including regular first-order methods, approximate second-order methods, as well as global optimizers, on these inverse problems, and analyze the results to provide insights on how to choose and design optimizers for differentiable programming. We hope such benchmarks can inspire the development of more effective optimizers, and further promote the applications of differentiable programming in various scientific and engineering domains.

</details>


### [78] [Graph Neural Networks for Influence Maximization in Social Networks: An Unsupervised Minimum Dominating Set Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13836)
*Erfan Ahmadi,Mina Shirazi,Behnam Bahrak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无监督图神经网络框架求解最小支配集（MDS）问题，无需训练时使用真值解，在12,000个合成图上训练，推理速度较元启发式快55倍、较监督学习快14倍，且在社会网络基准上取得最优或近优支配集，并具良好泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MDS是经典NP-hard组合优化问题，在社交网络分析（病毒营销、影响力最大化、公共卫生干预、信息传播）中应用广泛；寻找能覆盖整个网络的最小影响节点集在规模化求解上仍具计算挑战。GNN已被用于硬组合问题，但现有方法多依赖监督学习或真值标签，成本高且泛化受限，因此需要一个高效、无需真值解的可扩展求解框架。

Method: 提出一种无监督GNN框架求解MDS问题，训练阶段不需要ground-truth解；在12,000个具有多样结构性质的合成图上进行训练。

Result: 推理速度较元启发式基线最高快55倍，较监督学习方法最高快14倍；在真实社交网络基准上找到最优或近最优支配集；学习到的启发式能有效泛化到未见过的图分布。

Conclusion: 该无监督GNN方法在保持解质量（最优或近优）的同时显著提升推理效率并具备跨分布泛化能力，对大规模社交网络分析具有较强实用价值。

Abstract: The Minimum Dominating Set (MDS) problem is a classic NP-hard combinatorial optimization problem with critical applications in social network analysis, including viral marketing, influence maximization, public health interventions, and information dissemination. Identifying a minimal set of influential individuals whose reach covers an entire social network is central to these applications, yet remains computationally challenging at scale. Graph neural networks (GNNs) have emerged as powerful tools for learning over graphs, and recent work explores their application to hard combinatorial problems. This paper presents a novel unsupervised GNN framework for the MDS problem that eliminates the need for ground-truth solutions during training. Trained on 12,000 synthetic graphs with diverse structural properties, our method achieves up to 55x faster inference than metaheuristic baselines and up to 14x faster inference than supervised learning approaches, while finding optimal or near-optimal dominating sets on real-world social network benchmarks. Our learned heuristic generalizes effectively to unseen graph distributions, demonstrating strong practical applicability for large-scale social network analysis.

</details>


### [79] [Accuracy Is Not Service: A Decision-Aware Benchmark for Intermittent-Demand Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13840)
*Joo Ern Chin,Shih-Fen Cheng,Aldy Gunawan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在契约物流备件场景中，逐行预测精度与服务订单满足率之间存在错配；38种预测方法的基准测试显示二者排名负相关，且服务表现更取决于累计偏差方向而非点精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 契约物流备件按订单级服务计费，只有所有请求行都被满足订单才算有效，但预测方法却按行级精度选择，二者目标不一致。在需求间歇且块状、历史短、提前期长达数月的场景下，这种错配尤为关键，因此需要决策感知的评估方式。

Method: 构建覆盖经典、间歇性需求、机器学习、深度学习及预训练基础模型的38种预测方法基准；采用统一的决策感知协议，在来自真实合同的工业面板及两个公开数据集上评估，涉及20,330个真实多品项订单；并分析Chronos-2实例归一化中的偏差，提出免训练校正；同时发布RUF方法生成保真认证的合成面板以复现结果。

Result: 在工业面板上，预测精度排名与订单服务排名呈负相关，相关系数为-0.555。服务更紧密关联于累计预测偏差方向，包括零需求期过度预测，而非点精度。对Chronos-2实例归一化偏差的免训练校正将每物料满足代理从77.5%提升至92.0%（提升14.5个百分点，90%政策目标下），并将完整订单满足率从54%提升至63%。合成面板上结果可复现。

Conclusion: 对于间歇性需求，最低误差的预测不必然带来最高服务。偏差方向有助于解释这一差距，并且该差距无需重新训练即可缩小。

Abstract: A contract-logistics spare-parts operator is paid on order-level service: an order counts only if every requested line is fulfilled, yet forecasters are selected based on line-level forecast accuracy. This disconnect matters when demand is intermittent and lumpy, histories are short, and lead times span months. We benchmarked 38 forecasting methods spanning classical, intermittent-demand, machine-learning, deep-learning, and pretrained foundation models. A common decision-aware protocol evaluates them on an industrial panel drawn from a live contract and two public datasets. Forecast-accuracy rank and order-service rank are negatively correlated on the industrial panel, at -0.555, across methods evaluated on 20,330 real multi-item orders. Service is more closely associated with the direction of cumulative forecast bias, including over-prediction during zero-demand periods, than with point accuracy. Examining bias in Chronos-2's instance normalization yields a training-free correction that lifts the per-material fill proxy from 77.5% to 92.0% (14.5 percentage points) at the 90% policy target and raises the complete-order fill rate from 54% to 63%. For reproducibility, we release RUF (Regenerate-Until-Fidelity), a method for generating fidelity-certified synthetic panels on which the findings reproduce. For intermittent demand, the lowest-error forecast need not deliver the highest service. Bias direction helps explain this gap, which can be reduced without retraining.

</details>


### [80] [Pre-training with Graph Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13844)
*Jiaming Wang,Thomas Laurent,Xavier Bresson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在生化领域的图Transformer预训练中，使用计算属性作为标签的有监督预训练对下游任务增益最大，同时需约束模型容量以缓解过拟合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究图Transformer在生化领域应采用的预训练策略，明确何种预训练方式与模型容量设置能最大化下游任务性能。

Method: 针对生化领域的图Transformer，系统比较多种预训练策略，重点考察以计算属性作为标签的有监督预训练，并分析模型容量对过拟合的影响。

Result: 以计算属性为标签的有监督预训练在下游任务上取得最高性能增益；约束模型容量可有效缓解图Transformer的过拟合。

Conclusion: 生化领域图Transformer宜采用基于计算属性的有监督预训练，并适当约束模型容量以提升泛化与下游表现。

Abstract: This article investigates pre-training strategies for graph transformers in the biochemistry domain. By conducting comprehensive experiments, the study reveals that supervised pre-training using computed properties as labels provides the highest performance gain on downstream tasks. The results also highlight the importance of constraining model capacity to mitigate overfitting in graph transformers.

</details>


### [81] [Affinity-Aware Sharding for Delayed Tensor Parallelism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13846)
*Eloi de Reynal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 延迟张量并行（DTP）通过让设备先本地累加并广播部分输出、延后 $δ$ 步再收集其他设备的部分输出，消除了张量并行推理中的阻塞式 all-reduce。该文指出 DTP 破坏了 FFN 神经元与注意力 KV 头的置换对称性，使分片本身成为一种建模决策；通过在分片前对稠密模型做置换以最大化同设备上 KV 头与 FFN 神经元的亲和度，可加速蒸馏或再训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DTP 相对标准张量并行是真实的架构变更，稠密 Transformer 模型在适配后需要重新训练或蒸馏。作者希望降低这一适配成本，并揭示 DTP 下分片策略与模型性能之间的耦合关系。

Method: 将 DTP 视为打破 FFN 神经元内部与注意力 KV 头内部置换对称性的架构变化，从而把分片本身变成建模决策。用一阶近似度量"丢失某个头的贡献对每个神经元输出的损害"作为亲和度；用坐标上升优化器交替执行神经元的精确平衡分配与 KV 头划分的穷举搜索，最大化同设备共置亲和度。整个流程在单张 GPU 上对 Qwen3-0.6B 与 Danube3-500M 耗时不到两分钟。

Result: 在 $δ=1$ 时，亲和度优化的布局在全部 10k 步测试范围内，达到任意蒸馏目标所需步数约为朴素连续布局的一半到三分之二；每个优化种子都优于每个连续种子，并且优于十六个随机布局中的十五个。此外，初始化时的共置亲和度分数可预测训练后相对基座模型的 KL 散度，在从反优化到优化的十七个布局上 Pearson 相关系数为 $-0.81$ 与 $-0.89$。

Conclusion: DTP 中的分片布局是一个可优化的建模自由度；通过置换最大化设备内 KV 头与 FFN 神经元的亲和度，能显著加速蒸馏/再训练，且初始亲和度可作为训练后 KL 的有效预测指标。

Abstract: Delayed Tensor Parallelism (DTP) removes the blocking all-reduce of tensor-parallel Transformer inference. Every device adds its own partial output to its residual stream (and broadcasts it) immediately, but only gathers (receives) the other devices' partials $δ$ modules later. A TP to DTP change therefore amounts to a real architecture change, and dense Transformer models need to be retrained or distilled after adaptation. We show that DTP breaks the permutation symmetry of neurons inside FFNs and of KV heads inside attention modules, and that this symmetry breakage makes the sharding itself a modelling decision. We show that maximising the affinity between the KV heads and the FFN neurons co-located on a device, by permuting the dense model before sharding, speeds up the distillation or retraining process. The affinity is measured with a first-order approximation of the damage that losing a head's contribution does to each neuron's output, and the co-located affinity is maximised with a coordinate-ascent optimiser that alternates an exact balanced assignment of neurons with an exhaustive search over the KV head partitions. The whole procedure takes under two minutes on one GPU for Qwen3-0.6B and Danube3-500M. On these models, at $δ=1$, the affinity-optimised layouts reach any distillation target in about half to two thirds of the steps needed by the naive contiguous layouts, over the whole 10k-step range we tested, and every optimised seed beats every contiguous seed and all but one of the sixteen random layouts. We also show that the co-located affinity score at initialisation predicts the KL to the base model after training, across seventeen layouts ranging from anti-optimised to optimised (Pearson $-0.81$ and $-0.89$).

</details>


### [82] [ReH-FUSE: Reliability-Aware Hierarchical Fusion of Experts for Multimodal Emotion Recognition in Conversation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13857)
*Guan-Hua Wen,Hou-Chiang Tseng,Kuan-Yu Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出可靠性感知框架ReH-FUSE，用决策级路由器在文本、音频与跨模态专家间自适应融合，在IEMOCAP和MELD上取得稳定提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 会话情感识别中不同证据源的可靠性依赖具体样本：有时词汇内容是决定性的，有时语音提供互补线索，有时则需跨模态交互，固定融合方式无法显式应对这种变化。

Method: ReH-FUSE包含对话感知的文本、音频和跨模态专家；决策级路由器先建模文本与音频之间的相对偏好，再在所得单模态混合与跨模态专家之间进行平衡，从而将单模态竞争与跨模态选择解耦。

Result: 在IEMOCAP上三次独立运行取得74.34%加权F1与73.11%宏F1；在MELD上取得68.03%加权F1。受控消融显示，学习到的路由优于专家均匀平均，并从跨模态交互中获益。

Conclusion: 显式建模实例相关的证据可靠性，并通过分解式决策级路由实现单模态竞争与跨模态选择的分离，可有效提升会话情感识别性能。

Abstract: Multimodal emotion recognition in conversation (ERC) requires adapting to the instance-dependent reliability of different evidence sources. Lexical content may be decisive, vocal expression may provide complementary cues, or accurate recognition may require cross-modal interaction; fixed fusion does not explicitly account for this variation. We propose ReH-FUSE, a reliability-aware framework with dialogue-aware text, audio, and cross-modal experts. Its decision-level router first models the relative preference between text and audio and then balances the resulting unimodal mixture against the cross-modal expert. This factorization separates unimodal competition from cross-modal selection. Across three independent runs on IEMOCAP, ReH-FUSE achieves 74.34% weighted F1 and 73.11% macro F1; on MELD, it achieves 68.03% weighted F1. Controlled ablations show that learned routing outperforms uniform expert averaging and benefits from cross-modal interaction.

</details>


### [83] [An Uncertainty-Aware Hybrid Mathematical-Machine-Learning Model for Smart Irrigation Decision Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13864)
*Andrea Scariolo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究将四参数水量平衡模型与随机森林残差校正耦合，并用保形预测给出不确定性区间，再将区间下界转化为灌溉触发规则；在三年逐小时数据上，混合模型24小时RMSE为0.00925 m³/m³、技巧+9.4%，且区间规则在长预见期仍可信。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球约70%的淡水取用于农业，但灌溉调度多为被动响应：既不预测土壤水分走向，也不给出预报置信度。数据驱动模型准确但不可解释且仅给点值；水量平衡模型透明但结构误差大。二者单独均难以支撑不确定性下的可辩护灌溉决策。作者希望把物理模型与机器学习耦合，并将不确定性贯穿到决策端。

Method: 构建混合框架：以仅用训练数据标定的四参数水量平衡模型为核心；用随机森林只学习其物理残差进行校正；用保形预测（conformal prediction）附加90%名义区间；再用风险感知规则把区间下界转为灌溉触发阈值。在雨养型地中海农田站点三年逐小时原位观测上，按严格时间顺序划分，与持续性预报及九种基线对比，评分从1小时到1周。

Result: 24小时预见期下，混合模型RMSE为0.00925 m³/m³，技巧+9.4%，约为九种基线中最佳者的两倍，且九种中仅随机森林优于持续性。技巧不随预见期增长：3小时峰值+27.4%，1周降至+1.2%。保形分位数回归比恒定宽度保形预测校准更好且区间窄11%。风险感知规则将管理阈值穿越的提前检出率从0.905提升至1.000，代价是精度由0.975降至0.950、名义用水多3.7%；超过72小时后点预报劣于无预报规则，而基于区间的规则仍保持可靠。

Conclusion: 不确定性量化决定了预报驱动灌溉建议可被信任的预见期上限；在本研究中，物理层的透明性未带来可测量的精度损失，说明可解释物理核心与残差学习及区间决策可有效结合。

Abstract: Agriculture accounts for roughly 70% of global freshwater withdrawals, yet irrigation is still commonly scheduled reactively, with no forecast of where soil moisture is heading and no statement of confidence in that forecast. Data-driven models are accurate but opaque and point-valued; water-balance models are transparent but carry large structural error. Neither alone supports a defensible irrigation decision under uncertainty. This study coupled the two and carried uncertainty through to the decision: a four-parameter water-balance core, calibrated on training data only, was corrected by a Random Forest that learned nothing but the physical residual, conformal prediction attached 90%-nominal intervals, and a risk-aware rule converted the interval lower bound into an irrigation trigger. It was evaluated on three years of hourly in-situ measurements from a rainfed Mediterranean cropland station under a strict chronological split, scored against persistence, from one hour to one week. At the 24 h horizon the hybrid reached RMSE 0.00925 m^3 m^-3 and +9.4% skill, roughly double the best of nine baselines, of which only the Random Forest beat persistence. Skill did not grow with lead time: it peaked at +27.4% at three hours and fell to +1.2% at one week. Conformalised quantile regression was better calibrated and 11% sharper than constant-width conformal prediction. The risk-aware rule raised management-threshold crossings detected in advance from 0.905 to 1.000, at a precision cost of 0.975 to 0.950 and 3.7% more notional water, and beyond 72 h the point forecast fell below the no-forecast rule while the interval-based rule did not. Uncertainty quantification therefore governs the lead time over which forecast-driven irrigation advice remains trustworthy, and here transparency in the physical layer cost no measurable accuracy.

</details>


### [84] [The Filter Metric is Safety-Critical: Phantom Advantages in Group-Relative RL under Shaped Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13866)
*Juntao Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文识别并量化了复合塑形奖励下GRPO类算法“过滤指标—谓词”语义错配问题：当过滤依据塑形分数而非任务结果时，全失败组仍具非零组内方差而通过过滤，经标准差归一化放大为“幻影优势”，导致训练崩溃；改用二值结果过滤可稳定收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO及其衍生方法可通过动态采样丢弃“无对比”的rollout组，但实际实现暴露了一个可配置的过滤指标。作者发现：在复合塑形奖励（shaped reward）场景下，过滤所依据的指标与过滤谓词本应表达的语义之间存在错配，之前工作只确立了塑形放大与全失败过滤现象，但未隔离这一语义错配并直接测量原生删除/补充（deletion/refill）行为。

Method: 在GSM8K上进行受控对比（Qwen2.5-1.5B + LoRA，每臂四次运行：三次默认种子重跑与一次seed-123）。比较三种过滤策略：不过滤、按塑形分数过滤、按二值任务结果过滤。随后在verl原生recipe/dapo训练器上，固定模型、数据、奖励与训练器，仅更换过滤指标（score臂 vs accuracy臂），使用相同的自定义塑形奖励hook与未修改的训练器/过滤代码，并直接采集原生删除/补批遥测；另在λ∈{0.1,0.3,0.5}、1.5B/7B、MATH、GSPO等条件下做探索性验证，并测试关闭标准差归一化的效果。

Result: GSM8K上，不过滤与按塑形分数过滤最终EM为0.080±0.112与0.040±0.008，而按二值结果过滤稳定在0.754±0.005。verl dapo训练器上，score臂在40步中无需任何补批、终值为EM 0.160；accuracy臂在29/40步触发补批、终值为EM 0.763。所有测试的正塑形系数下不安全臂均崩溃；探索性单次实验在1.5B/7B、MATH与GSPO下复现失败；关闭标准差归一化可避免观察到的崩溃。

Conclusion: 复合奖励下的过滤应使用语义独立于塑形项的任务结果信号。按塑形分数过滤会因指标—谓词语义错配，使全失败组的残余方差被标准差归一化放大为幻影优势，引发策略崩溃；该问题在多模型、多任务、多算法上可复现，且可通过更换过滤指标或关闭标准差归一化缓解。

Abstract: Group-relative policy optimization (GRPO and descendants) can discard no-contrast rollout groups through dynamic sampling, while practical implementations expose a configurable filter metric. We identify and quantify a metric-predicate mismatch under composite shaped rewards. When filtering follows the shaped training score rather than the task outcome, all-fail groups retain nonzero within-group spread and pass the predicate; standard-deviation normalization then promotes shaping differences among failures to full-size phantom advantages. In a controlled GSM8K comparison (Qwen2.5-1.5B, LoRA), no filtering and shaped-score filtering end at EM 0.080 +/- 0.112 and 0.040 +/- 0.008, whereas binary-outcome filtering holds 0.754 +/- 0.005 across four runs per arm (three default-seed reruns and one seed-123 run; mean +/- sample SD). On verl's native recipe/dapo trainer, holding model, data, reward and trainer fixed and changing only the metric, the score arm requires no batch refill in any of 40 observed steps and ends at EM 0.160; the accuracy arm refills in 29/40 steps and ends at 0.763. Both use the same custom shaped-reward hook and unmodified trainer/filter code. Prior work established shaping-induced amplification and all-fail filtering; our contribution isolates the metric-predicate semantic mismatch and directly instruments native deletion/refill telemetry. Across tested positive coefficients lambda in {0.1, 0.3, 0.5}, unsafe arms collapse; exploratory one-run cells reproduce the failure at 1.5B/7B on MATH and under GSPO, while disabling standard-deviation normalization avoids the observed collapse. Filtering under a composite reward should use a task-outcome signal whose semantics are independent of shaping.

</details>


### [85] [A Multi-Resolution Multi-Domain Pre-Training Framework for Universal Traffic Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13878)
*Zhouyang Liu,Jindong Han,Hao Wang,Xinyue Liu,Hui Gao,Dongsheng Li,Hao Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FlexST：面向异构时空交通数据的模块化自适应预训练框架，在多分辨率扩散、领域自适应混合专家、统一周期编码三方面创新，在23个真实数据集上零/少样本显著优于SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时空交通数据是智能交通系统的核心，但高度异构；现有预训练模型采用"同质化建模范式"处理高度异构数据，存在根本性错配，导致泛化受限、计算昂贵、参数效率低。

Method: 提出FlexST预训练框架，强调模块化与自适应：1）多分辨率时空扩散模块，同时捕获短期波动与长程趋势，调和时空分辨率差异；2）领域自适应混合专家，动态路由数据至专用子网络，实现选择性知识迁移并避免跨域负干扰；3）统一周期编码策略，注入分辨率与领域感知的归纳偏置，协调数据集间的周期不一致。

Result: 在23个真实交通数据集上的大量实验表明，FlexST在零样本与少样本设置下显著优于现有SOTA基线，展现更优的泛化性、自适应性与效率。

Conclusion: 该工作为构建通用预训练模型、处理城市交通系统的复杂性与变异性提供了新方向。

Abstract: Spatio-temporal traffic data are central to intelligent transportation systems, yet their heterogeneity poses significant challenges for large-scale modeling. Existing pre-trained models often rely on a homogeneous modeling paradigm to handle highly heterogeneous traffic data. This fundamental mismatch not only limits model generalization but also leads to computationally expensive and parameter-inefficient designs. To this end, we propose FlexST, a novel pre-training framework that introduces modularity and adaptivity for traffic modeling. Specifically, we first propose a multi-resolution spatio-temporal diffusion module that captures both short-term fluctuations and long-range trends, effectively reconciling inputs with divergent temporal and spatial resolutions. After that, we construct a domain-adaptive mixture-of-experts that dynamically routes data to specialized sub-networks, enabling selective knowledge transfer while preventing negative interference across diverse domains. Moreover, we devise a unified periodic encoding strategy that injects resolution- and domain-aware inductive biases to harmonize periodic inconsistencies across datasets. Extensive experiments on 23 real-world traffic datasets demonstrate that FlexST significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in zero- and few-shot settings, showcasing superior generalization, adaptability and efficiency. This work offers a new direction for building general-purpose pre-trained models capable of handling the complexity and variability of urban traffic systems.

</details>


### [86] [Lie to me: Detecting Managerial Evasiveness in Earnings Calls via Conversational Audio Encoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13893)
*Huizhong Chen,Huan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种双分支晚融合框架，结合LLM文本评分与对话语音编码，检测财报电话会议中管理层的回避行为，以预测SEC迟交等不利事件，AUROC约0.89。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 财报电话会议是管理层在分析师监督下披露信息的主要渠道。已有研究将语音和词汇线索与未来不利结果关联，但通常在全通话层面聚合特征，未能充分利用问答环节的交互结构。

Method: 双分支晚融合框架：(i) LLM-as-a-judge，通过结构化二元评分表将问答文本映射为可解释的通话级向量X_text；(ii) 冻结的对话编码器，其时间隐状态由DeepVoice式序列读取器处理生成音频表示h。对(X_text, h)进行晚融合得到通话级风险分数p。采用企业分组的5折交叉验证。

Result: 在n=1,039通电话（212次迟交）上，融合模型AUROC约0.89，而文本评判器为0.55，仅通话时长为0.71。

Conclusion: 对话语音动态能够编码超越词汇内容和通话时长的管理层回避行为，为SEC不利结果提供更强的早期预警信号。

Abstract: Earnings conference calls are a primary channel through which managers disclose information under analyst scrutiny. Prior work has linked vocal and lexical cues to future adverse outcomes, but often pools features over an entire call and underuses the interactive structure of Q&A. We propose a two-branch late-fusion framework for detecting managerial evasiveness as a predictor of extrinsic SEC events (primarily late filings): (i) an LLM-as-a-judge that maps Q&A text to an interpretable call-level vector X_text via a structured binary rubric, and (ii) a frozen conversational encoder whose temporal hidden states are read by a DeepVoice-style sequential reader to produce an audio representation h. Late fusion of (X_text, h) yields a call-level risk score p. On n=1,039 calls (212 late filings) with firm-grouped 5-fold CV, fusion reaches AUROC approx. 0.89, versus 0.55 for the text judge and 0.71 for duration alone. These results show that conversational audio dynamics encode managerial evasiveness beyond lexical content and call length, yielding a stronger early-warning signal of adverse SEC outcomes.

</details>


### [87] [Machine Learning under Imperfect Data: Challenges and Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13914)
*Masoumeh Zareapoor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该综述性论文将"训练数据存在缺失、不平衡、弱监督、分布不匹配"等常见数据缺陷统一归因为四种共享机制：信息丢失、经验风险偏差、监督模糊和表征不稳定，并据此组织现有代表性方法、指出其局限并给出未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习模型通常假设训练与测试数据完整、平衡、有标注且同分布，但实际中这些条件常被违反：测量缺失或损坏、稀有类别表征不足、监督信号弱、部署环境与训练环境不一致。以往研究多将这些缺陷视为彼此独立的技术问题，本文则试图揭示它们通过少数共同机制影响学习过程，从而提供一个统一的分析视角。

Method: 本文是一篇简短综述，采用"机制导向"的组织方式，而非按任务或数据类型分类。它将上述数据缺陷统一归结为四种共享机制（信息丢失、经验风险偏差、监督模糊、表征不稳定），并围绕这些机制回顾代表性方法，涵盖以下主题：重建与生成、重平衡与表征校准、有限监督下的学习、跨领域与跨模态的适配，以及分布变化下的可靠性。

Result: 论文综合梳理了各方向代表性方法，并指出其内在局限：合理重建（plausible reconstruction）并不能保证下游决策正确；针对特定基准的修正（benchmark-specific correction）难以泛化；缺乏可信反馈时的适配（adaptation without trustworthy feedback）存在根本风险。

Conclusion: 论文提出未来研究方向：证据感知学习（evidence-aware learning）、保留不确定性的预测（uncertainty-preserving prediction），以及能够区分"视觉合理性"与"决策效用"的评测体系。

Abstract: Machine-learning models are commonly developed under an assumption that training and test data are sufficiently complete, balanced, labelled, and drawn from compatible distributions. In practice, one or more of these conditions is often violated. Measurements may be missing or corrupted, rare classes may be poorly represented, supervision may be weak, and the deployment environment may differ from the training environment. These imperfections are usually treated as separate technical problems, although they alter learning through a small number of shared mechanisms: loss of information, biased empirical risk, ambiguous supervision, and unstable representations. This short survey organises representative methods around these mechanisms. It reviews reconstruction and generation, rebalancing and representation calibration, learning with limited supervision, adaptation across domains and modalities, and reliability under distribution change. The discussion highlights the limits of plausible reconstruction, benchmark-specific correction, and adaptation without trustworthy feedback. It concludes with directions for evidence-aware learning, uncertainty-preserving prediction, and evaluation that separates visual plausibility from decision utility.

</details>


### [88] [Learning Through Energy Refinement and Manifold Projection: A Cooperative EBM-AE Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13917)
*Ryad Zemouri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出协同式的能量模型-自编码器（EBM-AE）框架，将EBM与去噪自编码器联合训练，并通过EBM→AE→EBM迭代采样，把流形投影与能量精修交替结合，用于生成、图像修复与OOD检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EBM理论上具有吸引力，能在真实样本处赋予低能量、在不可能观测处赋予高能量，但训练面临两大难题：Langevin采样的计算开销大，以及难以高效探索学到的数据流形。因此需要一种机制既能降低采样成本，又能让采样轨迹沿数据流形高效推进。

Method: 提出EBM-AE协同框架：将EBM与去噪自编码器联合训练；设计迭代式EBM→AE→EBM采样流程，其中Langevin动力学与自编码器投影交替执行。自编码器充当流形投影算子，对采样轨迹进行正则化；EBM则执行能量精修，将样本推向学习分布的低能量区域。

Result: 在MNIST上的大量实验表明，EBM与AE联合训练相比传统自编码器显著提升生成质量。在结构化掩码与随机掩码的图像修复任务中，流形投影贡献了主要的重建能力，而最终的EBM能量精修在重建问题越困难时收益越明显。

Conclusion: 将流形投影与能量最小化相结合，为生成、重建和OOD检测提供了一个有效且可解释的框架，并揭示了能量建模与表示学习之间的互补作用。

Abstract: Energy-Based Models (EBMs) provide a flexible framework for generative modeling by learning an energy landscape that assigns low energy values to realistic samples and higher energies to unlikely observations. Despite their theoretical appeal, training EBMs remains challenging due to the computational cost of Langevin sampling and the difficulty of efficiently exploring the learned data manifold. In this work, we propose a cooperative Energy-Based Model and Autoencoder (EBM-AE) framework that combines energy-based refinement with manifold projection. The proposed approach jointly trains an EBM with a denoising autoencoder and introduces an iterative EBM$\rightarrow$AE$\rightarrow$EBM sampling procedure in which Langevin dynamics and autoencoder projection alternately refine generated samples. Within this framework, the autoencoder acts as a manifold projection operator that regularizes sampling trajectories, while the EBM performs energy-based refinement toward low-energy regions of the learned distribution. Extensive experiments conducted on the MNIST dataset demonstrate that joint EBM-AE training substantially improves generation quality compared with a conventional autoencoder.
  Beyond unconditional generation, we evaluate the proposed framework on image inpainting tasks involving structured and random masks. The results show that manifold projection provides the majority of the reconstruction capability, whereas the final energy-based refinement becomes increasingly beneficial as the reconstruction problem becomes more challenging.
  Taken together, the results indicate that combining manifold projection and energy minimization provides an effective and interpretable framework for generation, reconstruction, and out-of-distribution detection, while offering new insights into the complementary roles of energy-based modeling and representation learning.

</details>


### [89] [Machine Learning in Fish Farming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13919)
*Fearghal O'Donncha,Nikos Papandroulakis,Jennie Korus,Abigail Langbridge,Alexander Timms,Konstantinos Topouzelis,Abdul Baseer Khan,Shree Rama Kamal Kumar Vegu,Mahtab Sarvmaili,Ryan Mowat,Rhanna Turberville,Tyler Sclodnick,Christopher Whidden*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本章综述机器学习在水产养殖中的应用，重点讨论其如何改进决策与运营效率，涵盖主流与新兴ML技术、典型应用、物联网协同以及未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水产养殖面临诸多挑战，亟需借助人工智能与机器学习提升决策质量与运营效率，推动产业向高效、可持续方向发展。

Method: 采用综述与案例分析相结合的方式：先阐述水产养殖挑战及AI作用，再概述ML技术，随后依次介绍应用、新兴趋势与未来方向；重点分析随机森林、卷积神经网络（CNN）、循环神经网络（RNN），以及图神经网络（GNN）和大语言模型（LLM）等新兴技术。

Result: ML技术已应用于生物量估计、物种识别、行为分析和环境预测等任务，并与物联网（IoT）结合实现实时监测与决策支持。

Conclusion: ML驱动的创新有望革新鱼类养殖，使水产养殖业更加高效、可持续且高产。

Abstract: This chapter explores how machine learning (ML) is transforming aquaculture, with a particular focus on enhancing decision-making processes and improving operational efficiency. The chapter is structured to first introduce the challenges in aquaculture and the role of AI and then provide an overview of ML techniques in the context of aquaculture, followed by applications, emerging trends, future directions, and case studies. The focus is on real-world applications of ML techniques, including Random Forest, Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs), and Recurrent Neural Networks (RNNs), as well as emerging technologies such as Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) and large language models (LLMs). Key applications include biomass estimation, species recognition, behavioural analysis, and environmental forecasting. The chapter also highlights the synergy between ML and the Internet of Things (IoT) for real-time monitoring and decision support. Ultimately, ML-driven innovations have the potential to revolutionise fish farming, leading to more efficient, sustainable, and productive practices in the aquaculture industry.

</details>


### [90] [Finite-Time Node Separation in Recurrent Graph Neural Networks with Persistent Gaussian Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13920)
*Mostafa Haghir Chehreghani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对带持续高斯噪声的循环图神经网络进行有限时间分析，证明任意两节点表示期望平方距离的精确二阶矩分解，得到每步普适下界 \(2\sigma^2 d\)，并给出非中心卡方刻画、近碰撞界、几乎必然非碰撞与置换等变性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作表明持续高斯扰动可保证循环GNN存在正平稳Dirichlet能量，从而避免渐近过平滑；但全局能量下界不能保证有限深度下各节点表示仍然彼此可区分。因此需要补充有限时间层面的节点级分离性分析。

Method: 设表示维度为 \(d\)、噪声标准差为 \(\sigma\)。作者对期望平方距离做精确二阶矩分解，无需收缩性假设或平稳性假设；进一步利用条件pairwise距离的非中心卡方刻画，其中非中心参数为确定性消息传递分离量除以 \(2\sigma^2\)；由此推导固定时间与有限时域下依赖动力学的近碰撞界。

Result: 证明任意正时间步的期望平方距离下界均为 \(2\sigma^2 d\)；给出条件非中心卡方刻画与动力学感知的有限时域近碰撞界；将先前中心高斯界恢复为最坏零分离情形；证明几乎必然成对非碰撞、一致有限时域保证，以及随机动力学的分布置换等变性和图输出的置换不变性。

Conclusion: 有限时间节点级分离性可由持续噪声严格保证，结果与已有渐近能量分析互补，为循环GNN在有限深度下的表示区分性提供理论支撑。

Abstract: Persistent Gaussian perturbations have been shown to prevent asymptotic oversmoothing in recurrent Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) by ensuring a positive stationary Dirichlet energy. However, this global energy bound does not guarantee that individual node representations remain distinct at finite depths. In this paper, we provide a complementary finite-time analysis of the same persistent-noise architecture. Let \(d\) denote the representation dimension and \(σ\) the noise standard deviation. We first prove an exact second-moment decomposition for the expected squared distance between any two node representations, yielding the universal lower bound \(2σ^2 d\) at every positive time step without contraction or stationarity assumptions. More precisely, conditional pairwise distances have a noncentral chi-square characterization: the noncentrality parameter is the deterministic message-passing separation normalized by \(2σ^2\). This yields dynamics-aware fixed-time and finite-horizon near-collision bounds that retain information discarded by the central worst-case analysis. The earlier central Gaussian bound is recovered as the worst-case zero-separation case. We additionally prove almost-sure pairwise noncollision, derive a uniform finite-horizon guarantee, and establish permutation equivariance in distribution for the stochastic dynamics and permutation-invariant graph outputs. Our results complement the asymptotic energy analysis of prior work and provide rigorous finite-time guarantees on node-level representation separation.

</details>


### [91] [Minibatch persistency, eight years later: what batch reuse costs in steps and joules, and what it saves in data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13922)
*Matteo Fischetti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 一项预注册实验在49M参数Transformer上系统检验小批次复用（minibatch persistency）：复用买到的既不是速度也不是能耗，而是数据，且只在大批次下成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小批次复用（自2019年并入data echoing以来）长期背负两个问题：其一，复用被质疑只是变相增大学习率；其二，它从未与同等精细调参的基线对比过。本文补上这项缺失的检验。

Method: 预注册研究：在FineWeb-Edu上训练49M参数Transformer，批大小B∈{32,128,512}，每格8个随机种子；对每个批大小、每个实验臂分别调学习率；对照臂为"无复用对照"，仅改变采样方式，其他不变。以到达固定损失的成本为指标，在四个维度上度量：优化器步数、新鲜token数、秒数、插座端焦耳数。随后在新种子与更新一代GPU上复现，并把三个实验臂统一到以步数为单位的同一调度上。

Result: 复用的收益体现在数据而非速度或能耗，且仅在大批大小下存在：B=32时其新鲜token读取量反而高于基线。在步数、秒数与焦耳数上，复用至多持平（at best free）。在表现最佳的B=512处，预注册对照在n=8种子下无法将复用效应与学习率调度位置的影响区分开。

Conclusion: 小批次复用适用于新鲜数据（而非算力）构成瓶颈成本的场景：语料会耗尽、流水线按样本付费、数据流无法回绕。若数据可以重复读取，间隔epoch（spaced epochs）表现相当甚至更好。

Abstract: Minibatch persistency reuses data instead of reading it: rather than drawing a fresh minibatch at every optimizer step, it takes K consecutive steps on the same one. Absorbed into data echoing in 2019, it has carried one objection -- that reuse merely imitates a larger learning rate -- and no baseline tuned as carefully as the method itself. This paper runs the missing test. A pre-registered study trains a 49M-parameter Transformer on FineWeb-Edu at minibatch size B in {32, 128, 512}, 8 seeds per cell, tuning the learning rate separately for every batch size and every arm, against a reuse-free control that changes the sampling and nothing else. Each headline claim is a cost to reach a fixed loss, read on four axes: optimizer steps, fresh tokens, seconds, and joules at the socket. We then replicate on new seeds and a newer GPU generation, and put the three arms on one schedule in steps. The outcome of our study is that what minibatch reuse buys is neither speed nor energy but data, and only at large minibatch size: at B = 32 it reads more fresh tokens than the baseline, not fewer. On steps, seconds and joules it is at best free; and at B = 512, where it looks best, a registered control cannot separate the effect of reuse from the position on the learning-rate schedule at n = 8 seeds. The technique is therefore worth using where fresh data rather than compute is the binding cost: a corpus that runs out, a pipeline that pays per sample, a stream that cannot be rewound. Where the data can simply be read again, spaced epochs do as well or better.

</details>


### [92] [Exploring napping paradigm for Recurrent Spiking Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13927)
*Andreas Massey,Stefano Nichele,Aliaksandr Hubin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出生物学启发的"微睡眠"范式napping，将比例权重缩放与持续随机膜噪声结合，在无监督循环脉冲神经网络中验证其可匹配权重归一化的稳定性，同时降低模型复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物体通过两种离线机制平衡内部世界模型的准确性与简洁性：睡眠通过突触渐进式降尺度降低复杂度，随机噪声则削弱精度、放松感觉输入对突触重组的约束。而工程化的脉冲神经网络（SNN）普遍采用瞬时的、无噪声的权重归一化，回避了这一平衡问题。

Method: 在Gabor预处理MNIST上，训练基于迹的STDP的无监督循环SNN。将napping（比例权重缩放+持续随机膜活动）在三种正则化强度下扫描其持续时间与膜噪声水平，并将最优配置与权重归一化进行对比。

Result: 三种正则化强度下，调优后的napping均能匹配权重归一化的准确率：准确率在短时长、低噪声时达到峰值，并随两者增大而单调下降。聚类结果出现分化——最强的几何分离出现在更长时长和更高噪声条件下，体现自由能两项的分离：准确率奖励数据拟合，结构奖励渐进、带噪降尺度所诱导的更简表示。

Conclusion: napping以额外的仿真开销换取表征结构增益，而权重归一化不具备该开销。因此，当优先目标是表征结构而非原始分类效率时，napping最具吸引力。

Abstract: Biological organisms minimize free energy by balancing two competing demands on their internal world model: it must be accurate enough to predict sensory input, yet simple enough to generalize beyond it. Two mechanisms regulate this balance offline: sleep reduces complexity through gradual synaptic downscaling, while stochastic noise attenuates precision, relaxing the constraint sensory input imposes on synaptic reorganization. Engineered Spiking Neural Networks (SNNs) leave this balance unaddressed, favoring instantaneous, noiseless weight normalization instead. This paper investigates the hypothesis that a biologically inspired micro-sleep paradigm, napping -- combining proportional weight scaling with continuous stochastic membrane activity -- can replicate the stability of normalization while shedding model complexity. We evaluate this in an unsupervised recurrent SNN trained via trace-based spike-timing-dependent plasticity (STDP) on Gabor-preprocessed MNIST. We tune napping across three regularization regimes by sweeping its duration and membrane noise level, then compare the best configuration against weight normalization. Across all three regimes, well-tuned napping matches the accuracy of normalization: accuracy peaks at brief durations and low noise, then declines monotonically as either grows. Clustering diverges, with the strongest geometric separation arising at longer durations and higher noise -- the two terms of free energy pulling apart, accuracy rewarding data fit and structure rewarding the simpler representation that gradual, noisy downscaling induces. This gain carries a simulation cost normalization avoids, so napping is most compelling where representational structure, rather than raw classification efficiency, is the priority.

</details>


### [93] [Optimal Transport for Efficient, Unsupervised Anomaly Detection on Industrial Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13940)
*Abigail Langbridge,Fearghal O'Donncha,James T Rayfield,Bradley Eck*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于最优传输（OT）的时间序列异常检测框架，无需标注数据、可实时自适应，并在航运、工业HVAC与公开基准三类数据集上优于传统方法，同时提供反事实解释以增强可审计性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业4.0对异常检测需求强烈，但现有方法存在标注依赖、泛化性差、可扩展性不足等问题；工业环境还普遍存在短期波动、噪声与数据缺失等挑战。

Method: 基于最优传输（OT）构建异常检测框架，学习正常与观测工况之间的映射；通过滑动参考窗口自适应数据动态性，几乎无需人工输入，可实时运行且无需标注训练数据；并给出反事实解释以支持审计。

Result: 在航运、工业HVAC系统及公开基准共三个工业数据集上评估，方法在识别异常和降低误报方面表现优异，超过传统方法，同时保持计算效率与配置简便性，并对短期波动、噪声和数据缺口具备鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该OT框架有效缓解了数据标注、泛化性与可扩展性等限制，适用于工业时间序列异常检测，并因反事实解释而具备更好的可审计性。

Abstract: Effective anomaly detection frameworks are a central pillar of the Industry 4.0 paradigm. In this paper, we introduce an Optimal Transport (OT)-based framework for anomaly detection, designed to detect deviations from normal behaviour in time-series sensor data. The OT-based method requires minimal user input and adapts to real-time data without the need for labelled training data. Our method effectively addresses existing limitations related to data labelling, generalisability, and scalability, demonstrating resilience against short-term fluctuations, noise, and data gaps - common challenges in industrial environments. Additionally, our method provides counterfactual explanations improving the auditability of the approach when deployed in industrial settings. The proposed method learns the mapping between normal and observed operating conditions through a sliding reference window that adapts to the dynamicity of the data. We evaluate our approach on three industrial datasets, from shipping, industrial HVAC systems, and publicly available benchmark data. The method was highly effective in identifying anomalies and reducing false positives, outperforming traditional methods, while maintaining computational efficiency and ease of configuration.

</details>


### [94] [Hardware-Aware Learned Representation Compression for Distributed In-Sensor Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13947)
*Chengwei Zhou,Abu Masum,Xuming Chen,Mehran Moghadam,Sreetama Sarkar,Arnab Sanyal,Md Abdullah-Al Kaiser,M. Hassan Najafi,Sercan Aygun,Gourav Datta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出OASIS，一种分布式传感器内视觉框架。它利用轻量级编码器在片外传输前生成紧凑、任务相关的表征，并支持4比特量化加霍夫曼编码与基于Sobol的超维计算两条部署路径。实验表明，该系统在视觉唤醒词分类、手部跟踪和眼动跟踪任务中减少总系统能耗约2倍至4.5倍，并保持有竞争力的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传感器内计算通过在传感器附近执行早期处理，降低高分辨率图像数据的传输成本。然而，与CMOS图像传感器集成的逻辑芯片在计算和内存方面受到严格限制，制约了传统深度神经网络的分割部署。因此，需要一种能够在资源受限条件下高效压缩并保留任务相关信息的视觉框架。

Method: OASIS采用轻量级编码器生成紧凑、任务相关的表征，编码器以任务、熵和重建目标进行端到端训练，解码器仅用于训练阶段。系统支持两条部署路径：第一条结合4比特量化与霍夫曼编码，并保留分类和稠密预测任务所需的空间结构；第二条使用基于Sobol的超维计算，将编码器潜在表征转换为固定维度二值超向量，用于关联记忆分类。数字近传感器流水线在AMD Xilinx Zynq UltraScale+ FPGA上实现，并结合板级功耗测量、Vivado实现后分析、电路仿真的CIS模型以及7纳米ASIC投影进行表征。

Result: 在基于SwinViT的VWW模型中，将3×3×8潜在表征映射为64维超向量，相对于128维配置额外实现1.77倍通信缩减，精度损失小于1个百分点，相�比原始8比特图像传输整体实现18,816倍缩减。在视觉唤醒词分类、手部跟踪和眼动跟踪任务中，OASIS将总系统能耗降低约2倍至4.5倍，同时保持有竞争力的精度。

Conclusion: OASIS展示了通信高效的传感器内视觉的实用硬件-算法协同设计路径，能够在严格资源约束下有效降低传输与能耗成本，同时维持任务精度。

Abstract: In-sensor computing reduces the cost of transmitting high-resolution image data by performing early-stage processing near the sensor. However, the logic chip integrated with a CMOS image sensor (CIS) is tightly constrained in compute and memory, limiting conventional deep neural network partitioning. We present OASIS, a distributed in-sensor vision framework that uses a lightweight encoder to generate compact, task-relevant representations before off-chip transmission. The encoder is trained end-to-end using task, entropy, and reconstruction objectives, while the decoder is used only during training. OASIS supports two complementary deployment paths. The first applies 4-bit quantization and Huffman coding while preserving the spatial structure required by classification and dense-prediction tasks. The second uses Sobol-based hyperdimensional computing (HDC) to transform the encoder latent into a fixed-dimensional binary hypervector for associative-memory classification. For the SwinViT-based VWW model, mapping a $3\times3\times8$ latent to a 64-dimensional hypervector provides an additional $1.77\times$ communication reduction with less than one percentage point of accuracy loss relative to the 128-dimensional configuration, yielding an overall $18{,}816\times$ reduction compared with raw 8-bit image transmission. We implement the digital near-sensor pipeline on an AMD Xilinx Zynq UltraScale+ FPGA and characterize it using direct board-level power measurements and Vivado post-implementation analysis, together with circuit-simulated CIS models and a 7-nm ASIC projection. Across visual wake-word classification, hand tracking, and eye tracking, OASIS reduces total system energy by approximately $2\times$-$4.5\times$ while maintaining competitive accuracy, demonstrating a practical hardware-algorithm co-design path for communication-efficient in-sensor vision.

</details>


### [95] [Linear Ensemble Sampling with Smaller Ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13954)
*Taehyun Hwang,Min-hwan Oh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种集合采样算法，通过在正则化Gram矩阵显著变化时才刷新集合，将充分集合规模从Θ(d log T)降低到Θ(d log d + d log log T)，同时保持任意有界臂集下Õ(d^{3/2}√T)遗憾，并在有限臂集下达到Õ(d√(T log K))的更强界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集合采样是一种实用的随机探索方法，但现有保证需要集合规模为Θ(d log T)，相对内在的Ω(d)下界存在关于时域T的对数差距，亟需缩小这一差距。

Method: 提出一种仅在正则化Gram矩阵发生显著变化时才刷新集合的集合采样算法。该机制将扰动分析局部化到Gram矩阵漂移受控的时段，从而通过控制漂移降低所需的充分集合规模。

Result: 充分集合规模降至Θ(d log d + d log log T)；在任意有界臂集下保持最优Õ(d^{3/2}√T)遗憾；当臂集有限且基数为K时达到更紧的Õ(d√(T log K))遗憾。实验表明算法在使用显著更小集合的情况下仍与基线具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 这是首个同时恢复随机线性bandit算法两种经典遗憾标度的集合采样保证，并支持无需重置历史数据的anytime实现。

Abstract: Ensemble sampling offers a practical approach to randomized exploration by maintaining a collection of models, but how small an ensemble can be while retaining strong regret guarantees remains unresolved. In particular, the existing guarantees use an ensemble size of $Θ(d\log T)$, leaving a logarithmic gap in the horizon $T$ relative to the intrinsic $Ω(d)$ ensemble-size barrier. We aim to narrow this gap by proposing an ensemble sampling algorithm that refreshes the ensemble only when the regularized Gram matrix changes substantially. This mechanism localizes the perturbation analysis to epochs with controlled Gram-matrix drift and reduces the sufficient ensemble size to $Θ(d\log d+d\log\log T)$, while preserving the state-of-the-art $\tilde O(d^{3/2}\sqrt T)$ regret for ensemble sampling with arbitrary bounded arm sets. We further show that, when the arm set is finite of cardinality $K$, the proposed algorithm achieves the sharper regret bound $\tilde O(d\sqrt{T\log K})$. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first ensemble-sampling guarantee that simultaneously recovers both canonical regret scalings known for randomized linear bandit algorithms: the $\tilde O(d^{3/2}\sqrt{T})$ rate for arbitrary bounded arm sets and the $\tilde O (d\sqrt{T\log K})$ rate for finite arm sets. The algorithm also admits an anytime implementation without resetting past data, and experiments show that it remains competitive with baselines while using substantially smaller ensembles.

</details>


### [96] [Tabby: An Open Pretraining Recipe for Time Series Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13956)
*Shifeng Xie,Bahaeddine Abdessalem,Zehao Xiao,Youssef Attia El Hili,Ambroise Odonnat,Zhiwei Dong,Lei Zan,Themis Palpanas,Jianfeng Zhang,Lujia Pan,Keli Zhang,Malik Tiomoko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文发布了一个名为Tabby的长上下文概率时间序列基础模型，并公开完整的构建配方，涵盖数据、训练流程与模型权重。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列领域缺乏一个同时具备长上下文能力、多任务通用性以及完全开源可复现构建流程的基础模型，作者希望填补这一空白。

Method: 采用仅编码器的Patch Transformer架构；预训练语料结合真实数据（GIFT-Eval-Pretrain+、BLAST）与合成数据（KernelSynth、基于随机结构因果模型在线生成时序动态的CauKerV2）；训练上联合使用渐进式收敛调度（产生可复用的中间检查点）与对中间层进行深度分位数监督的目标；骨干参数145M，支持最长8,192个观测点的上下文；另设prompt-tuning模块在冻结预训练权重下提升同分布预测性能。

Result: 在GIFT-Eval与分布外TIME基准上取得有竞争力的零样本预测性能；同一预训练骨干还支持UCR Archive分类任务与TSB-AD-U零样本异常检测。

Conclusion: Tabby验证了统一骨干可跨预测、分类、异常检测任务通用的可行性，训练流程与模型已在huawei-noah/trustworthyAI开源。

Abstract: In this report, we release Tabby, a long context probabilistic time series foundation model, together with a complete and open recipe of how it was built. Tabby adopts an encoder-only patch Transformer architecture and concentrates the contributions on the data and the training procedure. The pretraining corpus combines an extended real-world collection, GIFT-Eval-Pretrain+ and BLAST, with synthetic data from KernelSynth and CauKerV2, an online generator that composes temporal dynamics through randomly sampled structural causal models. Training couples a progressive convergence schedule, which yields reusable intermediate checkpoints, with a deep quantile supervision objective for intermediate layers. The resulting 145M parameter backbone supports contexts of up to 8,192 observations and serves forecasting, classification, and anomaly detection, while a prompt-tuning module further improves in-distribution forecasting performance with the pretrained weights frozen. Tabby achieves competitive zero-shot forecasting performance on GIFT-Eval and the out-of-distribution TIME benchmark, while the same pretrained backbone also supports classification on the UCR Archive and zero-shot anomaly detection on TSB-AD-U. We release training pipeline and model as open source at huawei-noah/trustworthyAI.

</details>


### [97] [Data-Efficient Agentic Graph Domain Adaptation via Reliability-Aware Prototype Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14045)
*Yingxu Wang,Kunyu Zhang,Siyang Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DEAG，一种可靠性感知原型学习框架，用于数据高效场景下的图域自适应（GDA）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多数GDA方法假设源图标签充足，但在数据高效的智能体场景下仅能保留有限的源证据，导致源语义不可靠，引发源锚定不稳定、目标关联不确定和目标边缘校准脆弱的问题。

Method: DEAG从保留的源支持度与嵌入紧致度估计类别可靠性；将经验原型与分类器方向融合以构建稳定可复用的源锚点；在锚点引导下进行原型感知的软目标关联，并将置信度加权的目标中心与源语义对齐；另引入源先验正则项以锐化目标预测，同时保持目标边缘与保留源证据一致。

Result: 在具有多种域偏移的图基准上，相同源数据预算下，DEAG的平均自适应性能优于有竞争力的GDA基线。

Conclusion: DEAG通过可靠性感知原型学习有效缓解有限源证据下的不稳定问题，提升了数据高效GDA的适应性。

Abstract: Agentic learning systems are often required to adapt after deployment by observing new data and reusing prior knowledge under limited supervision or feedback. For graph-structured prediction, Graph Domain Adaptation (GDA) naturally instantiates this setting by transferring knowledge from labeled source graphs to unlabeled target graphs under distribution shifts. However, most GDA methods assume sufficient labeled source graphs, which becomes restrictive in data-efficient agentic settings where only limited source evidence can be retained. Under such constraints, source semantics become unreliable, leading to unstable source anchoring, uncertain target association, and fragile targetmarginal calibration. To address these challenges, we propose DEAG, a reliability-aware prototype learning framework for data-efficient agentic GDA. DEAG estimates class reliability from retained source support and embedding compactness, and constructs stable reusable source anchors by blending empirical prototypes with classifier directions. Guided by these anchors, DEAG performs prototype-aware soft target association and aligns confidence-weighted target centers with source semantics. A source-prior regularizer further sharpens target predictions while keeping the target marginal consistent with retained source evidence. Experiments on graph benchmarks with diverse domain shifts show that DEAG improves average adaptation performance over competitive GDA baselines under the same source-data budget.

</details>


### [98] [Stabilizing Performative Feedback Loops with Minimal Model Deployments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14065)
*Gabriele Farina,Juan Carlos Perdomo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究算法预测反馈回路中的"表演性稳定"（performative stability）问题，提出一种在高精度区间内以近乎最少的模型部署次数找到随机化表演性稳定预测器的算法，并给出在损失良态且表演性效应较弱时将随机解去随机化为单一确定性预测器的结构性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 算法预测会主动塑造其面对的数据分布，形成"算法—环境"反馈回路。若不同预测模型诱发不同分布，如何高效学得对自身所诱发分布最优的预测规则（即表演性稳定解）成为核心难题。与分布固定的传统监督学习不同，学习者必须实际部署不同预测器并观测其诱发的分布，导致部署成本高昂，因此亟需减少所需部署次数的方法。

Method: 作者构建了表演性稳定与期望变分不等式（expected variational inequalities）之间此前未被充分探索的技术联系，并据此设计新的算法流程：在高精度区间内，不依赖于"预测如何塑造分布"的任何假设，即可找到随机化的表演性稳定预测器。进一步通过结构性分析，在损失满足良态条件且表演性效应较弱的假设下，将随机稳定解去随机化为满足先前确定性稳定概念的单一预测器。

Result: 算法在高精度区间内以近乎最少的模型部署次数找到随机化表演性稳定预测器，所需部署次数相较既有方法呈指数级减少。同时证明了在损失良态、表演性效应较弱的条件下，该随机稳定概念可去随机化为单一确定性稳定预测器。

Conclusion: 本文为表演性稳定学习提供了高效算法与结构性刻画：一方面大幅降低了达成随机化表演性稳定所需的模型部署成本，另一方面揭示了随机稳定与确定性稳定之间的转化条件，并通过与期望变分不等式的联系为后续研究提供了新的技术视角。

Abstract: When algorithmic predictions inform people's decisions, the models we deploy are performative and actively shape the data we see. This feedback loop between algorithms and their broader environments introduces a challenge in the mechanics of social prediction: If different predictive models induce different distributions, is it possible to efficiently learn a prediction rule that is optimal for the distribution that it induces? Formally, this solution concept is known as performative stability. A core challenge in learning a performatively stable predictor is that, unlike supervised learning where distributions are fixed, the learner must deploy different predictors and observe their induced distributions.
  The main contribution of our work is a new algorithmic procedure that, in the high-accuracy regime, finds a performatively stable model in nearly the minimum number of model deployments without making any assumptions regarding how predictions shape distributions. In particular, our procedure succeeds at finding a randomized performatively stable predictor using exponentially fewer model deployments than prior approaches. Our second main contribution is a structural result showing how this recent randomized notion of stability achieved by our algorithm can be derandomized into a single predictor satisfying the prior deterministic notion if one is willing to assume that the loss is well-conditioned and that performative effects are weak, as in early work in this area. On a technical level, our results come from building on an underexplored technical connection between performative stability and expected variational inequalities.

</details>


### [99] [Multi-Modal Tumor Survival Prediction via Graph-Guided Mixture of Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14072)
*H Mathavan,H Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种图引导的混合专家（MoE）框架，将多个已有模型作为工具自动调用，用于多模态肿瘤生存期预测，在 TCGA-LUAD 数据集上优于单模态和朴素集成模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在少样本场景表现优异，但在涉及多模态、多推理步骤的复杂现实任务（如基于临床数据、病理切片和基因组预测癌症患者生存期）中仍面临挑战。现有方法常因无法同时考虑所有模态，或未处理模态缺失、模态差异及多模态数据融合问题，导致性能不佳。

Method: 受 LLM（尤其是 MoE 模型）进展启发，提出图引导的 MoE 框架：用图结构管理预测过程，结合多个模型提升预测能力；不训练单一端到端基础模型，而是采用 MoE 引导的集成，将各模型作为工具自动调用。

Result: 在 TCGA-LUAD 数据集上的实验与分析表明，该方法性能优于各单模态模型和朴素集成模型。

Conclusion: 利用已有模型的优势并通过 MoE 框架进行引导，可在复杂现实任务中获得更优性能和更准确预测，为多模态肿瘤生存期预测提供新思路。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have displayed impressive capabilities in handling tasks that require few demonstration examples, making them effective few-shot learners. Despite their potential, LLMs face challenges when it comes to addressing complex real-world tasks that involve multiple modalities or reasoning steps. For example, predicting cancer patients' survival period based on clinical data, cell slides, and genomics poses significant logistical complexities. Although several approaches have been proposed to tackle these challenges, they often fall short in achieving promising performance due to their inability to consider all modalities simultaneously or account for missing modalities, variations in modalities, and the integration of multi-modal data, ultimately compromising their effectiveness. This thesis proposes a novel approach for multi-modal tumor survival prediction to address these limitations. Taking inspiration from recent advancements in LLMs, particularly Mixture of Experts (MoE)-based models, a graph-guided MoE framework is introduced. This framework utilizes a graph structure to manage the predictions effectively and combines multiple models to enhance predictive power. Rather than training a single foundation model for end-to-end survival prediction, the approach leverages a MOE-guided ensemble to manage model callings as tools automatically. By leveraging the strengths of existing models and guiding them through a MOE framework, the aim is to achieve better performance and more accurate predictions in complex real-world tasks. Experiments and analysis on the TCGA-LUAD dataset show improved performance over the individual modal and vanilla ensemble models.

</details>


### [100] [To do($x$) or not to do($x$): Medical Image Counterfactuals for Dataset Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14124)
*Yasin Ibrahim,Robin J. Evans,Konstantinos Kamnitsas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文区分了医学图像增强中"反事实"生成的两种含义（因果干预 vs 非因果编辑），并系统比较了Deterministic、Undirected、Causal三种条件生成策略对下游性能与公平性的影响，发现因果方法可带来实际收益，并为数据生成方案设计提供指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学图像分析常受数据集偏置影响，导致模型有偏、临床适用性受限。利用合成图像增强训练数据是缓解偏置的可行策略，其中反事实生成被广泛使用，但该术语存在两种不同理解：一是基于结构因果模型的因果干预，二是非因果的图像编辑或常规条件生成。这一概念混淆的实践后果尚不明确，因此需要厘清并评估其对医学图像增强的实际影响。

Method: 作者对比三种条件生成策略：(1) Deterministic，改变选定变量同时固定其余变量；(2) Undirected，依据学到的统计关联更新变量而不指定因果方向；(3) Causal，沿有向因果图传播干预。分析不同选择对生成图像的影响，并考察因果基础方法何时改善增强效果、何时收益有限，同时评估下游性能与公平性（即对敏感子群间数据集偏置的敏感性降低）。

Result: 实验表明，采用因果方法生成合成训练数据能够带来可观的收益，同时研究也揭示了因果方法在某些情形下收益有限的边界条件，明确了三种条件策略在图像表现与下游结果上的差异。

Conclusion: 区分并正确选择条件生成策略对医学图像增强至关重要；因果驱动的合成数据生成在实践中具有切实优势，这些发现为机器学习从业者有效设计数据生成协议提供了有价值指导。

Abstract: Medical image analysis is often hindered by biased datasets, which can lead to biased models and limited clinical applicability. A promising strategy for mitigating such biases is to augment training data with synthetic images. Counterfactual (CF) generation is one such strategy, though the term is used in two different senses: in some works, CFs are produced through causality-based interventions derived from structural causal models, whereas in others, they are produced by non-causal image edits or conventional conditional generative models, such as altering anatomy or adding pathologies. In this work, we study this distinction and evaluate its practical consequences for medical image augmentation. We compare three conditioning strategies: $\textit{Deterministic}$, which changes selected variables while holding the remaining variables fixed; $\textit{Undirected}$, which updates variables according to learned statistical associations without assigning causal directions; and $\textit{Causal}$, which propagates interventions along a directed causal graph. We analyse how these choices affect the resulting images, and explore when causally grounded methods improve dataset augmentation or bring limited benefit. In particular, we assess downstream performance and fairness, where fairness refers to reduced sensitivity to dataset biases across sensitive subgroups. Our experiments demonstrate that using a causal approach to synthetic training data generation can lead to tangible benefits, with these insights offering valuable guidance to machine learning practitioners for the effective design of data generation protocols.

</details>


### [101] [LIMBO: Lifelong Inference-Time Memory and Budget Optimization for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14138)
*Siddharth Sharma,Nilesh Prasad Pandey,Onat Gungor,Tajana Rosing*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将终身学习智能体中的经验回放视为“推理时记忆分配”问题，提出在线框架 LIMBO，联合优化记忆策略与推理预算，在保持性能的同时大幅降低推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体在复杂工作流中需持续获取新能力并保持旧任务能力，现有方法通过经验回放将历史交互注入提示词。但回放并非免费：每条重放轨迹都要与检索、推理、工具调用、验证争夺同一提示与算力预算。现有方法使用固定回放策略，无论回放对当前任务是否有益，导致资源分配低效。作者将这一问题定义为推理时记忆分配，作为终身智能体的独立问题类别。

Method: 提出 LIMBO，首个将记忆视为可控推理时资源、并针对每个到达任务联合优化记忆策略与推理预算的在线框架。与固定回放策略或依赖模型权重、教师监督、离线重训练的方法不同，LIMBO 在单次前向过程中在线学习该分配，显式平衡任务性能与推理成本，且不修改底层智能体。

Result: 在 LifelongAgentBench 上跨三种 LLM 主干网络，LIMBO 取得优于最先进记忆增强基线的成本-准确率权衡，在最多降低约 83% 推理成本（平均约 53%）的情况下，几乎匹配所有最强基线。

Conclusion: LIMBO 可跨模型与环境自适应调整策略而无需重训练，表明有效的资源分配可以在线学习，而非人工指定。

Abstract: As LLM agents become integrated into increasingly complex workflows, they must continually acquire new capabilities while retaining competence on previously learned tasks. Lifelong agents address this through experience replay, injecting past interactions into the prompt to leverage prior experience during inference. However, replay is not free: every replayed trajectory competes with retrieval, reasoning, tool use, and verification for the same limited prompt and compute budget, making effective resource allocation essential. Existing approaches allocate these resources using fixed replay policies, regardless of whether replay is beneficial for the current task. We identify this as inference-time memory allocation, a distinct problem class for lifelong agents, and introduce LIMBO: the first online framework to our knowledge that treats memory as a controllable inference-time resource and jointly optimizes memory strategy and inference budget for each incoming task. Unlike prior approaches that fix the replay policy or require model weights, teacher supervision, or offline retraining, LIMBO learns this allocation online in a single pass, explicitly balancing task performance and inference cost without modifying the underlying agent. Across three LLM backbones on LifelongAgentBench, LIMBO achieves better cost-accuracy tradeoffs than state-of-the-art memory-augmented baselines and nearly matches all strongest such baselines at up to ~83% lower inference cost (~53% on average). LIMBO adapts its policy across models and environments without retraining, demonstrating that effective allocation can be learned online rather than manually specified.

</details>


### [102] [T-SMART: Mechanism-Level Attribution for Tool-Augmented Time-Series Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14142)
*Ivan Delgado,Himansi Gupta,Bishal Khatri,Niharika Sapre,Lameta Shamoon,Onat Gungor,Tajana Rosing*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出神经符号框架 T-SMART，将语言理解、数值计算与结构化感知彻底解耦，通过受控配对消融量化各组件对时序问答的贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 在时序问答（TS-QA）中表现不佳，尤其当数值信号被序列化为文本、需显式计算时。现有工具增强系统把语言推理、计算与感知纠缠在一起，难以辨别性能提升究竟来自哪个组件。

Method: T-SMART 采用角色分离的神经符号架构：冻结的 LLM 负责解析问题并选择操作；确定性工具执行数值计算；结构化感知仅在需要时被调用。通过受控配对消融（paired ablations）分别评估各组件贡献。

Result: 确定性计算带来主导性收益，在序列化时序数据上较直接 LLM 推理准确率提升 31.7 个百分点；语言理解与感知仅提供较小的互补增益。

Conclusion: 工具增强 TS-QA 的收益主要来自可靠的数值执行，而非额外的语言模型推理；该框架为分析神经符号时序系统中各组件贡献提供了受控方法论。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can struggle with time-series question answering (TS-QA), especially when numerical signals are serialized as text and require explicit computation. Tool-augmented approaches improve performance, but existing systems often intertwine language reasoning, computation, and perception, making it difficult to determine which components drive the gains. We present T-SMART, a neurosymbolic framework that separates these roles: a frozen LLM interprets questions and selects operations, deterministic tools perform numerical computation, and structured perception is invoked only when needed. Controlled paired ablations show that deterministic computation provides the dominant benefit, improving accuracy by 31.7 percentage points over direct LLM reasoning on serialized time series, while language understanding and perception offer smaller complementary gains. These results indicate that tool-augmented TS-QA benefits primarily from reliable numerical execution rather than additional language-model reasoning and provide a controlled framework for analyzing component contributions in neurosymbolic time-series systems.

</details>


### [103] [Signatures of Steerability in Activation Space of Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14151)
*Prajjwal Bhattarai,Tuka Alhanai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出用简单的可分性统计量（separation metrics）来预测激活引导向量（activation steering）的下游有效性，并证明可分性与经验特征方向与真实特征方向的对齐度强相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激活引导（activation steering）是控制模型行为的一种经典且计算高效的方法，但其有效性在不同概念上差异显著。以往认为引导向量的泛化性取决于构建数据集的属性，这一"数据集依赖"论断缺乏严格论证，也缺少可操作的诊断工具来判断引导向量何时有效。

Method: 1）将"数据集依赖"的主张形式化、严格化；2）在多种设置下计算简单的可分性指标，并考察其与下游可引导性（steerability）的相关性，同时控制层数与数据集效应；3）设计一个合成的叠加（superposition）实验，检验可分性指标与经验特征方向同真实特征方向之间对齐度的关系。

Result: 简单的可分性指标与语言模型在下游任务中的可引导性呈现强相关，这种相关性在控制层和数据集效应后依然成立；在合成叠加实验中，可分性指标与经验特征方向和真实特征方向的对齐度同样强相关。

Conclusion: 简单的可分性统计量可作为实用诊断指标，用于预先判断引导向量在给定概念上是否可能奏效，从而为激活引导的可靠性提供低成本的可预测性依据。

Abstract: Steering language models using a set of contrastive representations has been a canonical and computationally efficient method for controlling model behavior. Despite this success in controlling certain model behaviors, the effectiveness of activation steering varies markedly across concepts; the generalization properties of steering vectors are often considered a function of the dataset used to construct them. We make this dataset-dependence claim more rigorous and show that simple separation metrics strongly correlate with the downstream steerability of language models across diverse settings, even after controlling for layers and dataset effects. Beyond prediction, we provide evidence from a synthetic superposition experiment that separation metrics are strongly correlated with alignment between the empirical and true feature direction. Our results suggest that simple separability statistics can serve as practical diagnostics for when steering vectors are likely to work.

</details>


### [104] [CyFM: Cylindrical Optimal Transport for Few-Step Complex-Valued Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14171)
*Marcel Musiałek,Iga Wolanin,Damian Ryczko,Anna Grelewska,Oleksii Furman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出圆柱流匹配（CyFM），将复数信号（MRI、频谱图）的幅度-相位图建模为乘积度量下的圆柱空间，并配合整体场最优传输耦合，在少步生成中显著优于笛卡尔基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复数信号通常被当作平坦的二通道欧氏数据处理，忽略了幅度-相位结构。作者希望考察：在幅度-相位图上将继承度量替换为解耦乘积度量会带来什么代价、获得什么收益。

Method: 通过解析桥梁分析笛卡尔路径的角速度分布；提出圆柱流匹配CyFM，严格约束回归目标；在圆柱度量下对整场做minibatch最优传输以耦合噪声与数据；并考察维度/分块分解式传输的失效问题。

Result: 笛卡尔路径诱导重尾角速度分布（幂律指数约1.0），独立耦合下近半数路径角速度超过π，圆柱路径从不越过该阈值。OT耦合的传输代价下降随场维度崩塌（标量对的86%降至64×64场的3%），但对少步生成的收益保持：圆柱模型少步误差降低3-60%。在k≤8各步数与各分辨率上CyFM误差均低于最佳笛卡尔基线，五个随机种子可分离，且无需蒸馏；收敛后两种几何无显著差异。

Conclusion: 乘积度量下的圆柱建模与整体场OT耦合能有效改善少步生成，而维度或分块的传输分解会破坏数据联合分布（"分解耦合陷阱"）。实验仅限合成复场。

Abstract: Complex-valued signals, such as Magnetic Resonance Imaging (MRI) and audio spectrograms, are almost always modelled as flat two-channel Euclidean data. For nonzero values the amplitude-phase chart $z \mapsto (|z|, z/|z|)$ identifies the signal domain with the cylinder $(0, \infty) \times S^1$, on which we deliberately replace the inherited metric $dA^2 + A^2 dθ^2$ by the decoupled product metric $dA^2 + dθ^2$. In this empirical study we measure what that substitution costs and what it buys. By computing exact analytical bridges, we demonstrate that Cartesian paths induce a heavy-tailed distribution of angular velocity (power law index $\approx 1.0$), with nearly half of the probability paths exceeding an angular speed of $π$ under independent coupling, a rate no cylindrical path ever exceeds. To resolve this, we analyze Cylindrical Flow Matching (CyFM), which strictly bounds the regression target, and couple noise and data by exact minibatch Optimal Transport computed jointly over whole fields in the cylindrical metric. Although the transport-cost reduction of this coupling collapses with field dimension (from 86% for scalar pairs to 3% for $64\times64$ fields), its benefit to few-step generation does not: it lowers the few-step error of the cylindrical model by 3-60% at every evaluated resolution. With this coupling, CyFM has a lower error than the best Cartesian baseline at every step count up to $k = 8$ and every evaluated resolution, with all five seeds separated and without distillation, and at convergence we detect no significant difference between the two geometries. Finally, we expose the "Factorized Coupling Trap," showing that dimension-wise or patch-wise transport factorizations silently destroy the joint distribution of the data. All experiments are on synthetic complex fields.

</details>


### [105] [Inherited Heads: Audio language models track speakers with their text backbone's attention, and an attention-mass ranking retrieves a different set](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14174)
*Bojro Das*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在"描述录音中特定说话人内容"任务上，音频语言模型表现低于随机猜测；但给约百个注意力头（不足模型十分之一）的logits加上固定偏置、无需训练，即可在90.7%–99.0%的试验中把描述重定向到指定说话人。这些头大多不是音频特有的：由纯文本模型（或其同族模型）在书面版任务上选出的前100个头，原样迁移后仍能重定向音频模型80.8%–95.0%。音频与文本头集合重合66–74/100（随机约20），且仅用重合部分几乎复现全部转向效果；但同规模随机抽样也接近，故三款模型均无法区分"共享性"与"规模性"解释。第二项发现涉及头的筛选评分：既有的"注意力占比"评分与"逐头归一化"变体所选头部重合度差异很大，后者在Ultravox中显著更稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文关注两个问题：(1) 音频语言模型是否真正依据声学信息（说话人身份）作答，以及能否用极低成本的干预手段定向控制其描述对象；(2) 头的重要性/定位评分方法是否可靠——不同评分选出的头部是否一致，以及评分失效时会带来何种灾难性后果。动机还在于探究这种可控性是否依赖音频特有的表征，抑或可直接从文本模型中"搬运"，从而揭示多模态模型内部机制的共享程度。

Method: 任务：录音含6位说话人，要求模型描述被问及的那一位。干预：在约100个注意力头的attention logits上加固定偏置（不到模型参数的十分之一，无需训练），将输出重定向到指定说话人。头选择来源：全音频模型自身、或该音频模型的文本底座/同族公开发布模型在书面版任务上的排名，取前100名原样迁移。分析：计算音频头集与文本头集的交集大小，并与随机基线（约20/100）比较；用仅重合部分做转向；用等规模随机抽样做对照。头评分比较：既有"注意力落在目标片段的比例"评分 vs 逐头归一化的"注意力随问题变化的幅度"评分，比较二者所选前100头的重合度及在Ultravox上的干预效果（判断输出落于无对应片段的失败率）。

Result: 基线：正确说话人描述率仅6%–16%，低于随机猜测的16.7%。干预后：重定向成功率达90.7%–99.0%。跨模态迁移：文本模型选出的前100头在音频模型上重定向成功率80.8%–95.0%，全程未使用任何音频信息。头集重合：音频与文本头集重合66–74/100，随机约20；仅重合部分即可复现几乎所有转向效果。但等规模随机抽样表现接近，三款模型均无法区分共享解释与规模解释。评分对比：三种评分所选前100头在三款模型上分别重合69、37、4个。在重合仅4个的Ultravox中，既有评分所选头导致输出无法对应任何片段的比例达69.7%，无干预为40.0%，归一化变体仅1.0%。这是六组实验中的一组；其余五组中既有评分优于随机抽样。

Conclusion: 注意力头级的固定偏置可无需训练地、高成功率地定向控制音频语言模型对说话人的描述，且所依赖的头多为跨模态共享（文本模型迁移即有效），但现有证据不足以证明"共享性"本身是生效关键，因为同规模随机抽样效果相当。此外，头定位评分方法的可靠性存疑：不同评分选出的头部差异巨大，既有评分在Ultravox上甚至比无干预更差，提示该领域的头选择评分需要更严格的稳健性检验。

Abstract: Asked to describe what one of six speakers in a recording talks about, audio language models describe the right one on 6 to 16% of trials, below the 16.7% a guess would give. Adding a fixed bias to the attention logits of a hundred heads, under a tenth of the model's and with no training, redirects the description to whichever speaker we choose, on 90.7% to 99.0% of trials. Those heads are largely not specific to audio. Rank the text-only language model an audio model was built from, or a released model of the same family, on a written version of the task, take its top hundred heads, and carry them over unchanged: they redirect the audio model on 80.8% to 95.0% of trials, with nothing about audio entering the selection. The audio and text head sets share 66 to 74 of 100 where chance would give about 20, and the shared part alone reproduces almost all of the steering. What that does not show is that sharing is what makes the heads work: an equal-sized draw from the same discovered hundred does nearly as well, and none of our three models separates the two explanations. A second finding concerns how such heads are found.
