Ranking heads by how much attention they place on the segment asked about, as an established score does, or by how much of their attention moves with the question, as a per-head normalised variant does, gives top hundreds that share 69, 37 and 4 heads across our three models. In Ultravox, where they share 4, the established score's heads leave output the judge cannot place on any segment on 69.7% of trials, against 40.0% with no intervention and 1.0% for the normalised variant. That is one arm of six; on the other five the established score steers above a random draw.

</details>


### [106] [A Machine Learning Framework for Fault Detection, Isolation, and Severity Prediction of Autonomous VTOL Aircraft](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14180)
*Ripon C. Sarker,Pedram H. Dabaghian,Raman Goyal,Atanu Halder*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于CNN的旋翼故障检测、隔离与严重程度预测框架，利用真实飞行数据学习时空特征，实现故障旋翼识别与损伤等级推断，实验分类准确率超99%、严重度估计准确率96%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主VTOL飞行器在复杂安全关键环境中，即使轻微部件退化也会迅速破坏多旋翼稳定性，需早期识别旋翼损伤；但真实飞行中传感器噪声、环境扰动及非线性气动特性使故障检测困难。

Method: 构建综合机器学习框架，采用卷积神经网络（CNN）从多元飞行动态中学习时空模式，直接推断故障旋翼及其损伤程度；先用数据生成模型产生的仿真数据验证，再在六旋翼飞行器上通过受控桨尖断裂进行实验验证。

Result: 实验数据中，模型对旋翼级故障分类准确率超过99%，在1%容差内严重程度估计准确率达96%，表现出较强泛化能力。

Conclusion: 该框架支持自主VTOL系统的实时健康监测，验证了真实飞行数据下旋翼故障检测、隔离与严重度预测的可行性。

Abstract: Fault detection in autonomous VTOL aircraft is critical because even minor component degradations can rapidly destabilize multirotor vehicles operating in complex, safety-critical environments, motivating robust fault detection and estimation strategies capable of identifying early signs of rotor damage; however, real-flight fault detection remains challenging due to sensor noise, environmental disturbances, and the nonlinear aerodynamics of multirotor platforms. This study proposes a comprehensive machine-learning framework for rotor fault detection, isolation, and severity prediction using real flight data. A convolutional neural network (CNN) architecture is developed to learn spatio-temporal patterns from multivariate flight dynamics, enabling direct inference of both the faulted rotor and its damage level. The framework is first validated using simulated data generated by a data-generative model, and experimental validation is then performed on a hexacopter by introducing controlled blade-tip breakage. The trained model achieves rotor-wise fault classification accuracies above 99% and severity estimation accuracy of 96% within a 1% tolerance in experimental data, demonstrating strong generalization and supporting real-time health monitoring for autonomous VTOL systems.

</details>


### [107] [ZAPS: Zero-Cost Active Proxy Search for Neural Architecture Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14184)
*Hassan Touayouch,Rabie Najem,Mohammed Benjelloun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ZAPS四阶段流水线，首次在同一主动学习框架内融合零成本代理信号与架构拓扑，在NAS-Bench-201/101上以200次评估的预算显著优于随机搜索、局部搜索、REA、BANANAS与TPE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NAS单候选评估需训练收敛，穷举不可行；零成本代理虽秒级估计却噪声大，且因代理间强相关，朴素聚合会叠加共同误差而非抵消。既有方法只用代理信号或只用架构拓扑，未在单一主动学习框架中兼顾二者。

Method: ZAPS四阶段：(i) 离线用ProxyFit贪心抗冗余准则选出紧凑、非冗余代理子集；(ii) 用混合K-means播种搜索以平衡开发与探索；(iii) 每轮迭代随标注集增长以bootstrap投票重选代理；(iv) 用XGBoost集成联合代理秩与one-hot拓扑编码排序候选，并以UCB采集函数查询。

Result: B=200预算下，NAS-Bench-201上CIFAR-10恢复真实top-100的52.3%、CIFAR-100的65.8%，领先所有基线；CIFAR-10上运行间标准差不足最强基线的一半。NAS-Bench-201优势随预算增大而收窄，而更难、无方法接近饱和的NAS-Bench-101上优势反而扩大。评价指标统一为实际评估架构中命中真实top-100的比例。

Conclusion: 将代理信号与拓扑编码在主动学习框架中统一，可显著提升评估稀缺条件下的搜索效率与稳定性；代理冗余是朴素聚合失效的根因，抗冗余筛选与动态重选是有效对策。

Abstract: Neural Architecture Search (NAS) automates network design, but evaluating a single candidate requires training it to convergence, making exhaustive search intractable. Zero-cost proxies estimate architecture quality at initialization in seconds, yet a single proxy is noisy, and combining several does not straightforwardly help: proxies are strongly correlated, so naive aggregation compounds their shared errors instead of averaging them out. Existing methods exploit either proxy signals or architectural topology - never both within a single active-learning framework. We introduce ZAPS (Zero-cost Active Proxy Search), a four-stage pipeline that closes this gap. ZAPS (i) selects a compact, non-redundant proxy subset offline via ProxyFit, a greedy anti-redundancy criterion; (ii) seeds the search with a hybrid K-means strategy that balances exploitation and exploration; (iii) re-selects proxies at every iteration by a bootstrapped vote as the labeled set grows; and (iv) ranks candidates with an XGBoost ensemble trained jointly on proxy ranks and one-hot topological encodings, queried through an Upper Confidence Bound (UCB) acquisition function. On NAS-Bench-201 under a budget of B=200 evaluations, ZAPS recovers 52.3% of the true top-100 architectures on CIFAR-10 and 65.8% on CIFAR-100, ahead of every baseline we consider - Random Search, Local Search, REA, BANANAS and TPE - and, on CIFAR-10, with less than half the run-to-run standard deviation of the strongest of them. The advantage is largest where evaluations are scarce: on NAS-Bench-201 it narrows as the budget grows, whereas on the harder NAS-Bench-101, which no method comes close to saturating, it widens instead. All methods are scored by a single criterion: how much of the true top-100 lies among the architectures they actually evaluated.

</details>


### [108] [Entropy-Punctured Bloom Filters for Memory-Efficient Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14187)
*John Cartmell,Mihaela Cardei,Ionut Cardei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"熵穿孔布隆过滤器"（entropy-punctured Bloom Filters），一种内存感知的编码策略，通过经验熵识别并移除低变化性的比特位，从而在保持预测结构的同时压缩特征表示，提升预测效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在存储、传输成本、带宽或隐私约束限制原始数据访问的机器学习场景中，内存高效的特征表示日益重要。布隆过滤器（BF）编码虽能提供紧凑的概率性特征表示，但其在结构化压缩下的行为及其在回归任务中的适用性仍未得到充分探索。

Method: 以量化特征的定长布隆过滤器编码为起点，利用经验熵识别低变异性的比特位并将其移除（"穿孔"），生成缩减后的表示。在无泄漏评估协议下、约匹配的表示大小条件下，将原始特征、主成分分析（PCA）、随机投影（RP）及布隆过滤器变体进行对比，并采用岭回归、XGBoost和神经网络进行评估，以每样本编码表示大小对应的R2衡量预测效率。

Result: 布隆过滤器编码与经典压缩表示相比仍具竞争力，同时实现显著的存储节省。基于熵的穿孔进一步缩小表示尺寸，且预测保真度损失极小，从而提升预测效率。

Conclusion: 熵穿孔布隆过滤器为内存受限的机器学习提供了一种有效的表示层压缩方法。

Abstract: Memory-efficient feature representations are increasingly important in machine learning settings where storage, transmission cost, bandwidth, or privacy constraints limit access to raw data. Bloom Filter (BF) encodings provide compact probabilistic representations of engineered features, but their behavior under structural compression and their applicability to regression tasks remain underexplored.
  In this work, we propose entropy-punctured Bloom Filters, a memory-aware encoding strategy that removes low-variability bit positions identified using empirical entropy. Starting from fixed-length BF encodings of quantized features, the proposed approach produces reduced representations that preserve predictive structure while improving predictive efficiency relative to encoded representation size.
  We evaluate the approach on diverse regression datasets, comparing raw features, Principal Component Analysis (PCA), Random Projection (RP), and Bloom Filter variants under leakage-free evaluation protocols and approximately matched representation sizes. Performance is assessed using ridge regression, XGBoost, and neural networks, with predictive efficiency measured as R2 relative to encoded representation size per sample.
  Results show that Bloom Filter encodings remain competitive with classical compressed representations while achieving substantial storage savings. Entropy-based puncturing further reduces representation size with minimal loss in predictive fidelity, yielding improved predictive efficiency. These findings demonstrate that entropy-punctured Bloom Filters provide an effective representation-level compression approach for memory-constrained machine learning.

</details>


### [109] [Data-free On-policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14193)
*Gengsheng Li,Mao Zheng,Mingyang Song,Jie Sun,Zeyuan Liu,Ruiqi Liu,Qiyong Zhong,Haiyun Guo,Junfeng Fang,Jinqiao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现on-policy distillation (OPD)对训练数据几乎不敏感：8个prompt即可媲美17k数据集；进一步提出Data-free OPD (DF-OPD)，由教师模型自行生成训练题目，在单教师与多教师蒸馏中均匹配甚至超越真实数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPD已成为前沿后训练流程的标准组成部分，但其训练数据到底贡献多少一直缺乏系统考察。作者希望厘清OPD中数据的实际作用，并据此重新评估数据在OPD中的角色。

Method: 在两种常见教师-学生配对上开展数据敏感性实验：对比8个prompt与17k题数据集，以及三个难度和teacher-student KL差异数倍的独立数据集；分析数据单元本质（state而非prompt）；将数学替换为竞赛编程以检验知识迁移；据此提出DF-OPD，由教师模型在简单prompt下自行生成训练问题，无需外部数据与过滤，仅保留两个策略；并应用于多教师蒸馏。

Result: 8个prompt即匹配17k问题数据集；三个独立数据集训练曲线几乎无差别；数学替换为竞赛编程仍恢复90%以上域内增益；DF-OPD匹配甚至超越真实数据，其生成问题在训练动力学的三项关键诊断上追踪教师自身后训练数据；多教师蒸馏中1k自生成问题填补98.5%的可用提升空间，超过7k真实样本的96.6%。

Conclusion: OPD迁移的是教师的推理模式而非与数据相关的知识；其数据单元为状态而非prompt，导致边际价值在8个prompt后迅速塌缩。DF-OPD表明无需外部数据即可有效进行OPD，这要求重新评估数据在OPD中的作用。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) has become a standard component of frontier post-training pipelines, yet how much its training data actually contributes has gone largely unexamined. On the two teacher-student pairings most common in practice, we find OPD almost indifferent to its data: eight prompts already match a 17k-problem dataset, and three independently built datasets whose difficulty and teacher-student KL differ several-fold produce nearly indistinguishable training curves. Two causes account for this. First, the unit of data in OPD is the state a prompt leads to, not the prompt itself: a single prompt keeps exposing new teacher correction as sampling continues, while the marginal value of additional prompts collapses after eight. Second, replacing mathematics with competitive programming still recovers over ninety percent of the in-domain gain, indicating that OPD transfers the teacher's mode of reasoning rather than knowledge related to the data. We take this to its limit with Data-free On-policy Distillation (DF-OPD), in which the teacher writes its own training questions under a simple prompt -- no external data, no filtering -- leaving a system of just two policies. DF-OPD matches and even surpasses real data, and the questions it produces track the teacher's own post-training data on three key diagnostics of training dynamics, which other real datasets do not. Applied to multi-teacher distillation, where the (prompt, domain) pairs normally have to be derived from post-training data that is often out of reach, 1k self-generated questions close 98.5% of the available headroom, even surpassing the 96.6% reached with 7k real examples. Moreover, together these results invite a reassessment of the role data plays in OPD.

</details>


### [110] [Graph-Transformer Fraud Detection with Self-Supervised Pretraining and Conformal Risk Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14234)
*Sergei,Komarov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GTFD图-Transformer欺诈检测器，融合结构与时序证据，配合保形风险控制与自监督预训练，在公司交易基准上AUROC 0.990、F1 96.1%，假阳性率降低约29%，协同欺诈团伙召回由85.1%提升至96.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公司交易网络中的金融欺诈日益呈现协同化特征，基于规则的引擎与将每笔交易孤立处理的经典学习模型难以有效检测。需要一种能同时利用交易网络结构关系与交易时序信息的检测方法，并在标签稀缺和对抗扰动下保持稳定。

Method: GTFD采用多模态融合架构：用多头图注意力网络编码支付图结构，用门控Transformer编码有序交易序列，并通过跨模态门控层融合两种视图。融合表示经保形风险控制头转换为无阈值异常分数，具备有限样本覆盖保证。训练采用自监督链路掩码预训练并结合对抗增强，以应对标签稀缺和对抗扰动。

Result: 在包含协同欺诈团伙的公司交易基准上，GTFD达到AUROC 0.990、F1 96.1%（精确率96.3%、召回率95.9%）、准确率98.4%；相较最强基线假阳性率降低约29%，协同欺诈团伙召回率从85.1%提升至96.5%。消融显示自监督预训练贡献约2.0 AUROC点，保形头贡献约1.9 AUROC点。对抗压力测试中，扰动幅度0.20时GTFD保持89.2%准确率，次优模型降至76.4%。

Conclusion: GTFD通过结构-时序跨模态融合、保形风险控制与自监督对抗训练，在协同欺诈检测上显著优于现有基线，并展现出更强的鲁棒性与低假阳性特性，适合标签稀缺与对抗环境下的公司交易欺诈检测。

Abstract: Financial fraud in corporate transaction networks has grown more coordinated and harder to detect with rule-based engines and with classical learning models that treat each transaction in isolation. This paper presents GTFD, a graph-transformer fraud detector that fuses structural and temporal evidence from a corporation's payment graph. GTFD encodes the graph with a multi-head graph attention network, encodes ordered transaction sequences with a gated transformer, and combines both views through a cross-modal gating layer. A conformal risk-control head converts the fused representation into threshold-free anomaly scores with finite-sample coverage guarantees, and the network is trained with self-supervised link-mask pretraining plus adversarial augmentation so it remains stable under scarce labels and under adversarial perturbation. On a corporate transaction benchmark enriched with coordinated fraud rings, GTFD reaches an AUROC of 0.990, an F1-score of 96.1% (precision 96.3%, recall 95.9%), and an accuracy of 98.4%. It reduces the false-positive rate by about 29% relative to the strongest baseline while raising coordinated fraud-ring recall from 85.1% to 96.5%. Ablations attribute roughly 2.0 AUROC points to self-supervised pretraining and 1.9 AUROC points to the conformal head, and adversarial stress tests show GTFD retains 89.2% accuracy at perturbation magnitude 0.20 where the next-best model falls to 76.4%.

</details>


### [111] [CoArena: Evaluating Computer-Use and Multi-Agent Systems in Real Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14239)
*Nitish Kovuru,Prateek Jannu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 CoArena——一种实时（real-time）评测范式，用真实用户提交的任务、同沙箱并发执行、盲评投票与在线排行榜替代静态基准，并形式化定义实时评测的五个可测属性及完整的 Bradley-Terry 评分方法论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态基准在发布时固定任务集，虽可复现，却会随任务集老化、泄漏进训练语料，且无法跟随用户实际使用方式的变化，因而逐渐偏离其本应测量的目标。

Method: 真实用户提交任务；两个系统（单模型或多智能体流水线）在同一工具接口、相同沙箱桌面下并发执行同一任务；用户在不知来源的情况下对两结果盲评；公共排行榜依据这些判断重新拟合。将 real-time 定义为五个可测属性并各给出方程与实例：任务持续到达、实时并发执行、在线评分更新、抗污染的新鲜度、从失败运行到可复用训练环境的有界反馈时延。评分方法基于 Bradley-Terry 成对模型，给出加权观测与平局的似然、带惩罚的最大似然估计、单票到达时的流式更新（同一似然上的随机梯度步，退化为 Elo），并由观测信息量与聚类稳健三明治估计置信区间、参数自助法给出排名带，还规定新系统入榜规则与估计收敛速率。投票质量通过评委间一致性统计、冗余评审及对平局/弃权的显式处理来保障。

Result: 以含 211 票的五系统示例，从投票矩阵完整推导出评分、置信区间与排名带；所有数值均由给定输入推导或标注为示例性，并非对已部署系统的实测。

Conclusion: 该工作的核心贡献是对"何为实时评测"的形式化刻画及配套的在线成对评分方法论，使评测可持续更新、抗污染并贴近真实使用，但示例数值仅具说明性，尚需实际部署验证。

Abstract: Static benchmarks for computer-use agents fix a task set at release and score every system against it once. That makes them reproducible, and it lets them drift from what they should measure: a fixed task set ages, leaks into training corpora, and cannot follow how people actually use agents from week to week. CoArena measures use directly. Real users submit tasks; two systems, each a single model or a multi-agent pipeline behind the same tool interface, execute the same task concurrently in identical sandboxed desktops; users judge the two outcomes without knowing which system produced them; and a public leaderboard is refit from those judgments. The central contribution is a formal account of what makes such an evaluation real-time. We define real-time as five measurable properties, each with an equation and a worked example: continuous task arrival, live concurrent execution, online rating updates, freshness with contamination resistance, and bounded feedback latency from a failed run to a reusable training environment. The rating methodology follows in full: the Bradley-Terry pairwise model, its likelihood with weighted observations and ties, the penalized maximum-likelihood estimator, and the streaming update applied when a single vote arrives (a stochastic-gradient step on the same likelihood, recovering Elo). It gives confidence intervals from the observed information and a cluster-robust sandwich, rank bands from a parametric bootstrap, the rule by which a new system enters the board, and the convergence rate of the estimate. Vote quality is treated with inter-judge agreement statistics, redundant judging, and explicit handling of ties and abstentions. A five-system example with 211 votes is carried from the vote matrix to ratings, intervals, and rank bands. Every number is derived from stated inputs or labeled illustrative; none is a measurement of a deployed system.

</details>


### [112] [Bayesian optimization with kernel ensembles and disagreement-based acquisition for source localization and acoustic inversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14262)
*Heng Zhang,Haotian Xiang,Florian Meyer,Qin Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于多核高斯过程加权集成的贝叶斯优化方法，用于联合声源定位与地声反演，降低对核函数选择的依赖并减少昂贵正演模型评估次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联合声源定位与地声反演的目标函数依赖昂贵的简正波传播模型，需在有限正演评估下获得准确参数估计。贝叶斯优化配合高斯过程代理可满足此需求，但其性能高度依赖核函数族的选择；在七维搜索空间中观测点稀少，任何单一核函数都难以在所有反演中稳定表现。

Method: 采用不同核函数族的高斯过程构成加权集成代理，使代理模型无需预先锁定单一核函数即可适应观测到的目标函数形态；同时结合最优条件（optimum-conditioned）采集函数，决定下一次进行昂贵目标评估的位置。

Result: 在仿真数据与实测SWellEx-96数据上，所提方法在所考察的贝叶斯优化策略中获得最低的平均最终目标值，并在多数坐标上降低了参数估计误差。消融实验表明，集成策略提供了对核函数选择的鲁棒性，而采集函数贡献了主要的优化增益。

Conclusion: 多核高斯过程加权集成可有效缓解贝叶斯优化对核函数选择的敏感性，配合最优条件采集函数能显著提升联合声源定位与地声反演的优化效率与参数估计精度。

Abstract: Joint source localization and geoacoustic inversion requires optimizing an objective built from an expensive normal mode propagation model. Bayesian optimization (BO) with a Gaussian process (GP) surrogate can obtain accurate parameter estimates within a limited number of forward model evaluations, but its performance depends on the choice of kernel family. With few observations in a seven-dimensional search space, no single kernel can be expected to perform consistently well across individual inversions. To reduce this dependence, we use a weighted ensemble of GPs with different kernel families, allowing the surrogate to adapt to the observed objective without committing to one kernel in advance. The ensemble is combined with an optimum-conditioned acquisition function that determines where the expensive objective should be evaluated next. Experiments on simulated and measured SWellEx-96 data show that the resulting method achieves the lowest mean final objective among the considered BO strategies and reduces parameter estimation error on most coordinates. Ablation results further show that the ensemble provides robustness to kernel choice, while the acquisition function accounts for most of the optimization gain.

</details>


### [113] [Biquaternionic Space with Complex-valued Attention for Temporal Knowledge Graph Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14279)
*Rushan Geng,Cuicui Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出双四元数空间与复值注意力模型BSCA，在五个时序知识图谱基准上取得竞争性甚至显著领先的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时序知识图谱嵌入模型多使用单一几何空间，难以刻画多样化的关系模式；同时常将实体表示视为静态，无法随时间和关系语境变化。

Method: BSCA在统一的双四元数框架内结合圆旋转与双曲旋转，并设计复值注意力机制，自适应融合时间条件与关系条件的实体表示。

Result: 在五个基准数据集上均具竞争力；在GDELT上提升最大，MRR达52.1%，而最强基线为38.1%。

Conclusion: 将多种几何旋转统一到双四元数空间并结合复值注意力，可有效建模时序与关系语境下的动态实体表示，显著提升TKGE链路预测性能。

Abstract: Temporal knowledge graph embedding (TKGE) models infer missing facts in knowledge graphs that evolve over time. Many existing models use a single geometric space, which can limit their ability to represent diverse relational patterns, or treat entity representations as static. We propose Biquaternionic Space with Complex-valued Attention (BSCA), a TKGE model that combines circular and hyperbolic rotations within a unified biquaternionic framework. A complex-valued attention mechanism adaptively fuses time-conditioned and relation-conditioned entity representations, allowing them to vary with temporal and relational context. Experiments on five benchmark datasets show competitive performance across datasets, with the largest improvement on GDELT: BSCA achieves an MRR of 52.1\%, compared with 38.1\% for the strongest baseline in our comparison.

</details>


### [114] [Learning Source Acquisition Policies by Offline Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14299)
*Ziqi Zhao,Run Xu,Qingjian Ni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: O-MPAC将有限时域的风险-成本目标从完整训练记录迁移到共享的源-动作打分器，在推理时利用部分观测与源元数据反复打分并施加硬成本掩码，在路由实验中达到0.965准确率，并在六个真实任务中多数取得最高预算积分准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在采集预算约束下预测时，需要选择特征组，而其价值可能取决于后续查询，因此需要一种能在部分观测下动态决策的方法。

Method: O-MPAC将有限时域的风险-成本目标从完整训练记录迁移到共享的源-动作打分器；推理时打分器使用部分观测与源元数据，在每次查询后重新打分，并施加硬成本掩码。此外分析了绑定教师目标与剩余规划时域对学习决策的影响，并对绑定极小值进行均匀监督以在源重标记下保持目标分布。

Result: 五种子路由实验中，在原始与context-last两种顺序下均达到0.965准确率；在六个真实任务上，验证在全部三十个划分中都选择不含动作交叉熵的H1；O-MPAC在五个任务上对源自适应GDFS、DIME、AACO+NN及静态策略取得最高平均预算积分准确率。

Conclusion: O-MPAC通过迁移风险-成本目标并在推理时动态重打分，在预算约束的特征采集预测中表现优于多种源自适应与静态基线方法。

Abstract: Predicting under an acquisition budget requires choosing feature groups whose value can depend on later queries. O-MPAC transfers finite-horizon risk-cost targets from complete training records into a shared source-action scorer. At inference time, the scorer uses partial observations and source metadata, re-scores after each query, and applies a hard cost mask. We analyze how tied teacher targets and the remaining planning horizon affect the learned decisions. Uniform supervision over tied minima preserves the target distribution under source relabeling. In a five-seed routing experiment, it achieves 0.965 accuracy under both original and context-last orders. On six real tasks, validation selects H1 without action cross-entropy in all thirty splits. O-MPAC has the highest mean budget-integrated accuracy on five tasks against source-adapted GDFS, DIME, AACO+NN and a static policy.

</details>


### [115] [Nonparametric Variance-Penalized Actor-Critic: Statistical Inference for Risk-Sensitive Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14327)
*Saunak Kumar Panda,Tong Li,Yisha Xiang,Ruiqi Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出无参数方差惩罚演员-评论家框架VPAC，用自举与随机缩放在线估计方差替代第二评论家网络，在保持单评论家架构的同时实现风险敏感RL，并给出几乎必然收敛性证明与实证验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 风险敏感强化学习中，方差惩罚是一类原则性方法，可显式在期望回报与策略稳定性之间权衡。但现有方法需要额外的第二评论家在线估计回报方差，带来架构复杂度提升与学习过程中估计误差叠加的问题。作者希望去除这一辅助网络，同时保持理论可分析性与方差惩罚的有效性。

Method: 提出非参数方差惩罚演员-评论家（VPAC）框架，用源于统计推断中随机逼近理论的自举（bootstrapping）与随机缩放（random scaling, RS）在线估计器替代方差评论家。该估计器无需辅助网络，维持单评论家架构，且构造上方差惩罚天然有界。基于常微分方程（ODE）方法，证明方差惩罚Q学习算法与双时间尺度演员-评论家变体的几乎必然收敛，收敛条件仅要求方差估计有界而非一致。

Result: 在离散与连续随机环境中评估，所提方法在方差降低上匹配或超过现有双评论家VPAC基线，同时消除第二评论家的开销。在高温超导（HTS）制造案例中，VPAC-RS实现稳态临界电流变异性降低74%，回合回报标准差降低63%，直接转化为良率一致性提升。

Conclusion: 非参数统计推断可作为风险敏感强化学习中辅助评论家的实用且理论可靠的替代方案。该方法在保持单评论家简洁架构、具备收敛保证的同时，实现了与双评论家方法相当或更优的风险控制效果，并在实际制造场景中体现应用价值。

Abstract: Variance penalization is a principled approach to risk-sensitive reinforcement learning (RL) that explicitly trades expected return for policy stability. Existing methods require a dedicated second critic to estimate return variance online, adding architectural complexity and compounding estimation error during learning. We propose a nonparametric variance-penalized actor-critic (VPAC) framework that replaces the variance critic with statistically grounded online estimators based on bootstrapping and random scaling, techniques drawn from the statistical inference literature for stochastic approximation. These estimators require no auxiliary network, maintain a single-critic architecture, and produce variance penalties that are bounded by construction, enabling clean convergence analysis. We establish almost-sure convergence for both a variance-penalized Q-learning algorithm and a two-timescale actor-critic variant via the ordinary differential equation (ODE) method, requiring only that variance estimates remain bounded rather than consistent. Empirically, we evaluate across discrete and continuous stochastic environments, demonstrating that the proposed methods match or exceed the variance reduction achieved by the existing dual-critic VPAC baseline while eliminating the overhead of a second critic. We further validate on a high-temperature superconductor (HTS) manufacturing case study, where VPAC-RS (Random Scaling) achieves a 74% reduction in steady-state critical current variability and a 63% reduction in episode return standard deviation, translating directly to improved yield consistency. Our results establish nonparametric statistical inference as a practical and theoretically sound alternative to auxiliary critics for risk-sensitive RL.

</details>


### [116] [Communication-Efficient LLM Adaptation over Decentralized GPU Meshes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14339)
*Sameera Ramasinghe,Shamane Siriwardhana,Thalaiyasingam Ajanthan,Hadi Mohaghegh Dolatabadi,Chamin P Hewa Koneputugodage,Gil Avraham,Violetta Shevchenko,James Snewin,Karol Pajak,Harry Xi,Alexander Long*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出异步双电路系统，用激活掩码压缩流水线与数据并行通信，并以延迟的"锚"先验纠正梯度，在低端GPU和互联网级连接下实现最高40倍以上吞吐提升且性能与稠密无压缩相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 去中心化训练可在低端GPU和互联网级带宽上训练大模型，但数据并行（DP）与流水线并行（PP）两个轴上的通信成为主要瓶颈；本文聚焦此场景下的后预训练适配问题。已有工作认为激进的激活压缩不可靠，本文试图重新审视该结论。

Method: 提出异步双电路系统：快速压缩训练电路通过在PP传输中使用激活掩码（activation masking）、在DP同步中使用压缩通信来提升吞吐；慢速锚电路在关键路径之外偶尔执行未掩码的前向-反向传播。并引入谱校正优化器（spectral correction optimizer），利用这些延迟的锚先验对掩码梯度去噪，而不阻塞快速流。

Result: 仅PP压缩即可带来最高约9倍吞吐提升；与DP压缩结合后，在约200Mbps的互联网级连接上提升超过40倍，同时在领域适配与持续预训练任务上匹配稠密未压缩的性能。

Conclusion: 在有锚先验校正的前提下，激活掩码能在高压缩率下支撑后预训练适配，推翻了此前"激进激活压缩不可靠"的判断；双电路异步架构可有效缓解去中心化训练中的通信瓶颈。

Abstract: Decentralized training enables large-model training over low-end GPUs and internet-grade connections, but communication along both data-parallel and pipeline-parallel axes becomes the primary bottleneck. We study post-pretraining adaptation in this setting. We propose an asynchronous two-circuit system: a fast compressed training circuit drives throughput using activation masking for pipeline-parallel (PP) transfer and compressed data-parallel (DP) synchronization, while a slow anchor circuit runs occasional unmasked forward--backward passes off the critical path. Then, we introduce a spectral correction optimizer that uses these delayed anchor priors to denoise masked gradients without blocking the fast stream. Although prior work has found aggressive activation compression unreliable, we show that masking supports post-pretraining adaptation at high compression rates when anchored this way. Pipeline-parallel compression alone yields up to a $9\times$ throughput gain, and combining it with data-parallel compression increases beyond $40\times$ over internet-grade $\sim 200$Mbps connections, while matching dense uncompressed performance across domain adaptation and continual pretraining.

</details>


### [117] [Multimodal deep learning from spectra for small-molecule structure identification: enhancing robustness with mixed-condition training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14360)
*Bowen Gao,Lei Zhu,Yiying Wang,Wenjie Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究将光谱学与化学领域知识引入混合条件训练，用于候选结构重排序，并结合专家混合（MoE）融合，显著提升多模态光谱模型在缺失、退化或不一致光谱条件下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际小分子结构鉴定中，互补的光谱证据常面临光谱缺失、质量下降或不匹配的问题，给多模态模型的可靠性带来挑战，因此需要提升模型在多种不完整或受损光谱条件下的稳健性。

Method: 构建可复现的评估协议，针对每种光谱模态设计扰动，并用化学信息驱动的光谱替换来覆盖光谱可用性、质量和一致性的变化；采用混合条件训练进行候选结构重排序，并使用专家混合（MoE）融合。基于Multimodal Spectroscopic Dataset（MSSD）的模拟MS、IR、1H和13C NMR光谱，评估79,462个测试样本、30种预设条件，每样本最多128个困难候选结构，并开展完整输入训练与混合条件训练、普通拼接与MoE融合的2×2因子对比。

Result: 混合条件训练在两种架构下均带来主要增益。MoE下，条件等权平均MRR从0.9203升至0.9763，相对提升6.08%；R@1从89.50%升至96.36%，提升6.86个百分点，相对提升7.67%。仅IR和仅MS/MS的MRR分别从0.4337升至0.9307、从0.3711升至0.8575，达到基线的2.15倍和2.31倍，同时完整输入性能保持较高水平。

Conclusion: 将领域知识整合进训练条件设计可有效提升模型稳健性，混合条件训练下MoE融合还能带来进一步增益，为多模态光谱结构鉴定提供了可复现的评估与训练范式。

Abstract: In practical molecular characterization, small-molecule structure identification benefits from complementary spectroscopic evidence, but missing, degraded, or mismatched spectra challenge multimodal models. Herein, we incorporate domain knowledge from spectroscopy and chemistry into mixed-condition training for candidate structure reranking, using a reproducible evaluation protocol and mixture-of-experts (MoE) fusion. The protocol incorporates perturbations tailored to each spectroscopic modality and chemically informed spectrum replacements to cover variations in spectral availability, quality, and consistency. A total of 79,462 test samples were evaluated across 30 predefined conditions using simulated spectra from the Multimodal Spectroscopic Dataset (MSSD) for mass spectrometry (MS), infrared (IR) spectroscopy, and 1H and 13C nuclear magnetic resonance (NMR), with up to 128 hard candidate structures per sample. A controlled two-by-two factorial comparison of complete-input training versus mixed-condition training and vanilla concatenation versus MoE fusion, with matched evaluation conditions, showed that mixed-condition training provided the main gains in both architectures. For MoE, mean reciprocal rank (MRR), averaged equally across conditions, increased from 0.9203 to 0.9763, a relative increase of 6.08%. Recall at rank 1 (R@1), averaged over the same conditions, increased from 89.50% to 96.36%, an increase of 6.86 percentage points and a relative increase of 7.67%. IR-only and MS/MS-only MRR increased from 0.4337 to 0.9307 and from 0.3711 to 0.8575, reaching 2.15 and 2.31 times their respective baseline values, while complete-input performance remained high. These results support integrating domain knowledge into training-condition design to improve robustness, with further gains from MoE under mixed-condition training.

</details>


### [118] [Towards Identifying the Dataset Biases Causing Phantom Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14449)
*Jonas Jürß,Pietro Liò*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 教师模型可通过"过滤偏见显式引用"的数据集将偏见传递给学生，且数据层面防御难以检测或消除；本文基于Sentence BERT嵌入的简单特征可在已知攻击者教师模型时以0.83、未知时以0.46的MCC识别偏见主题，并发现不同教师模型表达同一偏见所用的词汇不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究揭示教师模型能经过去除显式偏见引用的数据集将偏见隐式传递给学生，而现有数据层防御即使在已知目标偏见的情况下也无效。因此需要探究这些偏见在数据中留下的隐藏痕迹，为检测与理解偏见传递提供线索。

Method: 提出一种基于Sentence BERT嵌入的简单特征（签名），用以识别数据集所隐含的偏见主题。分别在攻击者所用教师模型已知与未知两种设定下进行识别实验，并对比不同教师模型表达同一偏见时所用词汇的差异。

Result: 该签名在教师模型已知时识别偏见主题的Matthews相关系数达0.83，在教师模型未知时为0.46。此外，观察到不同教师模型似乎通过不同词汇表达同一偏见。

Conclusion: 数据集尽管过滤了偏见的显式引用，仍残留可被Sentence BERT签名捕捉的隐式痕迹，尤其当教师模型已知时检测效果显著；且偏见表达具有模型依赖性，暗示偏见传递通过特定词汇模式留下可探测印记，为后续防御与归因提供了方向。

Abstract: Recent work has shown that a teacher model can transfer a bias to a student through a dataset from which every explicit reference to that bias has been filtered out, and that no data-level defense reliably removes or detects it even when knowing what bias to look for. Aiming to shed light on the hidden traces of these biases, we show that a simple signature based on Sentence BERT embeddings can identify the topic of such a bias with a Matthews correlation coefficient of 0.83 if the teacher model used by the attacker is known and 0.46 if it is not. Additionally, we observe that different teacher models appear to express the same bias through different vocabulary.

</details>


### [119] [Follow the Geometry, Not the Model: Cold Start Semi-Supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14451)
*Itai David,Daphna Weinshall*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出VAST方法，将半监督学习中的伪标签生成与分类器训练解耦：先在冻结自监督嵌入上通过Veracity Matrix推断无标签数据的概率信念，再蒸馏为归纳式分类器，并引入Veracity Propagation扩展覆盖范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代半监督学习将伪标签生成与分类器训练耦合，依赖分类器自身置信度筛选伪标签。在冷启动场景（每类仅有极少标签）下，分类器尚未学好就无法自我监督，该耦合方式存在病态问题。

Method: VAST（Veracity-Aware Semi-Supervised Training）：1）两阶段解耦，先从冻结自监督嵌入的几何结构中直接推断无标签集的概率信念，再蒸馏到归纳式分类器；2）Veracity Matrix——基于核的结构，沿数据流形聚合标签证据，可解释为逐观测幂似然模型下的Dirichlet后验；3）Veracity Propagation——自终止的信念传播步骤，将覆盖范围扩展到标注集的核邻域之外。

Result: 在统一协议下（所有方法使用相同的冻结嵌入与标注集），VAST在三个数据集的所有操作点上均优于最强的图基半监督基线，9次比较中7次取得统计显著提升，且输出可部署的归纳式分类器，无需转导式图推断。相较端到端置信门控半监督方法，后者在该场景下表现不佳，且未能稳定超过仅用标注数据的性能。

Conclusion: 通过解耦伪标签生成与分类器训练，VAST有效解决了冷启动半监督学习中的自监督病态问题，在归纳式设定下同时取得更优精度与更强部署实用性。

Abstract: Modern semi-supervised learning (SSL) couples pseudo-label generation and classifier training, using the classifier's own confidence to select the pseudo-labels that are then used to update the model. In the cold-start regime, where at most a few labels per class are available, this coupling is ill-posed, since the classifier cannot supervise itself before it has learned.
  To address this problem, we propose VAST (Veracity-Aware Semi-Supervised Training), which decouples these two stages. Probabilistic beliefs over the unlabeled set are first inferred directly from the geometry of a frozen self-supervised embedding and only then distilled into an inductive classifier.
  The construction rests on the Veracity Matrix, a kernel-based structure that aggregates label evidence across the data manifold and admits an interpretation as a Dirichlet posterior under a per-observation powered-likelihood model. Additionally, we introduce Veracity Propagation, a self-terminating belief-spreading step that extends coverage beyond the kernel neighborhood of the labeled set.
  Under a controlled protocol in which all methods receive identical frozen embeddings and labeled sets, VAST outperforms the strongest graph-based SSL baselines at every operating point across three datasets, with statistically significant gains in 7 of 9 comparisons, while producing a deployable inductive classifier rather than requiring transductive graph inference. Compared with end-to-end confidence-gated SSL, we further find that these methods underperform in this setting and, in our experiments, do not consistently exceed labeled-only performance.

</details>


### [120] [Retrieval-Guided Fine-Tuning as Noisy Estimation: Risk bounds and Architectural Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14485)
*Bhargav Lad,Yifan Hao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文从多任务线性回归视角对RAG-FT进行理论建模，证明检索失败率随任务分离度指数衰减，并揭示线性自注意力前向传播中固定无权聚合是导致不稳定的根源，而OLS代理保持稳定风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG-FT将检索数据直接纳入训练目标，但训练期间噪声检索的统计后果在理论上刻画不足，本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 将RAG-FT建模为多任务线性回归框架下的估计问题，用OLS代理单层线性自注意力，得到有限样本风险界；在同方差检索噪声下推导显式有限样本条件；引入距离比例噪声(DPN)模型，并在同一检索过程下比较OLS代理与字面无权线性自注意力前向传递两种估计器。

Result: 同方差噪声下检索失败随任务分离度与噪声之比指数衰减；得到RAG-FT风险低于仅目标训练和全语料训练的显式条件；在DPN下，注意力估计器偏差即使精确检索也按Θ(n^{2q})发散，而OLS风险对所有q>0保持Θ(d/n)；直接模拟验证了预测的速率分离。

Conclusion: 不稳定性并非源于噪声检索本身，而是来自字面LSA前向传播中固定、无权的聚合方式；按可靠性重新加权可经验性地消除该不稳定。

Abstract: Retrieval-Guided Fine-Tuning (RAG-FT) incorporates retrieved data directly into the training objective, but the statistical consequences of noisy retrieval during training remain theoretically undercharacterized. We study this question by modeling RAG-FT as an estimation problem in a multi-task linear regression framework, using an OLS proxy for single-layer linear self-attention to obtain finite-sample risk bounds. Under homoscedastic retrieval noise, we show that retrieval failure decays exponentially with task separation relative to noise, and derive explicit finite-sample conditions under which RAG-FT achieves lower risk than both target-only and full-corpus training. We then introduce a Distance-Proportional Noise (DPN) model, in which retrieval quality degrades with rank, and compare two estimators under the same retrieval process: the OLS proxy and the literal, uniform-weight forward pass of linear self-attention. We prove that the attention estimator's bias diverges as $Θ(n^{2q})$ even under exact retrieval, while OLS risk remains $Θ(d/n)$ for every noise exponent $q>0$. These results locate the instability not in noisy retrieval itself, but in the fixed, unweighted aggregation of the literal LSA forward pass, which reweighting by reliability empirically removes. We validate the predicted rate separation through direct simulation of the DPN model.

</details>


### [121] [EdgeHAR: An Edge-Native Compact Sensor Foundation Model for Human Activity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14498)
*He Zhang,Siyu Yuan,Siyu Liu,Sizhen Bian,Bin Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出边缘原生的紧凑传感器基础模型EdgeHAR，通过将传感器信号解耦为活动语义码、运动动态码和采集上下文码三类隐码，在有限目标域数据下高效适应新用户、设备、位置与活动类别，兼顾基础模型级迁移性与边缘端计算、内存、时延和隐私约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于传感器的HAR基础模型多面向云端规模部署，难以应对真实场景中的传感偏移（未见用户、设备、采样率、传感器位置），且将活动知识与采集差异纠缠在一起，导致迁移与适配成本高。

Method: EdgeHAR将传感器信号解耦为三个隐码：活动语义码（可复用活动知识）、运动动态码（时序模式）、采集上下文码（传感器特定变化）；并引入轻量适配模块，实现解耦式表示学习与高效域适应。

Result: 在异构HAR数据集上的实验表明，EdgeHAR在分布偏移下保持有竞争力的识别性能，同时显著降低部署成本。

Conclusion: EdgeHAR为普适传感系统提供了紧凑、边缘优先的基础模型范式，证明可在边缘约束下实现基础模型级迁移能力。

Abstract: Sensor-based human activity recognition (HAR) is fundamental to ubiquitous and wearable computing, yet existing foundation models are largely designed for cloud-scale deployment and struggle with real-world sensing shifts, including unseen users, devices, sampling rates, and sensor placements. We present \textbf{EdgeHAR}, an edge-native compact sensor foundation model designed for wearable intelligence. Unlike conventional models that entangle activity knowledge with acquisition variations, EdgeHAR learns transferable representations by factorizing sensor signals into three latent codes: an \textbf{(i)Activity-Semantic Code} capturing reusable activity knowledge, a \textbf{(ii)Motion-Dynamics Code} modeling temporal patterns, and an \textbf{(iii)Acquisition-Context Code} representing sensor-specific variations. This disentangled design enables efficient adaptation to new users, devices, placements, and activity classes with limited target-domain data. By incorporating lightweight adaptation modules, EdgeHAR achieves foundation-model-level transferability while satisfying edge constraints in computation, memory, latency, and privacy. Experiments across heterogeneous HAR datasets demonstrate that EdgeHAR maintains competitive recognition performance under distribution shifts with substantially reduced deployment cost. EdgeHAR establishes a practical paradigm for compact, edge-first foundation models for ubiquitous sensing systems.

</details>


### [122] [Should All Noises Be Treated Equally: Impact of Input Noise Variability on Neural Network Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14504)
*Salma Alsinan,Maksim Makarenko,Sixiu Liu,Ali Aldawood,Ibrahim Hoteit*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究噪声类型、尺度与复杂度对神经网络在地震初至拾取和去噪任务中泛化性与鲁棒性的影响，提出鲁棒性矩阵评估模型在分布外噪声下的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 野外地球物理数据常受复杂异质噪声污染，掩盖微弱地震事件并干扰自动解释。深度学习虽有用，但其性能对训练噪声性质高度敏感，尤其在分布外条件下，缺乏对噪声参数影响的系统认识。

Method: 模拟随钻地震数据，用随机噪声生成器对输入源噪声做受控增强，变化噪声类型、尺度和复杂度；训练多种神经网络于固定噪声类型与尺度，并在已知与未知噪声场景下评估；逐步引入复合噪声混合提升难度，构建鲁棒性矩阵表征各模型相对其训练配置的泛化能力。

Result: 较大噪声尺度可提升泛化性；噪声类型、任务复杂度与网络结构的有效匹配是最大化泛化收益的关键；复合噪声训练可缓解单一噪声训练的弱点，起到隐式正则化作用，提高鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 揭示了影响噪声地球物理环境下模型韧性的关键因素，为开发能在多样且不可预测噪声条件下有效泛化的深度学习模型提供指导。

Abstract: Geophysical data collected from active field sites are often contaminated by complex and heterogeneous noise, obscuring weak seismic events, and complicating automated interpretation. Although deep learning offers promising solutions for seismic processing, its performance is highly sensitive to the nature of training noise, especially under out-of-distribution (OOD) conditions. This study investigates the influence of noise parameters, such as type, scale, and complexity on the performance, generalization and robustness of neural networks in two geophysical tasks: first break picking and denoising. We simulate seismic-while-drilling data and apply controlled input source noise augmentation using stochastic generators to vary the noise characteristics. Different neural networks are trained on fixed noise types and scales, then evaluated across both seen and unseen noise scenarios. We incrementally increase the complexity of the noise by introducing compound noise mixtures and assess the performance of the model under increasingly challenging OOD conditions. This yields a robustness matrix that captures the generalizability of each model relative to its training configuration. Results indicate that larger noise scales boost generalization, and that effective alignment between noise type, task complexity, and architecture is key for maximizing generalization gains. In addition, training with compound noises mitigate weaknesses associated with single-noise training, acting as an additional implicit regularizer to improving robustness. These findings highlight key factors influencing model resilience in noisy geophysical environments and offer guidance for developing deep learning models that generalize effectively across diverse and unpredictable noise conditions.

</details>


### [123] [GNN4PPM: Multi-Target Predictive Process Monitoring with Relational Graph Convolutional Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14534)
*Ana Costa,Johannes Mäkelburg,Luise Pufahl*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GNN4PPM 将事件日志表示为捕获完整数据载荷的异质知识图，并用 R-GCN 训练嵌入，从而一次性预测所有后续事件及其完整数据内容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预测性流程监控（PPM）方法仅从已执行活动、时间戳和案例 ID 的角度看待事件，忽略了真实场景中事件日志里记录的更多信息，导致预测任务未能充分利用日志中丰富的数据。

Method: 该方法将事件信息表示为异质知识图，以 RDF 语义捕获事件日志，并采用关系图卷积网络（R-GCN）训练嵌入，从而一次性预测所有后续事件及其完整数据载荷。

Result: 与现有解决方案相比，GNN4PPM 表现良好；与最先进方法的实验验证了其在复杂场景下的准确性和适用性。

Conclusion: GNN4PPM 通过知识图与图神经网络的结合，突破了传统 PPM 仅关注活动与时间戳的限制，为在复杂真实场景中实现更全面、准确的流程预测提供了有前景的方向。

Abstract: Predictive Process Monitoring (PPM) aims at predicting at runtime and as early as possible the future states of a process execution. Common tasks include predicting the next event, the time to completion of a trace, and outcomes. Existing approaches typically consider an event from the perspective of the executed activities along with their timestamps and case identifiers. This leads to the disadvantage that in real-life settings, there is much more information recorded in the event log that is not captured or completely ignored when performing prediction tasks. We introduce GNN4PPM, an approach that predicts all next events along with their complete data payload at once. We represent event information in a heterogeneous knowledge graph that captures the event log as an RDF semantics, and train the embeddings with a Relational Graph Convolutional Network (R-GCN). Our approach is promising in comparison to existing solutions, and experiments with state-of-the-art solutions prove the accuracy and applicability of GNN4PPM in complex settings.

</details>


### [124] [Selecting k Paths with the Minimum Longest Path Length in the Stochastic Semi-Bandit Setting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14557)
*Shunsuke Aoki,Atsuyoshi Nakamura*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究有向图上每步从源点到汇点选取k条路径以最小化所选路径最大传输时间的在线问题，将随机传输时间与边容量约束建模为半强盗问题并提出求解算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 并行数据传输中多条路径的传输时间具有随机性且各边容量受限，需在线选取路径集合以最小化最大传输时间，这一问题此前缺乏针对性建模与算法。

Method: 将网络表示为有向图，每步选取k条从源点到汇点的路径，每条边每轮被经过的路径数不超过其容量，传输时间随机；据此形式化为半强盗（semi-bandit）问题，并设计相应算法。

Result: 摘要未给出具体实验或理论结果，仅说明提出了一种用于求解该半强盗问题的算法。

Conclusion: 该工作以半强盗框架刻画受容量约束的在线多路径并行传输问题，为最小化最大传输时间提供了算法方案。

Abstract: When performing parallel data transmission through a network using multiple paths, it is practically important to minimize the maximum transmission time among the selected paths. This study addresses an online problem in which $k$ paths from an origin vertex to a destination vertex must be selected at each time step within a network represented as a directed graph. Here, the number of paths going through each edge in each parallel data transmission is limited to its capacity, and the time required for transmission is determined stochastically. We formulate the semi-bandit problem of selecting a set of paths to minimize the maximum traversal time among the selected paths and propose an algorithm to solve it.

</details>


### [125] [Pathwise Individual Rationality in Federated Learning: A Mechanism-Architecture Co-Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14591)
*Amin Meghrazi,Srinivasan Parthasarathy,Andrew Perrault*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究联邦学习中的个体理性（IR）问题，即参与联邦的效用不应差于本地独立训练。作者分析路径性能发现客户端可能在数百轮内低于本地基线；证明了限制每轮贡献会导致学习崩溃，并提出结合短期参与保证与个性化模型评估的新设计，在实证中避免短期损失且不损害整体性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习参与有成本：客户端需权衡隐私、通信与计算成本以换取模型效能收益。核心动机是满足个体理性（IR）这一博弈论基本要求，即联邦提供的效用不劣于本地训练（autarky）。已有研究多关注渐近均衡保证，忽视了逐轮路径上的短期性能表现，而客户端可能在相当长时间内低于本地训练基线。

Method: 在不同客户端数据分布异质性模型下，以累积剩余的逐轮上界刻画联邦学习的路径性能；分析限制每轮贡献上限这一自然补救措施的效果；进而提出一种结合短期参与保证与个性化模型评估的新设计，并给出理论依据与实证验证。

Result: 客户端可能在数百轮内持续低于本地训练基线；对每轮贡献设上限的补救方案会适得其反，即使在低到中等异质性下也会导致学习崩溃；新设计使客户端避免短期损失且不损害整体性能，在中等异质性下表现良好，在严重异质性下相较本地基线仍有前景，但需付出一定精度代价。

Conclusion: 仅靠渐近均衡保证不足以保障联邦学习的个体理性，短期逐轮性能同样关键；限制贡献上限不可行。结合短期参与保证与个性化模型评估的设计能在维持公平激励的同时实现短期无损，为异质性环境下满足IR提供了可行方向。

Abstract: Participation in federated learning (FL) comes at a cost. Clients trade off privacy, communication, and compute costs for potentially greater gains in model efficacy. This paper explores this tradeoff under the aegis of individual rationality (IR) versus autarky, the basic game-theoretic requirement that the federation provide utility no worse than local training. Using the above as the design target, we examine pathwise performance of FL, as a per-round bound on cumulative surplus, not just as an asymptotic equilibrium guarantee under different models of client data distribution heterogeneity. Along this path, clients can remain below their local-training baseline for hundreds of rounds. The natural remedy is to cap each client's per-round contribution so that this shortfall stays bounded, and we prove that it backfires, collapsing learning even at low-to-modest heterogeneity.
  We then propose a novel design that combines short-term participation guarantees with personalized model evaluation, while maintaining fair incentives. We provide a theoretical basis for this new approach and empirically demonstrate that clients can avoid short-term losses without harming overall performance, even under moderate data distribution heterogeneity; under severe heterogeneity, the design shows promising outcomes for clients compared to their local baseline at some cost in accuracy.

</details>


### [126] [Diagnosing Temporal Misalignment in Multichannel Time-Series Classification with Minimum Description Length](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14595)
*Sebastian Buschjäger,Michael Frichert,Daniel Kuhe,Jian-Jia Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于最小描述长度（MDL）的、与分类器和标签无关的诊断方法，用于检测多通道时间序列中的相对时间错位，并在9个真实数据集上验证其能揭示并修正部署漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多通道时间序列分类通常假设传感器流已同步，但延迟、时钟漂移和预处理会引入相对延迟。现有同步方案多依赖硬件且难以回溯应用，导致同步问题常被忽视、分类性能欠佳。

Method: 对传感器分组施加候选时间平移，利用MDL衡量某组通过其余通道表示的编码效率；编码长度升高说明平移破坏了共享时间结构，最小值对应数据支持最强的对齐。该方法无需重训练、无需可信对齐参考。

Result: 在两个受控合成任务和九个真实数据集上，该指标能暴露对齐结构，并在诱导部署漂移下恢复精度；全数据集审计在FordChallenge、Opportunity、PAMAP2、UCIActivity等基准中发现稳定的非零MDL最优值，提示潜在系统性偏移。

Conclusion: 该方法为时间序列学习流程中检测、理解与校正时间错位提供了通用工具，代码已开源。

Abstract: Multichannel time-series classification commonly assumes synchronized sensor streams, although latency, clock drift, and preprocessing can introduce relative delays during data collection or after deployment. Existing synchronization solutions are often hardware-specific and difficult to apply retrospectively. Consequently, synchronization problems may remain undetected while classification performance is suboptimal. We introduce a classifier- and label-free diagnostic based on minimum description length (MDL). Our method applies candidate temporal shifts to sensor groups and measures how efficiently one group can be encoded through a representation of the remaining channels. An increased codelength indicates that the shift destroys shared temporal structure, whereas the minimum identifies the alignment most strongly supported by the data. Unlike learned synchronization methods, the diagnostic requires neither retraining nor a trusted aligned reference and can therefore test both training and deployment data for misalignments. Experiments on two controlled synthetic tasks and nine real-world datasets show that the metric exposes alignment structure and can recover accuracy under induced deployment drift. A whole-dataset audit further identifies stable nonzero MDL optima in established benchmarks including FordChallenge, Opportunity, PAMAP2, and UCIActivity, revealing potential systematic offsets that conventional model evaluation does not expose. Our method thus provides a general-purpose tool for detecting, understanding, and correcting temporal misalignment throughout the time-series learning pipeline. Our code is available under https://github.com/sbuschjaeger/mdl-temporal-misalignment.

</details>


### [127] [SH-WRNN: Implicit Spherical Harmonics Weight Field Routing Neural Networks for Asymmetric Edge Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14614)
*Zhibin Jiao,Xiangjing An*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出SH-WRNN，将神经网络权重矩阵由离散参数改为由球谐函数控制的连续可微场，通过经纬度路由动态提取权重，并在MNIST上验证了性能与推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统深度学习架构建立在全连接层之上，权重为大量静态离散参数，网络规模增大时参数数量与显存带宽开销剧增，鲜有工作挑战这一基础范式，作者希望从根本上重塑权重表示方式。

Method: 将核心突触权重矩阵从静态离散参数转化为由球谐函数控制的连续可微场。提出Implicit Spherical Harmonics Weight Field Routing Neural Network（SH-WRNN）：权重被约束在连续参数场中，取当前层权重时，在由连续场映射得到的矩形平面上用经纬度定位连接参数，纬度坐标由前一层激活神经元指定，经度坐标由前一层激活经矩阵乘法生成的键确定，通过评估该映射上的交点即时动态提取连接权重。此外提出非对称Surface Baking方案，收敛后将连续权重场一次性烘焙为静态参数曲面。

Result: 在MNIST上，紧凑配置(32, 10, 10)和(32, 3, 10)下单训练epoch分别达到91.05%和81.54%的稳健准确率。Surface Baking方案在推理阶段消除解析球谐计算，将动态矩阵提取降为高速局部内存切片，实现非对称算法加速且精度损失可忽略。

Conclusion: 该范式绕开GPU显存带宽垄断，为重塑CPU计算优势开辟了新路径，代码已开源。

Abstract: Deep learning architectures remain rigidly built upon traditional fully connected layers. While networks scale up, few challenge this foundational root. In this work, we reshape this paradigm by transforming the core synapse weight matrix from static, discrete parameters into a differentiable, continuous field governed by spherical harmonics functions. We introduce the Implicit Spherical Harmonics Weight Field Routing Neural Network (SH-WRNN), which constrains weight matrices within a continuous parametric field instead of optimizing millions of localized discrete weights. When retrieving the weight matrix of the current layer, connection parameters are localized using latitude and longitude on a rectangular plane mapped from the continuous field. The latitudinal coordinate is specified by activated neurons from the previous layer, while the longitudinal coordinate is determined by keys generated from previous layer activations via matrix multiplication. By evaluating intersections on this map, the network dynamically extracts its connection weights on-the-fly. Empirical validation on MNIST demonstrates that under compact configurations of (32, 10, 10) and (32, 3, 10), SH-WRNN achieves robust accuracies of 91.05% and 81.54% within a single training epoch. Furthermore, we propose an asymmetric Surface Baking scheme. Upon convergence, the continuous weight field is baked once into a static parametric surface. By eliminating analytical spherical harmonics calculations during inference and reducing dynamic matrix extraction to high-speed localized memory slicing, this scheme achieves asymmetric algorithmic acceleration with negligible accuracy degradation. This paradigm shift bypasses GPU memory-bandwidth monopolies, opening a novel path to reshape the advantages of CPU computing. Code is available at https://github.com/jzb1111/SphericalHarmonyRoutedNeuralNetWork.

</details>


### [128] [Know When to Stop, Where to Restart: Accelerating Multi-Turn Agentic On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14636)
*Zhiyu Gui,Kexin Huang,Jia Guo,Junkang Wu,Zihao Wang,Zhiqiang Zhang,Jun Zhou,Jiancan Wu,Xiang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: STRIDE 通过自适应早停与前缀缓存，在策略蒸馏中实现数据驱动的课程学习，在保持或超越全轨迹 OPD 性能的同时获得显著加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: on-policy distillation (OPD) 的成本主要来自学生模型的自回归 rollout，在多轮智能体场景下开销巨大。现有加速方法依据固定的离线预算截断或重定位监督信号，忽视了教师信号可靠性在轨迹内部及轨迹之间的显著差异。

Method: 作者在 τ²-bench 上的实证分析发现：有信息的监督集中在每一轮的前缀部分，且教师认可度的跨轮损失在时间上锁定于学生的首个错误动作，而非随轮次逐渐累积。据此提出 STRIDE，包含两项互补技术：(1) 自适应早停，当累积教师对数概率低于分布外阈值时终止 rollout；(2) 前缀缓存，缓存高质量前缀并在最弱的正确轮次处重新开始生成。二者共同构成数据驱动的课程，逐步扩展对后续轮次的覆盖。

Result: 在 τ²-bench retail 上，STRIDE 以 3.73× 加速比匹配全轨迹 OPD 并超越 30B 教师；以 2.34× 加速比超越基线本身；在跨域多教师训练下保持 4.51× 加速比。在 AIME 2025 上以 5.10× 加速比、AIME 2024 上以 3.08× 加速比优于全 OPD；两项评测平均而言，固定预算截断基线均低于全 OPD。

Conclusion: STRIDE 利用教师信号可靠性的结构性规律实现高效 on-policy 蒸馏，在智能体与非智能体任务上均取得加速与性能双重收益，优于固定预算截断基线。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) has become a standard approach for transferring capabilities from large teachers to compact students. Its cost, however, is dominated by autoregressive student rollouts and scales poorly in multi-turn agentic settings. Existing acceleration methods truncate or relocate the supervision signal according to fixed, offline budgets, despite substantial variation in teacher-signal reliability both within and across trajectories. Our empirical analysis on $τ^2$-bench reveals a clear structure in this variation: informative supervision is concentrated in the prefix of each turn, and, most importantly for multi-turn agentic training, the cross-turn loss of teacher endorsement is temporally locked to the student's first erroneous action rather than accumulating gradually over turns. Building on these findings, we propose STRIDE (Stop-and-Restart on-policy Distillation acceleration), which combines two complementary techniques: adaptive early stopping, which terminates a rollout once the cumulative teacher log-probability falls below an out-of-distribution threshold, and a prefix buffer, which caches high-quality prefixes and restarts generation at the weakest correct turn. Together, these mechanisms induce a data-driven curriculum that progressively extends coverage to later turns. On $τ^2$-bench retail, our method matches full-trajectory OPD and exceeds the 30B teacher at a $3.73\times$ speedup, surpasses the baseline itself at $2.34\times$, and retains a $4.51\times$ speedup under cross-domain multi-teacher training. As a supplementary generalization test beyond the agentic setting, STRIDE outperforms full OPD on AIME 2025 at a $5.10\times$ speedup and on AIME 2024 at a $3.08\times$ speedup; averaged across the two evaluations, both fixed-budget truncation baselines remain below full OPD.

</details>


### [129] [Symmetries and Singularities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14663)
*Vishnu Varadarajan,Mihir More,Aritra Das,Debayan Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对深度神经网络过度参数化导致有效复杂度难以度量的问题，利用图注意力模型中的图结构与注意力参数对称性，在教师-学生框架下建立解析框架，推导出考虑对称性诱导简并后的局部学习系数（LLC）显式估计，从而降低LLC估计的计算成本并提升可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络高度过度参数化，不同参数取值可实现相同的预测函数，因此仅凭参数量或Hessian秩难以度量其有效复杂度。奇异学习理论通过局部学习系数（LLC）刻画模型在给定解附近的有效复杂度，但现有LLC估计方法多依赖后验采样，对大型神经网络而言计算开销巨大，难以大规模精确估计。作者希望借助模型已知结构简化分析，使LLC估计更易处理。

Method: 研究图注意力模型的LLC，通过利用图结构与注意力参数两方面的对称性来简化分析；建立教师-学生（teacher-student）设定下的解析框架，并显式考虑由对称性诱导的简并（degeneracy）对LLC估计的影响，从而给出解析化的LLC估计。

Result: 提出了一个教师-学生设定下的解析框架，并在考虑对称性诱导简并后，得到了图注意力模型局部学习系数（LLC）的显式估计，避免了依赖昂贵的后验采样。

Conclusion: 利用模型内部已知的对称结构能够有效简化LLC分析，使图注意力模型的LLC估计更具可处理性和可扩展性，为大规模神经网络有效复杂度的理论与实证度量提供了一条新途径。

Abstract: Deep neural networks are highly over-parameterized, and different parameter values represent the same predictive function. This makes their effective complexity difficult to measure using only the number of parameters or the rank of the Hessian. Singular Learning Theory addresses this issue through the local learning coefficient (LLC), which characterizes the effective complexity of a model near a given solution. Existing methods for estimating the LLC often rely on posterior sampling, which can be computationally expensive for large neural networks. This makes accurate LLC estimation difficult at scale. In this work, we use known structures in the model to simplify the analysis and make LLC estimation more tractable. Specifically, we study the LLC of a graph attention model by exploiting symmetries in both the graph structure and the attention parameters. An analytic framework through a teacher--student setting, and explicit LLC estimates after considering the symmetry--induced degeneracies are developed.

</details>


### [130] [WaterKron and FlipFlop Hessian: Information-Theoretically Grounded Quantization with Kronecker-factored Hessians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14706)
*Johann Birnick,Rayan Saab*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出WaterKron方法，将双面GPTQ、行列相关注水缩放与熵编码结合，并引入Kronecker-Hessian失配因子Φ来指导后训练量化中Kronecker分解Hessian的最优选取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后训练量化中如何选取Kronecker分解的Hessian近似缺乏理论依据，作者希望为Hessian因子的选择提供率失真角度的理论准则。

Method: 将双面GPTQ与行列相关的waterfilling缩放及熵编码结合形成WaterKron；针对完整Hessian推导高码率失真，并定义显式的Kronecker-Hessian失配因子Φ；通过最小化Φ得到Gaussian协方差拟合问题，使用经典"flip-flop"迭代求解，从而得到FlipFlop Hessian。

Result: Φ量化了Kronecker Hessian近似带来的渐近失真惩罚；最小化Φ对应于Gaussian协方差拟合问题。实验显示FlipFlop Hessian在KL散度和困惑度上持续优于input-only、marginal和Frobenius等Hessian选择方案。

Conclusion: 研究为使用FlipFlop Hessian进行量化提供了率失真理论依据，并通过实验验证其优越性。

Abstract: How should a Kronecker-factored Hessian approximation be chosen for post-training quantization? We address this question through WaterKron, which combines two-sided GPTQ with row- and column-dependent waterfilling scales and entropy coding. We derive its high-rate distortion with respect to the full Hessian using an explicit Kronecker-Hessian mismatch factor $Φ$. This factor quantifies the asymptotic distortion penalty due to the Kronecker Hessian approximation and provides a criterion for selecting the factors optimally. Minimizing $Φ$ leads to a Gaussian covariance-fitting problem with classical ``flip-flop'' updates. We thus give a rate-distortion justification for using the resulting FlipFlop Hessian in quantization. We evaluate it empirically, finding that the FlipFlop Hessian consistently improves KL divergence and perplexity over input-only, marginal, and Frobenius-based Hessian choices.

</details>


### [131] [An immune world model for multiscale forecasting and therapeutic hypothesis generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14709)
*Taoyong Cui,Xi Wang,Zonghang Li,Jinchao Ding,Lingsen You,Yuzhi Xu,Wanghan Xu,Fang Wu,Kejun Ying,Wanli Ouyang,Pheng Ann Heng,Ling Yang,Zhenfei Yin,Yingcheng Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究构建了免疫世界模型（Immune World Model），一个受调控的进化式AI科学家搜索出的、以干预为条件的多尺度免疫状态预测模型，并在冻结后经独立验证，用于干预预测与治疗假设生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 免疫治疗作用跨细胞内在程序、组织生态与患者特异性免疫状态等多个尺度，而现有预测器多将这些尺度分开处理，缺乏统一的、以干预为条件的跨尺度建模框架。

Method: 采用受治理的进化式AI科学家（governed evolutionary AI Scientist）搜索候选架构与工作流，构建动作条件化的免疫世界模型，学习干预如何在细胞、组织与个体层面移动免疫状态；模型在独立确认前被冻结，并进行受治理的自我纠错审计。

Result: 冻结模型可泛化到未见干预与生物背景，恢复干预特异性细胞程序，整合细胞与组织信息以提升生态与患者反应预测，并预测未见扰动组合；模型引导分析提名IL-36γ联合SIRPα抑制为互补轴治疗假设，而自我纠错审计否决了所有筛选的细胞因子配对。

Conclusion: 免疫世界模型提供了一个多尺度免疫模拟框架，将AI科学家驱动的模型构建、干预预测与可前瞻性检验的治疗假设生成相连接。

Abstract: Immune therapies act across cell-intrinsic programs, tissue ecosystems, and patient-specific immune states, yet most predictors address these scales separately. We used a governed evolutionary AI Scientist to construct the Immune World Model, an action-conditioned model that learns how interventions move immune states across cellular, tissue, and individual levels. The Immune World Model--building Scientist searched candidate architectures and workflows, and the resulting world model was frozen before independent confirmation. The frozen model generalized to unseen interventions and biological contexts, recovered intervention-specific cellular programs, integrated cell and tissue information to improve ecosystem and patient-response prediction, and forecast unseen perturbation combinations. Immune World Model--guided analysis then combined measured perturbations with cross-axis inference to nominate IL-36$γ$ plus SIRP$α$ inhibition as a complementary-axis therapeutic hypothesis, whereas a governed self-correction audit rejected every screened cytokine pair. The Immune World Model provides a framework for multiscale immune simulation that connects AI Scientist-driven model construction, intervention forecasting, and the generation of prospectively testable therapeutic hypotheses.

</details>


### [132] [GRPO-QM: Target Preserving Exploration for Quantum Tomography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14711)
*Yufeng Wang,Parivesh Priye,Lu Wei,Haibin Ling*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该工作提出GRPO-QM：仅学习量子层析后验的探索策略，用精确Metropolis校正保证后验平稳；并系统诊断"学习"相对物理提议与先验的增益来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奖励式学习可能改变科学推断所要估计的后验分布本身。作者希望避免这种分布被优化目标扭曲，同时厘清"学习"在量子层析中究竟贡献了多少、增益来自何处，以及采样训练与精确训练的差距成因。

Method: GRPO-QM：组相对策略在可逆物理移动间选择探索动作，策略固定后由精确Metropolis校正确保目标后验保持平稳。随后进行三项诊断：与流模型的重建对比、受控初始化的奖励对比、以及构造闭式反例。最后在45个可枚举后验、每个3个训练种子上，比较同一条轨迹目标的精确梯度与采样梯度，并做目标缩放消融。

Result: 随测试流相对混合提议的增益主要来自物理提议机制与先验知识，而非学习本身。闭式反例表明：与接受移动相关的奖励可以在物理观测量仍高度相关时继续上升。控制初始化后，跨后验聚合会使奖励所隐含的单后验排序发生反转。在45个后验上，精确与采样梯度相对调优混合分别降低平均物理估计方差12.63%与6.32%，而对轨迹分数仅做平均、不重标定惩罚项只得0.43%。收益集中在4 shots处，到16 shots时符号反转。

Conclusion: 精确、可见的bank诊断给出一个可复现的采样训练失效模式，指出目标缩放修正能解决其中一部分差距，并量化了剩余差距，说明精确训练与采样训练的部分差异源于目标缩放。

Abstract: Reward-based learning can alter the very posterior distribution that scientific inference aims to estimate. GRPO-QM sidesteps this by learning only an exploration strategy for a stated quantum-tomography posterior: a group-relative policy chooses among reversible physical moves, and an exact Metropolis correction ensures the posterior remains stationary once the policy is fixed. We then examine what learning contributes beyond physical proposal mechanisms and prior knowledge. Reconstruction comparisons suggest that most of the gains over the tested flows come from those two components rather than from learning itself, and a closed-form counterexample explains why: a reward tied to accepted motion can increase even when a physical observable remains highly correlated. Reward comparisons that control for initialization also show that aggregating across posteriors can invert the ranking that a reward implies within any single posterior. Lastly, on 45 enumerated posteriors with three training seeds each, exact and sampled gradients of the same trajectory objective reduce mean physical estimation variance by $12.63\%$ and $6.32\%$ relative to a tuned mixture, while averaging the trajectory score without rescaling its penalty yields only $0.43\%$. This indicates that objective scaling accounts for part of the disparity between sampled and exact training. The regained benefit is concentrated at four shots and flips sign at sixteen, so these exact, visible-bank diagnostics identify a concrete, reproducible failure mode in sampled training, the objective-scaling fix that addresses part of it, and the remaining gap.

</details>


### [133] [Carryover Drafting: Recycling Rejected States for Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14717)
*Jahyun Koo,Sunghyeon Woo,Jaeeun Kil,Jeongtae Lee,Sungjae Lee,Kyomin Jung,Minsub Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Carryover Drafting，将推测解码中被拒绝token的验证器隐藏状态回收为临时KV上下文供草稿模型关注，并设计并行draft–verify–draft训练以对齐推理时状态，从而提升接受长度与端到端加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推测解码中验证步骤会为被接受和被拒绝的token都计算表示，但传统草稿模型只保留被接受token的表示，导致验证器在被拒绝token上的大量计算被浪费。作者发现这些被丢弃的隐藏状态包含对未来token有用的信息，可用于改进后续草稿，但利用该机会面临推理时回收开销增加草稿延迟、训练时标准并行训练无法产生与推理对齐的被拒绝状态两大挑战。

Method: Carryover Drafting将验证器对被拒绝token的隐藏状态回收为临时KV上下文，使草稿模型可选择性地关注它们；复用草稿模型现有接口，仅增加一个学习嵌入以区分被拒绝状态与已提交上下文；额外KV上下文每轮草稿替换，长度限制在一个proposal block内。同时提出并行draft–verify–draft训练，在保持各训练位置并行性的同时让草稿模型接触与推理对齐的被拒绝状态。

Result: 在DFlash及一个源自DSpark的半自回归草稿模型、两个目标模型上的实验表明，该机制相对对应基线将平均接受长度提升6.5–14.7%，端到端vLLM加速提升7.9–14.4%，在翻译任务上加速增益达28.8%。

Conclusion: 通过回收被拒绝token的验证器隐藏状态作为有界临时KV上下文，并配合推理对齐的并行训练，可在不显著增加延迟的情况下有效提升推测解码的接受长度与端到端推理速度，且方法通用、改动简单。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates LLM inference by verifying multiple drafted tokens in parallel, allowing a single target forward pass to accept several tokens. By construction, verification computes representations for both accepted and rejected tokens. Yet, conventional drafters retain only the representations of accepted tokens, leaving the substantial verifier computation spent on rejected tokens effectively wasted. We find that these discarded hidden states generated during target forward retain useful information about future tokens that can improve subsequent drafts. However, realizing this opportunity poses two distinct challenges. At inference, recycling overhead can increase drafting latency, diminishing the speedup gained from increased acceptance length. During training, standard parallel drafter training does not produce inference-aligned rejected states, while obtaining them through sequential rollouts would sacrifice parallelism across training positions. We introduce Carryover Drafting, which addresses both challenges. Carryover recycles rejected target hidden states as temporary KV context, allowing the drafter to selectively attend to them. It reuses the drafter's existing interface and adds only a single learned embedding to distinguish rejected states from committed context. The additional KV context is replaced each drafting round, keeping its length bounded by one proposal block. We introduce parallel draft--verify--draft training that exposes the drafter to inference-aligned rejected states while preserving parallelism across training positions. Experiments with DFlash and a DSpark-derived semi-autoregressive drafter across two target models show that this simple Carryover mechanism improves average acceptance length by 6.5--14.7% and end-to-end vLLM speedup by 7.9--14.4% over the corresponding baselines, with speedup gains reaching 28.8% on translation.

</details>


### [134] [Are Gradient Boosting Models Suitable for Intermittent Demand Forecasting?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14718)
*Vladislav Kislinskii,Mazhar Hameed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探讨梯度提升模型在间歇性需求预测中的表现。评估统计、专门化、机器学习与集成方法后发现：专门化方法在单一模型中表现最佳，梯度提升单独使用往往表现不佳，但将机器学习模型与专门化方法组合可将预测精度提升最高10%，说明简单集成即可超越单一模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 间歇性需求（需求发生频率低、时间序列含大量零值观测）在工业组织、消费品、航空、汽车与电子等多个领域普遍存在，此类需求的预测极具挑战。作者希望借助梯度提升模型提升这一场景下的预测性能，并探究机器学习与领域专门方法结合的价值。

Method: 在多个数据集上系统评估四类方法：统计方法、专门化方法（针对间歇性需求设计）、机器学习方法以及集成方法；重点考察梯度提升模型的表现，并测试其与专门化方法的组合集成。

Result: 在单一模型比较中，专门化方法表现最强；梯度提升单独使用往往表现不佳，低于专门化方法。但将机器学习模型与专门化方法结合后，预测精度最高提升约10%，表明即便简单的集成也能优于单一模型。

Conclusion: 对于间歇性需求预测，将机器学习与领域特定的预测技术相结合具有显著价值；简单集成即可带来精度增益，提示实践中应优先考虑混合集成策略而非单纯依赖梯度提升。

Abstract: Demand forecasting is critical in modern industry, offering opportunities to reduce costs and gain competitive advantage through improved inventory management. However, forecasting becomes particularly challenging for products with intermittent demand, where demand occurs infrequently and time series contain many zero observations. Such dynamics are common across diverse sectors, such as industrial organizations, consumer goods, aviation, automotive, and electronics. Motivated by these challenges, this paper explores the potential of gradient boosting models to improve forecasting performance. We evaluate statistical, specialized, machine learning, and ensemble approaches across multiple datasets. The results show that specialized methods achieve the strongest performance among individual models, while gradient boosting on its own tends to underperform. However, combining a machine learning model with a specialized approach improves forecasting accuracy by up to 10%, demonstrating that even simple ensembles can outperform single models. Overall, the findings highlight the value of combining machine learning with domain-specific forecasting techniques for intermittent demand.

</details>


### [135] [WaVeFuse: Regime-Adaptive Equity Index Forecasting via Channel-Wise Wavelet Denoising and Vertical Attention Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14733)
*Aashish Bohra,Vivek Vijay*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: WaVeFuse：小波去噪+多尺度时频编码+垂直注意力融合的双分支深度模型，用于股指预测，兼顾精度、稳健性与轻量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有混合深度学习股指预测存在三个瓶颈：(1) OHLCV噪声经技术指标传播放大；(2) 通道不加区分的多尺度分解混淆了异质频率特征；(3) 静态多分支融合无法适应市场状态切换。

Method: 统一双分支架构：Symlet-4小波去噪（2层、MAD软阈值）抑制OHLCV微结构噪声；由去噪价格计算7个低滞后技术指标，经因果通道级连续小波变换（Morlet，32尺度）编码为逐时刻尺度-空间矩阵；CNN-BiLSTM分支建模时序动态，双层Transformer（4头，dk∈{16,32}）建模跨尺度频谱依赖；两分支由2-token softmax门控的垂直注意力融合（VAF）动态重加权，以适应市场状态变化。

Result: 在KOSPI、DAX、NYSE Composite、Russell 2000（2010-2023）滚动前向验证下，R²=0.81-0.96，方向准确率70.5-78.3%；在12个数据集-周期配置上MAE较7个SOTA模型低8.9-20.2%；DM统计量4.62-10.38（p<0.001）优于调优XGBoost；消融验证各组件贡献；含10bp交易成本的回测中，四市场平均Sharpe=3.69，COVID-19冲击下最大回撤限制在7.5%；仅152k参数（0.68MB），GPU推理低于1.3ms。

Conclusion: WaVeFuse通过去噪、因果多尺度时频编码与动态分支融合，实现了计算高效、状态稳健的股指预测框架，适用于研究与决策支持部署。

Abstract: Hybrid Deep Learning for equity index forecasting is limited by three problems: propagation of OHLCV noise into derived technical indicators (TIs), channel-indiscriminate multi-scale decomposition that conflates heterogeneous frequency signatures, and static multi-branch fusion that cannot adapt to market regime shifts. WaVeFuse addresses these limitations through a unified dual-branch architecture. Symlet-4 wavelet denoising (level 2, MAD soft threshold) suppresses microstructure noise in OHLCV. Seven low-lag TIs computed from denoised prices are encoded by a causal channel-wise continuous wavelet transform (Morlet, 32 scales) into a per-timestep scale-space matrix. A CNN-BiLSTM branch captures temporal dynamics, while a dual-layer Transformer (heads=4, dk in {16, 32}) models inter-scale spectral dependencies, and their representations are integrated by a 2-token softmax gate Vertical Attention Fusion (VAF) that dynamically reweights branches as market regimes shift. Evaluated under walk-forward validation (WFV) on KOSPI, DAX, NYSE Composite, and Russell 2000 (2010-2023), WaVeFuse achieves R2 = 0.81-0.96 and directional accuracy 70.5-78.3%. It outperforms seven state-of-the-art models by 8.9-20.2% MAE across twelve dataset-period configurations. Diebold-Mariano statistics (4.62-10.38, p<0.001) confirm superiority over a well-tuned XGBoost benchmark across four indices. Ablation verifies component-wise contributions. Under realistic backtesting with 10 basis point transaction costs, WaVeFuse's directional strategy achieves a mean Sharpe ratio of 3.69 across four markets and limits maximum drawdown to 7.5% during the COVID-19 crash. With 152k parameters (0.68MB) and sub-1.3ms GPU inference, WaVeFuse delivers a computationally efficient, regime-robust framework suitable for research and decision-support deployment.

</details>


### [136] [OCT-FedSIR: Toward Trustworthy Federated Ophthalmic Learning under Annotation Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14734)
*Sina Gholami,Abdulmoneam Ali,Tania Haghighi,Rashadul H. Badhon,Behafarin Emam,Sally S. Y. Ong,Atalie C. Thompson,Theodore Leng,Ahmed Arafa,Jennifer I. Lim,Minhaj Nur Alam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出OCT-FedSIR可靠性感知谱框架，在客户端相关标注噪声与非IID异质下实现联邦OCT分类，平均准确率86.73%，优于RoFL与FedCorr，并可识别与纠正损坏标注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习可避免集中患者数据，但参与机构的标注可靠性无法保证；眼科影像中疾病患病率与类别构成差异易与损坏监督所致变化混淆，因此需要可靠性感知的联邦分类方法。

Method: OCT-FedSIR整合类别均衡谱估计、Stage-I logit调整、互补谱描述子、选择性谱重标注与噪声感知联邦优化；在Kermany、UIC与Wake Forest数据集上，评测试对性称噪声、结构化非对称噪声及三种非IID程度。

Result: 在117种实验条件下平均准确率86.73%（RoFL 79.94%、FedCorr 78.75%）；所有条件下均正确区分原始与损坏标注客户端，原始FedSIR识别在非对称噪声下稳健性不足；谱重标注恢复77.2%损坏标注，纠正精度91.3%，误纠率3.5%；保留已纠正客户端较谱剪枝平均高9.30个百分点。

Conclusion: 标注噪声通常可被识别并纠正，无需丢弃具信息量的客户端数据；OCT-FedSIR在异构与非对称噪声下具有更高的准确率与稳健性。

Abstract: Federated learning enables collaborative model development without centralizing patient data, but annotation reliability at participating institutions cannot always be assumed. In ophthalmic imaging, differences in disease prevalence and class composition can resemble changes caused by corrupted supervision. We introduce OCT-FedSIR, a reliability-aware spectral framework for federated OCT classification under client-dependent annotation noise and heterogeneous data distributions. OCT-FedSIR combines class-balanced spectral estimation, Stage-I logit adjustment, complementary spectral descriptors, selective spectral relabeling, and noise-aware federated optimization. We evaluated the framework on the Kermany, University of Illinois Chicago, and Wake Forest datasets under symmetric and structured asymmetric noise and three levels of non-IID heterogeneity. Across 117 experimental conditions, OCT-FedSIR achieved a mean accuracy of 86.73%, compared with 79.94% for RoFL and 78.75% for FedCorr. It correctly separated clients with original and corrupted annotations across all evaluated conditions, while the original FedSIR identification procedure was less robust, particularly under asymmetric noise. Spectral relabeling recovered 77.2% of corrupted annotations with 91.3% correction precision and a 3.5% false-correction rate. Retaining corrected clients outperformed spectral pruning by 9.30 percentage points on average. These findings show that annotation noise can often be identified and corrected without discarding informative client data.

</details>


### [137] [Calibrating Interpretability Instruments Before Trusting Their Verdicts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14754)
*Orion Reblitz-Richardson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文指出大模型可解释性中的因果测量存在六种"看似合理却错误"的失效模式，并给出每种模式的识别特征、触发条件与对应协议，最终归纳为四项测量纪律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关于大语言模型内部机制的因果主张依赖各类测量（投影、余弦、消融差值、互换补丁等），但这些测量会以特定且可诊断的方式失效——返回一个貌似合理的数值而非报错，导致"坏仪器"被误读为"真发现"。作者意在系统记录并预防此类误判。

Method: 在拒答与道德表征的因果可解释性研究项目中，跨多篇论文与四模型开放权重面板，识别并刻画六种测量失效模式；为每种模式给出可捕获它的"破绽"（tell），以及由可检测触发条件（重排归一化、massive activations、低维决策通道）驱动的协议。

Result: 六种失效模式：①协方差匹配零假设饱和，使任何方向都看似典型；②在重排归一化架构上，逐头归因会超出真实残差写入三倍；③互换补丁因结果被钉在天花板而出现符号混乱；④read-from结论可能只是"越过模型已决策层继续测量"的假象；每种模式在四模型中的一到两个上得到确立。

Conclusion: 测量纪律归结为四步：用正控阶梯校准、用正交单元验证、在花费算力前先计算统计功效、每个read-from结论都须标注相对于模型承诺深度的读数位置。证据限于单一项目内三家族四架构，跨项目外部复现为未来工作。

Abstract: Causal claims about large language model (LLM) internals rest on measurements. Those might include a projection, a cosine, an ablation delta, or an interchange patch among others. These measurements fail in specific, diagnosable ways that return a plausible number instead of an error, so a broken instrument can easily read as a finding. A covariance-matched null can saturate until every direction looks typical, a per-head attribution can overshoot the true residual write threefold on reordered-normalization architectures, an interchange patch can go sign-chaotic because its outcome is pinned at a ceiling, or a read-from verdict can be an artifact of measuring past the layer where the model already decided. This note documents six such failures from a causal interpretability program on refusal and moral representation, spanning several papers and a four-model open-weight panel; each mode is established on one or two of the four. For each we give the tell that catches it and a protocol keyed to a detectable trigger (reordered normalization, massive activations, a low-dimensional decision channel), so we and readers can check whether a given setup is exposed. The discipline reduces to four moves: calibrate against a positive-control ladder, certify with an orthogonal cell, compute power before spending compute, and state every read-from verdict at a depth referenced to the model's commitment. The evidence is four architectures across three families within a single program; external replication across programs is future work.

</details>


### [138] [Refusal Reads Only a Slice of What the Model Knows: Harm-Keyed Routing and Its Exceptions Across Model Families](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14759)
*Orion Reblitz-Richardson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 对齐后的拒绝行为只读取一个低秩的"危害方向"，而非模型内化的道德内容：道德子空间在预训练中形成、对齐仅旋转不重建；拒绝门是后训练新建的窄通道，约四分之三的因果输入来自道德子空间之外。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究表明对齐后的拒绝行为可被残差流中的单一方向编辑移除，但这只说明了"拒绝多容易被消除"，并未回答拒绝决策实际读取的是什么表征，也未将其与模型的道德理解能力区分开。作者希望厘清拒绝行为所依赖的因果输入来源。

Method: 在三个家族共四个开放权重模型上比较：追踪道德子空间与拒绝门在预训练/后训练中的形成过程；核心因果实验用OLMo-3做嵌套互换秩扫描（nested interchange rank sweep），在匹配请求间逐层替换道德子空间中越来越大的子切片，测量拒绝响应传递的比例；并跨模型家族对比读取内容。

Result: 道德理解是预训练原生能力，低秩道德子空间在预训练中固化，对齐只做一次旋转而不重建；拒绝门是后训练新建、预训练前驱微弱，写入狭窄的控制token通道，且拒绝决策与道德判断决策正交。随子空间基扩大，道德判断持续读取更多内容，而拒绝在单一危害方向处饱和，约3/4的拒绝因果输入位于道德子空间之外。跨家族不一致：Llama读取宽泛道德内容；Qwen读取超出单一危害线索但样本量下未定论；GPT-OSS读取危害，其拒绝可被自身推理链朝任一方向论证。

Conclusion: 拒绝行为绕过模型所知的大部分内容、只读取低秩危害切片，因此秩一编辑即可移除拒绝。留下的开放问题是：扩大拒绝所读取的内容是否也会深化该行为（即让拒绝更稳健）。

Abstract: Alignment applied after pretraining is shallow in a measurable way: a single direction in a model's residual stream can be edited out, and the model stops refusing harmful requests. That fact says how easily refusal can be removed, not what the refusal decision was reading in the first place. We ask what it reads, and we separate that from what the model comprehends. Across four open-weight models spanning three families, moral comprehension is native to pretraining: a low-rank moral subspace crystallizes during pretraining, and alignment rotates it once without rebuilding it. The refusal gate, in contrast, is a fresh post-training construction with only a weak pretraining precursor, written into a narrow control-token channel where the refusal decision is orthogonal to the moral-judgment decision. The central result is causal and comes from one model, OLMo-3. A nested interchange rank sweep patches successively larger slices of the moral subspace between matched requests and reads how much of refusal's response transfers: as the basis widens, moral judgment keeps reading more of it, while refusal levels off at the level of a single harm direction, and about three-quarters of refusal's causal input lies outside the moral subspace altogether. Refusal reads the harm percept, not the moral content that judgment reads on the same patches. The picture is not uniform across families. Llama reads broad moral content; Qwen reads beyond the single harm cue but is unresolved at our sample size; GPT-OSS reads harm, and its refusals can be argued in either direction by its own reasoning trace. Where refusal reads only a low-rank slice and routes around the bulk of what the model knows, a rank-one edit removes it. Whether widening what refusal reads would also deepen the behavior is the open question this raises.

</details>


### [139] [Privacy Preserving Gossip Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14778)
*Erkan Bayram,Mohamed-Ali Belabbas,Tamer Başar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种去中心化隐私保护学习算法：每个智能体持有一个私有样本与共享模型，样本被顺序学习，每次更新需保护已学样本的端点映射。智能体兼具学习者、教师、被保护者三重角色；基于Tuning without Forgetting(TwF)保证映射保持与不可区分性，用极小极大最优控制刻画差分隐私噪声，并通过隐私push-sum gossip聚合投影，证明了gossip与分布式投影的几何收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在去中心化学习中，各智能体需在不泄露私有样本的前提下顺序学习共享模型，同时不能破坏此前已学样本的端点映射。现有方法难以兼顾隐私保护与已学映射的不遗忘保持，因此需要一种对学习者、教师和被保护智能体分别提供隐私与性能保障的机制。

Method: 1) 以每个智能体仅持一个私有样本、共享同一模型为设定，样本顺序学习；2) 基于Tuning without Forgetting(TwF)保持先前学到的端点映射；3) 对学习者：证明当被保护智能体集合中存在与当前样本同标签的另一样本时，TwF提供不可区分性保证；4) 对教师：将差分隐私噪声建模为最坏情况扰动，构造极小极大最优控制问题，在保持梯度隐私水平的同时防止性能损失；5) 对被保护智能体：本地计算投影并用隐私push-sum gossip协议聚合；6) 证明去中心化gossip算法及TwF分布式投影的几何收敛。

Result: 证明了TwF在学习者处提供不可区分性保证（条件为被保护集合含同标签样本）；给出了教师的极小极大最优控制建模，使隐私噪声不致引起性能损失且维持梯度级隐私；证明了去中心化push-sum gossip算法与TwF的分布式投影均具几何收敛性。

Conclusion: 该工作将隐私保护、遗忘抑制与去中心化优化统一在一个框架内，为每个智能体赋予学习者、教师与被保护者三重角色并分别提供保障，理论上给出了不可区分性与几何收敛性结果，实现了去中心化场景下的隐私保护顺序学习。

Abstract: We propose a decentralized privacy-preserving learning algorithm in which each agent holds a single private sample and a shared model. Samples are learned sequentially, and each update must preserve the endpoint mappings at previously learned samples while protecting private data. This gives each agent three roles: (i) a learner that updates the model parameters, (ii) a teacher whose sample is learned at the current iteration, and (iii) a protected agent whose sample has already been learned. We build on Tuning without Forgetting (TwF) method to preserve previously learned mappings and show that TwF provides an indistinguishability guarantee for the learner whenever the set of protected agents contains another sample with the same label. For the teacher, we formulate a minimax optimal control problem that models the differential privacy noise as a worst-case disturbance to prevent performance loss while maintaining the same level of privacy for the gradient. For the protected agents, we compute the projections locally and aggregate them using a private push-sum gossip protocol. We prove geometric convergence of the decentralized gossip algorithm and of the distributed projection for TwF.

</details>


### [140] [Decision-Oriented Uncertainty Quantification for Risk Control in Earth System Spatiotemporal Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14821)
*Ji Lu,Huiran Duan,Bo Zhao,Xianglong Wang,Yiru Fang,Kuo Yang,Xiaoqin Feng,Jianping Gou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 面向地球系统时空基础模型，提出决策导向的不确定性量化框架：输出预测分布，经决策风险适配器映射为动作条件风险，并用效用感知校准在决策损失层面保证可靠性，从而直接支撑预警、调度与资源分配等风险敏感决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地球系统建模正从任务专用预测器转向具备通用时空表征能力的基础模型，可联合编码动态地球场、外部强迫与静态地理背景并做多步预报。但仅靠精确点预测或统计校准的区间，无法满足极端天气预警、防洪、可再生能源调度与应急资源分配等高影响应用。实际关键是：预测不确定性能否在特定行动、损失函数与风险偏好下转化为可靠的决策风险。

Method: 提出决策导向的不确定性量化框架：1）生成未来状态的预测分布；2）决策风险适配器将预测样本、决策情境与效用函数映射为动作条件风险；3）效用感知校准模块在下游决策损失层面（而非仅在预测值层面）施加可靠性约束；4）基于校准后的风险选择预警、调度、巡检或资源分配等行动。
