Result: 相较最强基线：决策后悔降低18.7%，漏报率由14.2%降至9.1%，期望效用提升11.6%，同时保持90.4%的预测覆盖率，并将决策校准误差由0.083降至0.047。

Conclusion: 决策导向的不确定性量化可提升地球系统基础模型在风险敏感应用中的稳健性与业务价值，说明不确定性应面向决策损失进行校准与利用，而非止步于预测精度或区间覆盖率。

Abstract: Earth system modeling is shifting from task-specific predictors toward foundation models with general spatiotemporal representation capabilities. Although these models can jointly encode dynamic Earth fields, external forcings, and static geographic context for multistep forecasting, accurate point predictions or statistically calibrated intervals alone are insufficient for high-impact applications such as extremeweather warning, flood control, renewable-energy dispatch, and emergency resource allocation. What matters in practice is whether predictive uncertainty can be translated into reliable decision risk under specific actions, loss functions, and risk preferences. We propose a decision-oriented uncertainty quantification framework for Earth system spatiotemporal foundation models. The framework produces predictive distributions of future states and uses a decision risk adapter to map forecast samples, decision context, and utility functions into action-conditional risks. A utility-aware calibration module further enforces reliability at the downstream decision-loss level rather than only at the forecast-value level. Calibrated risks are then used to select warning, dispatch, inspection, or resource-allocation actions. Compared with the strongest baseline, the proposed method reduces decision regret by 18.7%, lowers the missed-event rate from 14.2% to 9.1%, and improves expected utility by 11.6%, while maintaining 90.4% predictive coverage and reducing decision calibration error from 0.083 to 0.047. These results suggest that decision-oriented uncertainty quantification can improve the robustness and operational value of Earth system foundation models in risk-sensitive applications.

</details>


### [141] [Tackling Failure Modes of PINNs and PIKANs Using Conflict-Free Gradients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14841)
*Sidharth S. Menon,Irina Tezaur,Ameya D. Jagtap*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将区域分解与基于投影的梯度手术结合，提出归一化变体 Norm-PCGrad 缓解 PINNs 复合损失中的梯度冲突，并引入可分离架构降低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PINNs 等科学机器学习方法在复杂几何上求解 PDE 时 increasingly 依赖区域分解以提升可扩展性，但由此产生的由残差项、边界项与界面项构成的复合损失极易出现梯度冲突，导致训练退化。现有投影类梯度手术算法（PCGrad、ConFIG）在 3D 多重叠界面等场景下性能下降，需要更稳健的解决方案。

Method: 1) 系统评估两种基于投影的梯度手术算法 PCGrad 与 ConFIG，识别其在 3D 多重叠界面等场景下的性能退化；2) 提出归一化变体 Norm-PCGrad；3) 提出在 XPINN 等区域分解框架的选定子域中用 SPINN 等可分离架构替换原始 PINN，将计算复杂度由二次（或三次）降至线性；4) 将梯度手术扩展到 PIKANs。

Result: 在多种 2D 与 3D 区域分解基准上，Norm-PCGrad 相较无梯度手术训练以及 PCGrad、ConFIG 等现有算法均取得最低的相对 L2 误差，且额外计算开销可忽略；采用可分离架构有效降低计算成本；梯度手术在 PIKANs 上对 3D 区域分解带来显著精度提升。

Conclusion: 将区域分解与投影式梯度手术系统结合可有效缓解梯度冲突，Norm-PCGrad 在 2D/3D 问题中达到最优精度且开销微小；可分离架构提升区域分解框架效率，梯度手术对多种网络架构（PINN、KAN）具有通用性。

Abstract: Scientific machine learning methods such as physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) increasingly rely on domain decomposition for better scalability while solving partial differential equations (PDEs) over complex geometries, yet the resulting composite loss comprising residual, boundary, and interface terms is highly susceptible to conflicting gradients that degrade training. This work bridges domain decomposition with projection-based gradient surgery to systematically mitigate such conflicts in 2D and 3D settings. We evaluate two existing projection-based algorithms, PCGrad and ConFIG, and identify their performance degradation in specific scenarios such as 3D domains with multiple overlapping interfaces. To address this limitation, we propose Norm-PCGrad, a normalized variant that achieves state-of-the-art accuracy across a range of 2D and 3D domain decomposition problems. Across the benchmarks considered, Norm-PCGrad consistently achieves the lowest relative $L_2$ error compared to training without gradient surgery as well as to existing algorithms such as PCGrad and ConFIG, while incurring negligible additional computational overhead. To improve computational efficiency of domain decomposition frameworks such as Extended PINN (XPINN), we propose replacing vanilla PINNs in selected subdomains with separable architectures such as Separable PINN (SPINN), reducing the computational cost from quadratic (or cubic) to linear. We additionally demonstrate that gradient surgery extends to physics-informed Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (PIKANs), yielding substantial accuracy improvements for 3D domain decomposition and confirming the generality of the proposed approach across network architectures.

</details>


### [142] [Semantic Fibers and Cross-Gram Interference: A Calculus of Safety Drift in Overcomplete Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14861)
*Mohammed Ahnouch,Lotfi Elaachack*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文形式化了跨语言安全失效现象：同一模型对英文有害请求拒绝、对其忠实翻译却顺从。作者通过审计等价关系与商空间刻画，将安全漂移表示为跨Gram泛函，并引入受杠杆对偶χ²=1/ℓ−1约束的内在校准暴露度量，把观察到的漂移分为可重校准的读者故障、病态不可靠的精确校正、以及仅靠读出无法消除的表征碰撞三类，从而说明相同暴露度可对应不同的修复结论。框架可推广至锥值安全头，并用非绑定顺序交换恒等式作为线性控制接口诊断，其校准状态残差在三控制组合误差预测上达到中位数Spearman相关0.964（静态基线0.269）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机：已部署语言模型存在跨语言安全失效——英文拒绝但忠实翻译被顺从；该失效无法仅凭输出行为可靠刻画，需要可审计、可量化的内在表征层面分析框架，以区分"表观相同暴露"背后的不同根因，从而给出正确的修复判定。

Method: 方法：1）形式化一个审计等价关系与商空间，在给定商、表征、度量、特征字典、打分头、阈值与对比模型下，证明安全漂移具有精确线性代数刻画：漂移是纤维内对比的跨Gram泛函，其最坏容许值为支撑函数，边界不变性由零化子（annihilator）条件刻划。2）引入由杠杆对偶χ²=1/ℓ−1控制的内在校准暴露度量，将观察漂移分解为三类诊断区间。3）推广到锥值安全头；提出非绑定顺序交换恒等式作为线性控制接口诊断，用其校准状态残差预测未见状态与目标上的三控制组合误差。

Result: 结果：观察到的暴露可分为三种诊断上可区分的状态：(i) 可通过重校准移除的读取器故障；(ii) 精确但病态、不可靠的校正；(iii) 仅靠读出干预无法消除的表征层碰撞——因此相同的观察暴露会导出根本不同的修复结论。表征漂移被精确刻画为跨Gram泛函、其最坏值为支撑函数、边界不变性对应零化子条件。非绑定顺序交换恒等式的校准状态残差能在未见状态与目标上预测三控制组合误差，中位Spearman相关0.964，显著优于静态跨Gram基线的0.269。

Conclusion: 结论：跨语言安全失效本质上不可仅由输出行为刻画，须在受审计的表征等价关系与校准暴露度量下分析；同一暴露水平可能对应读者故障、病态校正或表征碰撞等不同结论，修复策略因此不同。该框架可扩展到锥值安全头，并为线性控制接口提供基于顺序交换恒等式的有效的可预测诊断。

Abstract: A deployed language model may refuse a harmful request in English yet comply with its faithful translation, revealing a cross-lingual safety failure that cannot be characterized reliably by output behavior alone. We formalize this phenomenon through an audited equivalence relation and show that, for a declared quotient, representation, metric, feature dictionary, scoring head, threshold, and contrast model, the resulting safety drift admits an exact linear-algebraic characterization. Specifically, the drift is a cross-Gram functional of the within-fiber contrast; its worst admissible value is a support function, while margin invariance is characterized by an annihilator condition.
  We introduce an intrinsic calibrated exposure measure, governed by the leverage duality $χ^2=1/\ell-1$, which separates observed drift into three diagnostically distinct regimes: a reader fault removable by recalibration, an exact correction that is too ill-conditioned to be reliable, and a representation-level collision that no readout-only intervention can remove. Thus, identical observed exposure can lead to fundamentally different remediation verdicts. The framework also extends to cone-valued safety heads. An untied order-swap identity provides a diagnostic for the linear control interface; its calibration-state residual predicts a distinct three-control composition error on unseen states and targets, achieving median Spearman correlation $0.964$, compared with $0.269$ for a static cross-Gram baseline. etc.....

</details>


### [143] [Interpolation Is Not Invariance: Pair Count Is Not Coverage in Transformation Audits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14870)
*Mohammed Ahnouch,Lotfi Elaachak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文区分审计覆盖的四个互补量（边数m、有效对比秩s、总体支撑秩r、图谱间隙η），指出仅以等价对数计数会高估约束强度，并给出秩r高斯对比模型下的可辨识条件、未观测风险分布及轨道拓扑约束，最后提出精确的块Woodbury留一轨道更新与源不相交部署门控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以“等价对计数”报告变换审计覆盖率是常见做法，但会产生误导：同语义对象生成的等价对彼此相关，且完整轨道图中存在代数冗余边，因此计数会显著高估审计实际施加的约束。作者希望刻画哪些量真正决定审计覆盖与部署可靠性，并解决由此导致的校准失败问题。

Method: 提出四量框架：边数m、有效对比秩s、总体支撑秩r、图谱间隙η。在秩r高斯对比模型下分析总体不变的校准读取器存在的充要条件（锚点在ker T中有分量）；用Beta分布刻画s<r时的未观测风险行为；用轨道拓扑（生成树与完全图的精确零约束等价）和图Poincaré不等式（漂移传播代价与1/η成正比）描述轨道结构；推导精确的块Woodbury留一轨道更新；设计源不相交部署门控，仅在不确定性界证明漂移更低且满足干净效用预算时替换原读取器。

Result: 总体不变的校准读取器存在当且仅当锚点在ker T中有分量；当s<r时未观测风险为R*/U，U~Beta((r-s)/2, s/2)，当s≥r时精确校准插值不可行；生成树与完全图施加相同的精确零约束；图Poincaré不等式以1/η的代价把边级漂移传播至整条轨道；循环审计可在不保留任何语义对象的情况下得到零对级留一误差。

Conclusion: 等价对计数不足以表征审计约束强度，需用四量框架联合评估。未观测风险与轨道拓扑共同决定审计是否可校准；所提出的留一轨道更新与源不相交门控能够在有限候选读取器中提供部署可靠性保障，除非不确定性界证明漂移更低，否则保留原读取器。

Abstract: Counting equivalent pairs is a common way to report transformation-audit coverage, but it can substantially overstate the constraints imposed by an audit: pairs generated from the same semantic object are correlated, and complete orbit graphs contain algebraically redundant edges. We therefore distinguish four complementary quantities---edge count $m$, effective contrast rank $s$, population support rank $r$, and graph spectral gap $η$---and characterize their roles in audit coverage and deployment reliability. Under a rank-$r$ Gaussian contrast model, a population-invariant calibrated reader exists exactly when the anchor has a component in $\ker T$. When an audit has rank $s < r$, its unobserved risk is $R^\star/U$, with $U \sim \operatorname{Beta}((r-s)/2,s/2)$; when $s \ge r$, exact calibrated interpolation is infeasible. The same distinction appears in orbit topology: a spanning tree imposes the same exact-null constraints as a complete graph, while a sharp graph Poincare inequality propagates edge-level drift to an entire orbit at a cost proportional to $1/η$. Cyclic audits can additionally yield zero pair-level leave-one-out error without holding out any semantic object.
  To address these failures, we derive exact block-Woodbury leave-one-orbit-out updates and introduce a source-disjoint deployment gate over finitely many candidate readers. The gate retains the original reader unless uncertainty bounds certify lower drift within a prescribed clean-utility budget. etc..

</details>


### [144] [AgentKV: Phase-Aware KV Eviction for Agentic LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14872)
*Taowen Tony Liu,Jeffrey T. H. Wong,Can Xiao,Bowen Yang,Hao Mark Chen,Yiren Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 AGENTKV，通过按阶段（think/act/tool 等）维护查询缓冲区并对缓存键统一打分，改进智能体场景下的 KV 缓存淘汰，并以多轮持久化服务路径在线压缩保留的 KV 页。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体服务消耗的 token 量可比聊天负载高数个数量级，对 KV 缓存容量与解码带宽造成压力。现有 KV 淘汰方法以最近 token 作为代表性查询来给缓存键打分，隐含假设未来注意力与近期注意力相似；但智能体生成中未来查询是 think、act、tool 等阶段的混合，主角度分析显示各成分占据可测量的不同查询子空间，因此基于近期性的代表会系统性低估后续阶段所需键的价值。

Method: AGENTKV 为每个阶段维护一个小型查询缓冲区，并将缓存键与这些缓冲区的并集进行打分，从而覆盖多阶段查询子空间。同时在持久化多轮服务路径中实现该方法：跨轮次携带压缩后的 KV 状态，并在线上压缩保留的 KV 页。

Result: 在两种模型、六个任务领域、三种 KV 预算下，AGENTKV 相比 R-KV 平均提升任务分数 5.5 分，相比 Tri-attention 提升 5.3 分；相对于上游全 KV 的 SGLang，输出 token 吞吐最高提升 1.80 倍。

Conclusion: 智能体生成的未来查询呈多阶段混合分布，破坏近期注意力相似性假设；按阶段维护查询代表并对并集打分可更准确地保留关键 KV，兼顾任务表现与吞吐，代码已开源。

Abstract: Agentic serving can consume orders of magnitude more tokens than chatbot workloads, stressing both KV-cache capacity and decode-time bandwidth. Most KV-eviction methods score cached keys against representative queries drawn from the most recent tokens, assuming future attention resembles recent attention. We show that agentic generation violates this assumption: future queries form a mixture over think, act, tool, and others phases, and principal-angle analysis shows these components occupy measurably different query subspaces, so recency representatives systematically undervalue keys that upcoming phases will need. We propose AGENTKV, which maintains a small query buffer per phase and scores cached keys against their union. We further implement AGENTKV in a persistent multi-turn serving path that carries compressed KV state across turns and compacts retained KV pages online. Across two models, six task domains, and three KV budgets each, AGENTKV improves task score by 5.5 points on average over R-KV and 5.3 over Tri-attention. Relative to upstream full-KV SGLang, AGENTKV improves output-token throughput by up to 1.80x. Code: https://github.com/LiuTaowen-Tony/agentkv.

</details>


### [145] [SeqMaestro: From nucleotide sequences to biological hypotheses through interpretable machine learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14882)
*Evgeny S. Saveliev,Krzysztof Kacprzyk,Charlotte Capitanchik,Neelanjan Mukherjee,Kate Matlin,Ryan Sheridan,Srinivas Ramachandran,Jernej Ule,David L. Bentley,Mihaela van der Schaar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SeqMaestro是一个无代码机器学习框架，通过两层接口将核苷酸序列转换为可解释机器学习生态中的特征表示，拟合多种透明模型组合，从而从序列中提出生物学假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核苷酸序列分析在调控基因组学、进化生物学和表型预测中至关重要。经典生物信息学方法（如motif和k-mer组成）可解释但灵活性有限；深度学习虽预测能力强，却难以检查其内部表示和决策机制；可解释机器学习（如稀疏线性模型、决策树）具有人类可理解的表示，但并非为直接处理核苷酸序列而设计。因此存在一个空白：既能直接作用于序列、又保持可解释性的分析框架。

Method: 提出SeqMaestro框架，核心是一个两层接口，将核苷酸序列与更广泛的可解释机器学习生态连接起来。基于该接口，系统可拟合多种可解释模型、特征表示与提取策略的组合，并利用不同透明模型间的变异性识别稳健的生物信号和更丰富的预测关系。系统还支持数据变换与清洗、模型拟合、超参数调优、可靠性分析，并将结果综合为有上下文背景的书面报告，全部通过无代码工作流实现。

Result: 通过结合多种透明模型与特征表示的变异性，SeqMaestro能够识别比单一特征重要性更稳健的生物信号和更丰富的预测关系；同时以无代码方式集成数据处理、建模、调优、可靠性分析与报告生成等完整流程。

Conclusion: SeqMaestro为无需大量编程或机器学习专业知识的科研人员提供了从核苷酸序列到生物学假设的可及路径，使可解释序列分析更易获得。

Abstract: Nucleotide sequence analysis is central to problems spanning regulatory genomics, evolutionary biology, and phenotype prediction. Classical bioinformatics methods extract interpretable sequence properties such as motifs and k-mer composition, but their flexibility is limited. In contrast, modern deep learning models can learn powerful predictive representations directly from raw sequences, yet their internal representations and decision mechanisms are difficult to inspect. Interpretable machine learning methods (e.g., sparse linear models and decision trees) provide human-understandable representations of predictive relationships but are not designed to operate directly on nucleotide sequences. Here, we introduce SeqMaestro, a machine learning framework that proposes biological hypotheses from nucleotide sequences using interpretable models. Our solution is centered around a two-layer interface that connects nucleotide sequences with the broader ecosystem of interpretable machine learning. SeqMaestro uses this interface to fit diverse combinations of interpretable models, feature representations, and extraction strategies, leveraging variability across transparent models to identify robust biological signals and richer predictive relationships than feature importance alone can provide. The system also supports data transformation and cleaning, model fitting, hyperparameter tuning, reliability analysis, and synthesis of results into a contextualized written report. By providing these capabilities through a no-code workflow, SeqMaestro is designed to make interpretable sequence analysis accessible to researchers without requiring extensive programming or machine learning expertise. SeqMaestro thereby provides an accessible route from nucleotide sequences to biological hypotheses.

</details>


### [146] [Cross-Block Conditioning in Deep Boltzmann Machines for Statistical Data Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14934)
*Junichiro Niimi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"观测块多预测"(observed-block multi-prediction)目标，使深度玻尔兹曼机能在统计数据融合的不完整观测场景下进行判别式训练，并据此分离联合模型各部分的贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统统计数据融合中两块面板共享协变量但结果块互不重叠，没有任何一行同时观测到两个结果，这排除了多预测训练所需的判别式准则（因为多预测训练需要对被留出的部分有真实标签）。因此需要一种在任意缺失模式下都有定义的判别式目标。

Method: 提出observed-block multi-prediction：将多预测目标限制为每行实际观测到的目标，对任意缺失模式均有定义，且当行完整时退化为原始准则。这一判别式准则使得可以将联合模型的贡献分解为表示(representation)部分与推断(inference)部分，从而检验联合模型是否必要。实验在两个消费者面板上进行，样本量与协变量宽度网格共35个单元格、875次运行，对比15种方法。另做置换实验：打乱一个结果块以破坏其与另一结果块的关联。

Result: 在全部35个单元格中，微调的DBM均为15种方法中最优。但该优势几乎不来自生成式预训练（仅在一个数据集的最小样本量下出现，在另一数据集上完全不存在），而是来自在预测一个结果块时以另一结果块为条件。该条件项贡献+0.19与+0.07个百分点，在35个单元格中全为正，且不像其他被测贡献那样随面板增大而衰减，既不需要第二隐层，也不需要更多推断。置换一个结果块以破坏关联后，增益完全消失，符合理论预期。

Conclusion: 条件于一个结果块来预测另一个结果块所带来的微小但不衰减的增益，依赖于任何将协变量映射到结果的模型都无法接受的证据，因此与那些会衰减的微小效应在本质上不同。

Abstract: Statistical data fusion combines two panels that share a block of covariates but observe disjoint outcome blocks, and in its traditional form no row observes both outcomes at once. That rules out the discriminative criterion one would rather train a Deep Boltzmann Machine with, since multi-prediction training needs ground truth for whatever it holds out. We propose observed-block multi-prediction, which restricts the multi-prediction objective to targets drawn from what each row actually observes. It is well defined for any missingness pattern and reduces to the original criterion when rows are complete. Having a discriminative criterion that survives the setting lets us ask whether the joint model is needed at all, by separating what it contributes into a representation part and an inference part. On two consumer panels, on grids over sample size and covariate width spanning 35 cells and 875 runs, the fine-tuned DBM is the best of fifteen methods in every cell; but almost none of that advantage comes from generative pre-training, which is confined to the smallest sample size on one dataset and absent on the other. It comes from conditioning on one outcome block when predicting the other. This term amounts to +0.19 and +0.07 percentage points, is positive in all 35 cells, and, unlike every other contribution we measure, neither decays as the panels grow, nor requires a second hidden layer, nor requires more inference. Permuting one outcome block to destroy its association with the other removes the gain entirely, which is what the account predicts. The margins are small. But a small effect that does not decay is a different object from one that does, because it rests on evidence that no model mapping covariates to outcomes can accept.

</details>


### [147] [Cloud Workflow Scheduling Based on Graph Attention-Driven Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14952)
*Zongjin Li,Shaohan Feng,Chunxi Yang,Wenbo Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GA-HRL框架，将云工作流调度建模为事件驱动分层半马尔可夫决策过程，用多头图注意力网络编码DAG依赖，并由任务调度与容器调度两个PPO智能体交替训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云工作流调度需同时兼顾截止期满足、容器利用率与能耗，且面临任务执行速度随机、通信代价依赖放置、任务与容器决策耦合等挑战；传统向量或矩阵状态难以刻画DAG的依赖拓扑，任务紧迫性与结构关系表达不足。

Method: 为任务分配预测子截止期以表征紧迫性，采用多头图注意力网络（GAT）从动态演化的DAG中提取依赖信息；将调度过程建模为事件驱动分层SMDP，工作流到达与任务完成触发调度事件。TS智能体先将就绪任务分配至可用容器或请求新容器，CS智能体再为请求的容器进行主机放置，两智能体以独立PPO交替训练。

Result: 在2018年Alibaba集群trace上的实验表明，GA-HRL保持有竞争力的工作流成功率；在成功率相当的场景下，通常获得更高的容器利用率与更低的能耗；在最大速度波动下，以小幅成功率损失换取显著降低的能耗。

Conclusion: GA-HRL通过图注意力表示与分层强化学习协同优化任务与容器决策，在成功率、容器利用率与能耗之间取得良好平衡，验证了图结构建模与事件驱动分层SMDP在云工作流调度中的有效性。

Abstract: Dynamic cloud workflow scheduling must balance deadline satisfaction, container utilization, and energy consumption while dealing with stochastic task-execution speeds, placement-dependent communication, and coupled task and container decisions. Workflows are naturally modeled as directed acyclic graphs (DAGs), but conventional vector- or matrix-based states do not fully capture their dependency topology. To better represent task urgency and structural relationships, we assign predicted sub-deadlines to tasks and use a multi-head graph attention network (GAT) to extract dependency information from the evolving DAGs. Based on these representations, we develop a Graph Attention-Driven Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning (GA-HRL) framework and model the scheduling process as an event-driven hierarchical semi-Markov decision process (SMDP). Workflow arrivals and task completions trigger scheduling events. At each scheduling event, the Task Scheduling (TS) agent first processes the currently ready tasks by assigning them to admissible existing containers or requesting new ones. The requested containers are then processed by the Container Scheduling (CS) agent for host placement before the environment advances. The two agents are trained alternately using separate Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO). Experiments on the 2018 Alibaba cluster trace show that GA-HRL maintains competitive workflow success rate and, in settings where success is comparable, generally achieves higher container utilization and lower energy consumption. Under the largest speed variation, it trades a small success-rate margin for substantially lower energy. Simulation code is available at: https://github.com/zongjin130/GA-HRL.

</details>


### [148] [High-Probability Nash Regret for Decentralized Learning in Markov $α$-Potential Games: Episodic and Fully Online Asynchronous Algorithms with Applications to Markov Congestion Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14959)
*S. Rasoul Etesami*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究无限时域折扣马尔可夫博弈中Nash均衡的分散式学习，聚焦马尔可夫α-势博弈与bandit反馈，提出KL投影自然策略梯度算法，分别给出episodic与全在线异步设定下有限时间高概率NE regret界，消除了分布失配系数，并将框架应用于独立资源马尔可夫拥塞博弈与在线作业调度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分散式Nash均衡学习在bandit反馈下通常受分布失配系数困扰，该系数可能随状态空间规模急剧增大，导致regret界不可扩展；同时全在线异步学习、估计预言机偏差与转移敏感性等实际因素缺乏有限时间高概率保证。

Method: 针对马尔可夫α-势博弈，设计KL投影自然策略梯度（NPG）算法：一是采样期间冻结策略的episodic设定，二是每步仅获单个实现成本样本、沿连续轨迹异步更新的全在线设定；并刻画状态级势结构，构造基于实现成本的分散式估计预言机，将框架实例化到独立资源马尔可夫拥塞博弈（IMCGs）。

Result: 在episodic与全在线设定下分别建立阶为Õ(T^{-1/4})与Õ(T^{-2/15})的有限时间高概率NE regret界（含固定近似项）；regret界消除分布失配系数，并兼容势近似、估计预言机偏差与转移敏感性；状态级势结构可给出对势近似误差α具加性依赖的更紧保证；对IMCGs建立近似势与转移敏感性性质并给出可扩展分散算法，应用于随机机器上的策略性在线作业调度。

Conclusion: 该工作首次为马尔可夫α-势博弈中全在线异步分散式学习提供有限时间高概率NE regret保证，去除regret界中的分布失配系数，容许固定估计预言机偏差，并为IMCGs提供具有限时间保证的可扩展分散式学习，同时给出在线作业调度应用。

Abstract: We study decentralized learning of Nash equilibria (NE) in infinite-horizon discounted Markov games under bandit feedback, focusing on Markov $α$-potential games. We develop KL-projected natural policy gradient (NPG) algorithms in two settings: an episodic setting with frozen policies during sampling and a fully online setting in which players receive a single realized cost sample per time step and update their policies asynchronously along a continuing trajectory. We establish finite-time high-probability NE regret bounds of order $\widetilde O(T^{-1/4})$ and $\widetilde O(T^{-2/15})$ for the episodic and fully online settings, respectively, up to fixed approximation terms. Crucially, our bounds eliminate the distribution-mismatch coefficient, which can scale prohibitively with the size of the state space, while accommodating potential approximation, estimation-oracle bias, and transition sensitivity. We further identify a state-wise potential structure that yields sharper guarantees with additive dependence on the potential approximation error $α$. We specialize the framework to independent-resource Markov congestion games (IMCGs), establish their approximate-potential and transition-sensitivity properties, and construct decentralized estimation oracles from realized costs. As an application, we introduce strategic online job scheduling on stochastic machines and obtain a scalable decentralized algorithm for learning stable dispatching policies. Overall, our results provide the first finite-time high-probability NE regret guarantees for fully online asynchronous decentralized learning in Markov $α$-potential games, remove distribution-mismatch coefficients from the regret bounds, accommodate fixed estimation-oracle bias, and provide scalable decentralized learning with finite-time guarantees for IMCGs.

</details>


### [149] [HiGFRL: Hierarchical Graph Fusion-Driven Reinforcement Learning for Dependency-Aware Task Scheduling in Heterogeneous Cloud](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14968)
*Tiangang Li,Shi Ying,Xiangbo Tian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出HiGFRL分层图融合强化学习框架，通过三级状态表示与双网络架构优化异构云集群中依赖感知任务的在线调度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构云集群中依赖感知任务的在线调度因DAG拓扑与多维资源约束的复杂耦合而极具挑战。现有基于GNN的DRL方法难以高效建模高阶拓扑依赖，且任务状态与资源状态耦合松散，导致调度决策短视。

Method: HiGFRL构建三级状态表示：静态超图、动态全局图和局部二分图，显式建模任务依赖与实时集群动态的交互。设计融合驱动双网络架构：上下文融合分配器将局部二分匹配特征与融合全局上下文结合以实现精准任务-节点分配；全局状态评估器利用动态全局图表征估计长期累积奖励期望。并引入拓扑先验引导的混合奖励机制，将静态拓扑先验蒸馏进学习过程以加速收敛。

Result: 基于真实阿里巴巴集群trace的实验表明，HiGFRL显著优于启发式与DRL基线：大规模高负载场景下Makespan最多降低32.55%，平均任务流时间与平均任务等待时间分别优化13.58%和13.79%。

Conclusion: HiGFRL显著提升集群吞吐量，并通过大幅降低排队延迟保证更优QoS。代码已开源。

Abstract: Online scheduling of dependency-aware tasks in heterogeneous cloud clusters is a fundamental yet challenging problem due to the complex interplay between DAG topologies and multi-dimensional resource constraints. While DRL has shown promise, existing GNN-based approaches often struggle to efficiently model high-order topological dependencies and suffer from loose coupling between task and resource states, leading to myopic scheduling decisions. To address these limitations, we propose HiGFRL, a Hierarchical Graph Fusion-Driven Reinforcement Learning framework. HiGFRL constructs a novel three-level state representation comprising a Static Hypergraph, a Dynamic Global Graph, and a Local Bipartite Graph to explicitly model the interplay between task dependencies and real-time cluster dynamics. Specifically, we design a fusion-driven dual-network architecture to optimize RL decision-making, where a Context Fusion Allocator integrates local bipartite matching features with fused global context to execute precise task-to-node allocation, and a Global State Evaluator leverages the global dynamic graph representation to accurately estimate expected long-term cumulative reward. Furthermore, we incorporate a topology-prior-guided hybrid reward mechanism that distills static topological priors into the learning process to accelerate convergence. Extensive experiments using real-world Alibaba cluster traces demonstrate that HiGFRL significantly outperforms heuristics and DRL baselines. Specifically, in challenging large-scale high-load scenarios, HiGFRL reduces the Makespan by up to 32.55%, and optimizes the average task flow time and average task wait time by 13.58% and 13.79%, respectively. Experimental results confirm that HiGFRL not only significantly improves cluster throughput but also ensures superior QoS by substantially reducing queuing delays. Code Release:https://github.com/igeng/HiGFRL.

</details>


### [150] [Learning to Solve Stochastic Controls with Unknown Drifts and Running Rewards: Theory, Algorithms and Convergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14972)
*Jin Ma,Gaozhan Wang,Jianfeng Zhang,Xunyu Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究漂移系数和运行奖励函数未知的连续时间、可能高维随机控制问题，在Wang、Zariphopoulou和Zhou(2020)的探索式强化学习框架（松弛控制+熵正则化）下，开发有理论保证、高效且可扩展的RL算法，同时学习最优值函数（满足探索式HJB方程）和最优探索式反馈控制策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于漂移系数和运行奖励函数等模型基本要素缺失，无法直接求解传统的随机控制问题，因此需要采用探索式强化学习框架，在模型未知的情况下学习最优值函数和最优控制策略，并希望对高维问题保持可扩展性。

Method: 在扩散系数不含控制的情形下，基于仅依赖原动力系统扩散部分的辅助状态过程，给出最优值函数及其梯度的概率表示；通过对适当定义的映射及其不动点的细致分析，引入策略迭代算法并证明其收敛性；最后研究一类扩散系数依赖于控制的特殊情形，此时需要Hessian的概率表示。

Result: 提出了有理论基础的策略迭代算法并证明了其收敛性；通过多个数值算例验证了算法的性能；对控制依赖扩散的特殊情形给出了需要Hessian概率表示的处理思路。

Conclusion: 该工作为模型未知的连续时间（含高维）随机控制问题提供了有收敛保证的RL算法，能够同时学习最优值函数与最优探索式反馈策略，并在数值实验中表现良好；对控制依赖扩散的情形需借助Hessian的概率表示进一步扩展。

Abstract: We study continuous-time and possibly high-dimensional stochastic control problems where drift coefficients and running reward functions are unknown. Due to these missing model primitives, we take the exploratory, reinforcement learning (RL) framework of Wang, Zariphopoulou, and Zhou(2020) with relaxed controls and entropy regularization. The objective is to develop theoretically grounded, efficient and scalable RL algorithms to learn both the optimal value functions (which also solve the exploratory HJB equation) and optimal exploratory feedback control policies. When the diffusion coefficients do not contain control, we employ probabilistic representations of both the optimal value function and its gradient based on an auxiliary state process depending only on the diffusion part of the original dynamics. With a delicate analysis on some properly defined mappings and their fixed points, this leads to the introduction of our policy iteration algorithms and their convergence. We demonstrate the performance of our algorithms through various numerical examples. Finally, we study a special control-dependent diffusion case where probability representation of the Hessian is called for.

</details>


### [151] [LiftGCN: Efficient Energy-Preserving Graph Learning via Joukowski Spectral Lifting for Finite Element Stress Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14977)
*Chen Zeng,Qiao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 LiftGCN，一种基于 Joukowski 谱提升的谱稳定图神经网络，用于高效、准确地预测有限元应力场中的局部高梯度与应力集中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限元应力场在孔洞、缺口及加载区附近存在强烈的局部非光滑性，产生尖锐空间梯度与高频图分量。常规图神经网络的消息传递本质上具有平滑性，会随深度逐渐衰减这些高频信息；而现有的酉传播方法虽能保持谱幅值，却依赖矩阵函数与高阶近似，传播复杂度高达 O(Ked)。

Method: LiftGCN 通过 Joukowski 关系将归一化图算子的实谱映射到单位圆上，并以简单的二阶递推实现对应的谱变换，避免矩阵指数、特征分解和高阶多项式截断。其线性 Joukowski 主干具有单位模特征根，在正定度量下具备能量保持结构，从而防止图频分量随深度指数衰减。每层仅需一次稀疏邻域聚合，传播复杂度为 O(ed)，并辅以轻量级局部非线性残差提供特征变换能力。

Result: 在有限元应力预测任务上，LiftGCN 在取得有竞争力的整体精度的同时，改善了应力集中与局部高梯度结构的重建效果，并显著降低了计算成本。

Conclusion: LiftGCN 以低复杂度实现了谱稳定的图传播，有效缓解了高频信息衰减问题，为有限元应力场等非光滑场的高保真预测提供了高效方案，代码已开源。

Abstract: Finite element stress fields often exhibit strong local non-smoothness, where stress concentrations near holes, notches, and loading regions induce sharp spatial gradients and high-frequency graph components. Although graph neural networks naturally operate on irregular finite element meshes, conventional message passing is inherently smoothing and progressively attenuates such high-frequency information. Unitary propagation alleviates this problem by preserving spectral magnitudes, but typically relies on matrix functions and high-order approximations with $O(Ked)$ propagation complexity. We propose LiftGCN, an efficient spectrally stable graph network based on Joukowski spectral lifting. LiftGCN maps the real spectrum of a normalized graph operator onto the unit circle through the Joukowski relation and realizes the resulting spectral transformation as a simple second-order recurrence, avoiding matrix exponentials, eigendecomposition, and high-order polynomial truncation. We show that the linear Joukowski backbone has unit-modulus characteristic roots and admits an energy-preserving structure under a positive-definite metric, preventing exponential attenuation of graph-frequency components with depth. Each layer requires only one sparse neighborhood aggregation, yielding $O(ed)$ propagation complexity, while lightweight local nonlinear residuals provide expressive feature transformations. Experiments on finite element stress prediction demonstrate that LiftGCN achieves competitive overall accuracy while improving reconstruction of stress concentrations and local high-gradient structures with substantially reduced computational cost. Our code is available at https://github.com/ChenZeng001/LiftGCN.

</details>


### [152] [HGTO: A Unified Graph-Based Physics-Informed Formulation for Structural Topology Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15001)
*Kangzheng Liu,Uday Kumar Punna,Leixin Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出HGTO，一种基于图的统一公式，将完整神经拓扑优化从坐标空间扩展到有限元图空间：单元密度在网格派生的单元图上参数化，结构状态在节点-单元超图上确定，二者通过共同的有限元关联结构耦合。数值研究表明其柔度与传统密度优化相当，计算成本显著低于代表性坐标型双场神经方法，并支持高分辨率/不规则网格、三维结构、有限变形与弹塑性响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于密度的拓扑优化通常是非嵌套序列（材料更新、结构分析、灵敏度评估），循环成本高。神经密度参数化与双场物理信息方法虽提供无数据替代方案，但多数将密度与位移表示为坐标场，未充分利用有限元网格内在的离散关系。因此需要一种能利用网格离散关联结构、兼顾效率与通用性的统一公式。

Method: 提出HGTO（统一图基公式）：将单元密度在由网格派生的单元图上进行参数化；将结构状态在相应的节点-单元超图上确定。有限元运动学、数值积分、本构响应与力组装在可微计算中保持显式定义的操作，使材料场与平衡状态通过共同的有限元关联结构耦合。

Result: 数值研究显示，HGTO的柔度与传统基于密度的优化相当，而计算成本显著低于代表性坐标型双场神经方法。同一耦合公式可适用于高分辨率与不规则网格、三维结构、有限变形及弹塑性响应。

Conclusion: HGTO通过图/超图表征统一了神经密度参数化与有限元结构分析，实现高效、通用且数据自由的拓扑优化，桥接坐标型神经方法与有限元离散结构。

Abstract: Density-based topology optimization is typically structured as a nested sequence of material updates, structural analyses, and sensitivity assessments. While neural density parameterization and dual-field physics-informed approaches provide data-free alternatives, most existing methods represent density and displacement as coordinate fields and make limited use of the discrete relationships inherent in the finite element mesh. The present study introduces HGTO, a unified graph-based formulation that extends complete neural topology optimization from coordinate space to finite-element graph space. Element densities are parameterized on the element graph derived from the mesh, and the structural state is determined on the corresponding node--element hypergraph. Finite element kinematics, numerical quadrature, constitutive response, and force assembly remain explicitly defined operations within the differentiable computation. The material field and equilibrium state are therefore coupled through a common finite-element incidence structure. Numerical studies show compliance comparable to conventional density-based optimization at substantially lower computational cost than a representative coordinate-based dual-field neural method. The same coupled formulation accommodates high-resolution and irregular meshes, three-dimensional structures, finite deformation, and elastoplastic response.

</details>


### [153] [Pick Your Poison: Learning to Select Poison Sets for Stronger LLM Backdoor Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15029)
*Aashiq Muhamed,Mona T. Diab,Virginia Smith,Andrew Ilyas,Matthew Jagielski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示现有后门投毒评估因随机采样毒集而严重低估最坏情况脆弱性，并将毒集选择形式化为带预言机预算的集合优化问题，提出 SAILS 方法，在多项设定下显著提升攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后门投毒攻击评估通常固定毒化样本数量并从候选池中随机采样。作者发现这种做法会严重低估最坏情况脆弱性：在 LLaMA-3-8B 的三种后门设定下，固定模型、干净数据与毒化数量不变时，攻击成功率仅因所选毒集不同就在 3% 到 80% 之间变化。因此需要一个系统化的毒集选择方法来评估真实的最坏情况风险。

Method: 作者将毒集选择形式化为带预言机预算的集合优化问题（oracle-budgeted set optimization），并提出 SAILS（Set-level Audit-Informed Iterative Learned Selection）。该方法从数百次微调-评估运行中学习一个集合打分器，对数百万候选毒集进行排序，仅对少量入围短名单进行审计。

Result: SAILS 相比最强的影响力基线方法，在留出集上的攻击成功率平均提升 30 个百分点；能够从小规模微调迁移到全规模微调；并可扩展到代码生成、智能体（agentic）以及仅 API 后门场景。

Conclusion: 随机采样毒集会显著低估后门脆弱性，毒集选择应被显式建模为集合优化问题。SAILS 通过学习集合打分器与审计短名单，能够高效发现高成功率毒集，具备跨设定、跨规模与跨任务的可迁移性与通用性。

Abstract: Backdoor poisoning attacks add poisoned examples to otherwise-clean finetuning data, pairing a trigger with a target behavior that the model learns to produce when the trigger appears. Existing evaluations typically fix the number of poisoned examples and sample them at random from a candidate pool. We show that this can severely underestimate worst-case vulnerability: across three LLaMA-3-8B backdoor settings, holding the model, clean data, and poison count fixed, attack success ranges from 3% to 80% depending only on which poison set is chosen.
  We formalize poison selection as oracle-budgeted set optimization and introduce SAILS (Set-level Audit-Informed Iterative Learned Selection), which learns a set scorer from a few hundred finetune-and-evaluate runs, ranks millions of candidate sets, and audits only a small shortlist. SAILS improves held-out attack success by 30 percentage points on average over the strongest influence baselines, transfers from small-scale to full-scale finetuning, and extends to code-generation, agentic, and API-only backdoors.

</details>


### [154] [MoARa: Module-Aware Rank Allocation and Structure-Preserving Decomposition for Low-Rank LLM Pre-training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15037)
*Keunyoung Kim,Nojun Kwak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MoARa通过模块感知的投影秩分配与分块幅度-方向分解，降低低秩梯度投影预训练的步数与墙钟时间，同时几乎不增加显存开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低秩梯度投影虽降低大语言模型预训练的优化器状态显存，但达到目标质量所需步数与墙钟时间仍有改进空间。作者将其归因于现有方法的两点设计：(1) 投影秩预算在投影敏感度异质的各Transformer模块间均匀分配；(2) 直接投影原始梯度会同时衰减其幅度与方向。

Method: 提出MoARa，结合基于静态profiling的模块感知投影秩分配与分块（block-wise）幅度-方向分解，默认块大小取注意力头维度附近。作为即插即用组件与GaLore结合。

Result: 在Llama、Qwen、DeepSeek的5种架构、300M至7B规模上验证：GaLore+MoARa在Llama 2 7B上以少37%步数、少34%墙钟时间达到标准GaLore最终困惑度，标准图编译下峰值预留显存开销仅0.2%。在评估的6种低秩预训练方法中，仅模块感知秩分配即可在全部6种上带来方向一致的步数下降。在兼容主机上，双组件设计最高实现41.7%步数下降与37.1%墙钟时间下降。

Conclusion: MoARa以极低显存代价显著加速低秩梯度投影预训练，模块感知秩分配具有跨方法与跨架构的普适性。

Abstract: Low-rank gradient projection reduces the optimizer-state memory cost of large language model (LLM) pretraining, but the steps and wall-clock time needed to reach a target quality remain a meaningful axis for improvement. We attribute this to two design choices in existing methods: the projection-rank budget is allocated uniformly across Transformer modules with heterogeneous projection sensitivity, and projecting a raw gradient attenuates its magnitude and direction jointly. We propose MoARa, which combines a static profiling-based module-aware projection-rank allocation with a block-wise magnitude-direction decomposition; the default block size is set in the neighborhood of the attention head dimension. Across five Transformer architectures spanning Llama, Qwen, and DeepSeek at 300M to 7B scales, GaLore with MoARa reaches standard GaLore's final perplexity in 37% fewer steps and 34% less wall-clock time on Llama 2 7B, with only 0.2% peak reserved memory overhead under standard graph compilation. Across the six low-rank pretraining methods we evaluate, module-aware rank allocation alone delivers directionally consistent step reductions on all six. On compatible hosts, the two-component design reaches up to 41.7% step reduction and 37.1% wall-clock reduction.

</details>


### [155] [Structured Features Overfit Where Random Features Grok](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15047)
*Chon-Fai Kam,Miloud Bessafi,Frederic Cadet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在结构化（带限傅里叶）特征映射上，过参数化岭回归不出现"先记忆后泛化"的 grokking 延迟，泛化性能由激活支撑（有效模式数）而非容量比决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Xu、Vardi 与 Safran (ICML 2026) 证明在无结构随机高斯特征映射上，过参数化岭回归会出现 grokking，且记忆与泛化之间的延迟随权重衰减按 1/λ 增长。本文旨在检验这一现象是否依赖于特征映射的无结构性，即结构化特征映射是否会呈现相同的延迟与记忆-泛化机制。

Method: 在 $\mathbb{Z}_p^2$ 上构造带限傅里叶特征映射，目标为落在可表达类内的单字符任务，固定正权重衰减，扫描带宽；对比不同激活模式数（将带宽掩码回 1089 个激活模式 vs 完整 4225 模式），并分析经验 Gram 矩阵的教师加权谱，区分容量比与激活支撑的作用。

Result: 在固定正权重衰减下增大带宽，峰值留出准确率从 1.00 单调降至 0.07，扫描过程中不存在任何记忆-泛化阶段；性能退化并非插值效应，其在容量比 q/n=0.638（远低于插值阈值）处开始，与插值阈值之上出现的精确零空间成因不同。真正具有尖锐边界的是激活支撑：固定名义维度、将带宽掩码回 1089 个激活模式可恢复 1.000 的留出准确率且跨随机种子零方差，而完整 4225 模式带宽则坍缩至 0.185。激活模式数通过经验 Gram 矩阵的教师加权谱起作用，而非通过容量比。

Conclusion: 结构化特征映射上不存在 grokking 式的记忆-泛化延迟，泛化行为由特征几何（激活模式数及其教师加权谱）决定，而非容量比或双下降的重述。

Abstract: Xu, Vardi and Safran (ICML 2026) prove that over-parameterized ridge regression over an unstructured random Gaussian feature map groks, with the delay between memorization and generalization growing as $1/λ$ in the weight decay. We show that on a structured feature map the same delay does not appear. For a band-limited Fourier feature map over $\mathbb{Z}_p^2$ carrying a single-character target that lies inside the expressible class, enlarging the band at fixed positive weight decay drives peak held-out accuracy monotonically from $1.00$ to $0.07$, with no memorize-then-generalize regime anywhere along the sweep. The degradation is not an interpolation effect. It sets in at capacity ratio $q/n = 0.638$, far below the interpolation threshold, on separate grounds from the exact null space that appears above it. What does have a sharp boundary is the active support. Holding the nominal dimension fixed and masking the band back to $1089$ active modes restores held-out accuracy of $1.000$ with zero variance across seeds, while the full $4225$-mode band collapses to $0.185$. The number of active modes acts through the teacher-weighted spectrum of the empirical Gram matrix and not through the capacity ratio, which makes this a statement about feature geometry and not a restatement of double descent.

</details>


### [156] [Ensemble Complexity in Photovoltaic Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15049)
*Sun Ze,Zhou Liguo,Xu Yuqing,Yu Lei,Jiang Mingming*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究通过匹配比较与消融实验，系统评估异构光伏预测集成中各组件对精度与计算代价的贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集成方法可提升光伏预测精度，但引入贡献甚微的成员会增加计算开销，因此需明确各组件收益与代价。

Method: 基于固定异构预测器库，在GEFCom2014及三个公开数据集上进行逐小时实验，采用时间顺序划分与三个随机种子；对比静态融合与匹配的提升方法，并开展天气门控、成员移除等消融与探索性分析。

Result: 在回顾性ERA5辅助下，静态融合相较匹配提升法将缩放平均绝对误差在PVDAQ、OPSD、Ausgrid上分别降低1.11%、4.41%、1.63%，但多重比较校正后仅OPSD仍显著；天气门控无一致增量收益；成员移除显示组级依赖与个体冗余并存；十五分钟案例中以树模型替换一个神经网络成员使归一化误差降低1.72%，但实测推理更慢。

Conclusion: 应进行逐组件评估，并明确限制天气数据可用性与测试集复用；集成收益需结合统计显著性与计算代价审慎判断。

Abstract: An ensemble can improve photovoltaic forecasts while adding components that contribute little or increase computation. We assess these effects through matched comparisons and ablations of a fixed heterogeneous predictor bank. Hourly experiments use GEFCom2014 and three additional public datasets, with chronological partitions and three seeds. Under retrospective ERA5 assistance, static fusion reduces scaled mean absolute error against matched boosting by 1.11%, 4.41%, and 1.63% on PVDAQ, OPSD, and Ausgrid; only OPSD remains supported after multiple-comparison correction. Weather gating offers no consistent incremental benefit. Exploratory member removals show group-level dependence alongside individual redundancy. A separate, previously inspected fifteen-minute case replaces one neural member with a tree predictor: normalized error falls by 1.72%, but measured inference is slower. These findings support component-wise evaluation with explicit limits on weather availability and test-set reuse.

</details>


### [157] [Not All Prompts Are Equal: Exploration-Guided Prompt Scaffolding for Multimodal Reinforcement Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15051)
*Yuanhao Yue,Qianli Ma,Chengyu Wang,Haoting Wang,Lei Shen,Jun Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出探索引导的提示脚手架框架，用EPS动态调整MLLM强化学习后训练中的提示分布，将低效用提示改写为更具信息量的形式，在Geo3K与MMK12上优于GRPO基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线强化学习中，不同训练提示对当前策略的信息量差异显著：部分已被饱和掌握，部分过于困难，无法提供可靠学习信号，但标准训练对二者分配相同的 rollout 预算，造成算力浪费与学习效率低下。因此需要一个能动态识别提示效用并加以利用的机制。

Method: 核心是探索潜力分数（Exploration Potential Score, EPS）：基于KL正则化策略改进理论推导的轻量级、基于rollout的提示效用代理指标，可直接由同策略rollout统计计算，无额外开销。对低效用提示不直接丢弃，而是用教师模型生成脚手架式改写，在保留原始任务意图的前提下使后续训练更具信息量，把教师监督重新定义为训练数据精炼而非输出模仿。该方法与GRPO结合，在多模态大模型后训练中应用。

Result: 在Geo3K与MMK12上结合GRPO训练，域内与分布外基准均稳定优于基线：域内相对提升最高达9.7%，MathVision提升11.5%，MMMU-Pro提升11.1%。

Conclusion: 通过EPS动态调节提示分布、以脚手架改写替代简单丢弃，可有效提升MLLM强化学习后训练的数据效率与泛化能力，为教师监督提供了数据精炼的新范式。

Abstract: Training prompts in online reinforcement learning (RL) differ substantially in how informative they are for the current policy: some are already saturated while others are too difficult to yield reliable learning signals, yet both receive equal rollout budget under standard training. We propose an exploration-guided prompt scaffolding framework that adapts the training prompt distribution dynamically throughout RL post-training of multimodal large language models (MLLMs). Central to our approach is the $\textit{Exploration Potential Score} (EPS)$, a lightweight rollout-based proxy for prompt utility derived from KL-regularized policy improvement theory, computable directly from on-policy rollout statistics without additional overhead. Rather than discarding low-utility prompts, we use a teacher model to generate scaffolded rewrites that preserve the original task intent while making subsequent training more informative, reframing teacher supervision as training-data refinement rather than output imitation. Integrated with GRPO on Geo3K and MMK12, our method consistently outperforms the baseline on both in-domain and out-of-distribution benchmarks, achieving up to 9.7\% relative improvement in-domain and gains of 11.5\% on MathVision and 11.1\% on MMMU-Pro.

</details>


### [158] [What Does an LLM Learn from Reinforcement Learning? A Mechanistic Interpretability Perspective with Fixed-SAE Track](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15064)
*Lingheng Du,Yiming Tang,Xufeng Duan,Dianbo Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Fixed-SAE Track 框架，通过在基座模型与全部 RL 检查点上训练共享稀疏自编码器并固定特征方向，从表征层面追踪 RL 对 LLM 内部表征的改变，发现 RL 漂移小、渐进、概念特定且集中于后层，主要通过增强少量"阶梯式"token 与格式脚手架来提升性能，而非重塑问题内容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL 被广泛用于大语言模型训练以提升特定能力，但其如何重塑模型仍缺乏理解；已有解释多停留在行为层面，未回答 RL 在表征层面是否创造全新特征、增强或抑制哪些既有特征。稀疏自编码器（SAE）虽能分解激活为可解释特征，却无法直接追踪跨训练的变化。

Method: 提出 Fixed-SAE Track：对每个考察层，在基座模型与所有 RL 检查点池化后的激活上训练一个共享 SAE，并固定所有特征方向，使表征漂移可严格地通过可解释 SAE 隐变量的激活来定义，并借此检测涌现的新特征。在多个数据集与多种 RL 算法上验证，并设计特征由构造已知的合成基准测试 RL 是否能注入真正的新特征。

Result: RL 引起的表征漂移很小、渐进、概念特定，并集中于后层，主要增强少量"阶梯式"token（ladder tokens）以及步骤分隔符、答案定界符等格式脚手架的采样率，而非重塑问题内容。基座模型注入这些特征可恢复约 80% 的 RL 性能增益。合成基准进一步检验了 RL 是否可产生构造意义上的全新特征。

Conclusion: RL 主要是引出模型已具备的能力（与 steering 类似），而非创造全新特征。Fixed-SAE Track 为追踪表征漂移提供了有原则的方法，并为理解 RL 如何改变 LLM 内部表征提供了表征层面的证据。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) is widely utilized in large language model training to improve targeted capabilities, yet how RL reshapes a model remains poorly understood. Prior attempts to explain how RL works largely offer behavioral perspectives, leaving open what RL gives a model at the representation level: can RL create genuinely novel features, and which existing features does it enhance or suppress? Recent developments in mechanistic interpretability suggest sparse autoencoders (SAEs) as a promising lens to decompose internal activations into human-interpretable features; however, they cannot be directly applied to tracking change across training. In this work, we introduce Fixed-SAE Track, a framework that trains one shared SAE per considered layer on activations pooled across the base model and all RL checkpoints, holding every feature direction fixed so that representation shifts are rigorously defined through the activations of interpretable SAE latents, including the detection of emerging novel features. Validated across multiple datasets and RL algorithms, we find that RL-induced drift is small, gradual, concept specific, and concentrated in late layers, mainly enhancing the sampling rates of a small set of ladder tokens, formatting scaffolding such as step breaks and answer delimiters, rather than reshaping problem content. Steering these features into the base model recovers around 80% of RL's performance gain, suggesting that RL primarily elicits capabilities the model already possesses, much as steering does. We further design a synthetic benchmark with features known by construction to test whether RL can instill genuinely novel features. We believe Fixed-SAE Track provides a principled approach to tracking representation shifts and offers representational evidence for understanding how reinforcement learning changes the inner representation of LLMs.

</details>


### [159] [Branched Optimal Transport Amortization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15072)
*Semyon Semenov,Viktor Kovalchuk,Meir Roketlishvili,Albert Baichorov,Fakhri Karray,Martin Takac,Arip Asadulaev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可扩展的分支流匹配算法，将Benamou-Brenier连续时间最优传输公式推广到分支最优传输问题，从而学习能沿公共路径汇聚再分叉的高维生成流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分支最优传输（BOT）方法能模拟河流、血管等自然树状结构的经济性与高效性，广泛用于网络设计。但该方法在深度生成模型、尤其大规模场景中研究不足。现有连续时间生成模型（如流匹配）无法捕捉真实数据中固有的层次与分支模式，且缺乏让流合并或共享路径以最小化总传输成本的机制。

Method: 受BOT中"规模经济"原理启发，作者提出一种新颖且可扩展的分支流匹配算法，用于求解高维分支最优传输问题。方法将Benamou-Brenier连续时间最优传输公式加以改造，以学习分支生成流，使概率质量能先沿公共路径聚集，再分叉通往多个不同目标，并由神经网络进行参数化以学习复杂的分支生成过程。

Result: 在生物学与图像生成等具有挑战性的高维任务上验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作将分支最优传输引入深度生成建模，提出了可扩展的分支流匹配算法，能够刻画数据中的层次与分叉结构并降低传输成本，在多个高维任务上展现出良好效果。

Abstract: Methods of Branched Optimal Transport (BOT) mimic the economy and efficiency of natural tree-like structures, such as those found in rivers and biological systems. These methods are widely applicable for designing efficient networks in society, from river basins and blood vessels to mail and gas distribution systems. However, they remain understudied in the context of designing deep generative models, particularly at a large scale. Standard continuous-time generative models, such as the flow matching approach, fail to capture the inherent hierarchical and branching patterns present in real-world data. Current models provide no mechanism for flows to merge or share pathways to minimize total transport cost. Inspired by the "economy of scale" principle in BOT, we introduce a novel, scalable branched flow-matching algorithm designed to solve the branched optimal transport problem in high dimensions. Our method adapts the Benamou-Brenier continuous-time optimal transport formulation to learn branched generative flows. These flows allow probability mass to aggregate along common pathways before branching out to diverse targets. Parametrized by neural networks, our method effectively learns complex branched generative processes. We demonstrate its effectiveness on challenging high-dimensional tasks in biology and image generation.

</details>


### [160] [$\mathbb{SL}(n)$ Representation Learning: An Intrinsic Mixed-Curvature Space with Higher Curvature Capacities and Deeper Order-Aware Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15083)
*Xingrun Li,Yusuke Mukuta,Xin Yang,Yinyu Ye,Tatsuya Harada*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出 $\mathbb{SL}(n)$ 空间，通过 $\det(A)=1$ 约束与左不变 Schatten-$p$ Finsler 结构统一实现混合曲率表示、非交换序敏感性与深层嵌套李括号组合，在多个图基准上显著超越现有表示流形基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有混合曲率表示学习主要依赖积流形，需人工指定不同曲率空间的组合方式，并将各曲率贡献分散于不同因子上，缺乏统一且自动耦合曲率、同时兼具序敏感与深层组合能力的表示几何。

Method: 构造 $\mathbb{SL}(n)$ 空间：以简单约束 $\det(A)=1$ 定义表示几何，配备左不变 Schatten-$p$ Finsler 结构；利用其非交换群结构提供固有顺序敏感性，并借助非幂零李代数在任意深度存在非零嵌套李括号，支持深层序感知组合。

Result: 几何上，$\mathbb{SL}(n)$ 在共同旗杆周围呈现逐点负、零、正旗曲率，混合曲率与曲率耦合能力相对内在几何上界渐近最大。实验上，在不同规模图基准中持续优于广泛表示流形基线：KEGG 平均失真较最强基线降低 44.3%，HumanCyc 降低 40.5%，OGBL-PPA 的 Hits@20 提升 42.8%；Flickr30k-Order 实验进一步支持其从有序组合中捕获高阶依赖的能力。

Conclusion: 一个看似简单的结构约束可在一个统一表示空间内产生出人意料的丰富几何、容量与组合能力，表明 $\mathbb{SL}(n)$ 为混合曲率表示学习提供了无需积流形人工设计的高效替代方案。

Abstract: Mixed-curvature representation learning seeks to capture rich geometric structures that cannot be adequately modeled by a single curvature regime. Existing approaches largely rely on product manifolds, which require manually specifying how different curvature spaces are combined and separate their curvature contributions across factors. We introduce the $\mathbb{SL}(n)$ space, a representation geometry defined by the simple $\det(A)=1$ constraint and a left invariant Schatten-$p$ Finsler structure. Despite this minimal construction, $\mathbb{SL}(n)$ exhibits pointwise negative, zero, and positive flag curvature around a common flagpole, while its mixed-curvature and curvature-coupling capacities are asymptotically maximal relative to the intrinsic geometric upper bound. Beyond geometry, its noncommutative group structure provides inherent order sensitivity, and its non-nilpotent Lie algebra admits nonzero nested Lie brackets at arbitrary depth, enabling deep order-aware composition. Empirically, $\mathbb{SL}(n)$ consistently outperforms a broad range of representation manifold baselines across graph benchmarks at different scales. It reduces average distortion over the strongest baselines by $44.3\%$ on KEGG and $40.5\%$ on HumanCyc, and improves Hits@20 by $42.8\%$ on OGBL-PPA. Experiments on Flickr30k-Order further support its ability to capture higher order dependencies from ordered composition. Together, these results show how a seemingly simple structural constraint can yield unexpectedly rich geometry, capacity, and composition within a unified representation space.

</details>


### [161] [Beyond Numerical Time Series: A Unified Benchmark for Multimodal Forecasting with Heterogeneous Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15087)
*Peng Chen,Zhihao Zhuang,Hongzhou Chen,Junhao Huang,Aiping Yang,Mengsen Wu,Yiding Liu,Xilin Dai,Zewei Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MUSE-Bench，一个面向异构上下文的多模态时间序列预测统一基准，涵盖14个数据集、8个领域和6类上下文，并在统一评估协议下系统评测多种预测范式，得出三条主要发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列预测基准以数值为中心，对塑造真实时间动态的上下文信息支持有限；现有多模态基准存在数据与上下文覆盖不足、评估设置割裂、过度依赖聚合评估等问题。因此需要一个统一、多模态、上下文异构的预测评测基准。

Method: 构建MUSE-Bench统一基准，包含14个数据集、覆盖8个领域与6类上下文（元数据、事件、节假日、新闻、图像、数值协变量）；在共享的非重叠预测窗口、共同目标观测以及一致的点预测与概率预测指标下，评测统计方法、数据特定方法、基础模型、多模态方法及通用LLM预测方法等多种范式；并通过消融实验考察上下文的影响。

Result: 主要发现有三：其一，数值型时间序列基础模型在总体排名中占主导，被评测的多模态基础模型Aurora落后于领先的数值TSFM，但优于所有被评测的数据特定模型；其二，消融显示外部上下文能提升四个被评测的上下文感知模型，而错误或时间错位的上下文会降低性能；其三，通用LLM作为直接预测器表现较差，LLM引导的细化未能带来一致改进。

Conclusion: MUSE-Bench支持对预测模型如何利用上下文进行系统评估，为未来多模态时间序列预测研究提供了基础。

Abstract: Most time series forecasting benchmarks remain numerical-centric and provide limited support for evaluating contextual information that shapes real-world temporal dynamics. Existing multimodal benchmarks also suffer from limited data and context coverage, fragmented evaluation settings, and overreliance on aggregate evaluation. In this paper, we propose \textbf{MUSE-Bench}, a unified benchmark for multimodal time series forecasting with heterogeneous context. It comprises fourteen datasets across eight domains and six types of context: metadata, events, holidays, news, images, and numerical covariates. We evaluate diverse forecasting paradigms, including statistical, data-specific, foundation, multimodal, and general-purpose LLM forecasting methods under shared non-overlapping forecast windows, common target observations, and consistent point and probabilistic metrics. Extensive experiments yield three main findings. First, numerical time series foundation models dominate the overall ranking, while Aurora, the evaluated multimodal foundation model, trails the leading numerical TSFMs but outperforms all evaluated data-specific models. Second, ablations show that external context improves the four evaluated context-aware models, whereas incorrect or temporally misaligned context degrades performance. Third, general-purpose LLMs perform poorly as direct forecasters, and LLM-guided refinement does not yield consistent improvements. MUSE-Bench enables systematic evaluation of how forecasting models utilize context and provides a foundation for future multimodal forecasting research.

</details>


### [162] [Refinement-based Flow Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15123)
*Bumgeun Park,Hyukjun Yang,Donghwan Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RFPO框架，通过Q引导样本精炼与自目标流匹配交替训练在线强化学习中的流策略，无需从目标分布直接采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流匹配策略表达能力强，但传统流匹配需从待建模分布中采样样本。当期望的动作分布仅由Q函数隐式定义时，从该分布直接采样通常不可解，这成为训练流策略的核心障碍。

Method: RFPO采用交替优化：1）用当前流策略从高斯噪声生成动作；2）通过有限步随机精炼过程，将动作向Q函数诱导的能量分布移动；3）将每个精炼后动作与其对应的初始噪声样本配对，作为流匹配训练的固定目标。如此反复精炼自身输出并从生成目标中学习，将Q引导融入策略，同时保留多模态表达能力，并给出分布动态的理论分析。

Result: 在六个连续控制任务上，RFPO几乎在所有任务上匹配或超越标准高斯策略基线；在六个具有多样几何结构的二维合成目标分布上，RFPO能捕捉复杂多模态结构且不发生模式崩溃。

Conclusion: RFPO有效解决了Q函数隐式定义分布下流策略训练的采样难题，在在线强化学习中兼顾性能与多模态表达能力，且理论分析与实验均验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Flow-based policies offer an expressive representation for online reinforcement learning, but conventional flow matching requires samples drawn from the distribution to be modeled. This poses a challenge when the desired action distribution is defined only implicitly by a Q-function, since directly sampling actions from the resulting distribution is generally intractable. We propose Refinement-Based Flow Policy Optimization (RFPO), a novel framework for training a flow policy in online reinforcement learning by alternating between Q-guided sample refinement and self-target flow matching. RFPO first generates actions from Gaussian noise using the current flow policy and then uses a finite-step stochastic refinement procedure to move them toward an energy-based distribution induced by the Q-function. Each refined action is then paired with its corresponding initial noise sample and used as a fixed target for flow-matching training. By repeatedly refining its own outputs and learning from the resulting targets, RFPO incorporates Q-guidance into the policy without requiring direct samples from the target distribution, while retaining the capacity to represent multiple action modes. We further provide a theoretical analysis of the distributional dynamics induced by RFPO. Across six continuous-control tasks, RFPO matches or outperforms a standard Gaussian-policy baseline on almost every task. Experiments on six synthetic two-dimensional target distributions with diverse geometries demonstrate that RFPO captures complex multimodal structure without mode collapse.

</details>


### [163] [Omni-Streaming Thinking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15128)
*Enjun Du,Siyi Liu,Ziyu Zheng,Jingyu Li,Yiwen Guo,Yongqi Zhang,Difan Zou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Omni-Streaming Thinking（OST），通过将证据、预测与"待验证主张"结构化分离存储并按模态分别核验，解决流式全模态模型中的"跨模态过早承诺"问题，在五个基准上平均相对提升超10%，并在新构建的OST-DiagBench上取得d-prime=2.95。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式全模态模型需基于已观测的视频块与同步音频决定"答什么、何时答"。视觉线索常在某句话或声音事件完整呈现之前就支持某种解释；若该解释作为事实进入记忆，即使后续音频出现矛盾，推理仍会继续沿用，造成"跨模态过早承诺"（premature cross-modal commitment）。

Method: 提出Omni-Streaming Thinking（OST），生成包含"已观测证据、未来证据预测、基于证据的主张"的结构化输出。每条主张初始标记为pending并关联一个未来验证区间；音频与视觉证据分开存储，在验证区间结束时OST用指定模态的证据核验该主张。一旦检出矛盾证据，触发refutation过程削弱该主张及其依赖状态的影响，并依据新证据引导状态更新；再由answer gate判断回答关键主张是否满足作答条件。基于冻结的Qwen3-Omni-30B-A3B-Instruct骨干网络加轻量适配实现。

Result: 在五个流式与视听基准上，OST平均相对超越最强开源基线10%以上。新提出的OST-DiagBench固定视频、编辑音频，测试一致、缺失、矛盾、共存与字幕-语音冲突等情形；OST达到d-prime=2.95，而开源基线最高仅1.38，同时减少了视觉诱发的听觉幻觉。

Conclusion: 将跨模态证据分离存储、对主张进行延迟核验与矛盾修正，并设置作答门控，可有效缓解流式全模态模型中的跨模态过早承诺问题，显著提升判别能力并抑制视觉诱导的听觉幻觉。

Abstract: Streaming omni-modal models must decide what and when to answer from the video chunks and synchronized audio observed so far. Visual cues often support an interpretation before an utterance or sound event is complete. If that interpretation enters memory as a fact, later reasoning can keep relaying it even after audio contradicts it. We call this failure premature cross-modal commitment. We propose Omni-Streaming Thinking (OST), which generates structured outputs that include evidence observed so far, forecasts of future evidence, and claims based on this evidence. Each claim is initially marked as pending and linked to a future verification interval. Audio and visual evidence are stored separately, and OST checks a claim against the evidence from the specified modality at the end of the verification interval. When contradictory evidence is detected, a refutation process reduces the influence of the claim and its dependent states, and then guides a state update using the new evidence. An answer gate decides whether the answer-critical claims meet the conditions for giving a response. Using a frozen Qwen3-Omni-30B-A3B-Instruct backbone with lightweight adaptation, OST outperforms the strongest open baselines on five streaming and audio-visual benchmarks by more than 10% relative on average. We also introduce OST-DiagBench, which holds video fixed and edits audio to test agreement, absence, contradiction, coexistence, and subtitle-speech conflict. OST reaches d-prime = 2.95, compared with at most 1.38 for open baselines, while reducing vision-induced auditory hallucinations.

</details>


### [164] [Multi-source Transfer Learning of Time Series with a Shapelet-based Distance Measure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15148)
*Jiseok Lee,Brian Kenji Iwana*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出免训练源域选择方法 Shapelet Matching，并构建基于 shapelet 相似度的多源迁移学习框架，用于时间序列分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列分类中迁移学习可缓解数据稀缺，但效果依赖源数据集选择；传统可迁移性估计需在每个候选源上完整预训练，计算开销大。

Method: 1) 从目标域与候选源域中提取判别性 shapelet；2) 通过比较 shapelet 集合量化数据集相似度（Shapelet Matching）；3) 依据相似度分数选择多个源数据集并合并为多源数据集进行预训练；4) 在目标任务上微调。

Result: 在 UCR Archive 的 128 个数据集上，用 temporal CNN 与 Transformer 两种骨干评估。多源预训练平均降低负迁移风险；Shapelet Matching 在 CNN 骨干上性能最强，在 patch-based Transformer 上保持竞争力，且无需为每个候选源预训练单独模型。

Conclusion: Shapelet Matching 是一种高效的免训练源域选择方法，结合多源预训练可有效缓解负迁移并提升时间序列分类迁移效果。

Abstract: Transfer learning is an effective technique for addressing data scarcity in deep learning for time series classification, but its success depends on the selection of source datasets. Conventional transferability estimation methods are often computationally expensive, as they require fully pre-training a model on each potential source dataset to assess its suitability. This paper introduces a novel, training-free source selection method named Shapelet Matching. Our approach first identifies discriminative shapelets from the target and potential source datasets. Then, Shapelet Matching quantifies dataset similarity by comparing the extracted sets of shapelets. To mitigate the risk of negative transfer from selecting an unsuitable single source, we introduce a multi-source transfer learning method. We select several source datasets based on their shapelet-based similarity scores, combine them into a single multi-source dataset, and use this aggregated dataset for pre-training. The model is then fine-tuned on the target task. We evaluated our method on 128 datasets from the UCR Archive using both temporal CNN and Transformer architectures. The empirical results demonstrate that our multi-source pre-training reduces the risk of negative transfer on average. Shapelet Matching achieves the strongest performance for the CNN backbone and remains competitive for patch-based Transformer architectures, while avoiding the cost of pre-training a separate model for every candidate source.

</details>


### [165] [Nearly Minimax-Optimal Regret for Linear Contextual Bandits with Arbitrary Adaptive Action Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15170)
*Tianyuan Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究具有任意动作菜单的随机线性上下文老虎机，建立了上下界匹配的遗憾界，并显著改进了对菜单规模K的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究任意动作菜单（可能依赖固定参数与交互历史）下的随机线性上下文老虎机，旨在刻画菜单规模K对遗憾的影响，并改进先前对K的依赖。

Method: 建立匹配（至多对数因子）的上界与下界分析：当2≤K≤d时证明上界Õ(K^{1/4}√(dT))，并在T≥d²时证明下界Ω(K^{1/4}√(dT))；当K≥d时分别给出包含min{√d,(d log K)^{1/4}}形式的上界与下界。

Result: 对2≤K≤d且T≥d²，上下界在对数因子内匹配，K的多项式依赖最优，相比先前Õ(√(dKT))上界将K依赖改进了K^{1/4}倍；对K≥d，多项式规模K时上下界均约为d^{3/4}√T，较标准Õ(d√T)改进d^{1/4}倍；当log K达到d量级时遗憾平滑恢复至d√T。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了任意动作菜单下随机线性上下文老虎机的最优（至多对数因子）遗憾刻画，明确了K的多项式最优依赖，并在各类K区间改进了现有速率。

Abstract: We study stochastic linear contextual bandits with arbitrary action menus that may depend on the fixed parameter and the interaction history. We establish matching upper and lower bounds, up to logarithmic factors. Let $d$ be the dimension, $K$ be the menu size, and $T$ the time horizon. For $2\le K\le d$, we prove an upper bound $\widetilde O(K^{1/4}\sqrt{dT})$. When $T\ge d^2$, we further prove a lower bound $Ω(K^{1/4}\sqrt{dT})$. Thus, for $T\ge d^2$ and $2\le K\le d$, the upper and lower bounds match up to logarithmic factors, and the polynomial dependence on $K$ is optimal. Compared with the previous $\widetilde O(\sqrt{dKT})$ bound, our upper bound improves the dependence on $K$ by a factor of $K^{1/4}$.
  For $K\ge d$, we prove an upper bound $\widetilde O_{d,T}\left(\sqrt{dT}\min\{\sqrt d,(d\log K)^{1/4}\}\right)$ and a lower bound $Ω\left(\sqrt{dT}\min\left\{\sqrt d,\left(\frac{d\log K}{\log(2d)}\right)^{1/4}\right\}\right)$. Here, $\widetilde O_{d,T}$ omits logarithmic factors only in $d$ and $T$. In particular, for polynomially large $K\ge d$, the upper and lower bounds both scale as $d^{3/4}\sqrt T$ up to logarithmic factors, improving the standard $\widetilde O(d\sqrt T)$ rate by a factor of $d^{1/4}$. As $K$ grows further, the regret smoothly recovers the $d\sqrt T$ scale once $\log K$ reaches order $d$.

</details>


### [166] [Temporal Self-Distillation: Faster Inference in Discrete Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15177)
*Shijian Xu,Andrea Miele,Metod Jazbec,Volker Roth,Eric Nalisnick,Ilija Bogunovic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出时间自蒸馏（TSD），一种on-policy单阶段方法，通过将早期去噪分布对齐到token最终提交时刻的分布，使扩散语言模型能更激进的并行解码，显著加速推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型（dLLMs）通过并行生成多个token实现快速推理，但过度激进的并行解码会导致性能严重下降，限制其加速潜力。

Method: TSD将模型在较早时间步的去噪分布蒸馏向其在该token最终提交时间步的分布，促使早期预测更好地预判模型最终输出。由于教师信号来自模型自身，无需离线教师生成，可无缝适用于base和post-trained策略。

Result: 在数学、规划和代码领域的七个基准上，TSD显著将速度-质量前沿推向低计算区间，实现了与离线蒸馏相当的加速效果。

Conclusion: TSD提供了一种简单、单阶段的dLLM加速方案，避免了复杂的两阶段流程，同时具备有竞争力的加速性能。

Abstract: Diffusion language models (dLLMs) promise fast inference by generating multiple tokens in parallel, but suffer severe performance degradation when parallel decoding is pushed too aggressively. We introduce Temporal Self-Distillation (TSD), a simple on-policy method that trains dLLMs for fast inference by distilling predictions across time. Specifically, TSD distills the model's denoising distribution at earlier timesteps toward its distribution at the final timestep at which a token is committed. This encourages earlier predictions to better anticipate the model's eventual output, enabling much more aggressive parallel decoding. Because its teacher signal comes from the model itself, TSD requires no offline teacher generation and applies seamlessly to both base and post-trained policies. Across seven benchmarks in mathematics, planning, and code, TSD substantially shifts the speed--quality frontier toward the low-compute regime. TSD thus provides a simple, single-stage approach to accelerating dLLMs, achieving speedups competitive with offline distillation while avoiding a complex two-stage pipeline.

</details>


### [167] [Rethinking Correctness for Uncertainty Estimation in Clinical Prediction with Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15180)
*Mingcheng Zhu,Jinning Liang,Tingting Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个双轴框架，用于评估临床预测中"正确性判据"的质量：与人类判断的一致性和对人类参照的UE性能的保真度。审计8种判据、3个任务、3个模型（450条标注预测），发现EM一致性最高、UE失真最低；判据选择可使误差检测AUROC变化达0.146并逆转UE方法排序，LLM-judge还选择性接受人类认定的错误输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言模型在电子健康记录与医学图像的临床预测中应用日益增多，识别不可靠预测对安全部署至关重要。不确定性估计（UE）可用于检测不可靠预测，但其评估依赖"正确性判据"来判断模型输出是否正确。若该判据与人类判断不一致，或扭曲下游UE性能，关于模型可靠性的结论会产生误导。因此需要系统审计正确性判据本身。

Method: 提出双轴评估框架：轴一衡量正确性判据与人类判断的一致程度，轴二衡量其对人类参照UE性能的保真度（失真程度）。在3个临床预测任务、3个模型上评估8种正确性判据，使用2位评审者标注的450条预测；并进一步在4种UE方法、23,254条临床预测上考察判据选择对误差检测性能的影响。

Result: 在审计任务中，规范精确匹配（EM）取得最高的人类一致性、最低的UE失真；基于BERT的匹配（BEM）与大模型评判（LLM-judge）也显示较强的人类一致性。跨4种UE方法与23,254条临床预测，判据选择使误差检测AUROC变化最高达0.146，并可逆转UE方法的相对排序。LLM-judge还会选择性接受无效或不确定输出，在30个人类认定的错误中接受了16个。

Conclusion: 正确性评估是临床UE评估的有机组成部分，而非中立的前置步骤；在比较UE方法之前，必须先验证正确性判据的效度，否则关于模型可靠性的结论可能被误导。

Abstract: Vision-language models are increasingly explored for clinical prediction from electronic health records and medical images, where identifying unreliable predictions is important for safe deployment. Uncertainty estimation (UE) enables detecting such predictions, but its evaluation depends on a correctness criterion that determines whether each model output is correct. If this criterion disagrees with human judgement or distorts downstream UE performance, conclusions about model reliability can be misleading. We introduce a two-axis framework that evaluates correctness criteria by their agreement with human judgements and fidelity to human-referenced UE performance. We assess eight criteria across three clinical prediction tasks and three models using 450 predictions annotated by two reviewers. Across the audited tasks, canonical exact matching (EM) achieved the highest observed human agreement and lowest UE distortion, while the BERT-based matching (BEM) and LLM-judge also showed strong human agreement. Across four UE methods and 23,254 clinical predictions, criterion choice changed error-detection AUROC by up to 0.146 and reversed the relative ranking of UE methods. The LLM-judge also selectively accepted invalid or uncertain outputs, accepting 16 of 30 such human-identified errors. These results demonstrate that correctness assessment is an integral component of clinical UE evaluation and should be validated before UE methods are compared.

</details>


### [168] [Convergence rates for generative drifting flows: fixed-scale obstructions and multihead acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15193)
*Arthur Stéphanovitch,Eddie Aamari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从理论上分析漂移模型（drifting models）的收敛速度，指出单一固定分辨率会导致细尺度特征"消失"、收敛极慢，并提出多头（multihead）方法在连续尺度上融合尺度归一化信息，证明其能恢复近标准参考分布下的指数收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 漂移模型在训练时进行渐进传输、生成时一步采样，是加速生成式AI的有前景路线。但作者希望先剥离有限数据与优化效应，在理想条件下厘清其漂移过程能否快速收敛到目标分布，并找出限制收敛速度的根本原因。

Method: 在理想条件下对漂移过程的收敛速率进行理论分析，考察其处理空间尺度的方式；指出单一固定分辨率会使目标分布的细尺度特征近乎不可见；进而提出多头方法，在连续的分辨率谱上组合尺度归一化的信息，并对该方法的收敛性给出证明。

Result: 固定分辨率下收敛极其缓慢，细尺度特征难以被捕捉；采用多头方法后，在接近标准参考分布的情形下可恢复指数收敛。

Conclusion: 固定分辨率是漂移模型收敛的关键瓶颈，而在连续尺度上融合尺度归一化的多头设计是一条实现更快速一步生成模型的简单途径。

Abstract: Drifting models offer a promising route to faster generative AI: they perform gradual transport during training, while generating new samples in a single step. This paper asks whether the underlying drifting process can converge rapidly to a target distribution under ideal conditions, before finite-data or optimization effects are introduced. We show that its convergence rate depends critically on how it handles spatial scale. With a single fixed resolution, fine-scale features of the target can become nearly invisible, leading to extremely slow convergence. We introduce a multihead approach that combines scale-normalized information across a continuum of resolutions. We prove that this multihead approach restores exponential convergence near standard reference distributions. These results identify fixed resolution as a key bottleneck and provide a simple route to faster one-step generative models.

</details>


### [169] [ProtoGuide: Prototype-Driven Guidance for Class-Conditional Graph Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15239)
*Salvatore Romano,Marco Grassia,Pietro Liò,Giuseppe Mangioni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 ProtoGuide：一种事后、与骨干无关的离散图扩散分类器引导框架，通过可微松弛邻接、冻结Siamese GNN与类原型评分实现目标定向引导，显著提升类条件图生成精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准类条件图扩散将类别信号嵌入去噪器训练中，使条件机制与模型耦合、无法灵活更换目标类别；而连续域的分类器引导因离散边采样导致梯度无法回传，难以直接迁移到离散图扩散。

Method: 在每个反向步，将去噪器逐边输出松弛为可微软邻接矩阵，用冻结的Siamese图神经网络编码，并与目标类原型及其最近竞争类原型打分；所得逐边梯度经余弦调度衰减后注入去噪器输出，全部组件保持冻结，仅通过更换原型重定向引导。

Result: 在五类真实网络与EDGE、DiGress两种架构骨干上，宏观分类准确率分别由50.7%升至73.5%、由73.6%升至83.8%，并在该配置下优于DiGress内置条件训练；无引导模型越弱处增益越大，类间增益不均。多数设置下逐图覆盖率保持较高，分布影响依类别而异；Best-of-N基线在充分过采样下可达同等精度但图多样性代价显著；独立初始化分类器、方向性检验与少样本分析支持引导的目标定向性与小支撑集稳健性。

Conclusion: ProtoGuide 以事后、骨干无关的方式在离散图扩散中重建了类分类器引导机制，实现无需重训练的目标类重定向与稳定精度提升，为离散图生成的可控条件化提供了通用方案。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion models are a prominent family for graph generation, but standard class-conditional mechanisms embed the class signal in the denoiser during training, tying the conditioning mechanism to the trained model. Classifier guidance avoids this coupling in continuous domains by steering a frozen model with a classifier's gradient, but discrete graph diffusion samples discrete edge states, so gradients cannot propagate through the sampled graph. We introduce ProtoGuide, a post-hoc, backbone-agnostic framework that recovers an analogous mechanism. At each reverse step the denoiser's per-edge output is relaxed into a differentiable soft adjacency, embedded by a frozen Siamese graph neural network, and scored against a target-class prototype and its nearest competitor; the resulting per-edge gradient, damped by a cosine schedule, is injected back into the denoiser output. All components stay frozen, so guidance is retargeted by supplying a different prototype. On five classes of real-world networks and two architecturally different backbones, EDGE and DiGress, ProtoGuide raises macro classification accuracy from 50.7% to 73.5% and from 73.6% to 83.8%, and outperforms DiGress's built-in conditional training under our configuration. Gains are largest where the unguided models are weakest, and are not uniform across classes. Per-graph coverage remains high in most settings, while distributional effects are class-dependent. A Best-of-N selection baseline matches this accuracy given enough oversampling, but at a substantial cost in graph diversity. An independently initialized classifier, a directionality test, and a few-shot analysis support target-directed steering and robustness to very small support sets.

</details>


### [170] [Bandits with Probing: Optimal Regret and the Limits of Winner Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15248)
*Yongjie Guan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文研究每轮最多探测 n 个臂中 k 个、观测其奖励最大值并带获胜标签的决策问题，给出两类 minimax 遗憾律及其匹配上下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心问题是"探测优势何时为学习付费"：当学习者每轮只能探测 k 个臂并只观测最大值（可能附带获胜标签）时，相较于全信息或单臂反馈，这种"探测优势"在何种奖励模型与反馈条件下能带来更优的遗憾阶，以及覆盖成本与学习成本如何权衡。

Method: 设定每轮探测至多 k 个臂、观测其奖励最大值于 [0,1]（winner 反馈下另获获胜标签），与最优固定臂比较。分别分析独立随机奖励、任意联合 i.i.d. 奖励、任意固定序列、以及给定块最大值单一符号对比等设定；构造 anytime 上界算法与允许任意自适应动作规模的下界论证。

Result: 得到两条 minimax 律：(1) 在独立随机奖励含 winner 反馈，或在给定块最大值单一符号对比的任意固定序列下，minimax 遗憾为 Φ_{n,k}(T)=min{(n-k)/n·T, (n-k)/k}（2≤k<n）；(2) 在 winner 反馈下，任意联合 i.i.d. 奖励与固定序列的 minimax 遗憾为 R_{n,k}(T)=(n-k)/n·min{T, (n+T)/k, sqrt(nT/k)}。两律均有通用常数与 anytime 上界。

Conclusion: 第一律将遗憾化为纯覆盖成本：同轮对比吸收稳定性成本，独立性支持精确重采样、其增益用于样本推进。第二律额外引入学习成本，在时域 n 处与覆盖成本相当；超过 nk 后，数值最大值优于仅用标签。下界允许任意自适应动作规模，说明结果具有普适性。

Abstract: A learner probes at most $k$ of $n$ arms each round, receives the maximum of their rewards in $[0,1]$, and competes with the best fixed arm. When does the probing advantage pay for learning? We determine two minimax laws. Under independent stochastic rewards with winner feedback (the maximum and a winning label), or on arbitrary fixed sequences given a single signed contrast between block maxima, the minimax regret has order $Φ_{n,k}(T)=\min\{\frac{n-k}{n}T,\frac{n-k}{k}\}$, $2\le k<n$. Under winner feedback, both arbitrary joint i.i.d. rewards and fixed sequences have minimax regret of order $R_{n,k}(T)=\frac{n-k}{n}\min\{T,\frac{n+T}{k},\sqrt{\frac{nT}{k}}\}$. Both laws have universal constants and anytime upper bounds. The first reduces regret to a pure coverage cost: same-round contrasts absorb the stability cost, and independence permits exact resampling whose gains fund sample advancement. The second adds a learning cost that becomes comparable to coverage at horizon $n$; beyond $nk$, numerical maxima improve over labels alone. The lower bound allows every adaptive action size.

</details>


### [171] [BioDCASE: Active Learning for Bioacoustics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15255)
*Ben McEwen,Rupa Kurinchi-Vendhan,Shiqi Zhang,Lukas Rauch,Marek Herde,Sara Beery*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了2026年BioDCASE生物声学主动学习挑战赛，对10种采样方法在4个子数据集上进行了系统评测，最优方法在学习曲线下面积上比随机采样平均高26.4%，且组合多种采集信号、多样性选择及批次内去冗余策略更有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生态监测日益依赖机器学习模型，其性能取决于标注数据的质量与数量，而被动声学监测中数据量巨大、标注成本高昂。主动学习可通过优先选择待标注样本缓解这一瓶颈，但已有方法因模型、预算、评价指标和数据集各异而难以比较进展，因此需要一个系统性评测平台。

Method: 组织2026年BioDCASE生物声学主动学习挑战赛，构建包含陆地与海洋数据的四个子数据集，对来自7支团队的10种采样方法进行统一评测，采用学习曲线下面积等指标，在相同标注预算下与随机采样对比。

Result: 最优方法在四个子集平均的学习曲线下面积比随机采样高26.4%；子集间性能差异显著，最佳提交在HSN子集上比随机采样提升67.1%，在ATBFL子集上提升8%；排名靠前的方案融合了多种采集信号，基于多样性的选择优于纯不确定性采样；从多样性向不确定性过渡及降低采集批次内冗余可改善训练；初步证据显示较大采集批次在标注后期可能更有益。

Conclusion: 该挑战赛为生物声学主动学习提供了系统化评测基准，表明组合多种采集信号、多样性选择与去冗余策略是有效方向，并提示采集策略可随标注进程动态调整，为后续AL方法设计提供了实证指导。

Abstract: Ecological monitoring increasingly relies on machine learning models, whose performance depends on the quality and quantity of labelled data. However, obtaining these labels is costly, particularly in passive acoustic monitoring, where vast amounts of data are collected but only a small proportion can feasibly be annotated. Active learning addresses this bottleneck by prioritizing which samples should be labelled. However, progress is difficult to measure, because published methods are evaluated under different models, budgets, evaluation metrics and datasets. To address this challenge, we present the 2026 Active Learning for Bioacoustics BioDCASE challenge: a systematic evaluation of sampling methods designed to identify effective AL strategies. Participant methods were evaluated across four subsets composed of terrestrial and marine data. Across ten proposed sampling methods from seven teams, the top-ranked method achieved an area under the learning curve 26.4 % higher than random sampling at the same annotation budget, averaged over four data subsets. Significant variation in performance was observed across subsets, with the top-performing submission achieving a 67.1 % gain for the HSN subset over random sampling and a gain of 8 % for the ATBFL subset. Top-ranking submissions combined multiple acquisition signals, and diversity-based selection outperformed pure uncertainty sampling. Furthermore, there is evidence that transitioning from diversity-based to uncertainty-based selection and explicitly reducing redundancy within acquisition batches improve model training. There is also initial evidence that larger acquisition batch sizes may be increasingly beneficial later in the labelling process.

</details>


### [172] [Learning CNF Formulas from Uniform Random Solutions: Near-Tight Sample Complexity for Valiant's Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15268)
*Weiming Feng,Yixiao Yu,Yiyao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了Valiant用于学习CNF公式的算法，在局部引理（local lemma）机制下分析其样本复杂度，并给出了匹配的下界与信息论最优性结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Valiant提出的学习n变量CNF公式（子句大小k、变量度d）的算法已有长期研究，但该算法在局部引理机制下的样本复杂度是否紧确（即上界与下界是否匹配）此前并不清楚。本文旨在严格刻画该算法的样本复杂度，并判定其最优性。

Method: 在i.i.d.均匀随机解设定与局部引理机制下，对固定的t≥1，当k≳(1+1/t)log d时分析Valiant算法的总变差误差ε所需的样本复杂度；对t>1构造与算法上界匹配的下界；对t=1（覆盖0<t<1）给出信息论下界来建立最优性。

Result: 在k≳(1+1/t)log d条件下，Valiant算法以Õ(n^⌈t⌉/ε)的样本复杂度达到总变差误差ε；对t>1证明了与上界匹配的算法下界；在t=1时通过信息论下界ÕΩ(n/ε)证明算法样本复杂度在对数因子内最优。

Conclusion: 本文精确刻画了Valiant算法在局部引理机制下的样本复杂度：t>1时上下界匹配，t=1时达到对数因子意义下的信息论最优。

Abstract: We revisit Valiant's algorithm (Commun. ACM'84) for learning $n$-variable CNF formulas with clause size $k$ and variable degree $d$ from i.i.d. uniform random solutions in the local lemma regime. For fixed $t\geq1$, under $k\gtrsim(1+1/t)\log d$, Valiant's algorithm achieves total variation error $\varepsilon$ with $\widetilde{O}(n^{\lceil t \rceil}/\varepsilon)$ sample complexity. For $t>1$, we prove a matching lower bound for Valiant's algorithm. At $t=1$ (covering $0<t<1$), we show Valiant's algorithm has optimal sample complexity up to logarithmic factors by an information-theoretic lower bound $\widetildeΩ(n/\varepsilon)$.

</details>


### [173] [Draining Fictitious Knots: Restoring Distance-Awareness Guarantees for High-Dimensional Spline Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15274)
*Masoud Ataei,Mohammad Javad Khojasteh,Vikas Dhiman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文识别了DAREK在高维下因"虚构结点"导致不确定性估计失效的问题，并提出"排流不确定性机制"恢复距离感知，实验中将采样距离感知从85%提升至98-99%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KANs配合样条激活在可解释函数逼近上有前景，DAREK通过距离感知误差界实现高效的不确定性量化；但DAREK采用按输入维度分量的表述，在高维下诱导出的结点可能不对应真实训练数据（虚构结点），使不确定性估计在远离真实观测处仍报告低不确定性，违反距离感知保证。

Method: 精确识别该失效模式并刻画其几何结构；提出排流不确定性机制（drainage uncertainty mechanism），从任一虚构结点区域向最近的真实结点构造单调递减的不确定性路径，作为实用启发式修正，在高维设置下恢复距离感知。

Result: 在2D合成基准与100维人脸数据集上，排流方法将采样距离感知（SDA）从85%提升至98-99%，在更低计算成本下达到与高斯过程相当的水平。

Conclusion: 排流方法缓解了虚构结点失效模式，在高维下恢复理论距离感知，是一种计算高效的不确定性修正方案。

Abstract: Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (KANs) with spline activations have recently shown promise for interpretable function approximation. Distance-Aware Error for Kolmogorov Networks (DAREK) introduces a computationally efficient bottom-up approach to uncertainty quantification by equipping KANs with distance-aware error bounds; yet, in high-dimensional settings, the theoretical guarantees can be weakened by the emergence of fictitious knots. Inspired by the Kolmogorov-Arnold representation theorem, DAREK adopts a componentwise formulation in which each input dimension is treated separately; as a result, induced knot locations may appear in the combined input space without corresponding to actual training data. These fictitious knots mislead the DAREK uncertainty estimator into reporting low uncertainty far from any real observation, violating the distance-awareness guarantee. We identify this failure mode precisely, characterize its geometric structure, and propose a drainage uncertainty mechanism that restores distance-awareness by constructing a monotonically decreasing uncertainty path from any fictitious knot region toward the nearest real knot. The proposed drainage method provides a practical heuristic correction that mitigates the fictitious-knot failure mode while restoring theoretical distance-awareness in high-dimensional settings. Experiments on a 2D synthetic benchmark and a 100-dimensional face dataset show that drainage raises sampled distance-awareness (SDA) from 85% to 98-99%, matching Gaussian processes at lower computational cost.

</details>


### [174] [Impute-EM: Native Mixed-State Diffusion Models for Heterogeneous Data Imputation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15284)
*Sergei Kholkin,Kirill Sokolov,Dmitry Baranchuk,Evgeny Burnaev,Alexander Korotin*
