Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Impute-EM 框架，通过 EM 式交替"用当前模型填补缺失—在完整数据上重训扩散主干"，并在混合类型异构数据上使用原生混合状态扩散（高斯+掩码类别），无需 one-hot 松弛，取得最佳分布保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构数据中数值、类别、二值变量常共存，缺失值普遍存在。现有填补方法（尤其扩散类）用 one-hot 松弛等连续替代物处理离散变量，导致模型状态空间与数据"混合离散-连续"结构不匹配。

Method: 提出 Impute-EM：EM 风格框架，交替执行 E 步（用当前模型填补缺失项）与 M 步（在补全数据上重拟合扩散主干）；用面向异构数据的原生混合状态扩散主干实例化，组合高斯分量与掩码类别分量，避免 one-hot 松弛。理论上在精确设定下刻画更新过程，证明观测的按掩码索引的边际分布收敛到目标，同时指出仅凭不完整观测一般无法辨识完整数据分布。

Result: 在混合类型表格填补任务上取得最佳分布保真度（下游建模所依赖）；文本填补作为对原生离散主干的受控验证。

Conclusion: 原生混合状态扩散结合 EM 式交替训练，可避免连续替代物带来的结构性错配，提升异构数据填补的分布保真度；但完整数据分布从缺失观测中不可辨识，是固有的理论限制。

Abstract: Missing values are ubiquitous in heterogeneous data mining, where numerical, categorical, and binary variables often coexist. Many imputation methods, especially diffusion-based ones, treat discrete variables through continuous surrogates such as one-hot relaxations rather than modeling them natively. This creates a mismatch between the model state space and the mixed discrete and continuous structure of the data. We propose Impute-EM, an Expectation Maximization style framework that alternates between imputing missing entries with the current model and refitting a diffusion backbone on completed data. We instantiate Impute-EM with native mixed-state diffusion backbones for heterogeneous data, combining Gaussian and masked categorical components without one-hot relaxations. In exact settings, we characterize the update and show that the observed mask-indexed marginals match the targets at the limit, while making explicit that the full data distribution is generally non-identifiable from incomplete observations alone. Empirically, Impute-EM delivers the best distributional fidelity on mixed-type tabular imputation, on which downstream modeling relies, with text imputation serving as a controlled validation of the native discrete backbone.

</details>


### [175] [Admissable: Training Reinforcement Learning Agents against Adversarial Missingness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15297)
*Paul Stahlhofen,Luca Hermes,Tim Kochs,Markus Vieth,Barbara Hammer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文关注强化学习中的对抗性特征缺失问题，形式化定义了该场景，提出对抗训练算法，并在三个MuJoCo环境中验证其提升了鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为使强化学习算法能应用于现实世界，必须在不利条件下仍保证安全。现实中出现的一种挑战是对抗性特征缺失：对手通过遮挡智能体观测中的特征来尽可能降低其性能。此前工作缺乏对该问题的形式化定义及其与相关概念（ℓ∞范数对抗扰动、缺失数据学习）的区分。

Method: 作者为强化学习形式化定义了对抗性缺失性，并将其与ℓ∞范数有界对抗扰动及缺失数据学习等相关概念进行比较；随后开发了一种对抗训练算法以提升对该缺失的鲁棒性。

Result: 在三个MuJoCo基准环境上的实验表明，该算法能有效提升对抗性缺失下的鲁棒性。与使用随机均匀缺失训练的基线相比，该方法在全部三个任务上均取得更好鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过形式化对抗性特征缺失并提出相应对抗训练算法，本文证明了该方法可提升强化学习在观测特征被对抗遮挡时的鲁棒性，为面向真实场景的安全强化学习提供了可行方向。

Abstract: In order to make Reinforcement Learning algorithms applicable in real world scenarios, safety must be ensured even under adverse operating conditions. In this work, we consider the challenge of adversarial feature missingness: a scenario in which an adversary occludes features from the agent's observation in order to reduce performance as much as possible. We formally define adversarial missingness for Reinforcement Learning and compare it to the related concepts of $\ell_\infty$-norm bounded adversarial perturbations and learning with missing data. We develop an adversarial training algorithm and show its effectiveness in increasing robustness against adversarial missingness on three MuJoCo benchmark environments. Compared to a baseline trained with random uniform missingness, our method achieves better robustness on all three tasks.

</details>


### [176] [When Correlations Mislead: Confounder-Aware Multi-View Urban Region Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15305)
*Sean Bin Yang,Ying Sun,Zongyi Xu,Tung Kieu,Jilin Hu,Bin Yang,Kristian Torp,Hua Lu,Torben Bach Pedersen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CURE：一种混淆感知的多视角城市区域表征学习框架，通过分离共享潜因子并抑制其影响，提升预测性能与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多视角城市区域表征方法主要通过增强视角间交互提升区域嵌入，但忽视了视角特有的区域结构，并可能传播由共享潜因子（混淆因素）诱导的相关性，从而降低下游预测的稳定性。

Method: CURE 首先以各视角自身的区域图结构进行编码，估计共享潜成分，并在跨视角交互前削减其投影影响；随后采用分层图感知融合模块，结合局部与全局区域上下文，聚合残差视角表征。

Result: 在三个真实城市数据上的实验表明，CURE 提升了预测性能，在视角缺失和含噪情况下保持鲁棒，并通过共享成分分离与上下文相关视角加权实现可靠的跨视角集成。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模并去除共享混淆成分，CURE 有效缓解多视角交互中的相关性传播问题，为城市区域表征学习提供了更稳定、鲁棒的融合方案。

Abstract: Urban region representation learning commonly combines heterogeneous data sources, such as mobility flows, points of interest, and land-use information, to support tasks including mobility analysis, public safety forecasting, and service demand estimation. Existing multi-view methods typically improve region embeddings by strengthening interactions across views. However, such methods often overlook view-specific regional structures and may propagate correlations induced by shared latent factors, which can reduce the stability of downstream predictions. To overcome this major limitation, we propose CURE, a confounder-aware framework for multi-view urban region representation learning. CURE first encodes each view with its regional graph structure, estimates a shared latent component, and then reduces its projected influence before cross-view interaction. A hierarchical graph-aware fusion module subsequently aggregates the residual view representations using local and global regional contexts Experiments on three real-world cities show that CURE improves predictive performance, remains robust under missing and noisy input views, and provides reliable cross-view integration through shared component separation and context-dependent view weighting.

</details>


### [177] [The Universe of Universes: Benefit Yield Functions, Implosion Thresholds, and Infrastructure-Aware Optimization in Multi-LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15314)
*Danielle Franklin,Vasu Raj Jain*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文提出"宇宙之宇宙"(UoU)框架，将主流LLM生态视为结构化检索语料，并形式化定义了集合性能的收益产出函数(BYF)与内爆阈值θ*。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM集成与多智能体混合系统仅将模型视为响应者并聚合输出，未研究性能随集合规模N在全模型宇宙中的变化规律；基准研究只确认单模型性能平台期，模型崩溃文献只涉及迭代训练导致的分布退化。二者均未形式化集合级内爆阈值、生态系统级的认知遗传漂移(EHD)，也未将AI制造速度作为θ*的协变量。

Method: 提出UoU框架，将整个主流LLM生态作为结构化检索语料库，构建面向跨模型检索增强生成的组合式自动推理(AR)与机器学习(ML)架构。

Result: 给出收益产出函数(BYF)的形式化刻画，即集合中每新增一个模型所带来的边际性能增益，并识别出内爆阈值θ*——BYF穿越零点、总体性能开始下降时的集合规模。

Conclusion: 该框架对国防部多模型AI采办政策及新兴的AI赋能系统测试科学具有直接启示意义。

Abstract: We introduce the Universe of Universes (UoU) framework, which treats the full ecosystem of major large language models (LLMs) as a structured retrieval corpus and proposes a compositional Automated Reasoning (AR) and Machine Learning (ML) architecture for cross-model retrieval-augmented generation. The central contribution is the formal characterization of the Benefit Yield Function (BYF), the marginal performance gain per additional model added to an ensemble, and the identification of the implosion threshold θ*: the ensemble size at which BYF crosses zero and aggregate performance begins to degrade. Existing LLM ensemble and mixture-of-agents systems treat models as responders and aggregate outputs, but do not study performance as a function of ensemble size N across the full model universe. Benchmark research confirms performance plateaus at the individual model level; model collapse literature establishes that iterative training on AI-generated outputs degrades individual model distributions. Neither body of work formalizes the ensemble-level implosion threshold, models Epistemic Hereditary Drift (EHD) at the ecosystem level, or treats AI manufacturing velocity as a co-variable of θ*. The framework has direct implications for DoD multi-model AI acquisition policy and the emerging science of testing AI-enabled systems.

</details>


### [178] [Robust and Efficient Communication for Multi-Agent Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15361)
*Rafael Pina,Varuna De Silva,Corentin Artaud*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出MARC框架，利用条件互信息原理与注意力架构及消息正则化机制，在多智能体强化学习中生成信息丰富且鲁棒的消息，并在通信瓶颈与有损信道下显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式智能中有效通信至关重要，但如何确保生成的消息既具信息量又能抵抗物理约束（如带宽瓶颈、有损信道）仍是重大挑战。现有方法在受限通信环境下难以兼顾信息性与鲁棒性。

Method: 提出多智能体正则化通信（MARC）框架，受条件互信息的信息论原理启发；采用基于注意力的架构，并引入独特的消息正则化机制，最小化对未来系统状态的不确定性，从而诱导学习高代表性的通信协议；在严格通信瓶颈与有损信道条件下进行评估。

Result: MARC在复杂协作任务中显著优于最先进方法；深入分析消息特性表明，即使在显著数据压缩下MARC仍保持高运行性能。

Conclusion: MARC为在资源受限环境中部署智能体提供了可扩展路径，验证了其在自主机器人网络与去中心化系统中的应用潜力。

Abstract: Effective communication is a cornerstone of distributed intelligence in Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (MARL), yet ensuring that generated messages are both informative and robust to physical constraints remains a significant challenge. This paper introduces Multi-Agent Regularized Communication (MARC), a novel framework inspired by information-theoretic principles of conditional mutual information. MARC employs an attention-based architecture coupled with a unique message regularization mechanism designed to minimize uncertainty regarding future system states, thereby inducing the learning of highly representative communication protocols. Crucially, we evaluate MARC under stringent communication bottlenecks and lossy channels, simulating the real-world constraints of autonomous robotic networks and decentralized systems. Our results demonstrate that MARC significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods in complex cooperative domains. Furthermore, we provide a deep analysis of message characteristics, proving that MARC maintains high operational performance even under significant data compression, offering a scalable path for deploying intelligent agents in resource-constrained environments.

</details>


### [179] [Representing Clinical Conditions on Vital Signs from Healthy Individuals using Latent Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15379)
*Rafael Pina,Varuna De Silva,Mindula Illeperuma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于条件变分自编码器的深度生成模型，用ICU数据训练，将健康人生命体征数据重塑造为符合特定临床病症模式，并设计距离度量验证其标签对齐性优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗等复杂信号处理场景中机器学习模型需大量数据训练，但常无法获取具代表性的数据集，尤其是针对特定临床病症的数据稀缺。

Method: 基于条件变分自编码器（CVAE）的深度生成模型，使用公开ICU数据集训练，在健康个体采集的生命体征数据上评估；并提出一种距离度量衡量生成样本与目标临床标签的对齐程度。

Result: 模型不仅能学习ICU数据的潜在动态，还能将健康个体的生命体征数据重塑为与特定临床病症相符的模式；所提距离度量下，生成样本较测试基线更贴合目标临床标签。

Conclusion: 条件变分自编码器可用于数据增强，将健康个体数据转化为目标临床病症模式，缓解医疗机器学习中代表性数据不足的问题。

Abstract: Machine learning can be crucial to help scale complex signal processing applications in scenarios such as healthcare. However, these machine learning models need rich datasets to be trained and there are often cases where it is not possible to access representative datasets. In this paper, we propose a deep generative model based on conditional variational autoencoders with the objective of augmenting the vital signs of healthy individuals in a way that mimics the patterns of a certain clinical condition. More specifically, we use a publicly available ICU (Intensive Care Unit) dataset to train our model and then evaluate it using the vital data that we have collected from healthy individuals. Our results demonstrate that the proposed model can not only learn the underlying dynamics of the ICU data but, more importantly, can reshape our collected data from healthy individuals in a way that is aligned with the vital signs of a certain clinical condition. We propose a distance metric that shows how our model can generate samples that are more aligned with the intended clinical labels when compared to the tested baselines.

</details>


### [180] [CodeTS: Verifiable Text-to-Time Series Generation via Executable Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15393)
*Xudong Yuan,Shunyu Liu,Tongya Zheng,Huiping Zhuang,Mingli Song,Kaixuan Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CodeTS框架，将文本到时间序列生成重构为"文本→代码→时间序列"的可验证流程，用代码作为中间接口，并通过多阶段执行奖励实现RLVR训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Text-to-TS方法缺乏从文本描述中显式推导生成逻辑的机制来指导时间序列合成；且缺少真实代码标注。

Method: 1) 将文本时间描述映射到显式代码空间，由可执行代码规定文本需求如何塑造目标时序模式，再通过代码执行得到时间序列；2) 利用结构化时间属性构建对齐的Text-Code-TS三元组进行监督初始化；3) 设计多阶段基于执行的奖励，验证格式有效性、代码可执行性与时序质量，使真实Text-TS对可为RLVR提供训练信号。

Result: 在短、中、长三种生成长度的八个基准上，CodeTS作为强零样本方案，优于基于LLM的基线，平均结果优于在目标数据集上训练的监督生成基线。

Conclusion: 以代码为中间接口的可验证框架能有效提升Text-to-TS生成，并借助执行反馈实现无需代码标注的可扩展训练。

Abstract: Text-to-Time Series Generation (Text-to-TS) provides a promising paradigm for synthesizing time series from natural language, enabling scenario-specific generation when real observations are scarce or costly to acquire. However, existing methods typically lack an explicit mechanism for deriving generation logic from textual descriptions to guide time series synthesis. In this paper, we propose CodeTS, a verifiable framework that uses code as an intermediate generation interface, reformulating Text-to-TS generation as a Text-to-Code-to-TS process. CodeTS first maps textual temporal descriptions into an explicit code space, where executable code specifies how textual requirements shape target temporal patterns, and then obtains the time series through code execution. To learn this code generation process reliably without real code annotations, CodeTS constructs aligned Text-Code-TS triplets from structured temporal attributes for supervised initialization. More importantly, we further design multi-stage execution-based rewards that verify format validity, code executability, and time series quality, enabling real Text-TS pairs to provide training signals for Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR). Extensive experiments on eight benchmarks across short, medium, and long generation lengths demonstrate that CodeTS provides a strong zero-shot solution for Text-to-TS generation, outperforming LLM-based baselines and achieving better averaged results than supervised generative baselines trained on the target datasets.

</details>


### [181] [Single-condition neural solvers encode transferable response spaces for parametric differential equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15432)
*Wenbo Cao,Weiwei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出线性化子空间迁移(LST)与主动迁移建模(ATM)：利用单条件训练的神经解模型输出雅可比作为可复用响应空间，通过最小化目标PDE系统残差跨条件恢复解；ATM以迁移后残差为覆盖指标主动获取额外响应空间。六类系统验证有效，较物理约束算子基线降低误差与离线成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参数化PDE的算子学习通常需在指定域上构建全局模型，依赖跨条件数据或代价高昂的物理约束训练。论文动机是寻找一种无需跨条件数据、可复用的局部参数化建模方式，降低离线构建成本并提升跨条件迁移的精度与效率。

Method: 提出LST：在单一条件下训练的神经解模型的输出雅可比定义了一个可复用的响应空间，通过在响应空间坐标上最小化目标PDE系统残差来恢复目标解。由于单一响应空间覆盖有限，进一步提出ATM，利用迁移后残差作为覆盖度指标，选择性从额外单条件模型获取响应空间进行富化。

Result: 在六类系统上，单条件响应空间可支撑跨条件迁移；当新增空间扩展了表示能力时，富化能提升精度。与所评估的物理信息算子基线相比，ATM降低了误差与离线构建成本，代表性案例中获得数量级精度提升，目标适配时间为毫秒至秒级。

Conclusion: 结果表明神经解模型可作为可复用的局部参数化模型，为参数化PDE的跨条件求解提供了低构建成本、快速适配的迁移框架。

Abstract: Operator learning for parametric partial differential equations (PDEs) typically builds global models over prescribed domains, requiring cross-condition data or costly physics-constrained training. Here we show that the output Jacobian of a neural solution model trained at one condition defines a reusable response space for cross-condition solution variations. We introduce Linearized Subspace Transfer (LST) to exploit this space and recover target solutions by minimizing the target PDE-system residual over response-space coordinates. Because any single response space has finite coverage, Active Transfer Modeling (ATM) uses post-transfer residuals as coverage indicators to selectively acquire response spaces from additional single-condition models. Across six systems, single-condition response spaces supported cross-condition transfer, with enrichment improving accuracy when added spaces expanded representation capacity. Relative to evaluated physics-informed operator baselines, ATM reduced error and offline construction cost, with orders-of-magnitude accuracy gains in representative cases and millisecond-to-second target adaptation. These results establish neural solvers as reusable local parametric models.

</details>


### [182] [On the role of the tokenizer in ECG transformer models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15433)
*Jiawei Li,Fabio Bonassi,Johan Sundström,Thomas B. Schön,Antônio H. Ribeiro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文比较8种ECG分词策略在4种Transformer类骨干上的表现，发现与心电形态对齐的分词（Median-beat、HeartLang）在预测性能和内存效率上均显著优于逐点/分块分词。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分词决定了输入ECG Transformer的生理内容以及注意力作用的序列，但其对性能与效率的影响尚未被系统隔离研究。

Method: 在CPSC2018九标签分类任务上，对比8种分词策略，跨Transformer、Informer、Reformer、FEDformer四种骨干，并控制输入投影与主干容量以隔离分词构造的影响。

Result: Median-beat与HeartLang分词的宏平均AUC达0.893和0.889，逐点与分块分词仅为0.822和0.824；池化两种生理感知表示带来8.2%相对提升；平均序列长度由1250降至158，峰值训练内存由5.21GB降至0.27GB。

Conclusion: 将分词与ECG形态对齐可在不增加骨干容量的前提下同时提升预测性能与内存效率。

Abstract: Tokenization determines both the physiological content presented to an ECG Transformer and the sequence over which attention operates. We compare eight tokenization strategies across Transformer, Informer, Reformer, and FEDformer on the nine-label CPSC2018 classification task. The input projection and principal backbone capacity are controlled to isolate the effect of token construction. Median-beat and HeartLang tokenization achieve mean macro-AUCs of 0.893 and 0.889 across the four backbones, compared with 0.822 and 0.824 for point-wise and patch-wise tokenization. Pooling the two physiology-aware representations yields an 8.2% relative improvement in macro-AUC. They also reduce mean sequence length from 1,250 to 158 tokens and mean peak training memory from 5.21 to 0.27 GB. The results show that aligning tokens with ECG morphology can improve both predictive performance and memory efficiency without increasing backbone capacity. The source code is available on https://github.com/LeeJarvis996/ecg_tokenizer.

</details>


### [183] [GSLAD: Prototype-Regularized Graph Structure Learning for Multivariate Time Series Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15483)
*Zepeng Zhang,Fuad Khuri,Keivan Faghih Niresi,Olga Fink*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GSLAD——原型正则化图结构学习框架，利用变量间结构偏差进行多变量时间序列异常检测，在四个工业基准上取得强性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无监督多变量时间序列异常检测通常依赖预测、重构或表征差异。但工业故障可能先改变变量间的结构模式，而单变量轨迹仍接近正常，导致异常信号微弱。

Method: 两阶段训练：第一阶段用预测监督优化条件感知图学习器与基于图的预测器；随后将推断的正常图聚类为多个结构原型，代表不同正常工况，并用边级变异性刻画结构不确定性。第二阶段以对原型的偏差正则化图学习器，促使结构模式稳定且具备工况特异性。推理时将不确定性归一化的结构偏差与预测偏差结合进行异常评分。

Result: 在四个工业基准数据集上整体表现优异，并验证了结构偏差对异常检测与诊断的有效性。

Conclusion: 结构偏差可作为有效异常信号，原型正则化的图结构学习能够提升多变量时间序列异常检测与诊断性能。

Abstract: Unsupervised multivariate time series anomaly detection methods typically identify anomalies through forecasting, reconstruction, or representation discrepancies. However, industrial faults may first alter inter-variable structural patterns while individual trajectories remain close to normal, resulting in weak anomaly signals. In this paper, we propose GSLAD, a prototype-regularized graph structure learning framework that uses structural deviations for anomaly scoring. GSLAD adopts a two-phase training strategy. First, a condition-aware graph learner and a graph-based forecaster are optimized with predictive supervision. The inferred normal graphs are then clustered into multiple structural prototypes representing different normal operating regimes, with edge-wise variability characterizing structural uncertainty. Deviations from these prototypes regularize the graph learner in the second phase, encouraging stable and regime-specific structural patterns. During inference, uncertainty-normalized structural deviation is combined with predictive deviation for anomaly scoring. Experiments on four industrial benchmarks demonstrate strong overall performance of GSLAD and confirm the effectiveness of structural deviation for anomaly detection and diagnosis.

</details>


### [184] [Data-driven Prediction of Satellite-observed Avalanche Activity from Snowpack Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15485)
*Jakob Grahn,Filippo Maria Bianchi,Bert Kruyt,Karsten Müller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究用SNOWPACK模拟驱动Transformer，预测Sentinel-1 SAR探测的雪崩活动指数（SAR-AAI），区域尺度预测与参考值相关系数r=0.803，但格点尺度较弱（r=0.549），未验证业务预报能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 雪崩预报依赖雪盖状况与近期雪崩活动的信息，但大范围山区野外观测稀疏，难以获取空间连续的活动数据，因此需要探索能否用物理雪盖模型模拟来间接预测卫星观测的雪崩活动。

Method: 汇编挪威及瑞典部分地区5个冬季的Sentinel-1雪崩探测结果，配套使用数值天气预报驱动、20×20 km网格、含不同海拔与预设坡度的SNOWPACK模拟。构建Transformer模型，以5天SNOWPACK输出预测次日的SAR-AAI（该指数按碎屑大小加权、将探测分散到可能发生日期并除以模拟堆积面积归一化）。用4个冬季训练、1个冬季验证。

Result: 在完整6天时段平均后，区域平均预测值与参考SAR-AAI相关系数r=0.803；模型能跟随时间和空间的总体变化，但预测更平滑并低估最强活动。同一平均方式下，20 km格点尺度一致性较弱，r=0.549。结果来自单次训练，未在完全独立的冬季上评估；卫星观测本身存在漏检、误检和时序不确定。

Conclusion: 结果不足以确立业务预报能力，但表明区域尺度的SNOWPACK模拟包含关于卫星观测雪崩活动大范围变化的信息，可为大区域雪崩活动监测提供潜在途径。

Abstract: Avalanche forecasting requires knowledge of snowpack conditions and recent avalanche activity, but field observations are sparse across large mountain regions. We explore whether SNOWPACK simulations can predict avalanche activity mapped by synthetic aperture radar (SAR). We compiled five winters of Sentinel-1 avalanche detections across Norway and parts of Sweden, alongside SNOWPACK simulations forced by numerical weather predictions on a 20 x 20 km grid at different elevations and predefined slope angles. A transformer used five days of SNOWPACK outputs to predict the following day's SAR-detected Avalanche Activity Index (SAR-AAI). This index weights larger debris more heavily, spreads detections across possible occurrence dates and normalises by modelled runout area. The model was trained on four winters and evaluated on one validation winter. Regional mean predicted and reference SAR-AAI correlated at r = 0.803 after averaging over complete six-day periods. The model followed broad changes in time and space but produced smoother predictions and underestimated the strongest activity. Agreement at the 20 km cell scale was weaker (r = 0.549) after the same averaging. These results come from a single training run without evaluation on an untouched winter. Satellite observations also contain missed and false detections and uncertain timing. The results therefore do not establish operational forecast skill, but suggest that regional SNOWPACK simulations contain information about broad variations in satellite-observed avalanche activity.

</details>


### [185] [Rotation-Based Subspace Tracking for Robust Kernel PCA on Streaming Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15488)
*Kris Lokere,John Fossaceca*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于旋转更新的在线鲁棒核PCA方法，通过在RKHS中将子空间估计向每个新到达特征向量旋转（而非仅依赖梯度下降），并结合鲁棒影响函数调节旋转幅度以抑制离群点，从而更快地动态跟踪流式数据中的非线性子空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际数据集常随时间变化（数据漂移）或以流式方式逐样本到达，难以批处理；真实数据还包含非线性结构与离群点。传统PCA无法提取非线性模式，核PCA虽可隐式映射到RKHS，但现有在线鲁棒核PCA算法假设子空间固定，一旦初始对齐完成，基于梯度下降的更新便失去继续跟踪变化的能力。

Method: 提出一种旋转式更新机制：在再生核希尔伯特空间（RKHS）中，通过将子空间估计向每个新到来的特征向量旋转来更新，而非仅依赖梯度下降。给出两种互补的旋转策略，并利用鲁棒影响函数对旋转幅度进行调节，以减轻离群点的影响。

Result: 在具有已知真实子空间的合成流式数据实验中，逐样本旋转比单纯梯度下降收敛更快，验证了该机制能有效动态跟踪流式数据中的非线性子空间。

Conclusion: 基于旋转的更新机制为在线鲁棒核PCA提供了一种有效的动态跟踪方案，克服了梯度下降在初始对齐后失效的问题，能够更好地应对数据漂移、流式到达、非线性及离群点等现实挑战。

Abstract: Machine learning models process large amounts of data, and Principal Component Analysis (PCA) is a widely used technique to reduce the dimensionality of the data and extract useful features. In practice, datasets often change over time (data drift) and/or arrive one sample at a time (streaming data), making it infeasible to process the entire dataset at once in batch mode. Real-world data also often contains nonlinear patterns, which traditional PCA cannot extract. Kernel PCA addresses this by implicitly mapping samples into a Reproducing Kernel Hilbert Space (RKHS). Raw data also often contains outliers, which can have an outsized effect on the estimated subspace unless the algorithm is made robust. However, existing online robust kernel PCA algorithms are designed to converge to a subspace that is assumed to be fixed, and gradient-descent-based updates lose their effectiveness at tracking further changes once this initial alignment is achieved. This paper introduces a rotation-based update mechanism, which updates the subspace estimate by rotating it toward each new incoming feature vector in Reproducing Kernel Hilbert Space, rather than relying on gradient descent alone. We present two complementary rotation strategies, and show that the extent of rotation can be moderated by a robust influence function to mitigate the effect of outliers. Through experiments on synthetic streaming data with a known ground-truth subspace, we show that per-sample rotations converge faster than gradient descent alone, demonstrating an effective mechanism for dynamically tracking a nonlinear subspace in streaming data.

</details>


### [186] [Same path, different: a mechanistic comparison of looped and stacked transformer encoders on 12-lead ECG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15498)
*Pawel Olszowiec,Michal Byra,Grzegorz Gruszczynski,Grzegorz Stefanski,Alberto Presta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文以 bViT 为例，在 PTB-XL 12 导联 ECG 分类任务上对照比较“权重共享循环”与“逐层堆叠”两种 Transformer 架构，发现二者在参数大幅减少下仍精度相当且潜表征几何相似，但动力学行为存在显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环 Transformer（复用同一权重而非堆叠 L 个独立层）因参数效率高而被广泛采用，但循环架构与堆叠架构在表征能力与动力学上的确切差异尚未被刻画。作者希望厘清这一区别。

Method: 在相同训练协议下，以 bViT（单一权重绑定块重复 L 次）和标准 ViT 为对象，在 PTB-XL 数据集的 12 导联 ECG 分类任务上进行受控对比训练，并利用几何相似度指标分析两者的潜表征，同时考察其动力学特性。

Result: 尽管 bViT 参数量减少约 8.9 倍，其精度与 ViT 持平；几何相似度指标显示两者构建的潜表征相当，且遵循等价的规范顺序。动力学方面差异明显：bViT 表现出更小的步长与患者间敏感性，且在远离数据流形处呈近中性行为；而 ViT 则出现表征维度塌缩与分布外特征扩张。

Conclusion: 循环与堆叠架构可构造可比的潜表征并达到相当精度，但动力学本质不同：bViT 更稳定、更保守，ViT 更易发生维度塌缩与分布外特征扩张。

Abstract: Recurrent Transformers reusing their weights rather than stacking $L$ distinct layers are becoming widely adopted due to their parameter efficiency [1,2,3]. However, the exact representational and dynamical differences between looped and stacked architectures remain uncharacterized. This paper presents a controlled study on the example of bViT model [1] applying one weight-tied block $L$ times. We train two models: bViT and standard ViT [4] on 12-lead electrocardiogram (ECG) classification tasks from the PTB-XL dataset under identical training protocols. Despite an $8.9\times$ parameter reduction, bViT achieves accuracy parity with ViT. Geometric similarity metrics demonstrate that both architectures construct comparable latent representations in an equivalent canonical order. Crucially, their dynamics differ: bViT exhibits smaller step sizes and inter-patient sensitivity, as well as near-neutral behavior away from the data manifold, whereas ViT exhibits collapsing dimensionality of representations and out-of-distribution feature expansion.

</details>


### [187] [End-to-End Verifiable and Robust Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15521)
*Doryan Lesaignoux,Enrique Mármol Campos,Gabriele Spini,José L. Hernández-Ramos,Stephan Krenn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种可验证联邦学习协议，将基于余弦相似度的离群点排除与选择性聚合过程公开可审计，在保持鲁棒性的同时实现聚合器的公共可验证性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习虽无需集中原始数据即可多方协同训练，但参与者或基础设施不可信时带来完整性风险。两个关键需求是：对投毒或拜占庭客户端更新的鲁棒性，以及对聚合器的可验证性（客户端或第三方可在不获知个体更新的前提下审计聚合结果）。现有工作多将二者割裂处理，且针对鲁棒性、排除离群点的聚合，高效公共可验证性仍很有限。

Method: 将鲁棒聚合流程设计为可公开审计：结合密码学承诺与非交互零知识证明，证明两件事——(i) 基于余弦相似度的离群点排除；(ii) 在所选集合上的聚合，同时不向验证者泄露任何单个客户端的更新。

Result: 在代表性投毒攻击下的实验中，方法保持高精度，所评估配置的平均精度损失低于 4%；验证开销实用，在研究所覆盖规模下证明工件可在数分钟内生成并完成验证。

Conclusion: 鲁棒离群点排除与公共可验证性可以在联邦学习场景中同时实现。

Abstract: Federated learning enables multiple parties to train a shared model without centralizing raw data with the help of an aggregator, but introduces integrity risks once participants or infrastructure are not fully trustworthy. Two requirements are particularly important: robustness to poisoned or Byzantine client updates, and verifiability of the aggregator so that clients or third parties can audit the reported aggregation without learning individual updates. Existing work has largely treated these goals separately, and efficient public verifiability for robust, outlier-excluding aggregation remains limited. We present a verifiable federated learning protocol that makes a robust aggregation pipeline publicly auditable. Our design combines cryptographic commitments with non-interactive zero-knowledge proofs to certify both (i) cosine-similarity-based outlier exclusion and (ii) aggregation over the selected set, without revealing individual client updates to verifiers. In experiments under representative poisoning attacks, our method maintains high accuracy, with an average accuracy loss below 4\% across the evaluated configurations, while keeping verification overhead practical: proof artifacts can be generated and verified within minutes at the scale studied. In summary, our results show that robust outlier exclusion and public verifiability can be jointly achieved in a federated learning setting.

</details>


### [188] [Beyond Noise: Understanding and Overcoming Temperature Effects in Analog DNN Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15527)
*Niklas Summ,Xiao Wang,Hendrik Borras,Bernhard Klein,Holger Fröning*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文基于模拟硬件实验平台，研究温度对模拟计算加速器非理想性及推理精度的影响，并比较多种仿真与硬件层面的缓解策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模拟计算的能效优势使其适合在移动与嵌入式等资源受限平台上部署高负载机器学习任务，但模拟加速器易受物理器件变异引起的噪声与非理想性影响，且这些效应随环境因素（如温度）变化，导致推理精度显著下降。因此有必要系统研究温度对模拟硬件非理想性及其推理精度的影响。

Method: 在代表性模拟硬件上开展全面实验研究：首先在多个工作温度下表征随机性与系统性非理想性的行为；随后比较一组基于仿真的缓解策略与基于硬件的缓解策略，评估其在温度引起的性能退化下的鲁棒性，涉及噪声感知训练、硬件在环训练以及温度感知校准等方法。

Result: 实验结果表明，温度引起的性能退化主要由系统性非理想性驱动，而非仅由随机噪声导致。噪声感知训练可提升鲁棒性；硬件在环训练与温度感知校准在不同热条件下提供了最强的精度保持能力。

Conclusion: 温度鲁棒性是模拟机器学习加速器实际部署中的关键问题，系统性非理想性是其主导因素；针对硬件特性与温度条件的训练与校准策略（特别是硬件在环训练和温度感知校准）能有效缓解温度引起的精度下降，为模拟加速器的可靠部署提供指导。

Abstract: The energy efficiency of analog computing makes it one of the most promising candidates for deploying resource-intensive machine learning workloads on constrained platforms such as mobile and embedded devices. However, analog accelerators are inherently susceptible to noise and non-idealities arising from physical component variations, whose behavior is further sensitive to environmental factors. These effects can significantly degrade inference accuracy. In this work, we conduct a comprehensive experimental study on a representative example of analog hardware to investigate the impact of temperature. We first characterize the behavior of stochastic and systematic non-idealities across a range of operating temperatures. Following this, we compare a set of simulation-based and hardware-based mitigation strategies aimed at improving robustness against temperature-induced performance degradation. Our results suggest that temperature-induced degradation is driven primarily by systematic non-idealities rather than stochastic noise alone. Noise-aware training improves robustness, while hardware-in-the-loop training and temperature-aware calibration provide the strongest accuracy retention across varying thermal conditions.

</details>


### [189] [The Misery of Mechanistic Interpretability: A Formal Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15533)
*Tobias Ladner,Matthias Althoff*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文首次为可解释替换网络（IRN）的忠实性提出形式化验证框架，通过可达性分析给出对抗场景下忠实性差距的可靠上界，并用验证感知训练显著收紧该界，从而为大型语言模型的机制可解释性提供首批形式化保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机制可解释性已成为理解前沿语言模型的主要视角，但模型内部结构复杂且本质为黑箱。可解释替换网络（IRN）虽能通过稀疏激活神经元暴露可解释特征，其忠实性通常仅在干净数据上经验性评估。作者发现即使语义上极其微小的输入扰动也会翻转主导 IRN 特征，从而改变人类可理解的解释，说明现有忠实性评估不可靠。

Method: 作者首先在五个开源权重模型家族（GPT-2 small、Gemma 2 2B、Gemma 3 1B、Llama 3.2 1B、R1-Distill-Qwen 1.5B）上实证展示微小扰动导致主导 IRN 特征翻转。随后提出首个针对 IRN 忠实性的形式化验证框架，利用可达性分析（reachability analysis）认证对抗场景下忠实性差距的可靠上界。此外，作者采用验证感知训练（verification-aware training）来收紧该认证界。

Result: 实证结果显示，语义上极小的输入扰动即可在五个模型家族中翻转主导 IRN 特征及其对应的人类可理解解释，暴露了经验性评估的脆弱性。可达性分析能够给出对抗场景下忠实性差距的可靠上界；验证感知训练则显著收紧该认证上界，恢复出安全审计人员可据以行动的特征级解释。

Conclusion: 该工作据作者所知，首次为大型语言模型的机制可解释性提供形式化保证。通过将可达性验证与验证感知训练结合，方法不仅揭示了经验评估的不足，还使 IRN 的忠实性获得可认证的上界，为安全审计提供更可靠的特征级解释基础。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability has become the dominant lens for understanding frontier language models, as their inner workings are complex and inherently black boxes. To gain insights into these models, interpretable replacement networks (IRNs) are trained at all layers, exposing interpretable features through sparsely activated neurons. However, the faithfulness of an IRN is usually evaluated only empirically on clean data, and we show that even semantically minor input perturbations flip the dominant IRN features-and thus the human-understandable interpretation-across five open-weight model families (GPT-2 small, Gemma 2 2B, Gemma 3 1B, Llama 3.2 1B, R1-Distill-Qwen 1.5B). We propose the first formal verification framework for the faithfulness of an IRN, where reachability analysis certifies a sound upper bound of the faithfulness gap in adversarial scenarios. Moreover, we show that verification-aware training of IRNs substantially tightens this certified bound, restoring a feature-level interpretation that safety auditors can act on. Together, these results give, to the best of our knowledge, the first formal guarantees for mechanistic interpretability of large language models.

</details>


### [190] [Specifying Reward Functions for RL Without Environment Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15544)
*Stephane Hatgis-Kessell,W. Bradley Knox,Emma Brunskill*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EARS：无需环境交互，通过LLM构建奖励特征并在想象轨迹上学习偏好权重，实现奖励函数规范


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 让人类利益相关者指定能产生期望结果的奖励函数是RL部署的关键挑战；现有偏好方法（如在线RLHF）需反复训练策略、采样真实轨迹并获取反馈，在环境交互昂贵或不安全时不可行

Method: 采用结构化LLM中介流程，从任务描述和观测空间构建少量表达性奖励特征，再在特征空间中策略性采样想象轨迹，并从想象轨迹对的偏好中学习特征权重

Result: 在三个长时程域（疫情封锁调控设计、糖尿病患者胰岛素管理、高速公路自动驾驶）上，无论使用真实偏好标签还是LLM标注偏好，EARS生成的奖励函数比直接提示LLM生成奖励函数的基线更贴近产生偏好的真实奖励函数或LLM上下文

Conclusion: 偏好式奖励规范在无环境采样下依然有效，可在收集真实轨迹昂贵或不可行的场景中实现实用奖励设计

Abstract: Enabling human stakeholders to specify reward functions that lead to their desired outcomes is a key challenge in deploying reinforcement learning agents. Preference-based methods such as online RLHF can reduce the burden of manual reward design, but they require repeatedly training policies, sampling trajectories from the real world, and eliciting feedback, making them impractical in settings where environment interaction is computationally expensive or unsafe. We introduce Experience-Free Autonomous Reward Specification (EARS), a method for learning reward functions from preferences without environment interaction. Our approach uses a structured LLM-mediated process to construct a small set of expressive reward features from a task description and the environment observation space, then strategically samples imagined trajectories in this feature space and learns feature weights from preferences over the imagined trajectory pairs. We evaluate on three long-horizon domains: pandemic lockdown regulation design, insulin administration for diabetes patients, and autonomous vehicle control on a highway. We compare EARS to baselines that also enable reward specification without environment interaction--namely, methods that directly prompt an LLM to generate a reward function. When learning from either ground-truth preference labels or preferences labeled by a LLM, EARS designs reward functions that are more aligned with the ground truth reward function that produced the preferences or LLM context than these baselines. These results suggest that preference-based reward specification remains effective without environment sampling, enabling practical reward design in settings where collecting real trajectories is costly or infeasible.

</details>


### [191] [The Token Before the Value Is the Key: How Hybrid Architectures Organize Induction Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15545)
*Ke Cheng,Xin Xu,Yixiao Chen,Lei Xin,Jianbo Zhao,Fanhu Zeng,Yue Liu,Jun Zhang,Jie Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过层级类型无关的成对探针（paired probes），追踪混合语言模型中"携带前驱信息（Carrying）"与"按内容匹配源（Matching）"两类归纳计算在异质层间的分配规律，发现Carrying集中于高效层、Matching集中于全局接收层，且局部贡献集中在滞后一位的token上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合语言模型在提升能力的同时也提升了效率，但架构互补性如何转化为可学习的计算尚不清楚。作者希望弄清归纳任务中三个既有角色（Carrying前驱信息、Matching按内容匹配源、Copying复制其值）在异质层（循环层/全局层、局部层/全局层）中如何被分配，以及架构先验与训练证据如何共同影响这些功能的形成时机。

Method: 提出层级类型无关（layer-type-agnostic）的成对探针，通过统一的块更新接口（block-update interface）同时追踪Carrying与Matching。在循环-全局与局部-全局两类混合架构中测量两类计算的层间分布。通过滞后一位掩码、移除卷积、提前降低学习率等干预改变前驱支持；通过源键恢复与固定值选择探测接收器对预备源的依赖。并变化局部窗口大小与归纳增强训练文本。

Result: 在循环-全局与局部-全局混合模型中，Carrying集中于高效层，Matching集中于全局接收层；局部贡献集中在滞后一位（即历史值紧邻的前一个token）。通过滞后一位掩码、卷积移除或早期学习率降低来改变前驱支持，可使Carrying与Matching在阶段间迁移；源键恢复与固定值选择揭示了接收器对预备源的依赖。这些干预也改变自然文本回忆效果，结果依赖配置与目标。变化局部窗口与归纳增强文本会改变功能性Carrying与Matching的早期发展。

Conclusion: 探针与干预将解释焦点上移：Matching的组织方式取决于Carrying如何被学习，而非相反。值前的那个token为混合架构、回路发育与回忆能力之间提供了具体联系。该结论将架构先验与训练证据连接到功能形成的时机。

Abstract: Hybrid language models can improve capability as well as efficiency, raising the question of how architectural complementarity becomes learned computation. We examine the established induction roles of Carrying predecessor information, Matching a source by content, and Copying its value. How are these position-sensitive and content-based computations allocated across heterogeneous layers? We introduce layer-type-agnostic paired probes that track Carrying and Matching through a common block-update interface. In recurrent--global and local--global hybrids, Carrying concentrates in efficient layers and Matching in global receivers. The measured local contribution concentrates on lag one: the token immediately before the historical value. Changing predecessor support through lag-one masking, convolution removal, or early learning-rate reduction can relocate Carrying and Matching between stages. Source-key restoration and fixed-value selection trace the receiver's dependence on the prepared source. These interventions also change natural-text recall, with outcomes depending on configuration and target. Varying local windows and induction-enriched training text changes the early development of functional Carrying and Matching, connecting architectural priors and training evidence to formation timing. Together, the probes and interventions shift the explanatory focus upstream: the organization of Matching follows how Carrying is learned. The token before the value provides a concrete link between a hybrid's architecture, circuit development, and recall. Code is available in https://github.com/ckpassenger/bind-match-copy/tree/main.

</details>


### [192] [Bayesian Optimisation Using Product-of-Experts Gaussian Process Models with Uncertainty Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15555)
*Yean Hoon Ong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出BO-pro-c，将带不确定性校准的专家乘积高斯过程（GP-pro-c）作为贝叶斯优化的代理模型，在多种BO设置下评估其性能，结果表明其优化性能具竞争力，且相较于单一全局GP的BO算法，简单遗憾降低0.9%、计算开销降低39.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统贝叶斯优化依赖单一全局高斯过程作为代理模型，其回归计算复杂度随训练点数呈三次方增长，限制了其在大规模优化问题中的适用性。GP-pro-c虽通过组合多个局部GP专家缓解了该问题，并在不确定性量化、计算成本和全局相关性保持方面具有优势，但其在BO中的应用尚未被充分研究。

Method: 提出BO-pro-c算法：以GP-pro-c（带不确定性校准的专家乘积高斯过程）作为贝叶斯优化的代理模型，在多样化的BO设置下对其性能进行评估。

Result: 实验结果显示，相对于基于单一全局GP模型的BO算法，BO-pro-c保持了具有竞争力的优化性能，同时实现简单遗憾降低0.9%、计算开销降低39.4%。

Conclusion: GP-pro-c可作为贝叶斯优化的有效代理模型，在维持优化性能的同时显著降低计算开销，具备在大规模优化问题中的应用潜力。

Abstract: Bayesian optimisation (BO) typically relies on a single global Gaussian process (GP) model as its surrogate model. However, GP regression has cubic computational complexity in the number of training data points, limiting its applicability to large-scale optimisation problems. The product-of-experts Gaussian process model with uncertainty calibration (GP-pro-c) mitigates this limitation by combining multiple local GP experts, enabling improved uncertainty quantification, reduced computational cost, and preservation of global correlations. Despite these desirable properties, the use of GP-pro-c in BO has not been thoroughly studied. This paper introduces BO-pro-c, a Bayesian optimisation algorithm that uses GP-pro-c as its surrogate model, and evaluate its performance across a diverse range of BO settings. Experimental results suggest that BO-pro-c maintains competitive optimisation performance while achieving a 0.9% reduction in simple regret and a 39.4% reduction in computational overhead relative to a BO algorithm based on a single global GP model.

</details>


### [193] [Multi-View Molecular Representation Learning with Hierarchical Graphs and Contextualized Fingerprints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15611)
*Gwang-Hyeon Yun,Jong-Hoon Park,Bing Hu,Helen Chen,Anita Layton,Young-Rae Cho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 HiFi-Mol 多视图分子预训练框架，分别预训练分层图编码器与上下文化指纹编码器，在下游微调时融合两种表示，在 MoleculeNet 脚手架划分下平均 ROC-AUC 提升 2.77%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子性质预测需从有限标注数据泛化到结构新颖的化合物。现有分子预训练方法多依赖单一视图：图方法建模原子-键拓扑，但缺乏片段级监督；指纹描述符编码化学模式，却通常仅作为固定辅助特征使用。两者信息互补但未被充分联合建模。

Method: HiFi-Mol 采用多视图框架，先分别预训练两个编码器再集成：(1) 图分支使用片段感知掩码（fragment-aware masking）与多分辨率监督，学习子结构感知表示；(2) 指纹分支对来自七个指纹家族的活性条目进行 token 化，并用掩码语言建模学习上下文化嵌入。微调阶段将投影后的多分辨率图特征与指纹嵌入拼接融合，用于下游预测。

Result: 在 MoleculeNet 基准的 scaffold split 下，八个分类任务的平均 ROC-AUC 较最优基线提升 2.77%，三个回归任务保持有竞争力的性能。分析表明片段感知掩码提升了图表示质量；分类结果显示出图与指纹变体各自的优势具有数据集依赖性，验证两视图提供互补的预测信号。

Conclusion: 分子预训练中显式分离并联合图拓扑与指纹化学模式两种视图，可有效提升有限标注下的泛化能力；图与指纹表示具有互补性，融合多视图是提升分子性质预测的可行方向。

Abstract: Molecular property prediction requires representations that generalize from limited labeled data to structurally novel compounds. Existing molecular pretraining methods often rely on a single view: graph-based approaches model atom-bond topology but provide limited fragment-level supervision, whereas fingerprint descriptors encode chemical patterns but are typically used as fixed auxiliary features. We propose HiFi-Mol, a multi-view framework that separately pretrains a hierarchical graph encoder and a contextualized fingerprint encoder before downstream integration. The graph branch uses fragment-aware masking with multi-resolution supervision to capture substructure-aware representations, while the fingerprint branch tokenizes active entries from seven fingerprint families and applies masked language modeling to learn contextualized embeddings. During fine-tuning, HiFi-Mol combines projected multi-resolution graph features with fingerprint embeddings for downstream prediction. Evaluated on MoleculeNet benchmarks under the scaffold split, HiFi-Mol achieves a 2.77% improvement in average ROC-AUC over the best baseline across eight classification tasks while maintaining competitive performance on three regression tasks. Further analyses reveal that fragment-aware masking improves graph representation quality, and classification results demonstrate dataset-dependent strengths of the individual graph and fingerprint variants, confirming that the two views provide complementary predictive signals.

</details>


### [194] [Where to Compute and How to Interact: Operator-Readable Adaptation with Gauge-Aware Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15620)
*Zixuan Shen,Quanxu Wan,Bingchuan Wang,Zhi Wang,Biao Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"算子可读性"原则，并据此设计了Gauge感知的自适应网格神经算子（GA-AMNO），通过物理信息驱动的自适应分配回答"在哪里计算"，通过几何条件低秩Gauge输运回答"如何交互"，在五个PDE基准上提升精度并验证分配与交互的各自作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自适应网格神经算子主要关注"在哪里计算"，而忽略了节点重定位后信息"如何交互"。网格自适应会改变局部采样尺度、邻域结构与几何上下文，使不同节点的表示不再直接可比，直接聚合会将物理变化与离散化引起的表示变化纠缠在一起。同时，分配与交互通过同一输出目标联合优化，仅从最终误差难以区分二者各自的作用。

Method: 提出"算子可读性"原则，要求自适应算子能够解释并检验为何在特定位置分配计算、以及在不均匀离散下表示如何交互。基于此提出GA-AMNO：物理信息驱动的自适应分配决定计算位置；几何条件低秩Gauge输运在聚合前将源特征映射到目标表示上下文。该方法使网格与求解器之间的信息交换可检视、可干预。作者建立了表示一致性聚合的充分条件，并分析了拓扑保持网格形变下的近似输运误差与连续性。

Result: 在五个PDE基准上取得更高的预测精度；受控干预与几何失配分析验证了分配与交互各自的作用，并表明Gauge输运在强几何失配下提升了跨离散化的表示兼容性。

Conclusion: 将自适应网格中的"分配"与"交互"解耦为可检验的两个环节，可提升神经算子预测性能，并增强其在不均匀离散下的表示一致性与可解释性。

Abstract: Adaptive meshes enable neural operators for partial differential equations (PDEs) to allocate spatial samples and computation according to local physical structures. Existing approaches, however, mainly address where to compute, with less attention to how information should interact after node relocation. Mesh adaptation changes local sampling scales, neighborhood structures, and geometric contexts, so representations formed at different nodes may not be directly comparable. Direct aggregation can therefore entangle physical variation with discretization-induced representation variation. Because allocation and interaction are jointly optimized through the same output objective, their individual roles are also difficult to distinguish from final errors alone. We introduce operator readability, requiring an adaptive operator to account for and test why computation is allocated to particular locations and how representations interact under the resulting nonuniform discretization. Based on this principle, we propose the Gauge-Aware Adaptive Mesh Neural Operator (GA-AMNO). Physics-informed adaptive allocation answers where to compute, while geometry-conditioned low-rank Gauge transport maps source features into target representation contexts before aggregation, answering how to interact. This makes mesh-to-solver information exchange inspectable and intervenable. We establish sufficient conditions for representation-consistent aggregation and analyze approximate transport errors and continuity under topology-preserving mesh deformations. Experiments on five PDE benchmarks demonstrate improved predictive accuracy, while controlled interventions and geometric-mismatch analyses verify the roles of allocation and interaction and show that Gauge transport improves cross-discretization representation compatibility under strong geometric mismatch.

</details>


### [195] [FedLTLib: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Federated Long-Tail Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15625)
*Changkun Lin,Junxiao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FL-LT基准平台FedLTLib，应对联邦学习中的"双重异质性"长尾分布问题，集成13种算法与多样化数据集，提供标准化评测框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动边缘网络中联邦学习数据由异构设备产生，呈现全局类别不平衡与本地统计偏斜叠加的长尾分布（即"双重异质性"），导致尾部类别性能严重下降。而现有FL-LT研究实验配置不一致、比较不公平，缺乏统一评测标准。

Method: 构建FedLTLib基准库：1）标准化评测框架，统一实验协议；2）纳入反映移动数据特征的多样化基准数据集；3）实现13种先进FL算法，含4种传统FL算法与9种FL-LT算法。

Result: 提供公平、可复现的评测环境，支持在统一协议下评估各算法在长尾分布下的鲁棒性与泛化能力。

Conclusion: FedLTLib为FL-LT研究提供标准化基准，推动移动计算生态中鲁棒智能的落地部署。

Abstract: Driven by the escalating demand for privacy-preserving computing, Federated Learning (FL) has witnessed remarkable progress, becoming a cornerstone technology for bridging distributed data silos in mobile edge networks. However, in real-world mobile computing environments, data is generated by heterogeneous mobile devices with varying user behaviors, leading to a significant Long-Tail Distribution. Unlike idealized balanced datasets, data in the wild manifests an acute imbalance where a minority of head classes dominate the sample space while a vast number of tail classes, often representing rare but critical edge-case events, are extremely scarce. This data heterogeneity, which we formally characterize as "Double Heterogeneity", referring to the superposition of global class imbalance and local statistical skew, precipitates severe performance deterioration on tail classes, thereby spurring the vital research direction of Federated Long-Tail Learning (FL-LT). To standardize evaluation and accelerate research in this field, we introduce FedLTLib, a comprehensive benchmark tailored for FL-LT. Addressing the critical issues of inconsistent experimental configurations and unfair comparisons in prior work, FedLTLib establishes a standardized evaluation framework. The platform not only incorporates diverse benchmark datasets reflecting mobile data characteristics but also implements 13 state-of-the-art FL algorithms (4 traditional FL algorithms and 9 FL-LT algorithms). By leveraging FedLTLib, researchers can perform fair and reproducible evaluations of algorithm robustness and generalization capabilities under a unified experimental protocol, ultimately advancing the deployment of robust intelligence in mobile computing ecosystems.

</details>


### [196] [Principal-timestep Restricted Init via Sparse Matrix-decomposition in Flow-matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15643)
*Jiayang Gu,Zheng Fang,Lichaun Xiang,Fanghui Liu,Xu Cai,Hongkai Wen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Prism-LoRA，通过主时间步选择与主通道过滤改进谱初始化，使梯度在流匹配扩散微调中更好地对齐，一致提升收敛速度与最终性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流匹配扩散模型微调成本高昂。LoRA 结合谱初始化在自回归语言模型中能加速收敛、提升性能，但在扩散微调中收益甚微甚至为负。作者归因于 LoRA 低秩参数化与流匹配目标固有的高秩梯度之间存在根本性错配：随机时间步采样引入方向异质的梯度信号，导致低秩约束下更新方向错位。

Method: 提出 Prism-LoRA（Principal-timestep Restricted Init via Sparse Matrix-decomposition）。包含两部分：(i) 主时间步选择，将初始化梯度限制在主导时间步子集内，以压低有效梯度秩；(ii) 主通道过滤，剔除任务无关通道，使单步谱初始化梯度与长程优化轨迹更对齐。

Result: 在多个扩散微调基准（主体驱动生成、可控生成、去模糊）上，方法一致提升收敛速度与最终性能，相对基线 LoRA 及现有谱初始化方法不仅性能更优，且收敛阶段更早。

Conclusion: 通过降低初始化梯度的有效秩并增强其与长程优化轨迹的对齐，Prism-LoRA 有效解决了扩散微调中谱初始化增益缺失的问题，为高效扩散微调提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Flow-matching diffusion models have recently emerged as a strong paradigm for high-fidelity visual generation. However, their prohibitively high fine-tuning cost limits scalability to downstream tasks. While Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) combined with spectral initialization has demonstrated accelerated convergence and improved performance in autoregressive language models by better aligning gradient directions, we find that it fails to deliver similar gains in diffusion fine-tuning, often yielding marginal or even negative improvements over vanilla LoRA.We attribute this discrepancy to a fundamental mismatch between LoRA's low-rank parameterization and the intrinsically high-rank gradients induced by the flow-matching objective. In particular, stochastic timestep sampling introduces directionally heterogeneous gradient signals across training steps, leading to misaligned updates under low-rank constraints.To address this issue, we propose Prism-LoRA,a Principal-timestep Restricted Init via Sparse Matrix-decomposition framework that improves gradient alignment during fine-tuning. Our method consists of two key components: (i) principal timestep selection, which restricts initialization gradients to a subset of dominant timesteps to suppress effective gradient rank, and (ii) principal channel filtering, which removes task-irrelevant channels, enabling the one-step spectral initialization gradient to better align with the long-horizon optimization trajectory. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method consistently improves both convergence speed and final performance across multiple diffusion fine-tuning benchmarks, including subject-driven generation, controllable generation, and deblurring, achieving not only performance improvement but also earlier stages of convergence over baseline LoRA and other spectral-init methods.

</details>


### [197] [Backward SDEs-based Diffusion for Physics-Constrained Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15702)
*Zihao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出"终端条件反演"框架，用BSDE将冻结的Score-SDE先验与任务终端可行性约束耦合，构造出有存在唯一性保证的反演映射，并用神经BSDE求解器实现，在稀疏视角CT重建上兼顾重建质量与严格测量可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练score-based扩散模型提供强无条件先验，但在逆问题中实现测量/物理一致性多依赖启发式引导、间歇投影或任务特定条件训练，推理结束时可行性与一致性缺乏保证。

Method: 给定冻结的Score-SDE先验与任务定义的终端可行性规格，构造相应后向随机微分方程（BSDE），其适应解定义从终端要求到指定噪声水平先验状态的原则性反演映射；在标准正则性条件下证明适应解的存在唯一性，并实现终端一致性。开发实用神经BSDE求解器，在不修改score定义系数的前提下组合任意预训练扩散先验与领域约束，输出锚定先验状态以支持邻域采样与不确定性刻画。

Result: 玩具数据集上验证了稳定的终端条件反演与分布一致的邻域采样；真实案例（稀疏视角CT重建）中，相较代表性免训练基线重建质量更优，同时在规定终端规格下满足严格测量可行性。

Conclusion: 将逆问题的可行性要求显式编码为终端条件并通过BSDE理论求解，可在不改动预训练扩散先验的前提下实现有理论保证的终端一致反演，并提供不确定性刻画，适用于医学成像等实际逆问题。

Abstract: Pretrained score-based diffusion models provide strong unconditional priors, yet enforcing measurement or physics consistency in inverse problems is often handled by heuristic guidance, intermittent projections, or task-specific conditional training, with limited guarantees of feasibility at the end of inference. We propose terminal-conditioned inversion for score-based SDE priors. Given a frozen Score-SDE prior and a task-defined terminal feasibility specification, we construct an associated backward stochastic differential equation whose adapted solution defines a principled inverse map from the terminal requirement to a prior state at a chosen noise level. Under standard regularity conditions, we establish existence and uniqueness of the adapted solution and obtain terminal consistency by construction. We further develop a practical neural BSDE solver that composes arbitrary pretrained diffusion priors with domain constraints without modifying the score-defined coefficients, producing an anchored prior state that enables neighborhood sampling for uncertainty characterization. Experiments on toy datasets validate stable terminal-conditioned inversion and distributionally consistent neighborhood sampling. As a real-world case study, we apply the framework to sparse-view CT reconstruction and achieve improved reconstruction quality over representative training-free baselines while satisfying strict measurement feasibility under the prescribed terminal specification. Project is available in: \href{https://laplacelab.github.io/BSDEDiffusion/}{https://laplace.center/icmlbsdeI/}

</details>


### [198] [Knowledge-Enriched Structured EHR Features for 30-Day Hospital Readmission Prediction on MIMIC-IV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15713)
*Mohamad Najafi,Hongyun Fu,Mathias Brochhausen,Jian Wu,Yaohang Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种不依赖临床文本的知识增强结构化特征表示，通过引入四类医学知识源提升30天再入院预测的可解释性与效率，AUROC达0.743，性能与临床文本模型相当但成本更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有30天再入院预测方法多依赖预训练语言模型处理出院小结，存在三大问题：需要临床文本、计算开销大、表示缺乏可解释性。作者希望在不使用临床笔记的前提下，获得性能可比且可解释、低成本的替代方案。

Method: 提出知识增强的特征表示，在结构化电子健康记录（EHR）数据基础上融合四类医学知识源：疾病本体映射、手术分类、药物成分词表、器官系统实验室指标聚合。每个特征维度对应一个具名临床概念，从而形成稀疏且可解释的患者表示。在MIMIC-IV v2.2队列上使用六种分类器进行评估，采用20折交叉验证。

Result: 最佳配置取得AUROC 0.743。该性能与既往在该数据集上报告的方法（包括仅用结构化数据和引入临床笔记的方法）相当，但计算成本显著更低。可解释性分析显示，人口统计学、器官系统实验室指标、药物成分特征以及一级本体疾病类别是预测的主要驱动因素，而更深层级本体的贡献可忽略。

Conclusion: 知识增强的结构化特征可作为临床笔记嵌入的具有竞争力且高效的替代方案，用于30天再入院预测。

Abstract: Recent approaches to 30-day hospital readmission prediction rely on pre-trained language models applied to discharge summaries. Although these methods achieve strong performance, they depend on the availability of clinical notes, incur substantial computational costs, and yield representations that lack interpretability. We propose a knowledge-enriched feature representation that augments structured Electronic Health Record (EHR) data with four medical knowledge sources: disease ontology mapping, procedure classification, drug ingredient vocabulary, and organ system laboratory aggregation, without using clinical notes. Each feature dimension corresponds to a named clinical concept, yielding a sparse and interpretable patient representation. The approach is evaluated with six classifiers on a MIMIC-IV v2.2 cohort. Under 20-fold cross-validation, the best configuration achieves an AUROC of 0.743. This performance is comparable to that of previously reported methods on this dataset, including both those using only structured data and those incorporating clinical notes, while requiring considerably less computational cost. Interpretability analysis shows that demographics, organ system labs, drug ingredient features, and first-level ontology disease categories drive prediction, while deeper hierarchy levels contribute negligibly. These findings indicate that knowledge-enriched structured features offer a competitive and efficient alternative to embeddings from clinical notes for 30-day readmission prediction.

</details>


### [199] [Assembling the CREW: A Collaborative Multi-agent Reinforcement Learning Framework for Automated Related Work Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15721)
*Hai-Dang Dang,Bao-Yen Pham,Bao Nguyen,Tran Thi Huong,Huynh Thi Thanh Binh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CREW框架，用IPPO优化的多智能体强化学习让LLM智能体动态选择动作（Retrieve、Disseminate、Compose、Critique），取代预设工作流，在RWG基准上提升生成质量并降低token成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动相关工作生成（RWG）可大幅减少撰写论文Related Work Section的人力与时间成本。但现有多智能体LLM方法依赖预定义工作流，每个智能体固定负责某一环节，这种刚性、静态的智能体间协调限制了合成复杂科学文献所需的自适应协作能力。

Method: 提出CREW（Collaborative Reinforcement Learning for Related Work Generation）框架：LLM智能体不再遵循启发式流水线，而是通过自主选择动作（Retrieve、Disseminate、Compose、Critique）实现动态协调；协调策略由独立近端策略优化（IPPO，Independent Proximal Policy Optimization）进行优化。

Result: 在标准RWG基准上的大量实验表明，相比强基线方法，该方法在生成质量上取得显著提升，同时大幅降低token开销。代码已开源（https://github.com/YenPBao/CREW-Collaborative-MARL.git）。

Conclusion: 以多智能体强化学习（IPPO）替代静态启发式流程，可实现LLM智能体对RWG任务的自适应协作，兼顾质量提升与成本控制，为相关工作自动生成提供了新的动态协调范式。

Abstract: Automatic Related Work Generation (RWG) significantly reduces the human time and effort required to author the Related Work Section (RWS) of a research paper. However, prior methods leveraging multi-agent Large Language Models (LLMs) typically rely on a predefined workflow, where each agent is responsible for a specific step in the entire process. This rigid, static inter-agent coordination limits the adaptive collaboration required to synthesize complex scientific literature. To address this limitation, we propose CREW (Collaborative Reinforcement Learning for Related Work Generation), a novel framework where LLM agents bypass heuristic pipelines to dynamically coordinate by autonomously selecting actions, such as Retrieve, Disseminate, Compose, and Critique, driven by a policy optimized via Independent Proximal Policy Optimization (IPPO). Extensive experiments on a standard RWG benchmark demonstrate that our approach yields substantial quality improvements over strong existing baselines, while significantly reducing token costs. Code is available at https://github.com/YenPBao/CREW-Collaborative-MARL.git

</details>


### [200] [Solving Finite-sum Coupled Compositional Optimization via Multi-block-Single-probe Estimator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15723)
*Wei Jiang,Sifan Yang,Yibo Wang,Lijun Zhang,Zechao Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文针对有限和耦合复合优化（FCCO）问题，提出Multi-block-Single-probe Variance Reduction (MSVR) 估计器，在每步仅能采样O(1)个块的随机值条件下高效追踪多块函数，并基于此设计算法，在多种凸性与PL条件下改进样本复杂度，还需在∇f_i线性时改善对m的依赖性，并在多任务深度AUC最大化上验证优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方差缩减方法（SPIDER、SARAH、STORM等）主要维护单个函数或梯度的估计序列。然而，在FCCO问题中需要追踪包含m个块的向量g(w)=(g_1(w),...,g_m(w))，而每步仅能访问O(1)个函数的随机样本。现有方法难以在部分块采样下有效追踪多块函数，因此需要新的方差缩减估计器。

Method: 提出MSVR（Multi-block-Single-probe Variance Reduction）估计器，用于在部分块采样条件下高效追踪g(w)。基于该估计器，为FCCO问题开发了若干算法，覆盖非凸、凸、强凸及Polyak-Łojasiewicz (PL)目标；当外层函数梯度∇f_i为线性时，进一步获得对m的改进依赖。

Result: 在非凸、凸、强凸和PL目标上均取得改进的样本复杂度；当∇f_i线性时进一步改善了对m的依赖性；在多任务深度AUC最大化上的实证研究验证了所提估计器的性能优于对比方法。

Conclusion: MSVR估计器有效解决了FCCO中部分块采样下多块函数追踪的方差缩减难题，并在理论与实验上均表现出优势，为多块耦合复合优化提供了新的高效求解框架。

Abstract: Traditional variance reduction methods (e.g., SPIDER, SARAH, STORM) have been extensively investigated for improving the convergence rates of stochastic optimization. These techniques typically maintain a sequence of estimators for a single function (or gradient) across iterations. However, what if we need to track multiple functions, but can only access stochastic samples of $\mathcal{O}(1)$ functions at each iteration? This scenario arises in an important emerging family of finite-sum coupled compositional optimization (FCCO) problems of the form $\frac{1}{m}\sum_{i=1}^m f_i(g_i(\mathbf{w}))$, where each $g_i$ is accessible only through a stochastic oracle. The key challenge is to track $\mathbf g(\mathbf{w})=(g_1(\mathbf{w}), \ldots, g_m(\mathbf{w}))$ over time, where $\mathbf g(\mathbf{w})$ has $m$ blocks but only $\mathcal{O}(1)$ blocks can be probed for their stochastic values at each step. To address this challenge, we propose a novel Multi-block-Single-probe Variance Reduction (MSVR) estimator to efficiently trace $\mathbf g(\mathbf{w})$ under partial block sampling. Building on the MSVR estimator, we develop several algorithms for FCCO problems, achieving improved sample complexities for non-convex, convex, strongly convex, and Polyak-Łojasiewicz (PL) objectives. We further obtain an improved dependence on $m$ when the outer function gradients $\nabla f_i$ are linear. Empirical studies on multi-task deep AUC maximization further demonstrate the superior performance of the proposed estimators.

</details>


### [201] [A Language-Guided Multimodal Foundation Model for Zero-Shot and Multi-Task Brain Signal Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15740)
*Mingzhi Chen,Yiyu Gui,Guibo Luo,Yuchao Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出METIS——一个面向脑信号分析的多模态基础模型，通过统一的"语言-信号"对齐框架实现零样本与多任务脑信号分析，在最大规模脑信号语料上预训练，零样本性能超越领先通用模型20.9%以上，并可媲美监督式任务专用模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑信号分析对神经科学与临床诊断至关重要，但现有方法存在明显局限：端到端模型需针对特定任务重训练且泛化能力弱；预训练模型缺乏语义深度、仍依赖大量微调；通用多模态基础模型虽在其他领域强大，却因表征错位与领域知识缺失而难以解读脑信号。

Method: 提出统一"语言-信号对齐"框架下的多模态基础模型METIS，在迄今规模最大、最具多样性的脑信号语料上预训练，该语料涵盖20个数据集、超过11,000名受试者、总时长逾70,000小时的记录。

Result: 在覆盖12个数据集的全面零样本评估中，METIS平均准确率超越领先通用模型20.9%以上；在无任何微调的情况下，其性能达到甚至超过有监督的任务专用模型；在少样本场景下平均AUROC优势超16.0%，跨数据集迁移中优势达15.9%，并展现出优异的数据效率与泛化能力。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了通用脑信号分析的新范式，为下一代神经技术奠定了基础。

Abstract: Brain signal analysis is essential for both neuroscience research and clinical diagnostics, yet current approaches face critical limitations. End-to-end models require task-specific retraining and exhibit limited generalization, while pre-trained models lack semantic depth and still depend on extensive fine-tuning. Meanwhile, general-purpose multimodal foundation models, though powerful in other domains, struggle to interpret brain signals due to representational misalignment and lack of domain knowledge. This study introduces a multimodal foundation model for zero-shot and multi-task brain signal analysis (METIS) through a unified language-signal alignment framework. METIS is pretrained on the largest and most diverse brain-signal corpus to date, comprising over 70,000 h of recordings from more than 11,000 subjects across 20 datasets. In a comprehensive zero-shot evaluation across 12 datasets, METIS outperformed the leading generalist model by over 20.9% in average accuracy. Remarkably, without any fine-tuning, METIS's performance matches or exceeds that of supervised, task-specific models. Furthermore, METIS demonstrates exceptional data efficiency and strong generalization, achieving an average AUROC advantage of over 16.0% in few-shot settings and 15.9% in cross-dataset transfer. This work establishes a new paradigm for general-purpose brain signal analysis, paving the way for next-generation neurotechnology.

</details>


### [202] [Sylvas: Synergistic Learning Value based Device Scheduling in Federated Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15763)
*Yuxuan Sun,Yuxuan Bai,Tan Chen,Sheng Zhou,Zhisheng Niu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Sylvas，一种面向无线边缘联邦持续学习（FCL）的基于协同学习价值的设备调度框架，从分布价值与标签价值两个维度评估设备数据价值，在资源约束下调度高价值设备以支持模型及时更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FCL 使全局模型能持续适应分布式、非平稳数据流，在智能交通、工业监测、无人系统等物联网场景中十分重要。在时空数据分布动态变化与标签稀缺条件下，关键挑战在于：如何量化每个边缘设备对全局学习性能的贡献，并在资源约束下调度最有价值的设备以实现模型的及时更新。

Method: Sylvas 从两个角度评估分布式数据的学习价值：一是分布价值（distributional value），从时空分布视角刻画设备数据对全局模型学习的贡献；二是标签价值（label value），刻画伪标注数据的数量与可靠性之间的权衡。将二者整合为协同学习价值（synergistic learning value）指标，据此在满足通信与计算资源约束的前提下调度高学习价值设备。

Result: 案例研究表明，Sylvas 能够在时空分布动态变化下支持模型的及时适应，并有效利用无标签数据。

Conclusion: Sylvas 通过协同学习价值度量实现 FCL 场景下的设备调度，兼顾分布贡献与标签质量，在资源受限的无线边缘环境下提升模型适应性与无标签数据利用效率。

Abstract: Federated continual learning (FCL) enables shared global models to continuously adapt to distributed and non-stationary data streams, making it important for Internet of Things applications such as intelligent transportation, industrial monitoring, and unmanned systems. Under spatio-temporal data distribution dynamics and label scarcity, a key challenge is how to quantify the contribution of each edge device to global learning performance and schedule the most valuable devices under resource constraints for timely model updating. This article presents Sylvas, a synergistic learning value based device scheduling framework for FCL at the wireless edge. Sylvas evaluates the learning value of distributed data from two perspectives: distributional value, which characterizes the contribution of device data to global model learning from a spatio-temporal distribution perspective, and label value, which captures the quantity and reliability tradeoff of pseudo-labeled data. By integrating these factors into a synergistic learning value metric, Sylvas schedules devices with high learning value while satisfying communication and computation resource constraints. Case studies demonstrate that Sylvas supports timely model adaptation under spatio-temporal distribution dynamics and effectively exploits unlabeled data.

</details>


### [203] [Transfer Learning for Socioeconomic Estimation in Forced-Displacement Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15773)
*Steven Ndung'u,Adel Daoud,Ismael Yacoubou Djima,Hai-Anh H. Dang,Patrick Michael Brock*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对被迫流离失所人群，将基于DHS数据预训练的多模态时空视觉Transformer迁移到南苏丹、喀麦隆和赞比亚的流离失所与收容社区情境，用UNHCR FDS/RMS社会经济指数评估，证明卫星地理协变量可解释营地相交网格中最高66%的社会经济差异（MAE 4.37），非营地相交区域41%（MAE 5.41）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 包容性家庭调查为被迫流离失所人群提供了关键的生活条件与福利基准，但成本高、周期长，在脆弱、冲突与暴力情境下轮次之间状况可能快速变化，存在时空数据缺口。面向一般人群的地球观测与机器学习工具尚未在流离失所情境中系统适配与评估，而该情境的居住条件、聚落模式与流离失所影响可能显著不同。

Method: 采用多模态时空视觉Transformer，先基于36个非洲国家约120万户家庭的DHS数据进行预训练，再迁移适配到南苏丹、喀麦隆和赞比亚的被迫流离失所与收容社区情境；以UNHCR FDS和RMS数据导出的社会经济指数作为标签，开发并评估更新后的适配模型，并以卫星导出的地理空间协变量进行预测。

Result: 卫星导出的地理空间协变量在营地相交网格中可解释高达66%的社会经济结果变异，MAE为4.37个指数点；在非营地相交区域可解释41%，MAE为5.41。

Conclusion: 该框架补充并增值于周期性家庭调查，通过定期更新、基于模型的社会经济估计填补关键的空间与时间数据空白，在调查轮次之间维持洞察，支持及时的人道主义优先排序与实地核查。

Abstract: Progress in inclusive household surveys has strengthened socioeconomic evidence for forcibly displaced populations, providing indispensable benchmarks on living conditions and welfare. However, these surveys remain resource-intensive and periodic, while conditions can change between rounds, particularly in settings affected by fragility, conflict, and violence. More frequently updated, spatially granular complementary evidence is therefore needed to identify where socioeconomic conditions may be changing between survey rounds and to inform operational prioritization. Earth observation and machine learning offer a scalable source of spatially explicit socioeconomic information. However, tools developed for general populations have not been systematically adapted and evaluated in forced displacement settings, where living conditions, settlement patterns, and displacement impacts may differ substantially. We address this gap by adapting a multimodal spatiotemporal vision transformer, pretrained on Demographic and Health Survey data from approximately 1.2 million households across 36 African countries, to forced displacement and host community settings in South Sudan, Cameroon, and Zambia. We develop and evaluate the updated, adapted model using socioeconomic indices derived from UNHCR FDS and RMS data. Our results show that satellite-derived geospatial covariates explain up to 66% of the variation in socioeconomic outcomes in camp-intersecting grids, with a mean absolute error (MAE) of 4.37 index points, and 41% in non-camp-intersecting areas, with an MAE of 5.41. The framework complements and adds value to periodic household surveys by filling critical spatial and temporal data gaps with regularly updated, model-based socioeconomic estimates. These estimates sustain insight between survey rounds and support timely humanitarian prioritization and field verification.

</details>


### [204] [MoveBench: A Benchmark for Global-Scale Wildlife Movement Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15780)
*Justin Kay,Shir Bar,Ellen O. Aikens,Martin Becker,Francesca Cagnacci,Juliet Cohen,Scott W. Forrest,Jessica Kendall-Bar,Madeleine Lucas,Macon Overcast,Meredith S. Palmer,Will Rogers,Nicholas J. Russo,Christian Rutz,Larissa T. Beumer,Michael Brown,Ying-Chi Chan,Sarah C. Davidson,Diego Ellis Soto,Anne G. Hertel,Roland Kays,Benjamin Koger,Guram Mikaberidze,Thomas Mueller,Ruth Oliver,Thorsten Papenbrock,Robert Patchett,Jared A. Stabach,Dane Taylor,Scott W. Yanco,Sara Beery*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MoveBench——首个大规模野生动物移动概率预测基准，包含跨110个物种、127个国家的260万GPS位点及16亿环境栅格瓦片（160个协变量），并设计概率评估协议，系统评测四类方法后发现：现有方法对未来时间点的泛化优于对未见个体，深度学习并不稳定优于简单基线，环境协变量选择显著影响性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轨迹预测在人、车领域已有进展，但野生动物轨迹具有空间无约束、高度随机、受环境条件影响等独特挑战，缺乏标准化的大规模评测平台与适配概率性现象的评估协议，阻碍了该任务的比较与方法进步。

Method: 构建MoveBench基准：整合2.6M GPS位置（800+个体、110物种、127国家），配对1.6B环境栅格瓦片覆盖160个已知或假定影响移动的协变量；提出面向移动轨迹预测的概率评估协议，以克服点预测指标对随机现象的局限；在多个时空尺度上对四类方法家族开展全面实证评测。

Result: 评测揭示三点主要发现：(1) 现有预测方法对未来时间点的泛化优于对未见个体；(2) 深度学习方法并未一致优于更简单的基线；(3) 环境协变量的选择对性能有显著影响。

Conclusion: MoveBench为野生动物移动预测方法提供了标准化评估并奠定数据与协议基础，有助于推动这一生态学重要任务上的方法论进展。

Abstract: Understanding and predicting wildlife movement is critical for ecology and conservation. While trajectory forecasting has advanced for human and vehicle movement, wildlife trajectories present distinct challenges: they are unconstrained in space, highly stochastic, and influenced by environmental conditions. We introduce MoveBench, the first large-scale benchmark for probabilistic wildlife movement forecasting, containing 2.6M GPS locations from 800+ individuals across 110 species in 127 countries, paired with 1.6B environmental raster tiles capturing 160 covariates known or hypothesized to influence movement. We propose a probabilistic evaluation protocol for movement trajectory forecasts, addressing limitations of point-prediction metrics for inherently stochastic phenomena. Through comprehensive empirical evaluation of four method families across multiple temporal and spatial scales, we reveal that: (1) existing predictive methods generalize better to future timepoints than to unseen individuals, (2) deep learning approaches do not consistently outperform simpler baselines, and (3) environmental covariate selection significantly impacts performance. MoveBench enables standardized evaluation of movement forecasting methods and provides a foundation for methodological advances on this ecologically important task.

</details>


### [205] [Sharp Rates and a One-Line Correction for Spectral Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15825)
*Dier Tang,Jing Yee Tan,Guangyue Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文否定了"任务先验各向同性"这一传统假设，指出自监督特征迁移的风险只取决于任务协方差 Λ 在算子主奇异方向上的压缩与对齐程度，而非 Λ 是否各向同性，并据此给出可诊断、可修正的理论框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自监督编码器一次训练、冻结后被轻量探针复用于训练时未知的下游任务，实践者需要判断"现成特征何时足够好、何时需要修正"。CCA、HGR 最大相关与谱对比损失的总体最优解都返回跨视图依赖算子的前 k 个奇异子空间，其正当性依赖"各向同性"假设——若任务先验无方向偏好，则该子空间普适最优。作者指出该假设是错误的，需要重新刻画任务先验如何进入迁移风险。

Method: 将迁移风险归结为任务协方差 Λ=E[ΔΔ^T] 作用在算子主奇异方向上的压缩，核心不是 Λ 是否各向同性，而在于其偏好方向是否与算子谱序一致。证明匹配的双侧速率：最坏遗憾恰为 1−1/κ(Λ)，通过对齐系数 A_k 精化为 1−A_k，定位到前 2k 子空间，在谱间隙下退化为二阶，并证明任何任务无关表示都无法改进。进一步论证对齐是普遍现象：高维下不相干偏好相互抵消，T 个多样任务迫使 α=Õ(√(d_x/T))。统计量计算复杂度为 O(kd_x^2)；当信号显示失配时，对正对项做一行重加权即可证明恢复精确最优性。

Result: 所提诊断方法可在小规模标注预算下回答实践者的问题，并在任务库不足以支撑请求宽度时拒绝输出。在受控数据上，将遗憾从 0.86 降至 0.003；在 CIFAR-100 编码器上，正确预测无需修正。

Conclusion: 使自监督特征可迁移的是任务多样性而非对称性；迁移风险由任务先验与算子谱的对齐结构决定，可据此在小标注预算下进行诊断，并通过简单重加权在失配时恢复最优性。

Abstract: A self-supervised encoder is trained once, frozen, and reused through lightweight probes on tasks nobody named at training time; the practitioner's question is when the off-the-shelf features are good enough and when they need fixing. Canonical correlation analysis, HGR maximal correlation, and the population optimum of the spectral contrastive loss all return the top-$k$ singular subspace of a cross-view dependence operator, justified by isotropy: if the task prior has no directional preference, that subspace is universally optimal. We show isotropy is the wrong hypothesis. The prior enters the transfer risk only through the task covariance $Λ=\mathbb{E}[ΔΔ^\top]$, and only through its compression onto the operator's leading singular directions; what matters is not whether $Λ$ is isotropic but whether its preferred directions are ordered consistently with the operator's spectrum. We prove matching two-sided rates---worst-case regret is exactly $1-1/κ(Λ)$, refines to $1-A_k$ for an alignment coefficient $A_k$, localizes to the top-$2k$ subspace, becomes second order under a spectral gap, and is improvable by no task-agnostic representation---and show why alignment is generic: incoherent preferences cancel in high dimension, and $T$ diverse tasks force $α=\widetilde O(\sqrt{d_x/T})$, a quantitative account of why task diversity, not symmetry, makes self-supervised features transfer. The governing statistics cost $O(kd_x^2)$, and when they signal misalignment a one-line reweighting of the positive-pair term provably restores exact optimality. The result is a diagnostic that answers the practitioner's question from a small labelled budget and refuses when the task bank cannot support the width requested; on controlled data it takes a regret of $0.86$ down to $0.003$, and on a CIFAR-100 encoder it correctly predicts that no correction is needed.

</details>


### [206] [Per-Matrix Optimality Is Not Enough: Three-Level Optimization for Low-Rank LLM Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15838)
*Huicheng Zhang,Xiyao Feng,Ze-Tong Li,Chengkai Zhu,Xiao Shi,Xiwei Pan,Jinguo Liu,Ge Bai,Xin Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出三级链式优化框架（白化SVD、块级联合优化、端到端损失精修），仅用256条校准序列将Transformer压缩中的复合误差逐级消除，在LLaMA-7B 60%压缩下将WikiText-2困惑度从42.1降至11.4。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逐矩阵SVD截断在白化Frobenius范数下虽满足Eckart-Young最优，但各矩阵独立压缩产生的误差会经由Transformer块的非线性前向传播逐层累积，单矩阵最优并不等于块级或模型级最优。受量子多体方法中层次化变分优化启发，需将优化范围从单矩阵扩展到块乃至全模型。

Method: 构建三级优化链，均仅依赖256条校准序列，不使用指令或恢复数据：L1为白化SVD截断；L2为块级联合优化，将同一Transformer块内多个矩阵耦合求解；L3为端到端语言建模损失精修。逐步扩大优化作用域。

Result: 在LLaMA-7B 60%压缩下，WikiText-2困惑度沿链依次为42.1、19.1、11.4。块级阶段起正则化作用：跳过它会使PTB困惑度恶化24点，且额外端到端训练未能弥合该差距。增益在20%–80%压缩率、5种最大13B参数架构及分布内/分布外基准上均成立；但跨架构行使用各自专用配置，压缩率扫描未在统一协议下进行。增加校准数据后，跳过块级阶段变得有竞争力，揭示离线计算—数据权衡。

Conclusion: 仅在困惑度与压缩保真度上主张改进；下游任务准确率仍显著低于稠密模型。块级联合优化是误差累积的关键正则化环节，其价值随校准数据增加而下降，形成计算与数据的权衡。

Abstract: Per-matrix singular value decomposition (SVD) truncation is Eckart-Young optimal in the whitened Frobenius norm, but errors from independently compressed matrices compound through the block's nonlinear forward pass. Inspired in part by hierarchical variational optimization in quantum many-body methods, we introduce a three-level chain that widens optimization scope from individual matrices to Transformer blocks to the full model: whitened SVD~(L1), block-level joint optimization~(L2), and end-to-end language-modeling loss refinement~(L3), all from 256 calibration sequences, with no instruction or recovery data. On LLaMA-7B at 60% compression, the chain reduces WikiText-2 perplexity from 42.1 to 19.1 to 11.4. The block-level stage acts as a regularizer: skipping it worsens Penn Treebank (PTB) perplexity by 24 points, a gap that additional end-to-end training did not close in our experiments. Perplexity gains hold across 20-80% compression, five architectures up to 13B parameters, and both in-distribution and out-of-distribution benchmarks, though the cross-architecture rows use architecture-specific configurations and the ratio sweep was not run under one common protocol. With more calibration data, skipping the block-level stage becomes competitive, revealing an offline compute--data trade-off. We therefore claim improvements only in perplexity and compression fidelity; downstream accuracy remains well below the dense model.

</details>


### [207] [Task-Directed Residual AddUNet:Perfect-Reconstruction Routing for Full-Rate Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15857)
*Vikram R. Lakkavalli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将 AddUNet 及其全速率实现解释为完美重构（PR）系统，并提出残差全速率 PR 架构，用于任务导向的表征学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为将表征设计与重构设计解耦：让守恒性成为结构属性，而把学习集中于任务导向的路由。

Method: 证明受限加性 U-Net 的 survivor-skip 结构等价于临界采样多速率 PR 滤波器组；提出去除互补子带限制但保持 PR 的全速率形式；进一步提出残差全速率 PR 架构，将任务无关/冗余结构从任务面向的 survivor 中渐进路由出去并显式保留；对任意形状兼容的线性/非线性路由算子保证精确重构，不要求可逆、匹配合成滤波器组、重构损失或学习解码器；同时将保留残差输出的 identity-shortcut ResNet 识别为全速率 PR 系统。

Result: 实验验证线性可分因素的单通道路由达到机器精度；在 TIMIT 上，固定识别器与训练协议，所提前端将测试 PER 从 28.60±2.09% 降至 25.76±0.41%，并保持精确重构；说话人探测表明结构守恒本身并不蕴含任务特定不变性。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了 AddUNet 的 PR 解释与全速率 PR 泛化，提出残差全速率 PR 架构，实现结构守恒与任务导向路由解耦，并在语音识别任务上验证了有效性且保持精确重构。

Abstract: This paper establishes a perfect-reconstruction (PR) interpretation of AddUNet and its full-rate realization, and introduces a Residual Full-Rate PR architecture for task-directed representation learning. The survivor--skip structure of a constrained additive U-Net is shown to be exactly equivalent to a critically sampled multirate PR filter bank. The full-rate formulation removes the complementary-subband restrictions of the critically sampled system while preserving PR. A Residual Full-Rate PR architecture is then proposed to progressively route task-irrelevant, nuisance, or redundant structure away from the task-facing survivor while retaining the routed information explicitly. Exact reconstruction is guaranteed for arbitrary shape-compatible linear or nonlinear routing operators, without requiring invertibility, a matched synthesis bank, reconstruction loss, or learned decoder. The resulting architecture decouples representation design from reconstruction design: conservation is structural, while learning is devoted to task-directed routing. The same formulation identifies an identity-shortcut ResNet with its residual output retained as a full-rate PR system. Experiments verify exact single-channel routing of linearly separable factors to machine precision. On TIMIT, the proposed front-end improves test PER from $28.60\pm2.09\%$ to $25.76\pm0.41\%$ with the recognizer and training protocol held fixed, while maintaining exact reconstruction. Speaker probing further shows that structural conservation does not itself imply task-specific invariance.

</details>


### [208] [LLM-Based Schema-Aware Split Learning for Privacy-Preserving Mental Distress Prediction Across Heterogeneous Surveys](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15871)
*Md Khalid Syfullah,Alvi Ataur Khalil*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种模式感知的拆分学习框架，用LLM作为共享语义编码器统一异构心理调查问卷模式，在保护隐私的同时实现跨机构协作建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社会与生活方式复杂度上升导致心理困扰日益普遍，各机构积累了大量心理健康调查数据。协作分析可生成有效的可泛化预测模型，但隐私约束与调查设计差异（问题、量表、格式不同）阻碍了数据直接整合。

Method: 采用模式感知的拆分学习（SL）框架：以LLM作为共享语义编码器，将每条调查记录序列化为自然语言描述，把异构模式统一为通用格式；通过LoRA对LLM进行心理困扰评估微调，并将模型拆分到客户端与服务器。客户端仅保留原始问卷响应并运行轻量前端，原始记录不出机构；资源密集型主干在服务器运行，降低客户端算力需求。

Result: 使用LLaMA-3.2-3B-Instruct，仅2000条训练样本即达到平均ANLS 0.708，在9种设置中的8种优于联邦学习（FL），客户端计算量降低三个数量级，并能泛化到未见数据集。

Conclusion: 该框架实现了对异构心理健康调查数据的准确、隐私保护且资源高效的协作学习。

Abstract: Rising societal and lifestyle complexity has been linked to a growing prevalence of mental distress worldwide. Educational institutions, workplaces, clinics, etc. collect large volumes of mental health survey data to understand and reduce this burden. Collaborative analysis of such data could yield effective generalizable predictive models. Privacy constraints and varied survey designs (i.e., different questions, scales, and formats) hinder direct integration. We propose a schema-aware split learning (SL) framework that preserves privacy, using a large language model (LLM) as a shared semantic encoder to harmonize heterogeneous survey schemas across institutions. We serialize each survey record into a natural-language description, unifying disparate survey schemas into a common format. The LLM is fine-tuned for mental distress assessment via Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) and partitioned across client and server. Clients retain the raw survey responses locally and run only a lightweight front-end, so original records never leave the institution that collected them. The resource-intensive backbone runs on the server, minimizing client-side computation. Using LLaMA-3.2-3B-Instruct, the framework attains an average ANLS of 0.708 with only 2,000 training samples, surpasses federated learning (FL) in eight of nine settings, and cuts per-client computation by three orders of magnitude, while generalizing to unseen datasets. Overall, it enables accurate, privacy-preserving, and resource-efficient collaborative learning from heterogeneous mental health survey data.

</details>


### [209] [Learning Multimodal One-step Flow Policy via Value-weighted Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15883)
*Jaehun Shon,Jinha Choi,Jongwook Jeon,Jongmin Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 OptiFlow，将一步流策略学习建模为结构化样本分配问题，通过状态级熵最优传输耦合值感知参考流策略与高效一步策略，实现离线强化学习中多模态行为的稳定学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
