Motivation: 离线强化学习只能从固定数据集学习策略，而数据集常含多模态动作分布。流策略能自然刻画多模态行为，但学习高效的单步流策略困难：标准值引导易导致模式坍缩，或利用分布外区域的过估计偏差。

Method: OptiFlow 将单步流策略学习视为结构化样本分配问题，联合训练值感知的参考流策略与高效单步策略，并通过状态级熵最优传输耦合两者的动作样本。每个状态下用 critic 估计值定义蒸馏目标动作的优先级，动作距离代价保证几何上兼容的配对；避免直接对 critic 最大化，从而在分布内进行利用。

Result: 实验表明 OptiFlow 能有效捕捉最优多模态行为，并在多个离线 RL 基准上取得强劲性能。

Conclusion: 通过将单步流策略锚定到高价值、受数据集支持的模态，OptiFlow 在避免分布外发散风险的同时实现了分布内利用，为离线强化学习中的多模态策略学习提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Offline reinforcement learning aims to learn a policy solely from fixed datasets, which often contain multimodal action distributions. Flow policies can naturally represent such multimodal behaviors, but learning an efficient one-step flow policy remains challenging: standard value guidance often leads to mode collapse or exploits overestimation bias in out-of-distribution regions. To address this, we introduce One-step Flow policy via Optimal Transport (OptiFlow), a framework for one-step flow policy learning as a structured sample-allocation problem. OptiFlow jointly trains a value-aware reference flow policy and an efficient one-step policy, coupling their action samples through state-wise entropic optimal transport. For each state, critic-estimated values define the priority of distillation target actions, while the action-distance cost ensures geometrically compatible pairings. By avoiding direct critic maximization, our transport-guided approach enables in-distribution exploitation by anchoring the one-step policy to high-value, dataset-supported modes without the risk of out-of-distribution divergence. Experimental results demonstrate that OptiFlow effectively captures optimal multimodal behaviors and achieves strong performance across diverse offline RL benchmarks. Our code is available at https://github.com/Yonsei-DILLab/OptiFlow.

</details>


### [210] [Privacy-enhanced federated learning via asynchronous aggregation and local differential perturbation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15885)
*Zhen Zhong,Shini Yang,Liesheng Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出融合动态差分隐私、轻量同态加密与本地差分隐私的隐私增强联邦学习框架，并采用带版本控制的异步聚合策略。在CIFAR-10与Purchase-100上，ε=0.1时精度仍达82.6%，通信开销较FedAvg降低21.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式数据环境下安全协作训练面临隐私泄露风险，且传统联邦学习（如FedAvg）在严格隐私约束下精度下降明显、通信开销高，同时难以适配异步训练环境，亟需兼顾隐私保护、模型性能与可扩展性的方案。

Method: 构建隐私增强联邦学习框架，集成三类机制：动态差分隐私（DDP）、轻量级同态加密（HE）与本地差分隐私（LDP）；并引入带版本控制的异步聚合策略，以支持异步分布式训练下的参数聚合与一致性管理。

Result: 在CIFAR-10与Purchase-100基准数据集上验证：即使在严格隐私预算（ε=0.1）下，分类准确率最高可达82.6%；通信开销相较FedAvg降低21.3%。

Conclusion: 该框架在分布式机器学习场景中有效平衡了隐私保护与模型性能，为大规模分布式协同计算提供了可扩展的技术基础。

Abstract: This study proposes a privacy-enhanced federated learning framework to address secure collaborative training in distributed data environments. The framework integrates Dynamic Differential Privacy (DDP), lightweight Homomorphic Encryption (HE), and Local Differential Privacy (LDP) mechanisms to ensure data privacy protection during model training. Additionally, the framework employs an asynchronous aggregation strategy with version control to support distributed training in asynchronous environments. Experimental validation on the CIFAR-10 and Purchase-100 benchmark datasets demonstrates that the method maintains high classification accuracy (up to 82.6%) even under stringent privacy constraints (ε = 0.1), while reducing communication overhead by 21.3% compared to FedAvg. Experimental results demonstrate that this framework effectively balances privacy protection and model performance in distributed machine learning scenarios, providing a scalable technical foundation for large-scale distributed collaborative computing.

</details>


### [211] [Discrete Beckmann Transport Models for One-Step Language Modeling and Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15903)
*Sophia Tang,Shiyi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DBTM（离散Beckmann传输模型），基于时间无关流的自主动输运映射，可一步将空间任意点映射到单纯形顶点的固定点，无需教师模型蒸馏，在语言建模与推理任务上实现一步/少步生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散扩散与流模型是自回归语言模型的有力替代方案，但将多步采样压缩为少步通常需要蒸馏预训练教师模型，这带来两个问题：一是学生模型质量被教师模型上限所限制，二是需要昂贵的两阶段训练流程。

Method: 构建时间无关的流，其自主动输运映射可证明地一步将环境空间中任意点送至单纯形顶点上的固定点。该固定点性质由守恒方程刻画，其残差可直接从数据最小化，从而免除教师流与时间条件化。部分训练的地图等价于有限时间截断的流，生成即迭代该映射直至收敛到固定点。进一步将映射扩展为部分上下文插值形式，额外的函数评估充当细化步骤而非ODE积分步骤。

Result: 在语言建模与推理任务上，DBTM支持一步与少步生成，在生成质量和准确率上均优于离散扩散与连续流基线。

Conclusion: DBTM通过固定点守恒方程实现了无需教师蒸馏、无需时间条件化的离散生成框架，将采样压缩问题转化为迭代映射至固定点，并可用额外评估作细化，为高效少步离散生成提供了新路径。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion and flow models are a promising alternative to autoregressive language models, but compressing many-step sampling into fewer steps typically requires distilling a pretrained teacher model. This caps the student at the teacher's quality and requires a costly two-stage training pipeline. We introduce Discrete Beckmann Transport Models (DBTM), built on a time-independent flow whose autonomous transport map provably carries any point in the ambient space to a fixed point on the vertices of the simplex in a single step. We show that this fixed-point property is characterized by a conservation equation whose residual can be minimized directly from data, removing the requirement for a teacher flow and time conditioning. Under this construction, a partially trained map corresponds to the flow truncated at finite time, so generation reduces to iterating one map until it reaches a fixed point. We further extend the map to a partial-context interpolant where additional function evaluations act as refinement steps rather than ODE integration steps. On language modeling and reasoning tasks, DBTM enables one- and few-step generation that improves quality and accuracy over discrete diffusion and continuous flow baselines.

</details>


### [212] [Safe Meta-Reinforcement Learning via Information Space Reachability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15915)
*Zeyang Li,Sunbochen Tang,Navid Azizan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种安全元强化学习框架，通过在信息空间中定义安全价值函数并推导其自洽条件与贝尔曼方程，将其融入安全过滤与约束策略优化，实现适应过程中的安全保障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 元强化学习虽能使智能体以有限经验适应未见任务，但现实任务中的安全需求阻碍了其应用，而此前工作对此探索不足。

Method: 在同时刻画物理状态与智能体对潜在任务信念的"信息空间"中推理安全性；引入衡量智能体无限期避开不安全区域概率的安全价值函数；证明该函数满足自洽条件与贝尔曼方程，从而可经元强化学习习得；据此提出安全元强化学习算法，学习安全价值函数并用于安全过滤与约束策略优化。

Result: 在元强化学习基准上的实验验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 将安全约束显式纳入元强化学习的适应过程，通过信息空间中的安全价值函数建模，可同时保证可学习性与安全过滤能力，为安全元强化学习提供可行方案。

Abstract: Meta-reinforcement learning (meta-RL) enables agents to adapt to unseen tasks with limited experience. Despite its promise, the application of meta-RL in real-world tasks is hindered by safety requirements, which have been underexplored in prior work. In this paper, we propose a safe meta-RL framework that explicitly accounts for safety during adaptation. Our key insight is to reason about safety in the information space, which captures both the physical state and the agent's belief over the underlying task. Within this space, we introduce a safety value function that measures the probability of the agent avoiding unsafe regions indefinitely. We show that this function satisfies a self-consistency condition and a Bellman equation, which make it learnable via meta-RL. Based on this formulation, we develop a safe meta-RL algorithm that learns the safety value function and leverages it for safety filtering and constrained policy optimization. Experiments on meta-RL benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method.

</details>


### [213] [Privacy-Aligned Personalized Federated Learning with Compact Adaptation and Variable-Length Gaussian Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15950)
*Yilin Xu,Chun Hei Michael Shiu,Chih Wei Ling,Linqi Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对个性化联邦学习中记录级差分隐私与高维重复更新之间的结构性错配，提出"一次释放私有客户端上下文 + 固定系数空间重复适应"的方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 记录级差分隐私下，客户端特定变化是低维的，而训练却反复释放高维更新，二者存在结构性错配，导致隐私与通信开销过高。

Method: 将客户端上下文分解为生成器并一次性私有释放，把重复适应限制在固定系数空间；因子化生成器引入自适应优化几何以重塑噪声更新；并通过变长量化实现系数更新的高斯机制，其量化误差直接充当隐私扰动。

Result: 消融实验表明私有训练收益大多由径向演化保留；在 MNIST 和 CIFAR-10 上匹配或优于全模型私有适应，CIFAR-10 在 ε=16 时受保护上行链路减少 2.67 倍且未来客户端精度相当。

Conclusion: 通过降维、自适应几何与量化-扰动一体化，可在保持隐私效用的同时显著降低上行通信成本。

Abstract: Record-level differential privacy exposes a structural misalignment in personalized federated learning when client-specific variation is low-dimensional while training repeatedly releases high-dimensional updates. In this paper, we address this misalignment by releasing a private client context once and confining repeated adaptation to a fixed coefficient space. Beyond dimensionality reduction, the factorized generator induces an adaptive optimization geometry that reshapes noisy updates, and controlled ablations show that most of its private-training gain is retained by radial evolution. To further reduce the communication cost, we realize the Gaussian mechanism for coefficient updates directly through variable-length quantization with finite expected code length, so that the quantization error itself serves as the required privacy perturbation rather than extra distortion. Across MNIST and CIFAR-10, our design matches or outperforms full-model private adaptation across privacy budgets and client heterogeneity, while reducing protected uplink by a factor of 2.67 at \(\varepsilon=16\) on CIFAR-10 with comparable future-client accuracy.

</details>


### [214] [A Chosen Future Can Still Be Rewritten: Causal Writability in Video Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15980)
*Xingyun Wang,Haomin Zheng,Man Yuan,Leqian Yang,Ziming Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 视频模型生成物理错误运动时，并非"未学会"正确运动，而是"学会了却未能调用"；正确运动信号仍保留在模型内部，可通过低维编辑重新写入并控制生成视频，这一能力称为"因果可写性"（causal writability）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 厘清视频模型中物理错误运动的成因：是模型未能学习正确运动，还是学习了却无法有效调用。这一问题关系到模型内部物理表征的本质及其可干预性。

Method: 训练数据为红色质量块缓慢振荡、蓝色质量块快速振荡的视频，随后测试红色质量块呈现快速运动的冲突情形。在该冲突场景下，即使模型生成缓慢运动，也用由简单物理变量预测得到的低维编辑来尝试恢复正确的快速运动，并测量编辑在网络不同深度处的效果，识别"承诺闭合"边界。

Result: 在固定强度下发现清晰的深度边界：同一编辑在边界之前能改变视频，边界之后则无效，该闭合标志某一写入操作的"承诺"点。运动信号依然存在，更强的下游写入可恢复物理运动，但增益过大则出现过冲。早期的因果可写性可预测训练后期能被纠正的错误：这类错误在更多网络深度上可写，而持续存在的错误则不然。上述因果可写性及其锐利闭合现象在预训练的13亿参数视频模型中同样复现。

Conclusion: 物理错误源于正确运动的"调用失败"而非"学习缺失"，模型内部保留了可被激活的正确运动表征。因果可写性是一种可测量、可泛化的模型属性，跨模型规模与训练范式成立，为理解和修正视频模型的物理错误提供了新的干预视角。

Abstract: When a video model generates physically incorrect motion, did it fail to learn the correct motion, or did it learn it but fail to use it? We show the latter: the correct motion remains available inside the model and can still be made to control the generated video. We train on videos where red masses oscillate slowly and blue masses oscillate quickly, then test a red mass with fast observed motion. Even when the model generates slow motion in this conflicting case, a low-dimensional edit predicted from simple physical variables restores the correct fast motion. We call this ability causal writability. At fixed strength, we find a sharp depth boundary: the same edit changes the video before the boundary but not after it. This closure marks commitment for that write. The motion signal nevertheless remains, and a stronger downstream write can restore physical motion, while excessive gain overshoots. Early causal writability predicts which errors training later corrects: those errors are writable at more network depths than errors that persist. We reproduce both causal writability and its sharp closure in a pretrained 1.3B video model, supporting generality across model scale and training regime.

</details>


### [215] [The Router Within: Eliciting Native Skill Routing from a Frozen LLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15982)
*Ruishuo Chen,Xun Wang,Yu Chen,Zhuoran Li,Longbo Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Gavel（Glance And Verdict from a frozen LLM），一种无需在上下文中放入技能文本的技能路由方法：利用冻结的 agent LLM 自身前向传播中已蕴含的路由信号，仅训练两个线性映射即可读出。Gavel 先在安装时通过一次前向构建每个技能的紧凑表示库，并用"glance"步骤对全库打分；再对候选技能执行"verdict"，读取模型自身的似然与 yes/no 判定，并以专家乘积方式与 glance 融合。该方法在三个公开基准及新基准 SkillTraj（372 条模拟 agent 轨迹）上零样本迁移，性能优于需要额外 1.2B–16B 外部参数的渐进式披露与检索重排流水线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM agent 借助技能可突破参数化知识限制，但收益取决于能否选中正确技能。现有部署方案存在两类缺陷：一是把每个技能的元数据全部预加载进上下文，导致注意力分散且库规模受限；二是将选择过程移出上下文，同时也移出了 agent 自身能力范围。作者希望证明冻结的 agent LLM 前向传播中本就携带路由信号，从而在无技能文本入上下文的前提下实现高效、可扩展的技能路由。

Method: Gavel 分两步。第一步"glance"：用两个线性映射（唯一被训练的参数）将任务与各技能的中间层状态投影，并对整个技能库与安装时一次前向构建的紧凑 per-skill banks 打分，快速筛选候选。第二步"verdict"：恢复候选技能的完整前向传播，读取模型自身的似然与 yes/no 判断，再与 glance 分数以专家乘积（product of experts）方式融合。仅需训练一次即可零样本迁移。

Result: 在 Qwen3-32B 上，Gavel 优于渐进式披露（progressive disclosure）以及增加 1.2B–16B 外部参数的 retrieve-and-rerank 流水线：书面任务上最高领先 13.4 分，技能需求在 rollout 中途出现时最高领先 21.9 分。路由准确率随骨干模型增强而提升；在 bash-agent harness 中，同一 32B 模型于 Skill-Use 上触发正确技能的频率高于在 Codex 中运行的更大规模前沿模型。

Conclusion: 冻结的 agent LLM 自身前向传播已携带足够的技能路由信号，仅两个线性映射即可读出，无需技能文本进入上下文。Gavel 以极低参数开销实现全库可扩展路由，训练一次即可零样本迁移，并在书面任务、中途技能需求及真实 harness 场景中均超越使用大量外部参数的现有方法，且随骨干模型增强而持续受益。

Abstract: Skills extend an LLM agent beyond its parametric knowledge, and the gain they promise rests on picking the right one. Deployed harnesses route by preloading every skill's metadata into the context, which disperses the agent's attention and caps the library size. Retrieval pipelines move the selection out of the context, but also out of the agent's capability. We show that the frozen agent LLM already carries the routing signal in its own forward passes, and that two linear maps suffice to read it out with no skill text in the context. Gavel (Glance And Verdict from a frozen LLM) reads it in two steps. A glance projects the task's and each skill's mid-layer states through the two maps, the only parameters trained, and scores the full library against compact per-skill banks that one forward pass builds at installation. A verdict then resumes the shortlisted skills' forward passes and reads the model's own likelihood and yes/no judgment, fused with the glance as a product of experts. Trained once, Gavel transfers zero-shot to three public benchmarks and SkillTraj, our new benchmark of 372 simulated agent trajectories. On Qwen3-32B it outperforms progressive disclosure and retrieve-and-rerank pipelines that add 1.2B to 16B external parameters, by up to 13.4 points on written tasks and up to 21.9 when the need for a skill arises mid-rollout. Routing accuracy improves as the backbone does, and in a bash-agent harness the same 32B triggers the correct skill on Skill-Use more often than far larger frontier models running in Codex.

</details>


### [216] [Bellman Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15987)
*Zhuoqing Song,Haotian Xu,Xikun Zhang,Lidong Bing*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Bellman Policy Optimization（BPO），一种无需critic的策略优化方法，通过Bellman方程将Policy Mirror Descent重构为轨迹级目标，避免中间状态价值估计，并在数学推理基准上验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR可提升LLM推理能力，但现有方法往往需要估计中间状态的价值（critic），带来额外复杂度和不稳定性。作者希望借助Policy Mirror Descent与Bellman方程，设计一种无需critic且理论等价的方法。

Method: BPO从Policy Mirror Descent推导而来，针对带终止奖励的自回归生成场景，利用Bellman方程将PMD改写为轨迹级目标，从而无需估计中间状态价值。作者证明该重构目标与原PMD目标具有相同的唯一最优解，并通过近似该目标导出实用BPO损失，其失配校正权重为互补token概率的平滑比值。

Result: 在数学推理基准上的实验表明BPO具有有效性。

Conclusion: BPO提供了一种critic-free、理论上有依据的轨迹级策略优化方法，为RLVR场景下的LLM推理能力提升提供了新思路。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) improves the reasoning capabilities of large language models (LLMs). We introduce Bellman Policy Optimization (BPO), a critic-free method derived from Policy Mirror Descent (PMD). For autoregressive generation with terminal rewards, BPO uses the Bellman equations to reformulate PMD as a trajectory-level objective. The reformulation avoids estimating state values at intermediate states. We prove that it has the same unique optimal solution as the original PMD objective. We derive the practical BPO loss by approximating this objective. Its mismatch-correction weight is a smoothed ratio of complementary token probabilities. Experiments on mathematical reasoning benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of BPO.

</details>
